int main() {
    int n,w;
    cin >> n >> w;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (a[i] <= w) mp[a[i]] = true;
        for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++) {
            if (a[i] + a[j] <= w) mp[a[i] + a[j]] = true;
            for (int k = j + 1;k <= n;k++)
                if (a[i] + a[j] + a[k] <= w) mp[a[i] + a[j] + a[k]] = true;
        }
    }
    cout << mp.size() << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\Coupon\coupon.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k,x;
    long long ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> k >> x;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (k == 0) break;
        int use = min(k,a[i] / x);
        k -= use,a[i] -= use * x;
    }
    sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,greater<int>());
    if (k > 0) for (int i = k + 1;i <= n;i++) ans += a[i];
    else for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += a[i];
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\Distribution\distribution.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,mint = 1e9,start = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> t[i];
        if (t[i] < mint) mint = t[i],start = i;
    }
    ans[start] = t[start];
    for (int i = start + 1;i <= start + n;i++) {
        int cur = i > n ? i - n : i;
        int last = cur == 1 ? n : cur - 1;
        ans[cur] = min(t[cur],ans[last] + s[last]);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\K Swap\KSwap.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> a[i];
		v[i % k].push_back(a[i]);
	}
	for (int i = 0;i < k;i++) sort(v[i].begin(),v[i].end());
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		if (i % k == 1) curv++;
		b[++curb] = v[i % k][curv];
	}
	bool flag = true;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) flag &= (b[i] >= b[i - 1]);
	cout << (flag ? "Yes" : "No") << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\Many Segments\ManySegments.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int t,l,r;
		cin >> t >> l >> r;
		l *= 2,r *= 2;
		switch(t) {
			case 1: {a[i] = {l,r}; continue; }
			case 2: {a[i] = {l,r - 1}; continue; }
			case 3: {a[i] = {l + 1,r}; continue; }
			case 4: {a[i] = {l + 1,r - 1}; continue; }
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
			if (a[i].first <= a[j].second && a[j].first <= a[i].second) ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\Swiss-System Tournament\Swiss-SystemTournament.cpp: check ---
int check(char x,char y) {
    if (x == y) return 0;
    if (x == 'G' && y == 'P') return -1;
    if (x == 'C' && y == 'G') return -1;
    if (x == 'P' && y == 'C') return -1;
    return 1;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\Swiss-System Tournament\Swiss-SystemTournament.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {
    if (x.score != y.score) return x.score > y.score;
    return x.id < y.id;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj1\Swiss-System Tournament\Swiss-SystemTournament.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
            cin >> a[i][j];
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) p[i] = {i,0};
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
            int id1 = p[2 * j - 1].id,id2 = p[2 * j].id;
            if (check(a[id1][i],a[id2][i]) == 1) p[2 * j - 1].score++;
            else if (check(a[id1][i],a[id2][i]) == -1) p[2 * j].score++;
        }
        sort(p + 1,p + 2 * n + 1,cmp);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) cout << p[i].id << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj2\Another Sigma Problem\AnotherSigmaProblem.cpp: calc ---
int calc(int x) {
	int ret = 0;
	while (x) {
		ret++;
		x /= 10;
	}
	return ret;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj2\Another Sigma Problem\AnotherSigmaProblem.cpp: qpow ---
long long qpow(long long a,long long b,long long p)
{
	long long ans = 1;
	while (b)
	{
		if (b & 1) ans = ans * a % p;
		a = a * a % p;
		b >>= 1;
	}
	return ans;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj2\Another Sigma Problem\AnotherSigmaProblem.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
		cin >> a[i];
		s[i] = (s[i - 1] + a[i]) % mod;
		len[i] = calc(a[i]);
	}
	for (int j = 2;j <= n;j++)
		ans += (s[j - 1] * qpow(10,len[j],mod) % mod + 1ll * a[j] * (j - 1) % mod) % mod;
	cout << ans % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj2\Robot Takahashi\RobotTakahashi.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,ans = 0; string s;
    cin >> n >> s;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].w;
        a[i].isAdult = (s[i - 1] == '1');
    }
    sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        child[i] = !a[i].isAdult ? child[i - 1] + 1 : child[i - 1], 
        adult[n - i + 1] = a[n - i + 1].isAdult ? adult[n - i + 2] + 1 : adult[n - i + 2];
    ans = max(ans,max(child[n],adult[1]));
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (a[i].w != a[i + 1].w) ans = max(ans,child[i] + adult[i + 1]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

int main() {
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	s = ' ' + s;
	if (s[1] == '1') {cout << "No" << endl; return 0;}
	flag[1] |= (s[7] - '0');
	flag[2] |= (s[4] - '0');
	flag[3] |= (s[2] - '0') | (s[8] - '0');
	flag[4] |= (s[1] - '0') | (s[5] - '0');
	flag[5] |= (s[3] - '0') | (s[9] - '0');
	flag[6] |= (s[6] - '0');
	flag[7] |= (s[10] - '0');
	for (int i = 1;i < 7;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= 7;j++)
			for (int k = i + 1;k < j;k++)
				if (flag[i] && flag[j] && (!flag[k])) {
					cout << "Yes" << endl;
					return 0;
				}
	cout << "No" << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj2\Vertical Reading\VerticalReading.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	string s,t;
	cin >> s >> t;
	int len = s.size();
	s = ' ' + s;
	for (int w = 1;w < len;w++)
		for (int c = 1;c <= w;c++) {
			string tmp = "";
			for (int i = 0;i * w + c <= len;i++)
				tmp += s[i * w + c];
			if (tmp == t) {cout << "Yes" << endl; return 0;}
		}
	cout << "No" << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj3\A - B problem\A-Bproblem.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int a,b;
	cin >> a >> b;
	int ans1 = (900 + a % 100) - b;
	int ans2 = (100 + a % 100) - b;
	int ans3 = (a / 100 * 100 + 90 + a % 10) - b;
	int ans4 = (a / 100 * 100 + 0 + a % 10) - b;
	int ans5 = (a / 10 * 10 + 9) - b;
	int ans6 = (a / 10 * 10 + 0) - b;
	int ans7 = a - (100 + b % 100);
	int ans8 = a - (b / 100 * 100 + 0 + b % 10);
	int ans9 = a - (b / 10 * 10 + 0);
	int ans = ans1;
	ans = max(ans, ans2);
	ans = max(ans, ans3);
	ans = max(ans, ans4);
	ans = max(ans, ans5);
	ans = max(ans, ans6);
	ans = max(ans, ans7);
	ans = max(ans, ans8);
	ans = max(ans, ans9);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj3\A^B^C\A^B^C.cpp: qpow ---
long long qpow(long long a,long long b,long long p) {
    long long res = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) res = res * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    long long a,b,c;
    cin >> a >> b >> c;
    a %= 10;
    if (a == 0 || a == 1 || a == 5 || a == 6) cout << a << endl;
    else if (a == 2 || a == 3 || a == 7 || a == 8) cout << qpow(a, qpow(b, c, 4) + 4, 10) << endl;
    else cout << qpow(a, qpow(b, c, 2) + 2, 10) << endl;

}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj3\Quadruple\Quadruple.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> k;
    k = k < 0 ? -k : k;
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int j = 2;j <= 2 * n - k;j++) {
        int i = j + k;
        ans += 1ll * calc(i) * calc(j);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\2025winter\hj3\log\log.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    long long n;
    cin >> n;
    long long L = 1, R = 2e9, k = 0;
    while (L <= R) {
        long long mid = L + (R - L) / 2;
        if (mid * (mid + 1) / 2 <= n + 1) {
            k = mid;
            L = mid + 1;
        } else {
            R = mid - 1;
        }
    }
    cout << n - k + 1 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\ARC-Architects.cpp: rand2 ---
int rand2(int a, int b){
  rand2_status = rand2_status * 1103515245ll + 12345;
  int x = rand2_status;
  if (x < 0) x = -x; // -2^31 sie nie zdarza :D
  x >>= 1;
  x = a + x % (b - a + 1);
  return x;
}

int random_test()
{
    return rand2(1, A);
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\ARC-Architects.cpp: decreasing_test ---
int decreasing_test()
{
    int tmp;
    if(bre == 0) {
        bre = rand2(0, (N - lib_n + 1 - len_sub));
        tmp = A;
        A -= rand2(0, (A - 1) / len_sub);
        len_sub--;
    }
    else {
        bre--;
        tmp = rand2(1, A);
    }
    return tmp;
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\ARC-Architects.cpp: increasing_test ---
int increasing_test()
{
    return bou - decreasing_test();
}

{

        printf("%s\n", com);
    exit(0);
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\ARC-Architects.cpp: wczytaj ---
int wczytaj()
{
    if(unlikely(init == 0))
        finish(ERROR, "Program zawodnika nie wywolal funkcji inicjuj!!!");
    if(unlikely(lib_n > N || is_end == 1))
        finish(ERROR, "Program zawodnika wywolal funkcje wczytaj po otrzymaniu informacji o koncu ciagu!!!");
    if(unlikely(lib_n == N))
        return 0;
    lib_n++;
    switch (TYP) {
      case 0:
        scanf("%d", &A);
        if(A == 0)
          is_end = 1;
        return A;
        break;
      case 1: return random_test(); break;
      case 2: return increasing_test(); break;
      case 3: return decreasing_test(); break;
      default:
              finish(ERROR, "Nieznany typ testu");
    }
    return -1;
}

        finish(ERROR, "Program zawodnika nie wywolal funkcji inicjuj!!!");
    printf("%d\n", jakoscProjektu);
    if(++con_k == K)
        finish(CORRECT, "");
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\ARC-Architects.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int k = inicjuj(),cur = 0;
    do a[++cur] = wczytaj(); while (a[cur] != 0);
    cur--;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) {
        while (!q.empty() && a[q.back()] < a[i]) q.pop_back();
        q.push_back(i);
        if (i >= cur - k + 1) wypisz(a[q.front()]),q.pop_front();
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\Cow-Frisbee-S.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!stl.empty() && h[stl.top()] <= h[i]) stl.pop();
		l[i] = stl.empty() ? 0 : stl.top();
		if (l[i]) ans += i - l[i] + 1;
		stl.push(i);
	}
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
		while (!str.empty() && h[str.top()] <= h[i]) str.pop();
		r[i] = str.empty() ? 0 : str.top();
		if (r[i]) ans += r[i] - i + 1;
		str.push(i);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n,k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        while (!qmin.empty() && qmin.front() + k <= i) qmin.pop_front();
        while (!qmin.empty() && a[qmin.back()] >= a[i]) qmin.pop_back();
        qmin.push_back(i);
        if (i >= k) cout << a[qmin.front()] << ' ';
    }
    cout << endl;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        while (!qmax.empty() && qmax.front() + k <= i) qmax.pop_front();
        while (!qmax.empty() && a[qmax.back()] <= a[i]) qmax.pop_back();
        qmax.push_back(i);
        if (i >= k) cout << a[qmax.front()] << ' ';
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\寻找段落.cpp: check ---
bool check(double x) {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i] - x;
    deque<int> q; // q 维护可选左端点的最小值
    for (int i = S;i <= n;i++) { // 枚举右端点，则左端点最多就是 i - S
        while (!q.empty() && q.front() + T < i) q.pop_front();
        while (!q.empty() && s[q.back()] >= s[i - S]) q.pop_back();
        q.push_back(i - S);
        if (!q.empty() && s[i] - s[q.front()] >= 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

--- 2026-7\单调队列与单调栈\寻找段落.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    double ans = -1e9;
    cin >> n >> S >> T;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        l = min((double)a[i],l);
        r = max((double)a[i],r);
    }
    while (r - l > eps) {
        double mid = (l + r) / 2.00;
        if (check(mid)) l = mid,ans = mid;
        else r = mid;
    }
    printf("%.3lf\n",ans);
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\多维dp\传纸条.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int m,n;
    cin >> m >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) cin >> a[i][j];
    memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0][1][1] = a[1][1];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n + m - 2;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= m && i + 2 - j > 0;j++) // 必须做越界约束
            for (int k = 1;k <= m && i + 2 - k > 0;k++) {
                long long val;
                if (j == k) val = a[j][i + 2 - j];
                else val = a[j][i + 2 - j] + a[k][i + 2 - k];
                dp[i][j][k] = max(max(dp[i - 1][j][k],dp[i - 1][j - 1][k]),max(dp[i - 1][j][k - 1],dp[i - 1][j - 1][k - 1])) + val;
            }
    cout << dp[n + m - 2][m][m] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\多维dp\多米诺骨牌.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,sum = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i] >> b[i];
        sum += a[i] + b[i];
    }
    memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0][0] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 6 * n;j++) {
            if (j >= a[i]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - a[i]]);
            if (j >= b[i]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - b[i]] + 1);
        }
    int minsub = 1e9,ans = 1e9;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 6 * n;i++) {
        if (abs(i - sum + i) < minsub)
            minsub = abs(i - sum + i),ans = dp[n][i];
        else if (abs(i - sum + i) == minsub) // 一样的话也要更新最少次数
            ans = min(ans,dp[n][i]);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\多维dp\榨取kkksc03.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,M,T;
    cin >> n >> M >> T;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> m[i] >> t[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j <= M;j++)
            for (int k = 0;k <= T;k++) {
                dp[i][j][k] = dp[i - 1][j][k];
                if (j >= m[i] && k >= t[i])
                    dp[i][j][k] = max(dp[i][j][k],dp[i - 1][j - m[i]][k - t[i]] + 1);
            }
    cout << dp[n][M][T] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\多维dp\海盗船.cpp: main ---

    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\多维dp\糖果.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    memset(dp[0],-0x3f,sizeof dp[0]);
    dp[0][0] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j < k;j++)
            dp[i][j] = max(dp[i - 1][j],dp[i - 1][((j - a[i]) % k + k) % k] + a[i]); // 选的话模后的结果必为正
    cout << dp[n][0] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\多维dp\糖果.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    memset(dp[1],-0x3f,sizeof dp[1]);
    dp[1][0] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        memset(dp[i + 1],-0x3f,sizeof dp[i + 1]);
        for (int j = 0;j < k;j++)
            dp[i + 1][j] = max(dp[i + 1][j],dp[i][j]), 
            dp[i + 1][(j + a[i]) % k] = max(dp[i + 1][(j + a[i]) % k],dp[i][j] + a[i]);
    }
    cout << dp[n + 1][0] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P552.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(1);
    vis[1] = true,dis[1] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (auto v : G[u])
            if (!vis[v]) {
                vis[v] = true;
                dis[v] = dis[u] + 1;
                q.push(v);
            }
    }
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P553.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	priority_queue<Edge> q;
	dis[1] = 0;
	q.push({1,dis[1]});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				q.push({v,dis[v]});
			}
		}
	}
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P554.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
	priority_queue<Edge> q;
	dis[a] = 0;
	q.push({a,dis[a]});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				q.push({v,dis[v]});
			}
		}
	}
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P554.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    for (int i = 0;i < N;i++) G[i].clear();
    mp.clear();
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v,c;
        cin >> u >> v >> c;
        mp[{u,v}] = c;
    }
    cin >> d;
    for (int i = 1;i <= d;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back({v,mp[{u,v}]}); G[v].push_back({u,mp[{u,v}]});
        mp.erase({u,v});
    }
    for (auto x : mp) {G[x.first.first].push_back({x.first.second,0}); G[x.first.second].push_back({x.first.first,0});}
    cin >> a >> b;
    dijkstra();
    cout << ((dis[b] == 0x3f3f3f3f) ? -1 : dis[b]) << endl;
    return ;
}

        if (n == 0 && m == 0) break;
        solve();
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P555.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(x);
    dis[x] = 0,vis[x] = 1;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = 0;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + w) continue;
            dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
            if (!vis[v]) q.push(v);
            vis[v] = 1;
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P555.cpp: rspfa ---
void rspfa() {
    memset(rdis,0x3f,sizeof rdis);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(x);
    rdis[x] = 0,rvis[x] = 1;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        rvis[u] = 0;
        for (auto [v,w] : rG[u]) {
            if (rdis[v] <= rdis[u] + w) continue;
            rdis[v] = rdis[u] + w;
            if (!rvis[v]) q.push(v);
            rvis[v] = 1;
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P555.cpp: main ---

        int a,b,t;
        cin >> a >> b >> t;
        G[a].push_back({b,t});
        rG[b].push_back({a,t});
    }
    spfa(); rspfa();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dis[i] + rdis[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P899.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(long long u,long long pa) {
    s[u] = 1;
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        s[u] += s[v];
        dp[u] += dp[v] + s[v] * w;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P899.cpp: change ---
void change(long long u,long long pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        ans[v] = ans[u] + (n - 2 * s[v]) * w;
        change(v,u);
    }
}

--- 2026-7\散题\P899.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        long long x,y,z;
        cin >> x >> y >> z;
        G[x].push_back({y,z});G[y]. push_back({x,z});
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    ans[1] = dp[1];
    change(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\Lost-Cows.cpp: query ---
int query(int id) {
    int ret = 0;
    while (id) ret += tree[id],id -= lowbit(id);
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\Lost-Cows.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) add(i,1);
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
        int l = 1,r = n;
        while (l <= r) {
            int mid = l + r >> 1;
            if (query(mid) > a[i]) r = mid - 1;
            else l = mid + 1;
        }
        ans[i] = l;
        add(l,-1);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

void add(int x,int y,int val) {
    for (int i = x;i <= n;i += lowbit(i))
        for (int j = y;j <= m;j += lowbit(j))
            tree[i][j] += val;
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\二维树状数组1.cpp: query ---
long long query(int x,int y) {
    long long ret = 0;
    for (int i = x;i > 0;i -= lowbit(i))
        for (int j = y;j > 0;j -= lowbit(j))
            ret += tree[i][j];
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\二维树状数组1.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    cin >> n >> m;
    int op;
    while (cin >> op) {
        if (op == 1) {
            int x,y,k;
            cin >> x >> y >> k;
            add(x,y,k);
        } else {
            int a,b,c,d;
            cin >> a >> b >> c >> d;
            cout << 1ll * query(a - 1,b - 1) - query(a - 1,d) - query(c,b - 1) + query(c,d) << '\n';
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\二维树状数组3.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int x,int y,int k) {
    a.add(x,y,k);
    b.add(x,y,k * x);
    c.add(x,y,k * y);
    d.add(x,y,k * x * y);
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\二维树状数组3.cpp: change ---
void change(int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2,int k) {
    modify(x1,y1,k);
    modify(x1,y2 + 1,-k);
    modify(x2 + 1,y1,-k);
    modify(x2 + 1,y2 + 1,k);
}

--- 2026-7\树状数组\二维树状数组3.cpp: sum ---
int sum(int x,int y) {
    int ret = (1ll * x * y + x + y + 1) * a.query(x,y);
    ret -= 1ll * (y + 1) * b.query(x,y);
    ret -= 1ll * (x + 1) * c.query(x,y);
    ret += d.query(x,y);
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\A-Simple-Problem-with-Integers.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\A-Simple-Problem-with-Integers.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1);
        tr[rid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1);
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\A-Simple-Problem-with-Integers.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val;
        tr[id].sum += val * (tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1);
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1ll;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,val);
    else add(lid,l,mid,val), add(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\A-Simple-Problem-with-Integers.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].sum;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Balanced-Lineup-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
    lg[1] = 0;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) stmax[i][0] = stmin[i][0] = h[i];
    for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
        for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
            stmax[i][j] = max(stmax[i][j - 1],stmax[i + (1 << (j - 1))][j - 1]),
            stmin[i][j] = min(stmin[i][j - 1],stmin[i + (1 << (j - 1))][j - 1]);
    while (q--) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        int k = lg[b - a + 1];
        cout << max(stmax[a][k],stmax[b - (1 << k) + 1][k]) - min(stmin[a][k],stmin[b - (1 << k) + 1][k]) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Hotel-G.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    tr[id].sum = tr[id].lmax = tr[id].rmax = r - l + 1;
    if (l == r) return ;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Hotel-G.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        if (tr[id].lazy == 1) {
            tr[lid].lazy = tr[rid].lazy = 1;
            tr[lid].sum = tr[lid].lmax = tr[lid].rmax = 0;
            tr[rid].sum = tr[rid].lmax = tr[rid].rmax = 0;
        }
        else if (tr[id].lazy == 2) {
            tr[lid].lazy = tr[rid].lazy = 2;
            tr[lid].sum = tr[lid].lmax = tr[lid].rmax = tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1;
            tr[rid].sum = tr[rid].lmax = tr[rid].rmax = tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1;
        }
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Hotel-G.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
    if (tr[lid].sum == tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1) // 左边都是空
        tr[id].lmax = tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1 + tr[rid].lmax;
    else tr[id].lmax = tr[lid].lmax;
    if (tr[rid].sum == tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1) // 右边都是空
        tr[id].rmax = tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1 + tr[lid].rmax;
    else tr[id].rmax = tr[rid].rmax;
    tr[id].sum = max(max(tr[lid].sum,tr[rid].sum),tr[lid].rmax + tr[rid].lmax); // 全左，全右，左右都有
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Hotel-G.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int flag) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        if (flag == 1) tr[id].sum = tr[id].lmax = tr[id].rmax = 0;
        else if (flag == 2) tr[id].sum = tr[id].lmax = tr[id].rmax = tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1;
        tr[id].lazy = flag;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,flag);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,flag);
    else add(lid,l,mid,flag), add(rid,mid + 1,r,flag);
    pushup(id);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Hotel-G.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r,int len) {
    if (l == r) return l;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    if (tr[lid].sum >= len) return query(lid,l,mid,len);
    else if (tr[lid].rmax + tr[rid].lmax >= len) return mid - tr[lid].rmax + 1;
    else return query(rid,mid + 1,r,len);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Light-Switching-G.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = 0;
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Light-Switching-G.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy) {
        tr[lid].lazy ^= 1;
        tr[rid].lazy ^= 1;
        tr[lid].sum = tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1 - tr[lid].sum;
        tr[rid].sum = tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1 - tr[rid].sum;
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Light-Switching-G.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy ^= 1;
        tr[id].sum = tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1 - tr[id].sum;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1ll;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r);
    else add(lid,l,mid), add(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\Light-Switching-G.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].sum;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】ST表.cpp: read ---
int read()
{
    int x=0,f=1;char ch=getchar();
    while (!isdigit(ch)){if (ch=='-') f=-1;ch=getchar();}
    while (isdigit(ch)){x=x*10+ch-48;ch=getchar();}
    return x*f;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】ST表.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    n = read(); m = read();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) a[i] = read();
    lg[0] = -1;
    for (int k = 1;k <= N;k++) lg[k] = lg[k >> 1] + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = a[i];
    for (int j = 1;j <= 20;j++)
        for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
            st[i][j] = max(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << (j - 1))][j - 1]);
    while (m--) {
        int l,r;
        l = read(); r = read();
        int k = lg[r - l + 1];
        cout << max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = a[l];
        tr[id].mx = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
    tr[id].mx = max(tr[lid].mx,tr[rid].mx);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1);
        tr[rid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1);
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int val) { // 区间修改
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val;
        tr[id].sum += val * (tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1);
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,val);
    else add(lid,l,mid,val), add(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) return tr[id].sum;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树1.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树1.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1);
        tr[rid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1);
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树1.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val;
        tr[id].sum += val * (tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1);
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,val);
    else add(lid,l,mid,val), add(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树1.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].sum;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树2.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r,tr[id].lzym = 1;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].sum = (tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum) % m;
    // 嗯对就是说再也不拷代码了啊啊啊，从max拷过来忘记初始化父亲sum了。我宝贵的一小时啊。
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树2.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if ((tr[id].lazy || tr[id].lzym != 1) && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lzym *= tr[id].lzym;
        tr[rid].lzym *= tr[id].lzym;
        tr[lid].sum *= tr[id].lzym;
        tr[rid].sum *= tr[id].lzym;
        tr[lid].lazy *= tr[id].lzym;
        tr[rid].lazy *= tr[id].lzym;

        tr[lid].lzym %= m,tr[rid].lzym %= m,tr[lid].sum %= m,tr[rid].sum %= m,tr[lid].lazy %= m,tr[rid].lazy %= m;

        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1);
        tr[rid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1);

        tr[lid].lazy %= m,tr[rid].lazy %= m,tr[lid].sum %= m,tr[rid].sum %= m;

        tr[id].lazy = 0,tr[id].lzym = 1;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树2.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val;
        tr[id].sum += val * (tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1);
        tr[id].lazy %= m,tr[id].sum %= m;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,val);
    else add(lid,l,mid,val), add(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树2.cpp: mul ---
void mul(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lzym *= val;
        tr[id].sum *= val;
        tr[id].lazy *= val; // 这里加法lazy也要加倍以正确下传
        tr[id].lzym %= m,tr[id].sum %= m,tr[id].lazy %= m;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) mul(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) mul(rid,l,r,val);
    else mul(lid,l,mid,val), mul(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\【模板】线段树2.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].sum % m;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return (query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r)) % m;
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作1.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) return ;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作1.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int k1,int k2) {
    if (tr[id].l == tr[id].r) {
        tr[id].mx = k2;
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    modify(k1 <= mid ? lid : rid,k1,k2);
    tr[id].mx = max(tr[lid].mx,tr[rid].mx);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作1.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].mx;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return max(query(lid,l,mid),query(rid,mid + 1,r));
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作2.cpp: build ---

}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作2.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].mx += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].mx += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作2.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val;
        tr[id].mx += val;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,val);
    else add(lid,l,mid,val), add(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].mx = max(tr[lid].mx,tr[rid].mx);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\序列操作2.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].mx;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return max(query(lid,l,mid),query(rid,mid + 1,r));
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\最大数maxnumber.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
    int k = lg[r - l + 1];
    return max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\最大数maxnumber.cpp: insert ---
void insert() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    n = (n + t) % d,a[++len] = n,st[len][0] = a[len];
    for (int j = 1;j <= lg[len];j++)
        st[len - (1 << j) + 1][j] = max(query(len - (1 << j) + 1,len - 1),a[len]);
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\策略游戏.cpp: init ---
void init(int n,int st[][M],int a[],bool op) {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = a[i];
    for (int j = 1;j <= 20;j++)
        for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++) {
            if (op) st[i][j] = max(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << (j - 1))][j - 1]);
            else st[i][j] = min(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << (j - 1))][j - 1]);
        }
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\策略游戏.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r,int st[][M],bool op) {
    int k = lg[r - l + 1];
    return (op ? max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]) : min(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]));
}

--- 2026-7\线段树\维护序列.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r,tr[id].lzym = 1;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].sum = (tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum) % p;
}

        tr[lid].lzym %= p,tr[rid].lzym %= p,tr[lid].sum %= p,tr[rid].sum %= p,tr[lid].lazy %= p,tr[rid].lazy %= p;

        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1);
        tr[rid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1);

        tr[lid].lazy %= p,tr[rid].lazy %= p,tr[lid].sum %= p,tr[rid].sum %= p;

        tr[id].lazy = 0,tr[id].lzym = 1;
    }
}

int query(int id,int l,int r) {

    else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\Cow-Exhibition-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    long long ans = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i] >> f[i];
    for (int j = 0;j <= 2 * DIFF;j++) dp[0][j] = -1e18;
    dp[0][DIFF] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        for (int j = 0;j <= 2 * DIFF;j++) { // 智商和
            dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
            if (j - s[i] >= 0 && j - s[i] <= 2 * DIFF) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - s[i]] + f[i]);
        }
    }
    for (int j = DIFF;j <= 2 * DIFF;j++) // 枚举正智商和
        if (dp[n & 1][j] >= 0) ans = max(ans,dp[n & 1][j] + j - DIFF);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\Cut-Ribbon.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int n;
    cin >> n >> w[1] >> w[2] >> w[3];
    memset(dp,-1,sizeof dp);
    dp[0] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 3;i++)
        for (int j = w[i];j <= n;j++)
            if (dp[j - w[i]] != -1) dp[j] = max(dp[j],dp[j - w[i]] + 1);
    cout << dp[n] << endl;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\Cut-Ribbon.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\Elimination.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int m,k;
    cin >> v[1] >> v[2] >> w[1] >> m >> k;
    w[2] = 1;
    memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0][0] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j <= 10000;j++) {
            dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
            if (j >= w[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = min(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i & 1][j - w[i]] + v[i]);
        }
    int ans = 1e9;
    for (int i = w[1] * m - k;i <= 10000;i++)
        ans = min(ans,dp[0][i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\Video-Game-Troubles-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,v,ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> v;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> p[i] >> G[i];
        for (int j = 1;j <= G[i];j++)
            cin >> gp[i][j] >> pv[i][j];
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) { // i 台游戏机
        for (int j = 0;j <= v;j++) f[j] = g[j]; // 上一个含游戏机价格的 g 就是初始值
        for (int j = 1;j <= G[i];j++) // j 个游戏
            for (int k = v;k >= gp[i][j];k--) { // 目前背包容量为 k
                f[k] = max(f[k],f[k - gp[i][j]] + pv[i][j]); // 01 背包
            }
        for (int j = 0;j <= v;j++)
            g[j] = max(g[j],f[j - p[i]]); // 更新 g，要么不选要么扣掉游戏机的钱选上
    }
    for (int i = 0;i <= v;i++)
        ans = max(ans,g[i]); // 预算以内求最大 (不要求刚好付完所以要循环)
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\多人背包.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Food x,Food y) {
    return x.c * y.b < x.b * y.c; // 排序后的顺序是最优的
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\多人背包.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int t,n;
    cin >> t >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].a;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].b;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].c;
    sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = t;j >= s[i].c;j--) // 用时
            dp[j] = max(dp[j],dp[j - s[i].c] + s[i].a - j * s[i].b);
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 0;i <= t;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\摆花.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    dp[0][0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j <= m;j++) {
            dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
            for (int k = 1;k <= a[i];k++) {
                if (j >= k) dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][j - k],dp[i][j] %= MOD;
            }
        }
    cout << dp[n][m] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\最佳课题选择.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> a[i] >> b[i];
    memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0][0] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) // i 个主题
        for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++) // 总共写了 j 篇论文
            for (int k = 0;k <= j;k++) { // 这个主题写了 k 篇
                long long t = a[i] * pow(k,b[i]);
                dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - k] + t);
            }
    cout << dp[m][n] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\竞赛真理.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,t;
    cin >> n >> t;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> v1[i] >> w1[i] >> v2[i] >> w2[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j <= t;j++) {
            dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
            if (j >= w1[i]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - w1[i]] + v1[i]);
            if (j >= w2[i]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - w2[i]] + v2[i]);
        }
    cout << dp[n][t] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\背包的第k优解.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int k,m,n,tail1 = 0,tail2 = 0;
    cin >> k >> m >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i] >> v[i];
    memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0][1] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = m;j >= w[i];j--) {
            for (int u = 1;u <= k;u++)
                st1[u] = dp[j - w[i]][u] + v[i],st2[u] = dp[j][u];
            tail1 = tail2 = 1;
            for (int u = 1;u <= k;u++)
                if (st1[tail1] > st2[tail2]) dp[j][u] = st1[tail1],tail1++;
                else dp[j][u] = st2[tail2],tail2++;
        }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) ans += dp[m][i];
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-7\背包问题\集合-Subset-Sums.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    int sum = n * (n + 1) / 2;
    if (sum % 2 != 0) {
        cout << 0 << endl;
        return 0;
    }
    dp[0][0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        for (int j = 0;j <= i * (i + 1) / 2;j++) { // 这一步的数字和
            dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
            if (j >= i) dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][j - i];
        }
    }
    cout << dp[n][sum / 2] / 2 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\Slope-Trick与WQS二分\Tree-I.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-8\Slope-Trick与WQS二分\Tree-I.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- 2026-8\Slope-Trick与WQS二分\Tree-I.cpp: kruskal ---
void kruskal() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= e;i++) {
        int x = a[i].s,y = a[i].t;
        if (find(x) == find(y)) continue;
        if (a[i].col == 0) cnt++;
        sum += a[i].c;
        Union(x,y);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\Slope-Trick与WQS二分\Tree-I.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> v >> e >> need;
    for (int i = 1;i <= e;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].s >> a[i].t >> a[i].c >> a[i].col;
        a[i].s++, a[i].t++;
    }
    int l = -100,r = 100,ans;
    while (l <= r) {
        int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
        for (int i = 1;i <= e;i++)
            if (a[i].col == 0) a[i].c += mid;
        for (int i = 1;i <= v;i++) f[i] = i;
        sort(a + 1,a + e + 1,[](Edge x,Edge y) {return x.c == y.c ? x.col < y.col : x.c < y.c;});
        cnt = 0, sum = 0;
        kruskal();
        if (cnt >= need) {
            l = mid + 1;
            ans = sum - need * mid;
        } else
            r = mid - 1;
        for (int i = 1;i <= e;i++)
            if (a[i].col == 0) a[i].c -= mid;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\Slope-Trick与WQS二分\序列-sequence.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        q.push(a[i]);
        if (q.top() > a[i]) {
            ans += q.top() - a[i];
            q.pop(); q.push(a[i]);
        }
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\Slope-Trick与WQS二分\种树.cpp: del ---
void del(int x) {
    a[a[x].pre].nxt = a[x].nxt,
    a[a[x].nxt].pre = a[x].pre, 
    vis[x] = true;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\Kuro-and-Topological-Parity.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    if (a[1] == -1 || a[1] == 0)
        dp[1][0][1][0] = 1, ans += (p == 1 && n == 1);
    if (a[1] == -1 || a[1] == 1)
        dp[1][1][0][0] = 1, ans += (p == 1 && n == 1);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\Sasha-and-Array.cpp: init ---
void init() {a[0][0] = a[1][1] = 1, a[0][1] = a[1][0] = 0;}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\Sasha-and-Array.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {sum[id] = sum[lid] + sum[rid];}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\Sasha-and-Array.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    sum[lid] = lazy[id] * sum[lid];
	lazy[lid] = lazy[id] * lazy[lid];
    sum[rid] = lazy[id] * sum[rid];
	lazy[rid] = lazy[id] * lazy[rid];
	lazy[id].init();
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\Sasha-and-Array.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
	lazy[id].init();
	if (l == r) {
		sum[id].a[0][0] = 1, sum[id].a[1][0] = 0;
        Matrix qp = qpow(p,a[l] - 1);
        sum[id] = qp * sum[id];
		return ;
	}
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	build(lid,l,mid);
	build(rid,mid + 1,r);
	pushup(id);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\Sasha-and-Array.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int L,int R,Matrix x) {
	if (L <= l && r <= R) {
		sum[id] = x * sum[id], lazy[id] = x * lazy[id];
	    return ;
	}
	pushdown(id);
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	if (L <= mid) add(lid,l,mid,L,R,x);
	if (R > mid) add(rid,mid + 1,r,L,R,x);
	pushup(id);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\【模板】矩阵快速幂.cpp: qpow ---
void qpow(Matrix a,int b) {
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a;
        a = a * a;
        b >>= 1;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\小白逛公园.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
    if (tr[lid].mxr < 0 && tr[rid].mxl < 0)
        tr[id].max = max(tr[lid].mxr,tr[rid].mxl);
    else
        tr[id].max = max(tr[lid].mxr,0ll) + max(tr[rid].mxl,0ll);
    tr[id].max = max(tr[id].max,max(tr[lid].max,tr[rid].max));
    tr[id].mxl = max(tr[lid].mxl,tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].mxl);
    tr[id].mxr = max(tr[rid].mxr,tr[rid].sum + tr[lid].mxr);
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\小白逛公园.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
	if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = tr[id].mxl = tr[id].mxr = tr[id].max = a[++cur];
		return ;
	}
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	build(lid,l,mid);
	build(rid,mid + 1,r);
	pushup(id);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\小白逛公园.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int x,int y) {
	if (l == r) {
		tr[id].sum = tr[id].mxl = tr[id].mxr = tr[id].max = y;
	    return ;
	}
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	if (x <= mid) modify(lid,l,mid,x,y);
	else modify(rid,mid + 1,r,x,y);
	pushup(id);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\矩阵加速（数列）.cpp: qpow ---
void qpow(Matrix a,int b) {
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a;
        a = a * a;
        b >>= 1;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\动态dp与dp套dp\矩阵加速（数列）.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> n;
    if (n <= 3) {
        cout << 1 << endl;
        return ;
    }
    memset(m.a,0,sizeof m.a);
    m.a[1][1] = m.a[2][1] = m.a[1][3] = m.a[3][2] = 1;
    memset(ans.a,0,sizeof ans.a);
    for (int i = 1;i <= 3;i++) ans.a[i][i] = 1;
    qpow(m,n);
    cout << ans.a[2][1] << endl;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\Candies.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		s[0] = dp[i - 1][0];
		for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++) s[j] = (s[j - 1] + dp[i - 1][j]) % mod;
		for (int j = 0;j <= k;j++)
			dp[i][j] = (s[j] - (j - a[i] > 0 ? s[j - a[i] - 1] : 0) + mod) % mod;
			// 理论上 s 减出来不会是负数，但 s 取模会导致其不单调递增 
	}
	cout << dp[n][k] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\Flowers.cpp: lowbit ---
int lowbit(int x) {return x & -x;}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\Flowers.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int val) {while (id <= n) tree[id] = max(tree[id],val), id += lowbit(id);}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\Flowers.cpp: query ---
int query(int id) {
    int ret = 0;
    while (id) ret = max(ret,tree[id]), id -= lowbit(id);
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\LUK-Triumphal-arch.cpp: init ---
void init(int u,int pa) {
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		son[u]++;
		init(v,u);
	}
	l = min(l,son[u]), r = max(r,son[u]);
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\LUK-Triumphal-arch.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa,int mid) {
	dp[u] = son[u] - mid;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u,mid);
		dp[u] += max(dp[v],0);
	}
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\LUK-Triumphal-arch.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);  
	}
	init(1,0);
	int mid = 0,ans = 0;
	while (l <= r) {
		mid = (l + r) >> 1;
		dfs(1,0,mid);
		if (dp[1] <= 0) r = mid - 1, ans = mid;
		else l = mid + 1;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\创世纪.cpp: dfs ---
int dfs(int u,bool flag) { // flag 为 1 则删去边不选，为 0 则必选 
	vis[u] = true;
	int minn = 1e9;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == rt) continue;
		dfs(v,flag);
		dp[u][0] += max(dp[v][0],dp[v][1]);
		// 限制的子节点至少一个没有被投放 
		minn = min(minn,max(dp[v][0],dp[v][1]) - dp[v][0]);
	}
	dp[u][1] = 1 + dp[u][0] - minn;
	if (flag && u == a[rt]) dp[u][1] += minn;
	return max(dp[u][0],dp[u][1]);
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\创世纪.cpp: solve ---
int solve(int u) {
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	for (rt = u;!vis[rt];rt = a[rt]) vis[rt] = true;
	int tmp = dfs(rt,0);
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	dfs(rt,1);
	return max(tmp,dp[rt][0]);
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\创世纪.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> a[i];
		G[a[i]].push_back(i); // 建反图，即被限制指向限制的 
	} 
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (!vis[i]) ans += solve(i);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\期末考试滑溜滑溜补习班.cpp: rnd ---
int rnd () {
	static const int MOD = 1e9;
	return Seed = ( 1LL * Seed * 0x66CCFF % MOD + 20120712 ) % MOD;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化1\跑路.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v); 
		dp[u][v][0] = true, dis[u][v] = 1; 
	}
	for (int len = 1;len <= 31;len++)
		for (int k = 1;k <= n;k++)
			for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
				for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
					if (dp[i][k][len - 1] && dp[k][j][len - 1])
						dp[i][j][len] = true, dis[i][j] = 1;
	for (int k = 1;k <= n;k++)
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
				dis[i][j] = min(dis[i][j],dis[i][k] + dis[k][j]);
	cout << dis[1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化2\The-Bakery.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r,int x) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].maxn = dp[x][l - 1];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid,x);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r,x);
    tr[id].maxn = max(tr[lid].maxn,tr[rid].maxn);
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化2\The-Bakery.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].maxn += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[rid].maxn += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化2\The-Bakery.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val;
        tr[id].maxn += val;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) modify(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) modify(rid,l,r,val);
    else modify(lid,l,mid,val), modify(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    tr[id].maxn = max(tr[lid].maxn,tr[rid].maxn);
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化2\The-Bakery.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].maxn;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return max(query(lid,l,mid),query(rid,mid + 1,r));
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化2\The-Bakery.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        pre[i] = last[a[i]] + 1, last[a[i]] = i;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        memset(tr,0,sizeof tr);
        build(1,1,n,i - 1);
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
            modify(1,pre[j],j,1);
            dp[i][j] = query(1,1,j);
        }
    }
    cout << dp[k][n] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\动态规划及其优化2\柠檬.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> s[i];
        
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++) a[j] = a[j - 1] + (s[j] == s[i]);
        for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++) // 朴素
            dp[i] = max(dp[i],dp[j - 1] + s[i] * a[i] * s[i] + s[i] * (a[j] - 1) * (a[j] - 1) - 2 * s[i] * a[i] * a[j]);
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\Bouquet.cpp: qpow ---
long long qpow(long long a,long long b,long long p) {
    long long ans = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\Bouquet.cpp: C ---
long long C(long long a,long long b) {
    long long ans = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= b;i++)
        ans *= (a - b + i), ans %= mod, 
        ans *= qpow(i,mod - 2,mod), ans %= mod;
    return ans; 
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\Cell-Distance.cpp: qpow ---
long long qpow(long long a,long long b,long long p) {
    long long ans = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\Cell-Distance.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    f[0] = g[0] = 1;
    int k = n * m - 2;
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) f[i] = f[i - 1] * i % mod;
    g[k] = qpow(f[k],mod - 2,mod);
    for (int i = k - 1;i >= 1;i--)
        g[i] = g[i + 1] * (i + 1) % mod;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\Cell-Distance.cpp: query ---
long long query(long long n,long long m) 
{return f[n] * g[m] % mod * g[n - m] % mod;}

        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\Large-Heap.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) p[i] = qpow(2,i,mod); // 提前处理，加快速度，减少不必要的运算量
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
            inv[i][j] = (p[i] - 1) * qpow(p[i] + p[j] - 2,mod - 2,mod) % mod;
    for (int i = n - b;i <= n;i++) dp[n - a - 1][i] = 1;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\[ZJOI]排列计数.cpp: init ---
void init(int u) {
    if (u > n) return ;
    siz[u] = 1;
    init(2 * u); init(2 * u + 1);
    siz[u] += siz[2 * u] + siz[2 * u + 1];
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\[ZJOI]排列计数.cpp: query ---
int query(int a,int b,int p) {
    if (b == 0) return 1;
    return fac[a % p] * qpow(fac[b % p] * fac[a % p - b % p] % p,p - 2,p) % p * query(a / p,b / p,p) % p;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\[ZJOI]排列计数.cpp: dfs ---
int dfs(int u) {
    if (u > n) return 1;
    return query(siz[u] - 1,siz[u * 2],m) * dfs(u * 2) % m * dfs(u * 2 + 1) % m;
}

int C2(int i) {return (1ll * i * (i - 1) / 2) % MOD;}

}

}

    g[mx] = qpow(f[mx],mod - 2,mod);

        g[i] = g[i + 1] * (i + 1) % mod;

    }
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\幸运数字.cpp: unique ---
void unique() {
    for (int u = 1;u <= cur1;u++) {
        bool flag = false;
        for (int v = 1;v <= cur2;v++)
            if (m[u] % unq[v] == 0) {
                flag = true;
                break ;
            }
        if (!flag) unq[++cur2] = m[u];
    }
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\幸运数字.cpp: gcd ---
int gcd(int x,int y) {return (x ? gcd(y % x,x) : y);}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\幸运数字.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int pos,int lcm,int x,int tmp) {
    if (lcm > x) return ;
    if (pos < 1) { // 越界，搜索出口
        if (tmp % 2) ret += x / lcm;
        else if (tmp) ret -= x / lcm;
        return ;
    }
    __int128 tmp2 = (__int128)lcm / gcd(lcm,unq[pos]) * unq[pos];
    if (tmp2 <= x) dfs(pos - 1,(long long) tmp2,x,tmp + 1);
    dfs(pos - 1,lcm,x,tmp);
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\幸运数字.cpp: calc ---
int calc(int x) {
    ret = 0;
    int pos = upper_bound(unq + 1,unq + cur2 + 1,x) - unq;
    dfs(pos - 1,1,x,0);
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\排列计数.cpp: qpow ---
long long qpow(long long a,long long b,long long p) {
    long long ans = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\排列计数.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    f[0] = g[0] = 1;
    int k = 2e6;
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) f[i] = f[i - 1] * i % mod;
    g[k] = qpow(f[k],mod - 2,mod);
    for (int i = k - 1;i >= 1;i--)
        g[i] = g[i + 1] * (i + 1) % mod;
    h[1] = 0,h[2] = 1,h[3] = 2;
    for (int i = 4;i <= k;i++) h[i] = (h[i - 1] + h[i - 2]) % mod * (i - 1) % mod;
}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\排列计数.cpp: query ---

    return ;
}

        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;

    }

}

--- 2026-8\排列组合\有趣的数列.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    for (int i = 2;i <= 2 * n;i++) {
        if (!flag[i]) prime[++cur] = i;
        for (int j = 1;j <= cur && prime[j] * i <= 2 * n;j++) {
            flag[prime[j] * i] = true; // 所有因子 prime[j] 的所有倍数都标记
            if (i % prime[j] == 0) break;
        }
    }
    return ;
}

        for (int j = c[i];j <= 1e5;j++)
            dp[j] += dp[j - c[i]];

--- 2026-8\排列组合\苹果树.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    // 组合数
    for (int i = 0;i <= n;i++) {
        c[i][0] = c[i][i] = 1;
        for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++)
            c[i][j] = (c[i - 1][j] + c[i - 1][j - 1]) % p;
    }
    // 阶乘
    fac[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        fac[i] = fac[i - 1] * i % p;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: create ---
int create(int id) {tr[++idx] = {0,0,1,(int)gen(),id}; return idx;}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {tr[id].sz = tr[tr[id].ls].sz + tr[tr[id].rs].sz + 1;}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: merge ---
int merge(int x,int y) {
    if (!x || !y) return x + y;
    if (tr[x].fix > tr[y].fix) {
        tr[x].rs = merge(tr[x].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
        return x;
    } else {
        tr[y].ls = merge(x,tr[y].ls);
        pushup(y);
        return y;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: split ---
void split(int id,int k,int &x,int &y) { // <= k 进 x, > k 进 y
    if (!id) {x = y = 0; return ;}
    if (tr[id].val <= k) {
        x = id;
        split(tr[id].rs,k,tr[id].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
    } else {
        y = id;
        split(tr[id].ls,k,x,tr[id].ls);
        pushup(y);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: split ---
void split(int id,int k,int &x,int &y) { // 前 k 个进 x
    if (!id) {x = y = 0; return;}
    if (tr[tr[id].ls].sz + 1 <= k) {
        x = id;
        split(tr[id].rs,k - tr[tr[id].ls].sz - 1,tr[id].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
    } else {
        y = id;
        split(tr[id].ls,k,x,tr[id].ls);
        pushup(y);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: insert ---
void insert(int x){ // 插入
    int a,b; split(rt,x,a,b);
    rt = merge(merge(a,create(x)),b);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: erase ---
void erase(int x){ // 删除一个 x
    int a,b,c;
    split(rt,x - 1,a,b); split(b,x,b,c);
    b = merge(tr[b].ls,tr[b].rs);
    rt = merge(merge(a,b),c);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: kth ---
int kth(int id,int k){ // 第 k 小，不用 split
    while(true) {
        int L = tr[tr[id].ls].sz;
        if(k <= L) id = tr[id].ls;
        else if(k == L + 1) return tr[id].val;
        else k -= L + 1, id = tr[id].rs;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: rnk ---
int rnk(int x){ // x 的排名
    int a,b;
    split(rt,x - 1,a,b);
    int res = tr[a].sz+1;
    rt = merge(a,b);
    return res;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: pre ---
int pre(int x) { // 前驱：小于 x 的最大值
    int a, b;
    split(rt,x - 1,a,b);
    int res = kth(a,tr[a].sz);
    rt = merge(a,b);
    return res;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\FHQ-Treap.cpp: nxt ---
int nxt(int x) { // 后继：大于 x 的最小值
    int a, b;
    split(rt,x,a,b);
    int res = kth(b,1);
    rt = merge(a,b);
    return res;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\Multiset.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int k,int val) {
    tr[id].sum += val;
    if (l == r) return ;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    if (k <= mid) modify(lid,l,mid,k,val);
    else modify(rid,mid + 1,r,k,val);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\Multiset.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r,int k) {
    if (l == r) return l;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    if (k <= tr[lid].sum) return query(lid,l,mid,k);
    else return query(rid,mid + 1,r,k - tr[lid].sum);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\Multiset.cpp: f ---
int f(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l == r) return l;
    int m = (l + r) >> 1;
    if (tr[lid].sum) return f(lid, l, m);
    return f(rid, m + 1, r);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\Multiset.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n,q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int a;
        cin >> a;
        modify(1,1,N - 5,a,1);
    }
    while (q--) {
        int k;
        cin >> k;
        if(k > 0) modify(1,1,N - 5,k,1);
        else modify(1,1,N - 5,query(1,1,N - 5,~k + 1),-1);
    }
    cout << (!tr[1].sum ? 0 : f(1,1,N - 5)) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\XOR的艺术.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].val = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].val = tr[lid].val + tr[rid].val;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\XOR的艺术.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (!tr[id].lazy) return ;
    tr[lid].val = tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1 - tr[lid].val;
    tr[rid].val = tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1 - tr[rid].val;
    tr[lid].lazy ^= 1, tr[rid].lazy ^= 1, tr[id].lazy = 0;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\XOR的艺术.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy ^= 1;
        tr[id].val = tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1 - tr[id].val;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) modify(lid,l,r);
	else if (l > mid) modify(rid,l,r);
	else modify(lid,l,mid), modify(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].val = tr[lid].val + tr[rid].val;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\XOR的艺术.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].val;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
	else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
	else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\XOR的艺术.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) a[i + 1] = s[i] - '0';
    build(1,1,n);
    while (m--) {
        int op,l,r;
        cin >> op >> l >> r;
        if (op == 0) modify(1,l,r);
        else cout << query(1,l,r) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】动态开点线段树.cpp: create ---
int create(int l,int r) {
    tr[++idx] = new seg_tree();
    tr[idx] -> l = l, tr[idx] -> r = r;
    return idx;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】动态开点线段树.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (!(tr[id] -> lazy) || tr[id] -> l == tr[id] -> r) return ;
    int mid = (tr[id] -> l + tr[id] -> r) >> 1;
    if (!lid) lid = create(tr[id] -> l,mid);
    if (!rid) rid = create(mid + 1,tr[id] -> r);
    tr[lid] -> lazy += tr[id] -> lazy;
    tr[lid] -> sum += tr[id] -> lazy * (tr[lid] -> r - tr[lid] -> l + 1);
    tr[rid] -> lazy += tr[id] -> lazy;
    tr[rid] -> sum += tr[id] -> lazy * (tr[rid] -> r - tr[rid] -> l + 1);
    tr[id] -> lazy = 0;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】动态开点线段树.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id] -> l && tr[id] -> r <= r) {
        tr[id] -> lazy += val, 
        tr[id] -> sum += 1ll * val * (tr[id] -> r - tr[id] -> l + 1);
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id] -> l + tr[id] -> r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) {
        if (!lid) lid = create(tr[id] -> l,mid);
        modify(lid,l,r,val);
    } else if (l > mid) {
        if (!rid) rid = create(mid + 1,tr[id] -> r);
        modify(rid,l,r,val);
    } else {
        if (!lid) lid = create(tr[id] -> l,mid);
        modify(lid,l,mid,val);
        if (!rid) rid = create(mid + 1,tr[id] -> r);
        modify(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    }
    tr[id] -> sum = (lid ? tr[lid] -> sum : 0) + (rid ? tr[rid] -> sum : 0);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】动态开点线段树.cpp: query ---
long long query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l <= tr[id] -> l && tr[id] -> r <= r) return tr[id] -> sum;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id] -> l + tr[id] -> r) >> 1;
    long long ret = 0;
    if (r <= mid) {
        if (!lid) return 0;
        ret = query(lid,l,r);
    } else if (l > mid) {
        if (!rid) return 0;
        ret = query(rid,l,r);
    } else {
        if (lid) ret += query(lid,l,mid);
        if (rid) ret += query(rid,mid + 1,r);
    }
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: create ---
int create(int id) {tr[++idx] = {0,0,1,(int)gen(),id}; return idx;}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {tr[id].sz = tr[tr[id].ls].sz + tr[tr[id].rs].sz + 1;}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: merge ---
int merge(int x,int y) {
    if (!x || !y) return x + y;
    if (tr[x].fix > tr[y].fix) {
        tr[x].rs = merge(tr[x].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
        return x;
    } else {
        tr[y].ls = merge(x,tr[y].ls);
        pushup(y);
        return y;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: split ---
void split(int id,int k,int &x,int &y) { // <= k 进 x, > k 进 y
    if (!id) {x = y = 0; return ;}
    if (tr[id].val <= k) {
        x = id;
        split(tr[id].rs,k,tr[id].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
    } else {
        y = id;
        split(tr[id].ls,k,x,tr[id].ls);
        pushup(y);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: insert ---
void insert(int x){ // 插入
    int a,b; split(rt,x,a,b);
    rt = merge(merge(a,create(x)),b);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: erase ---
void erase(int x){ // 删除一个 x
    int a,b,c;
    split(rt,x - 1,a,b); split(b,x,b,c);
    b = merge(tr[b].ls,tr[b].rs);
    rt = merge(merge(a,b),c);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: kth ---
int kth(int id,int k){ // 第 k 小，不用 split
    while(true) {
        int L = tr[tr[id].ls].sz;
        if(k <= L) id = tr[id].ls;
        else if(k == L + 1) return tr[id].val;
        else k -= L + 1, id = tr[id].rs;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: rnk ---
int rnk(int x){ // x 的排名
    int a,b;
    split(rt,x - 1,a,b);
    int res = tr[a].sz+1;
    rt = merge(a,b);
    return res;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: pre ---
int pre(int x) { // 前驱：小于 x 的最大值
    int a, b;
    split(rt,x - 1,a,b);
    int res = kth(a,tr[a].sz);
    rt = merge(a,b);
    return res;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: nxt ---
int nxt(int x) { // 后继：大于 x 的最小值
    int a, b;
    split(rt,x,a,b);
    int res = kth(b,1);
    rt = merge(a,b);
    return res;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】普通平衡树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    while (n--) {
        int opt,x;
        cin >> opt >> x;
        switch (opt)
        {
            case 1 : {
                insert(x);
                break;
            }
            case 2 : {
                erase(x);
                break;
            }
            case 3 : {
                cout << rnk(x) << endl;
                break;
            }
            case 4 : {
                cout << kth(rt,x) << endl;
                break;
            }
            case 5 : {
                cout << pre(x) << endl;
                break;
            }
            case 6 : {
                cout << nxt(x) << endl;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

long long sum(int x) {
    long long ans = 0;
    while (x > 0) ans += c[x],x -= lowbit(x);
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】线段树1.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
	tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
	if (l == r) {
		tr[id].sum = a[l];
		return ;
	}
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	build(lid,l,mid);
	build(rid,mid + 1,r);
	tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】线段树1.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
	if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
		tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
		tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
		tr[lid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1);
		tr[rid].sum += tr[id].lazy * (tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1);
		tr[id].lazy = 0;
	}
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】线段树1.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
	if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
		tr[id].lazy += val;
		tr[id].sum += val * (tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1);
		return ;
	}
	pushdown(id);
	int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1ll;
	if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,val);
	else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,val);
	else add(lid,l,mid,val), add(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
	tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\【模板】线段树1.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
	pushdown(id);
	if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].sum;
	int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
	if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
	else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
	else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\火柴排队.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {
	return x.num < y.num;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\火柴排队.cpp: merge ---
int merge(int a[],int l,int r) {
	if (l >= r) return 0;
	int mid = l + r >> 1;
	long long lcnt = merge(a,l,mid) % mod,rcnt = merge(a,mid + 1,r) % mod,cnt = 0,i = l,j = mid + 1,cur = l;
	while (i <= mid && j <= r) {
		if (a[i] <= a[j]) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = a[j++],cnt = (cnt + mid - i + 1) % mod;
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
    while (j <= r) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
	return (cnt + lcnt + rcnt) % mod;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础知识学习与应用\火柴排队.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> a[i].num;a[i].id = i;}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> b[i].num;b[i].id = i;}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp); sort(b + 1,b + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) c[a[i].id] = b[i].id;
	cout << merge(c,1,n) << endl;
	return 0;
}

	int ret = 0;
	while (id) ret += tree[id],id -= lowbit(id);
	return ret;

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\Why-Did-The-Cow-Cross-the-Road-I-P.cpp: solve1 ---
void solve1() {
	memset(tree,0,sizeof tree);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pos[b[i]] = i; // pos 代表 b[i] 在数组中的出现位置 
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) c[i] = pos[a[i]]; // 构造映射 a[i] 在 b 中的位置 
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		add(c[i],1);
		pa += i - query(c[i]); // 总数 i 减去 c[i] 前非逆序对个数 
	}
	ans = min(ans,pa);
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		pa += n - c[i], pa -= c[i] - 1;
		ans = min(ans,pa);
		// 若 a[i] 移动到最前面，则 c[i] 移动到最后面 
		// 则少了 n - c[i] 个逆序对，多了 c[i] - 1 个逆序对 
	}
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\Why-Did-The-Cow-Cross-the-Road-I-P.cpp: solve2 ---
void solve2() {
	memset(tree,0,sizeof tree);
	pa = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pos[a[i]] = i; // pos 代表 b[i] 在数组中的出现位置 
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) c[i] = pos[b[i]]; // 构造映射 a[i] 在 b 中的位置 
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		add(c[i],1);
		pa += i - query(c[i]); // 总数 i 减去 c[i] 前非逆序对个数 
	}
	ans = min(ans,pa);
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		pa += n - c[i], pa -= c[i] - 1;
		ans = min(ans,pa);
		// 若 a[i] 移动到最前面，则 c[i] 移动到最后面 
		// 则少了 n - c[i] 个逆序对，多了 c[i] - 1 个逆序对 
	}
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\Why-Did-The-Cow-Cross-the-Road-I-P.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	solve1();
	solve2();
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\数学计算.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r,tr[id].val = 1;
    if (l == r) return ;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\数学计算.cpp: change ---
void change(int id,int l,int r,int k) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
    	tr[id].val = k;
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) change(lid,l,r,k);
    else if (l > mid) change(rid,l,r,k);
    else change(lid,l,mid,k), change(rid,mid + 1,r,k);
    tr[id].val = (tr[lid].val * tr[rid].val) % m;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\数学计算.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].val % m;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return (query(lid,l,mid) * query(rid,mid + 1,r)) % m;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\数学计算.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	memset(tr,0,sizeof tr); 
	cin >> q >> m;
	build(1,1,q);
	for (int i = 1;i <= q;i++) {
		int op,x;
		cin >> op >> x;
		if (op == 1) change(1,i,i,x);
		else change(1,x,x,1);
		cout << query(1,1,i) << endl;
	}
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构基础练习题\神秘的703.cpp: qmax ---
int qmax(int id) {
	int ret = 0;
	while (id) ret = max(tree[id],ret),id -= lowbit(id);
	return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Flowers.cpp: query ---

    int ret = 0;
    while (id) ret = max(ret,tree[id]), id -= lowbit(id);
    return ret;

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Hotel-G.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    tr[id].sum = tr[id].lmax = tr[id].rmax = r - l + 1;
    if (l == r) return ;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Hotel-G.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        if (tr[id].lazy == 1) {
            tr[lid].lazy = tr[rid].lazy = 1;
            tr[lid].sum = tr[lid].lmax = tr[lid].rmax = 0;
            tr[rid].sum = tr[rid].lmax = tr[rid].rmax = 0;
        }
        else if (tr[id].lazy == 2) {
            tr[lid].lazy = tr[rid].lazy = 2;
            tr[lid].sum = tr[lid].lmax = tr[lid].rmax = tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1;
            tr[rid].sum = tr[rid].lmax = tr[rid].rmax = tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1;
        }
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Hotel-G.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
    if (tr[lid].sum == tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1) // 左边都是空
        tr[id].lmax = tr[lid].r - tr[lid].l + 1 + tr[rid].lmax;
    else tr[id].lmax = tr[lid].lmax;
    if (tr[rid].sum == tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1) // 右边都是空
        tr[id].rmax = tr[rid].r - tr[rid].l + 1 + tr[lid].rmax;
    else tr[id].rmax = tr[rid].rmax;
    tr[id].sum = max(max(tr[lid].sum,tr[rid].sum),tr[lid].rmax + tr[rid].lmax); // 全左，全右，左右都有
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Hotel-G.cpp: add ---
void add(int id,int l,int r,int flag) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        if (flag == 1) tr[id].sum = tr[id].lmax = tr[id].rmax = 0;
        else if (flag == 2) tr[id].sum = tr[id].lmax = tr[id].rmax = tr[id].r - tr[id].l + 1;
        tr[id].lazy = flag;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) add(lid,l,r,flag);
    else if (l > mid) add(rid,l,r,flag);
    else add(lid,l,mid,flag), add(rid,mid + 1,r,flag);
    pushup(id);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Hotel-G.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r,int len) {
    if (l == r) return l;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    if (tr[lid].sum >= len) return query(lid,l,mid,len);
    else if (tr[lid].rmax + tr[rid].lmax >= len) return mid - tr[lid].rmax + 1;
    else return query(rid,mid + 1,r,len);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\Lost-Cows.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) add(i,1);
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
        int l = 1,r = n;
        while (l <= r) {
            int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
            if (query(mid) > a[i]) r = mid - 1;
            else l = mid + 1;
        }
        ans[i] = l;
        add(l,-1);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}
