--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\小白逛公园.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
	if (tr[lid].mxr < 0 && tr[rid].mxl < 0)
		tr[id].max = max(tr[lid].mxr,tr[rid].mxl);
	else
		tr[id].max = max(tr[lid].mxr,0ll) + max(tr[rid].mxl,0ll);
	tr[id].max = max(tr[id].max,max(tr[lid].max,tr[rid].max));
	tr[id].mxl = max(tr[lid].mxl,tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].mxl);
	tr[id].mxr = max(tr[rid].mxr,tr[rid].sum + tr[lid].mxr);
	tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\小白逛公园.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
	if (l == r) {
		tr[id].sum = tr[id].mxl = tr[id].mxr = tr[id].max = a[++cur];
		return ;
	}
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	build(lid,l,mid);
	build(rid,mid + 1,r);
	pushup(id);
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\小白逛公园.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int x,int y) {
	if (l == r) {
		tr[id].sum = tr[id].mxl = tr[id].mxr = tr[id].max = y;
		return ;
	}
	int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
	if (x <= mid) modify(lid,l,mid,x,y);
	else modify(rid,mid + 1,r,x,y);
	pushup(id);
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: create ---
int create(int id) {tr[++idx] = {0,0,1,(int)gen(),id,false}; return idx;}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
    tr[id].sz = tr[tr[id].ls].sz + tr[tr[id].rs].sz + 1;
    if (tr[id].ls) tr[tr[id].ls].fa = id;
    if (tr[id].rs) tr[tr[id].rs].fa = id;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (!tr[id].rev) return ;
    tr[id].rev = false;
    swap(tr[id].ls,tr[id].rs);
    if (tr[id].ls) tr[tr[id].ls].rev = !tr[tr[id].ls].rev;
    if (tr[id].rs) tr[tr[id].rs].rev = !tr[tr[id].rs].rev;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: merge ---
int merge(int x,int y) {
    if (!x || !y) return x + y;
    pushdown(x); pushdown(y);
    if (tr[x].fix > tr[y].fix) {
        tr[x].rs = merge(tr[x].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
        return x;
    } else {
        tr[y].ls = merge(x,tr[y].ls);
        pushup(y);
        return y;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: split ---
void split(int id,int k,int &x,int &y) { // 前 k 个进 x
    if (!id) {x = y = 0; return;}
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[tr[id].ls].sz + 1 <= k) {
        x = id;
        split(tr[id].rs,k - tr[tr[id].ls].sz - 1,tr[id].rs,y);
        pushup(x);
    } else {
        y = id;
        split(tr[id].ls,k,x,tr[id].ls);
        pushup(y);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: rnk ---
int rnk(int x){ // x 的排名
    while (!st.empty()) st.pop();
    int cur = x;
    for (int cur = x;cur;cur = tr[cur].fa) st.push(cur);
    while (!st.empty()) {
        pushdown(st.top());
        st.pop();
    }
    int ret = tr[tr[x].ls].sz + 1;
    for (int cur = x;cur;cur = tr[cur].fa)
        if (cur == tr[tr[cur].fa].rs)
            ret += tr[tr[tr[cur].fa].ls].sz + 1;
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: reverse ---
void reverse(int l,int r) {
    int a,b,c;
    split(rt,l - 1,a,b);
    split(b,r - l + 1,b,c);
    tr[b].rev = !tr[b].rev;
    rt = merge(merge(a,b),c);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\排序机械臂.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> p[i].val;
        p[i].id = i;
    }
    sort(p + 1,p + n + 1,[](Input x,Input y) {return x.val != y.val ? x.val < y.val : x.id < y.id;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) rt = merge(rt,create(i));
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int rank = rnk(p[i].id);
        cout << rank << ' ';
        reverse(i,rank);
    }
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\永无乡.cpp: create ---
int create(int l,int r) {
    tr[++idx] = new seg_tree();
    tr[idx] -> l = l, tr[idx] -> r = r;
    return idx;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\永无乡.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (!(tr[id] -> lazy) || tr[id] -> l == tr[id] -> r) return ;
    int mid = (tr[id] -> l + tr[id] -> r) >> 1;
    if (!lid) lid = create(tr[id] -> l,mid);
    if (!rid) rid = create(mid + 1,tr[id] -> r);
    tr[lid] -> lazy += tr[id] -> lazy;
    tr[lid] -> sum += tr[id] -> lazy * (tr[lid] -> r - tr[lid] -> l + 1);
    tr[rid] -> lazy += tr[id] -> lazy;
    tr[rid] -> sum += tr[id] -> lazy * (tr[rid] -> r - tr[rid] -> l + 1);
    tr[id] -> lazy = 0;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\永无乡.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id] -> l && tr[id] -> r <= r) {
        tr[id] -> lazy += val, 
        tr[id] -> sum += 1ll * val * (tr[id] -> r - tr[id] -> l + 1);
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id] -> l + tr[id] -> r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) {
        if (!lid) lid = create(tr[id] -> l,mid);
        modify(lid,l,r,val);
    } else if (l > mid) {
        if (!rid) rid = create(mid + 1,tr[id] -> r);
        modify(rid,l,r,val);
    } else {
        if (!lid) lid = create(tr[id] -> l,mid);
        modify(lid,l,mid,val);
        if (!rid) rid = create(mid + 1,tr[id] -> r);
        modify(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    }
    tr[id] -> sum = (lid ? tr[lid] -> sum : 0) + (rid ? tr[rid] -> sum : 0);
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\永无乡.cpp: query ---
long long query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l <= tr[id] -> l && tr[id] -> r <= r) return tr[id] -> sum;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id] -> l + tr[id] -> r) >> 1;
    long long ret = 0;
    if (r <= mid) {
        if (!lid) return 0;
        ret = query(lid,l,r);
    } else if (l > mid) {
        if (!rid) return 0;
        ret = query(rid,l,r);
    } else {
        if (lid) ret += query(lid,l,mid);
        if (rid) ret += query(rid,mid + 1,r);
    }
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\食物链.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\食物链.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
	int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
	if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- 2026-8\数据结构进阶练习\食物链.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 3 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
		int op,x,y;
		cin >> op >> x >> y;
		if (x > n || y > n) {
			ans++;
			continue;
		}
		if (op == 1) {
			if (find(x) == find(y + n) || find(y) == find(x + n)) ans++;
			else {Union(x,y); Union(x + n,y + n); Union(x + 2 * n,y + 2 * n);}
		} else {
			if (find(x) == find(y) || find(y) == find(x + 2 * n)) ans++;
			else {Union(x,y + 2 * n); Union(x + n,y); Union(x + 2 * n,y + n);}
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\作诗.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    int len = (int) sqrt(n), bnum = (n + len - 1) / len;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pos[i] = (i - 1) / len + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= bnum;i++) {
        block[i].l = (i - 1) * len + 1, block[i].r = min(i * len,n);
        for (int j = block[i].l;j <= block[i].r;j++) num[i][a[j]]++;
        for (int j = 0;j <= c;j++) num[i][j] += num[i - 1][j];
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= bnum;i++) {
        for (int j = i;j <= bnum;j++) {
            ans[i][j] = ans[i][j - 1];
            for (int k = block[j].l;k <= block[j].r;k++) {
                box[a[k]]++;
                if (!(box[a[k]] % 2)) ans[i][j]++;
                else if (box[a[k]] != 1) ans[i][j]--;
            }
        }
        for (int j = 0;j <= c;j++) box[j] = 0;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\作诗.cpp: read ---
int read() {
	int x = 0,f = 1; char ch = getchar();
	while (ch < '0' || ch > '9'){if (ch == '-') f = -1; ch = getchar();}
	while (ch >= '0' && ch <= '9'){x = x * 10 + ch - 48; ch = getchar();}
	return x * f;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\作诗.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    n = read(); c = read(); m = read();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) a[i] = read();
    init();
    int lastans = 0;
    while (m--) {
        int l = read(),r = read();
        l = (l + lastans) % n + 1, r = (r + lastans) % n + 1;
        if (l > r) swap(l,r);
        lastans = solve(l,r);
        printf("%d\n",lastans);
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\小Z的袜子.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    int len = (int) sqrt(n), block_num = (n + len - 1) / len;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pos[i] = (i - 1) / len + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= block_num;i++)
        block[i].l = (i - 1) * len + 1, block[i].r = min(i * len,n);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\小Z的袜子.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int x,int y) {
    sum -= cnt[c[x]] * cnt[c[x]];
    cnt[c[x]] += y;
    sum += cnt[c[x]] * cnt[c[x]];
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\小Z的袜子.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    for (int i = 1,l = 1,r = 0;i <= m;i++) {
        ans[i].id = q[i].id;
        for (;r > q[i].r;r--) modify(r,-1);
        for (;r < q[i].r;r++) modify(r + 1,1);
        for (;l < q[i].l;l++) modify(l,-1);
        for (;l > q[i].l;l--) modify(l - 1,1);
        if (q[i].l == q[i].r) {
            ans[i].a = 0, ans[i].b = 1;
            continue;
        }
        int len = q[i].r - q[i].l + 1;
        ans[i].a = sum - len, ans[i].b = 1ll * len * (len - 1);
        long long gcd = __gcd(ans[i].a,ans[i].b);
        ans[i].a /= gcd, ans[i].b /= gcd;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\小Z的袜子.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
    init();
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> q[i].l >> q[i].r;
        q[i].id = i;
    }
    sort(q + 1,q + m + 1,[](Question x,Question y) {return pos[x.l] != pos[y.l] ? x.l < y.l : x.r < y.r;});
    solve();
    sort(ans + 1,ans + m + 1,[](Answer x,Answer y) {return x.id < y.id;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cout << ans[i].a << '/' << ans[i].b << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\弹飞绵羊.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    int len = (int) sqrt(n), block_num = (n + len - 1) / len;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pos[i] = (i - 1) / len + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= block_num;i++)
        block[i].l = (i - 1) * len + 1, block[i].r = min(i * len,n);
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
        if (i + a[i] >= block[pos[i] + 1].l)
            step[i] = 1, to[i] = i + a[i];
        else 
            step[i] = step[i + a[i]] + 1, to[i] = to[i + a[i]];
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\弹飞绵羊.cpp: query ---
int query(int x) {
    int ret = 0;
    while (x <= n) ret += step[x], x = to[x];
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\弹飞绵羊.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int x,int y) {
    a[x] = y;
    for (int i = block[pos[x]].r;i >= block[pos[x]].l;i--)
        if (i + a[i] >= block[pos[i] + 1].l)
            step[i] = 1, to[i] = i + a[i];
        else 
            step[i] = step[i + a[i]] + 1,to[i] = to[i + a[i]];
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\弹飞绵羊.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    init();
    int m;
    cin >> m;
    while (m--) {
        int i,j;
        cin >> i >> j;
        // 节点从 0 开始编号
        if (i == 1) cout << query(j + 1) << endl;
        else {
            int k;
            cin >> k;
            modify(j + 1,k);
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\教主的魔法.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    len = (int) sqrt(n), num = (n + len - 1) / len;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pos[i] = (i - 1) / len + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= num;i++) {
        block[i].l = (i - 1) * len + 1, block[i].r = min(i * len,n);
        sort(cpy + block[i].l,cpy + block[i].r + 1);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\教主的魔法.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int area,int l,int r,int w) {
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) a[i] += w;
    for (int i = block[area].l;i <= block[area].r;i++) cpy[i] = a[i];
    sort(cpy + block[area].l,cpy + block[area].r + 1);
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\教主的魔法.cpp: add ---
void add(int l,int r,int w) {
    if (pos[l] == pos[r]) {
        modify(pos[l],l,r,w);
        return ;
    }
    modify(pos[l],l,block[pos[l]].r,w);
    modify(pos[r],block[pos[r]].l,r,w);
    for (int i = pos[l] + 1;i < pos[r];i++) block[i].lazy += w;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\教主的魔法.cpp: calc ---
int calc(int area,int l,int r,int c) {
    int ret = 0;
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) ret += (a[i] + block[area].lazy >= c);
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\教主的魔法.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r,int c) {
    if (pos[l] == pos[r]) return calc(pos[l],l,r,c);
    int ret = 0;
    for (int i = pos[l] + 1;i < pos[r];i++)
        ret += block[i].r - (lower_bound(cpy + block[i].l,cpy + block[i].r + 1,c - block[i].lazy) - cpy) + 1;
    return calc(pos[l],l,block[pos[l]].r,c) + ret + calc(pos[r],block[pos[r]].l,r,c);
}

--- 2026-8\根号算法选讲\教主的魔法.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        cpy[i] = a[i];
    }
    init();
    while (q--) {
        char op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == 'M') {
            int l,r,w;
            cin >> l >> r >> w;
            add(l,r,w);
        } else {
            int l,r,c;
            cin >> l >> r >> c;
            cout << query(l,r,c) << endl;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

void solve() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> x[i];
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][i] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		for (int j = i;j <= n;j++)
			dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][])
	}
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试1\geminae.cpp: check ---
bool check(char x,char y) {
	if (x == y) {
		if ((a[x] >= 2) && (b[x] >= 2)) return true; // 两个相同的顺序可以颠倒，不特判 
		else return false;
	}
	else if ((a[x] && a[y] && b[x] && b[y]) && ((fra[x] <= lsa[y]) && (frb[x] <= lsb[y]))) return true;
	// else if 条件分析: s1,s2 中字母存在; 两字符串中 x 都在 y 前，必须从左往右 
	return false;
}

--- 2026-8\测试1\geminae.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	memset(a,0,sizeof a);
	memset(b,0,sizeof b);
	memset(fra,0x3f,sizeof fra);
	memset(lsa,0,sizeof lsa);
	memset(frb,0x3f,sizeof frb);
	memset(lsb,0,sizeof lsb);
	int n,m,cnt = 2;
	string ans = "";
	cin >> n >> m;
	string s1,s2;
	cin >> s1 >> s2;
	s1 = '?' + s1, s2 = '?' + s2;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		a[s1[i]]++, fra[s1[i]] = min(fra[s1[i]],i), lsa[s1[i]] = max(lsa[s1[i]],i);
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		b[s2[i]]++, frb[s2[i]] = min(frb[s2[i]],i), lsb[s2[i]] = max(lsb[s2[i]],i);
	// 字典序顺次枚举各种答案排列，检查是否可行 
	for (char i = 'A';i <= 'Z';i++)
		for (char j = 'A';j <= 'Z';j++)
			if (check(i,j)) {
				cout << i << j << endl;
				return ;
			}
	cout << -1 << endl;
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试1\pipeline-40.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	int n,x,y,z;
	cin >> n >> x >> y >> z;
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int point = 0;
		for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++) {
			if (dp[i] < max(i * x,dp[j - 1]) + y + z * (i - j + 1))
				dp[i] = max(i * x,dp[j - 1]) + y + z * (i - j + 1),point = j;
		}
		cout << point << endl;
	}
		
			
	cout << dp[n] << endl;
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试1\pipeline.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	int n,x,y,z;
	cin >> n >> x >> y >> z;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i] = 1e18;
	dp[0] = 0;
	int cur = 1,l = 1,r = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (l <= r && dp[q[r] - 1] + y + z * (-q[r] + 1) >= dp[i - 1] + y + z * (-i + 1)) r--;
		q[++r] = i;
		while (cur <= i && dp[cur - 1] <= i * x) cur++;
		if (cur != 1) dp[i] = min(dp[i],i * x + y + z * (i - cur + 2));
		while (l <= r && q[l] < cur) l++;
		if (l <= r) dp[i] = min(dp[i],dp[q[l] - 1] + y + z * (-q[l] + 1) + z * i);
	}
	cout << dp[n] << '\n';
	return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试2\auto\auto.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    bool flag2 = false,flag3 = false;
    string str2 = "",str3 = "";
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (check(s,a[i]) == 1) {
            cout << "OK" << '\n';
            return ;
        }
        else if (check(s,a[i]) == 2 && !flag2) flag2 = true, str2 = a[i];
        else if (check(s,a[i]) == 3 && !flag3) flag3 = true, str3 = a[i];
    }
    if (flag2) cout << str2 << '\n';
    else if (flag3) cout << str3 << '\n';
    else cout << "Not Found" << '\n';
    return ;
}

int max(int a, int b) {
    return a > b ? a : b;
}

--- 2026-8\测试3\darksideofthemoon.cpp: calc ---
int calc(int t,int x,int y) {
    memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
    dp[0][0] = t - x - y + 1;
    for (int i = 0;i <= x;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j <= y;j++)
            dp[i][j + 1] += (dp[i][j] + 1) / 2, dp[i + 1][j] += dp[i][j] / 2;
    return dp[x][y];
}

--- 2026-8\测试3\oracle.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l, tr[id].r = r;
    // cout << l << ' ' << r << endl;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].lazy = a[l];
        return ;
    }
    int m = mid[++cur];
    build(lid,l,m);
    build(rid,m + 1,r);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试3\oracle.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    // cout << "query: " << tr[id].l << ' ' << tr[id].r << endl;
    if (tr[id].l == l && tr[id].r == r) return tr[id].lazy;
    // 两侧一定只有一侧包含该节点
    if (tr[lid].l <= l && tr[lid].r >= r) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (tr[rid].l <= l && tr[rid].r >= r) return query(rid,l,r);
}

--- 2026-8\测试3\oracle.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    // cout << "pushdown: [" << tr[id].l << ',' << tr[id].r << ']' << " to [" << tr[lid].l << ',' << tr[lid].r << ']' << " and [" << tr[rid].l << ',' << tr[rid].r << ']' << endl;
    tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy, tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
    tr[id].lazy = 0;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试3\oracle.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int k) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += k;
        // cout << "add: " << tr[id].l << ' ' << tr[id].r << endl;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    if (tr[lid].l <= l && tr[lid].r >= r) modify(lid,l,r,k);
    else if (tr[rid].l <= l && tr[rid].r >= r) modify(rid,l,r,k);
    else modify(lid,l,tr[lid].r,k), modify(rid,tr[rid].l,r,k);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\测试3\oracle.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("oracle.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("oracle.out","w",stdout);
    int n,q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) cin >> mid[i];
    build(1,1,n);
    while (q--) {
        int op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == 1) {
            int l,r;
            cin >> l >> r;
            cout << query(1,l,r) << endl;
        } else {
            int l,r,k;
            cin >> l >> r >> k;
            modify(1,l,r,k);
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

        ans += (n - i * i + 1) % mod * i % mod * i % mod, ans %= mod;

}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\Link-Cut-Centroids.cpp: init ---
void init(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        init(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v], mxs[u] = max(mxs[u],siz[v]);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\Link-Cut-Centroids.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    if (pa != 0 && G[u].size() == 1) {
        lf = u, fa = pa;
        return ;
    }
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa || v == heavy[2]) continue; // 不可以是 heavy2 和 heavy1 的共同儿子
        dfs(v,u);
        if (lf) return ;
    }
}

    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;

    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\会议.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\会议.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    init(1,0);
    int point = 0,mnsiz = 1e9;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (max(siz[1] - siz[i],mxs[i]) < mnsiz)
            mnsiz = max(siz[1] - siz[i],mxs[i]), point = i;
    }
    dep[0] = -1;
    dfs(point,0);
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += dep[i];
    cout << point << ' ' << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\巡逻.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa,bool record) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    if (record) fa[u] = pa;
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u,record);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\巡逻.cpp: calc ---
void calc(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto &[v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        if (flag[u] && flag[v]) w = -1;
        calc(v,u);
        tmp = max(tmp,dp[u] + dp[v] + w);
        dp[u] = max(dp[u],dp[v] + w);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\点分治-树剖-直径-重心-虚树\巡逻.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back({b,1}); G[b].push_back({a,1});
    }
    dfs(1,0,0);
    int s,mxdep = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (mxdep < dep[i]) mxdep = dep[i], s = i;
    memset(dep,0,sizeof dep);
    dfs(s,0,1);
    mxdep = 0;
    int t = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (mxdep < dep[i]) mxdep = dep[i], t = i;
    mxdep--;
    if (k == 1) {
        cout << 2 * (n - 1) - mxdep + 1 << endl;
    } else {
        for (int i = t;i;i = fa[i]) flag[i] = true;
        calc(1,0);
        cout << 2 * n - mxdep - tmp << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    dfs(1,0);

    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (mxdep < dep[i]) mxdep = dep[i], s = i;
    memset(dep,0,sizeof dep);
    dfs(s,0);
    mxdep = 0;

        if (mxdep < dep[i]) mxdep = dep[i];

}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\Compress-Words.cpp: solve ---
void solve(string s) {
    int len = s.size();
    for (int i = 1;i < len;i++) {
        int j = prefix[i - 1];
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) j = prefix[j - 1];
        if (s[i] == s[j]) j++;
        prefix[i] = j;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\Compress-Words.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    string ans = "";
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        string s;
        cin >> s;
        string t = s + '#' + ans.substr(ans.size() - min(s.size(),ans.size()),min(s.size(),ans.size()));
        solve(t);
        for (int j = prefix[t.size() - 1];j < s.size();j++) ans += s[j];
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\KMP.cpp: init ---
void init(string s) {
    int len = s.size();
    for (int i = 1;i < len;i++) {
        int j = prefix[i - 1];
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) j = prefix[j - 1];
        if (s[i] == s[j]) j++;
        prefix[i] = j;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\KMP.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    string s1,s2;
    cin >> s1 >> s2;
    int len1 = s1.size(),len2 = s2.size();
    string t = s2 + '#' + s1;
    init(t);
    for (int i = len2 + 1;i <= len1 + len2;i++)
        if (prefix[i] == len2) cout << i - 2 * len2 + 1 << endl;
    init(s2);
    for (int i = 0;i < len2;i++) cout << prefix[i] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\兔子与兔子.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    s = '?' + s;
    powP[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i < s.size();i++)
        hs[i] = (hs[i - 1] * P % mod + s[i]) % mod, powP[i] = powP[i - 1] * P % mod;
    int m;
    cin >> m;
    while (m--) {
        int l1,r1,l2,r2;
        cin >> l1 >> r1 >> l2 >> r2;
        if ((hs[r1] - hs[l1 - 1] * powP[r1 - l1 + 1] % mod + mod) % mod == (hs[r2] - hs[l2 - 1] * powP[r2 - l2 + 1] % mod + mod) % mod) cout << "Yes" << endl;
        else cout << "No" << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\动物园.cpp: solve ---
long long solve(string s) {
    memset(prefix,0,sizeof prefix);
    memset(ans,0,sizeof ans);
    ans[1] = 1;
    long long ret = 1;
    int len = s.size();
    for (int i = 1;i < len;i++) {
        int j = prefix[i];
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) j = prefix[j];
        if (s[i] == s[j]) j++;
        prefix[i + 1] = j, ans[i + 1] = ans[j] + 1;
    }
    int j = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i < len;i++) {
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) j = prefix[j];
        if (s[i] == s[j]) j++;
        while (j * 2 > i + 1) j = prefix[j];
        ret = ret * (ans[j] + 1) % mod;
    }
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\动物园.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        string s;
        cin >> s;
        cout << solve(s) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\失配树.cpp: init ---
void init(string s) {
    int len = s.size();
    for (int i = 1;i < len;i++) {
        int j = prefix[i - 1];
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) j = prefix[j - 1];
        if (s[i] == s[j]) j++;
        prefix[i] = j;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\失配树.cpp: build ---
void build(string s) {
    init(s);
    for (int i = 1;i <= s.size();i++) {
        G[prefix[i - 1]].push_back(i);
        G[i].push_back(prefix[i - 1]);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\失配树.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\失配树.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i])
            u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\失配树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    build(s);
    dfs(0,0);
    int m;
    cin >> m;
    while (m--) {
        int p,q;
        cin >> p >> q;
        int lca = LCA(p,q);
        if (lca == p || lca == q) lca = fa[lca][0];
        cout << lca << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单字符串算法\字符串哈希.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> s[i];
        hs[i] = s[i][0];
        for (int j = 1;j < s[i].size();j++)
            hs[i] = (hs[i] * P % mod + s[i][j]) % mod;
    }
    sort(hs + 1,hs + n + 1);
    int ans = unique(hs + 1,hs + n + 1) - hs - 1;
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\Kay-and-Snowflake.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v], mxs[u] = max(mxs[u],siz[v]);
    }
    int heavy = u,minn = mxs[u];
    // heavy 代表重心，minn 记录去除重心的最大连通块最小值
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        int tmp = max(mxs[ans[v]],siz[u] - siz[ans[v]]),pos = ans[v],last = pos;
        while (p[pos] != u) {
            pos = p[pos];
            if (max(mxs[pos],siz[u] - siz[pos]) < tmp)
                tmp = max(mxs[pos],siz[u] - siz[pos]), last = pos;
            else break;
        }
        if (minn > tmp) minn = tmp,heavy = last;
    }
    ans[u] = heavy;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\Kay-and-Snowflake.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> p[i];
        G[p[i]].push_back(i); G[i].push_back(p[i]);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    while (q--) {
        int v;
        cin >> v;
        cout << ans[v] << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

}

int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return u;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i]) u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\Max-Flow-P.cpp: add ---
void add(int u,int pa) {
    a[u] = d[u];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        add(v,u);
        a[u] += a[v];
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\Max-Flow-P.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        G[x].push_back(y); G[y].push_back(x);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        int s,t;
        cin >> s >> t;
        int lca = LCA(s,t);
        d[s] += 1,d[t] += 1,d[lca] -= 1,d[fa[lca][0]] -= 1;
    }
    add(1,0);
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,a[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

    }

}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\会议.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    init(1,0);
    int point = 0,mnsiz = 1e9;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (max(siz[1] - siz[i],mxs[i]) < mnsiz)
            mnsiz = max(siz[1] - siz[i],mxs[i]), point = i;
    }
    dep[0] = -1;
    dfs(point,0);
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += dep[i];
    cout << point << ' ' << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[1] = 0;
    q.push({1,dis[1]});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = 1;
        for (auto p : G[u]) {
            int v = p.v,len = p.len;
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] > dis[u] + len) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + len;
                q.push({v,dis[v]});
            }
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: kruskal ---
void kruskal() {
    sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.w > y.w;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
    cnt = n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u = a[i].u,v = a[i].v;
        if (find(u) == find(v)) continue;
        cnt++;
        tree[cnt].push_back(find(u)); tree[find(u)].push_back(cnt);
        tree[cnt].push_back(find(v)); tree[find(v)].push_back(cnt);
        f[find(u)] = f[find(v)] = cnt, treew[cnt] = a[i].w;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++) fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : tree[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        dis[u] = min(dis[u],dis[v]);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: query ---
int query(int x,int y) {
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[x] > (1 << i) && treew[fa[x][i]] > y) x = fa[x][i];
    return dis[x];
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++) G[i].clear();
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * N;i++) tree[i].clear();
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis); memset(f,0,sizeof f); memset(dep,0,sizeof dep); memset(fa,0,sizeof fa);
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].u >> a[i].v >> a[i].len >> a[i].w;
        G[a[i].u].push_back({a[i].v,a[i].len,a[i].w});
        G[a[i].v].push_back({a[i].u,a[i].len,a[i].w});
    }
    dijkstra();
    kruskal();
    dfs(cnt,0);
    int q,k,s,lastans = 0;
    cin >> q >> k >> s;
    while (q--) {
        int v0,p0;
        cin >> v0 >> p0;
        v0 = (k * lastans + v0 - 1) % n + 1, p0 = (k * lastans + p0) % (s + 1);
        lastans = query(v0,p0);
        cout << lastans << endl;
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\归程.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\最小生成树.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\最小生成树.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\最小生成树.cpp: kruskal ---
int kruskal() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x = a[i].x,y = a[i].y,z = a[i].z;
        if (find(x) == find(y)) continue;
        ans += z;
        Union(x,y);
    }
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\最小生成树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y >> a[i].z;
    sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y){return x.z < y.z;});
    int ans = kruskal();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (f[i] == i) cnt++;
    if (cnt > 1) cout << "orz" << endl;
    else cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    s[u].dfn = ++id, re[id] = u; // 建立双向映射
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\简单树论与树上技巧\树链的并.cpp: lca ---
int lca(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i])
            u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\Game-in-Momotetsu-World.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int h,w;
    cin >> h >> w;
    for (int i = 1;i <= h;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= w;j++) {
            cin >> a[i][j];
            b[i][j] = (a[i][j] == '+' ? 1 : -1);
        }
    for (int i = h - 1;i >= 1;i--) dp[i][w] = b[i - 1][w] - dp[i + 1][w];
    for (int i = w - 1;i >= 1;i--) dp[h][i] = b[h][i - 1] - dp[h][i + 1];
    for (int i = h - 1;i >= 1;i--)
        for (int j = w - 1;j >= 1;j--)
            dp[i][j] = max(b[i + 1][j] - dp[i + 1][j],b[i][j + 1] - dp[i][j + 1]);
    if (dp[1][1] > 0) cout << "Takahashi" << endl;
    else if (dp[1][1] < 0) cout << "Aoki" << endl;
    else cout << "Draw" << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\Permutation-Game.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        pos[a[i]] = i;
    }
    for (int i = n - 1;i >= 1;i--)
        for (int j = 0;i * j + pos[i] % i <= n;j++)
            if (a[i * j + pos[i] % i] > i) dp[i] |= !dp[a[i * j + pos[i] % i]];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (dp[a[i]]) cout << 'A';
        else cout << 'B';
    }
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\Potions.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    int ans = 0;
    long long sum = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int a;
        cin >> a;
        if (a > 0) ans++, sum += a;
        else {
            sum += a;
            q.push(a);
            if (sum < 0) sum -= q.top(), q.pop();
            else ans++;
        }
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\骑士精神.cpp: evaluate ---
int evaluate() {
    int s = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 5;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
            if (c[i][j] == '*') continue;
            if (c[i][j] != e[i][j]) s++;
        }
    return s;
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\骑士精神.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,int cnt) {
    int d = evaluate();
    if (d == 0) flag = true,ans = min(ans,cnt);
    if (flag) return ;
    if (cnt + d > m) return ;
    for (int i = 0;i <= 7;i++) {
        int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
        if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nx > 5 || ny > 5) continue;
        swap(c[x][y],c[nx][ny]);
        dfs(nx,ny,cnt + 1);
        swap(c[x][y],c[nx][ny]);
    }
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\骑士精神.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int X,Y;
    flag = 0,ans = 1e9;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 5;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
            cin >> c[i][j];
            if (c[i][j] == '*') X = i,Y = j;
        }
    for (m = evaluate();m <= 15;m++) {
        dfs(X,Y,0);
        if (flag) break;
    }
    cout << (ans == 1e9 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-8\综合练习\骑士精神.cpp: main ---

    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\贪心\Megalomania.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].a >> s[i].b;
    sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.b < y.b;});
    int t = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        t += s[i].a;
        if (t > s[i].b) {
            cout << "No" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    cout << "Yes" << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\贪心\Summer-Vacation.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    long long ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].a >> s[i].b;
    sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.a < y.a;});
    int cur = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) { // i 枚举剩余天数，必须天数倒着算来贪心 
        while (s[cur].a <= i && cur <= n) {
            q.push(s[cur].b); // 可以采用 
            cur++; // 在这里加，不然会出错 
        }
        if (!q.empty()) {
            ans += q.top();
            q.pop(); 
        }
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-8\贪心\数据备份.cpp: del ---
void del(int x) { // 删除编号为 x 的数 
    a[a[x].pre].nxt = a[x].nxt,
    a[a[x].nxt].pre = a[x].pre;
}

--- 2026-8\贪心\超级钢琴.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	return s[st[l][k]] > s[st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]] ? st[l][k] : st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k];
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\dp选讲\Medals.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    for (int i = 1; i <= r; i++)
        for (int j = 1; j <= n; j++)
            if (((i + a[j] - 1) / a[j]) & 1) b[i] |= (1 << (j - 1));
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\dp选讲\Medals.cpp: popcount ---
int popcount(int x) {
    int cnt = 0;
    while (x)
        cnt += x & 1, x >>= 1;
    return cnt;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\dp选讲\Medals.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid) {
    memset(dp, 0, sizeof dp);
    for (int i = 1; i <= mid; i++) dp[b[i]]++;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        for (int j = 0; j < (1 << n); j++)
            if (((j >> i) & 1) == 0) dp[j | (1 << i)] += dp[j];
    for (int i = 0; i < (1 << n); i++)
        if (mid - dp[(1 << n) - 1 - i] < popcount(i) * k) return false;
    return true;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\dp选讲\Round-screws.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int x, int y) {
    for (int i = 0; i < (1 << 9); i++)
        f[x >> 9][i] = min(f[x >> 9][i], (i ^ (x & ((1 << 9) - 1))) + y);
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\dp选讲\Round-screws.cpp: query ---
int query(int x) {
    int ret = 1e18;
    for (int i = 0; i < (1 << 9); i++)
        ret = min(ret, f[i][x & ((1 << 9) - 1)] + ((i ^ (x >> 9)) << 9) - C);
    return ret;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\dp选讲\Round-screws.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> n >> C;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) cin >> a[i];
    a[0] = a[n + 1] = 0;
    memset(dp, 0x3f, sizeof dp);
    memset(f, 0x3f, sizeof f);
    dp[0] = C * (n + 1);
    modify(0, dp[0]);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n + 1; i++) {
        dp[i] = query(a[i]);
        modify(a[i], dp[i]);
    }
    cout << dp[n + 1] << endl;
}

    int o;
    cin >> o;
    return o;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\交互-构造-ad-hoc进阶\Menji-we-miss-you.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (vis[v]) continue;
        dfs(v);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
    }
    if (dep[u] <= depth && max(siz[u],tot - siz[u]) < tmp)
        ans = u, tmp = max(siz[u],tot - siz[u]);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\交互-构造-ad-hoc进阶\Menji-we-miss-you.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> n;
    rt = 1, tot = n;
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
    memset(dep,0,sizeof dep);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) G[i].clear();
    int l = 0,r = 0;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        int u;
        cin >> u;
        G[u].push_back(i);
        dep[i] = dep[u] + 1;
        r = max(r,dep[i]);
    }
    while (l <= r) {
        int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
        if (interact(1,mid)) r = mid - 1, depth = mid;
        else l = mid + 1;
    }
    while (true) {
        tmp = n + 1;
        dfs(rt);
        if (interact(ans,depth - dep[ans])) {
            rt = ans, tot = siz[ans];
            if (dep[ans] == depth) break;
        }
        else vis[ans] = 1;
    }
    cout << "! " << ans << endl;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\交互-构造-ad-hoc进阶\Subset-with-Zero-Sum.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
    memset(ans,0,sizeof ans);
    cur = 0;
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int x;
        cin >> x;
        to[i] = i - x;
    }
    int u;
    for (u = 1;!vis[u];u = to[u]) vis[u] = true;
    int tmp = u;
    ans[++cur] = u, u = to[u];
    while (u != tmp) ans[++cur] = u, u = to[u];
    cout << cur << endl;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
        cout << ans[i] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
}

    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\交互-构造-ad-hoc进阶\XOR-and-Swap.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 0;i < 1 << n;i++) {
        cin >> p[i];
        pos[p[i]] = i;
    }
    for (int i = 0;i < 1 << n;i++) cin >> q[i];
    for (int i = 0;i < 1 << n;i++) {
        if (p[i] == q[i]) continue;
        int tmp = pos[q[i]];
        swap(p[i],p[tmp]);
        pos[p[i]] = i, pos[p[tmp]] = tmp;
        ans.push_back({i,tmp});
    }
    cout << ans.size() << endl;
    for (auto it : ans) cout << it.first << ' ' << it.second << endl;
    return 0;
}

        if (color[v] == -1) dfs(v,!c);
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0), cout.tie(0);
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    memset(color,-1,sizeof color);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        if (lstu[x])
            G[lstu[x]].push_back(i), G[i].push_back(lstu[x]), lstu[x] = 0;
        else lstu[x] = i;
        if (lstv[y])
            G[lstv[y]].push_back(i), G[i].push_back(lstv[y]), lstv[y] = 0;
        else lstv[y] = i;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (color[i] == -1) dfs(i,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        cout << (color[i] ? 'r' : 'b');
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\Redundant-Paths-G.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x,int pre) {
    dfn[x] = low[x] = ++idx;
    st[++top] = x;
    for (auto [y,i] : G[x]) {
        if (i == pre) continue;
        if (!dfn[y]) {
            tarjan(y,i);
            low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
            if (low[y] > dfn[x]) bridge[i] = true;
        }
        else low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
    }
    if (low[x] == dfn[x]) {
        int y;
        cnt_ecc++;
        do {
            y = st[top--];
            id[y] = cnt_ecc;
        } while (x != y);
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\Redundant-Paths-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {

    cin >> f >> r;
    for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back({v,i});
        G[v].push_back({u,i});
    }
    tarjan(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= f;i++)
        for (auto [j,t] : G[i]) {
            int x = id[i],y = id[j];
            if (x != y) d[x]++;
        }
    for (int i = 1;i <= cnt_ecc;i++)
        if (d[i] == 1) ans++;
    cout << (ans + 1) / 2 << endl;
    return 0;
}

bool dfs(int u) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (vis[v]) continue;
        vis[v] = true;
        if (!match[v] || dfs(match[v])) {
            match[v] = u;
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】割点（割顶）.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int u,int fa) {
    dfn[u] = low[u] = ++idx;
    bool flag = false;
    int child = 0;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (!dfn[v]) {
            child++;
            tarjan(v, u);
            low[u] = min(low[u],low[v]);
            if (low[v] >= dfn[u]) flag = true;
        }
        else if (v != fa) low[u] = min(low[u],dfn[v]);
    }
    if (fa == 0 && child < 2) flag = false;
    if (flag) ans++, isans[u] = true;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】无向图三元环计数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0), cout.tie(0);
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> u[i] >> v[i];
        deg[u[i]]++, deg[v[i]]++;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        if (deg[u[i]] > deg[v[i]]) G[v[i]].push_back(u[i]);
        else if (deg[u[i]] == deg[v[i]] && u[i] > v[i]) G[v[i]].push_back(u[i]);
        else G[u[i]].push_back(v[i]);
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        for (auto j : G[i]) last[j] = i;
        for (auto j : G[i])
            for (auto k : G[j])
                if (last[k] == i) ans++;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】点双连通分量.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int u,int pa) {
    dfn[u] = low[u] = ++idx;
    stk[++top] = u;
    int child = 0;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        if (!dfn[v]) {
            child++;
            tarjan(v,u);
            low[u] = min(low[u],low[v]);
            if (low[v] >= dfn[u]) {
                vector<int> now;
                int x;
                do {
                    x = stk[top--];
                    now.push_back(x);
                } while (x != v);
                now.push_back(u), dcc.push_back(now);
            }
        }
        else low[u] = min(low[u], dfn[v]);
    }
    if (pa == 0 && child == 0) dcc.push_back({u});
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】缩点-强联通分量.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int u) {
    dfn[u] = low[u] = ++idx;
    stk.push(u), flag[u] = true;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (!dfn[v]) {
            tarjan(v);
            low[u] = min(low[u],low[v]);
        }
        else if (flag[v]) low[u] = min(low[u],dfn[v]);
    }
    if (low[u] == dfn[u]) {
        cnt++;
        int top = 0;
        do {
            top = stk.top(), stk.pop();
            scc[top] = cnt, flag[top] = false;
        } while (top != u);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】缩点-强联通分量.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cnt;i++)
        if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        topo[++cur] = u;
        for (auto v : nG[u]) {
            dp[v] = max(dp[v],dp[u] + val[v]);
            if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】缩点-强联通分量.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> u[i] >> v[i];
        G[u[i]].push_back(v[i]);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (!dfn[i]) tarjan(i);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) val[scc[i]] += a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        if (scc[u[i]] != scc[v[i]]) nG[scc[u[i]]].push_back(scc[v[i]]), in[scc[v[i]]]++;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cnt;i++) dp[i] = val[i];
    topo_sort();
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cnt;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】边双连通分量.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int u,int edge) {
    dfn[u] = low[u] = ++idx;
    for (auto [v,id] : G[u]) {
        if (id == edge) continue;
        if (!dfn[v]) {
            tarjan(v,id);
            low[u] = min(low[u],low[v]);
            if (low[v] > dfn[u]) flag[id] = true;
        }
        else low[u] = min(low[u],dfn[v]);
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\【模板】边双连通分量.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
    vis[u] = true, now.push_back(u);
    for (auto [v,id] : G[u]) {
        if (flag[id]) continue;
        if (vis[v]) continue;
        dfs(v);
    }
    return ;
}

        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back({v,i}), G[v].push_back({u,i});
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (!dfn[i]) tarjan(i,0);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        if (!vis[i]) now.clear(), dfs(i), dcc.push_back(now);
    cout << dcc.size() << endl;
    for (auto it : dcc) {
        cout << it.size();
        for (int u : it) cout << ' ' << u;
        cout << endl;
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\建造军营.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int u,int edge) {
    dfn[u] = low[u] = ++idx;
    stk[++top] = u,exist[u] = true;
    for (auto [v,id] : G[u]) {
        if (id == edge) continue;
        if (!dfn[v]) {
            tarjan(v,id);
            low[u] = min(low[u],low[v]);
            if (low[v] > dfn[u]) flag[id] = true;
        }
        else if (exist[v]) low[u] = min(low[u],dfn[v]);
    }
    if (low[u] == dfn[u]) {
        cnt++;
        int x;
        do {
            x = stk[top--];
            exist[x] = false;
            scc[x] = cnt;
        } while (x != u);
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\建造军营.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dp[u][0] = pw[cnt2[u]];
    dp[u][1] = (pw[cnt1[u]] - 1 + mod) % mod * pw[cnt2[u]] % mod;
    siz[u] = cnt2[u];
    for (auto v : nG[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v] + 1;
        dp[u][1] = (dp[u][1] * ((2 * dp[v][0] % mod + dp[v][1]) % mod) % mod + dp[u][0] * dp[v][1] % mod) % mod;
        dp[u][0] = dp[u][0] * (2 * dp[v][0] % mod) % mod;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\归程.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[1] = 0;
    q.push({1,dis[1]});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = 1;
        for (auto p : G[u]) {
            int v = p.v,len = p.len;
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] > dis[u] + len) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + len;
                q.push({v,dis[v]});
            }
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\归程.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\归程.cpp: kruskal ---
void kruskal() {
    sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.w > y.w;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
    cnt = n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u = a[i].u,v = a[i].v;
        if (find(u) == find(v)) continue;
        cnt++;
        tree[cnt].push_back(find(u)); tree[find(u)].push_back(cnt);
        tree[cnt].push_back(find(v)); tree[find(v)].push_back(cnt);
        f[find(u)] = f[find(v)] = cnt, treew[cnt] = a[i].w;
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\归程.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++) fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : tree[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        dis[u] = min(dis[u],dis[v]);
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\归程.cpp: query ---
int query(int x,int y) {
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[x] > (1 << i) && treew[fa[x][i]] > y) x = fa[x][i];
    return dis[x];
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\归程.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++) G[i].clear();
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * N;i++) tree[i].clear();
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis); memset(f,0,sizeof f); memset(dep,0,sizeof dep); memset(fa,0,sizeof fa);
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].u >> a[i].v >> a[i].len >> a[i].w;
        G[a[i].u].push_back({a[i].v,a[i].len,a[i].w});
        G[a[i].v].push_back({a[i].u,a[i].len,a[i].w});
    }
    dijkstra();
    kruskal();
    dfs(cnt,0);
    int q,k,s,lastans = 0;
    cin >> q >> k >> s;
    while (q--) {
        int v0,p0;
        cin >> v0 >> p0;
        v0 = (k * lastans + v0 - 1) % n + 1, p0 = (k * lastans + p0) % (s + 1);
        lastans = query(v0,p0);
        cout << lastans << '\n';
    }
}

    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\飞行路线.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Node> q;
    q.push({s,0,0});
    dis[0][s] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().u,l = q.top().l; q.pop();
        if (vis[l][u]) continue;
        vis[l][u] = true;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[l][v]) continue;
            if (dis[l][v] <= dis[l][u] + w) continue;
            dis[l][v] = dis[l][u] + w;
            q.push({v,l,dis[l][v]});
        }
        if (l == k) continue;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[l + 1][v]) continue;
            if (dis[l + 1][v] <= dis[l][u]) continue;
            dis[l + 1][v] = dis[l][u];
            q.push({v,l + 1,dis[l + 1][v]});
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\图论专题复习与提升\飞行路线.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m >> k >> s >> t;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int a,b,c;
        cin >> a >> b >> c;
        G[a].push_back({b,c}); G[b].push_back({a,c});
    }
    dijkstra();
    long long ans = 1e18;
    for (int i = 0;i <= k;i++) ans = min(ans,dis[i][t]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\Censoring-G.cpp: Hash ---
ll Hash(int l,int r) {return (hs[r] - hs[l - 1] * power[r - l + 1] % mod + mod) % mod;}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\Censoring-G.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    power[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= s.size();i++) power[i] = power[i - 1] * P % mod;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        ll tmphs = 0;
        len.push_back(t[i].size());
        for (int j = 0;j < t[i].size();j++)
            tmphs = (tmphs * P % mod + t[i][j]) % mod;
        mp[tmphs] = true;
    }
    sort(len.begin(),len.end(),greater<int>());
    len.erase(unique(len.begin(),len.end()),len.end());
    m = len.size();
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\Censoring-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> s;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> t[i];
    init();
    for (int i = 0;i < s.size();i++) {
        st[++top] = s[i];
        hs[top] = (hs[top - 1] * P % mod + s[i]) % mod;
        for (int j = 0;j < m;j++) {
            if (top < len[j]) continue;
            if (mp.count(Hash(top - len[j] + 1,top))) top -= len[j];
        }
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= top;i++) cout << st[i];
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\First-G.cpp: insert ---
void insert(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
        x = ch[x][i];
        sum[x]++;
    }
    cnt[x]++;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\First-G.cpp: topo_sort ---
bool topo_sort() {
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 0;i < 26;i++)
        if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
    int cnt = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        cnt++;
        for (auto v : G[u])
            if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
    }
    if (cnt < 26) return true;
    else return false;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\First-G.cpp: check ---
bool check(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (int i = 0;i < 26;i++) G[i].clear();
    memset(in,0,sizeof in);
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (cnt[x] == 1) return false;
        for (int j = 0;j < 26;j++)
            if (ch[x][j] && j != i) {
                G[i].push_back(j);
                in[j]++;
            }
        x = ch[x][i];
    }
    if (topo_sort()) return false;
    else return true;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\You-Are-Given-Some-Strings.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    for (int i = 0;i < t.size();i++) {
        power[i] = power[i - 1] * P % mod;
        hs[i] = (hs[i - 1] * P % mod + t[i]) % mod;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int tmphs = 0;
        for (int j = 0;j < s[i].size();j++)
            tmphs = (tmphs * P % mod + s[i][j]) % mod;
        len.push_back(s[i].size());
        mp[s[i].size()].insert(tmphs);
    }
    sort(len.begin(),len.end());
    len.erase(unique(len.begin(),len.end()),len.end());
    m = len.size();
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\【模板】AC自动机.cpp: insert ---
void insert(string s,int &idx) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
        x = ch[x][i];
    }
    cnt[x]++, idx = x;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\【模板】AC自动机.cpp: query ---
void query(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) x = ch[x][c - 'a'], sum[x]++;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\【模板】AC自动机.cpp: build ---
void build() {
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 0;i < M;i++) {
        if (ch[1][i]) {
            fail[ch[1][i]] = 1;
            q.push(ch[1][i]);
            G[1].push_back(ch[1][i]);
        }
        else ch[1][i] = 1;
    }
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int x = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (int i = 0;i < M;i++) {
            if (ch[x][i]) {
                int y = ch[x][i];
                fail[y] = ch[fail[x]][i];
                G[fail[y]].push_back(y);
                q.push(y);
            }
            else ch[x][i] = ch[fail[x]][i];
        }
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\【模板】AC自动机.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        dfs(v);
        sum[u] += sum[v];
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\【模板】Manacher.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    string s = "#";
    char c;
    while (cin >> c) s += c, s += '#';
    int len = s.size();
    for (int i = 0,l = 0,r = -1;i < len;i++) {
        int k = (i > r) ? 1 : min(d[l + r - i],r - i + 1);
        while (0 <= i - k && i + k < len && s[i - k] == s[i + k]) k++;
        d[i] = k--;
        if (i + k > r)
            l = i - k, r = i + k;
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 0;i < len;i++) ans = max(ans,d[i] - 1);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\公共串.cpp: Hash ---
ll Hash(int id, int l,int r)
{return (hs[id][r] - hs[id][l - 1] * power[r - l + 1] % mod + mod) % mod;}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\公共串.cpp: check ---
bool check (int mid) {
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) mp[i].clear();
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        for (int j = 1; j + mid - 1 <= s[i].size(); j++)
            mp[i][Hash(i,j,j + mid - 1)] = true;
    for (int i = 1; i + mid - 1 <= maxlen; i++) {
        int range = Hash(maxid, i, i + mid - 1);
        bool flag = true;
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) flag &= mp[j][range];
        if (flag) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\公共串.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    power[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        cin >> s[i];
        if (s[i].size() > maxlen)
            maxlen = s[i].size(), maxid = i;
        s[i] = '?' + s[i];
        hs[i][0] = 0;
        for (int j = 1; j < s[i].size(); j++)
            hs[i][j] = (hs[i][j - 1] * P % mod + s[i][j]) % mod;
    }
    for (int i = 1; i <= maxlen; i++)
        power[i] = (power[i - 1] * P) % mod;
    int l = 0, r = maxlen, ans = 0;
    while (l <= r) {
        int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
        if (check(mid)) l = mid + 1, ans = mid;
        else r = mid - 1;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\最长异或路径.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs (int u, int pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        s[v] = s[u] ^ w;
        dfs(v, u);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\最长异或路径.cpp: insert ---
void insert(int s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (int cur = 30; cur >= 0; cur--) {
        bool i = (s >> cur) & 1;
        if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
        x = ch[x][i];
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\字符串专题\最长异或路径.cpp: query ---
int query(int s) {
    int x = 1, ret = 0;
    for (int cur = 30; cur >= 0; cur--) {
        bool i = (s >> cur) & 1;
        if (ch[x][!i]) ret |= (1 << cur), x = ch[x][!i];
        else x = ch[x][i];
    }
    return ret;
}

int lowbit(int x) {return x & -x;}

void add(int x,int val,int lim) {while (x <= lim) tree[x] += val, x += lowbit(x);}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\Good-Pairs.cpp: query ---
long long query(int x) {
    long long ans = 0;
    while (x > 0) ans += tree[x], x -= lowbit(x);
    return ans;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\Good-Pairs.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        b[i] = -a[i];
    }
    int ans = calc(a) + calc(b) - n;
    sort(b + 1,b + n + 1);
    int same = 0,cnt = 0,last = b[1];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (b[i] != last) cnt = 0,last = b[i];
        same += cnt, cnt++;
    }
    cout << ans - same << endl;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\Red-Sequence.cpp: update ---
void update(int a,int c,int w) {
    for (int x = a;x <= mx;x += x & -x) {
        int y = lower_bound(vec[x].begin(),vec[x].end(),c) - vec[x].begin() + 1;
        for (int z = y;z < bit[x].size();z += z & -z)
            bit[x][z] = max(bit[x][z],w);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\Red-Sequence.cpp: query ---
int query(int a,int c) {
    int res = -1e9;
    for (int x = a;x > 0;x -= x & -x) {
        int y = upper_bound(vec[x].begin(),vec[x].end(),c) - vec[x].begin();
        for (int z = y;z > 0;z -= z & -z)
            res = max(res,bit[x][z]);
    }
    return res;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\不归之人与望眼欲穿的人们.cpp: clean ---
void clean() {
    tmp[cur + 1] = {0,-1};
    int tmpcur = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
        if (tmp[i].y != tmp[i + 1].y) tmp[++tmpcur] = tmp[i];
    cur = tmpcur;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\不归之人与望眼欲穿的人们.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int i) {
    memset(f[i],0,sizeof f[i]);
    int tmpcur = 0;
    pre[i].clear(), suf[i].clear(), cur = 0;
    for (int j = block[i].l;j <= block[i].r;j++) {
        if ((tmpcur | a[j]) != tmpcur) tmpcur |= a[j], pre[i].push_back({j,tmpcur});
        for (int k = 1;k <= cur;k++) tmp[k].y |= a[j];
        tmp[++cur] = {j,a[j]}, clean();
        for (int k = 1;k <= cur;k++)
            f[i][j - tmp[k].x + 1] = max(f[i][j - tmp[k].x + 1],tmp[k].y);
    }
    suf[i].assign(tmp + 1,tmp + cur + 1);
    for (int j = 1;j <= block[i].r - block[i].l + 1;j++) f[i][j] = max(f[i][j],f[i][j - 1]);
    all[i] = tmpcur;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\天降之物.cpp: nc ---
char nc() {
        if (p1 == p2) {
            p2 = (int)fread(buf, 1, BUF_SIZE, stdin);
            p1 = 0;
            if (p2 == 0) return EOF;
        }
        return buf[p1++];
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\天降之物.cpp: read ---
int read() {
        int x = 0, f = 1;
        int ch = nc();
        while (ch != EOF && (ch < '0' || ch > '9')) {
            if (ch == '-') f = -1;
            ch = nc();
        }
        while (ch != EOF && ch >= '0' && ch <= '9') {
            x = x * 10 + ch - '0';
            ch = nc();
        }
        return x * f;
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\天降之物.cpp: flush ---
void flush() {
        fwrite(outbuf, 1, op, stdout);
        op = 0;
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\天降之物.cpp: write ---
void write(int x) {
        static char out[32];
        int len = 0;
        if (x < 0) {
            outbuf[op++] = '-';
            if (op == BUF_SIZE) flush();
            x = -x;
        }
        do {
            out[len++] = static_cast<char>(x % 10 + '0');
            x /= 10;
        } while (x > 0);
        while (len--) {
            outbuf[op++] = out[len];
            if (op == BUF_SIZE) flush();
        }
        outbuf[op++] = '\n';
        if (op == BUF_SIZE) flush();
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\天降之物.cpp: writeIkaros ---
void writeIkaros() {
        const char* s = "Ikaros\n";
        for (int i = 0;i < 7;i++) {
            outbuf[op++] = s[i];
            if (op == BUF_SIZE) flush();
        }
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\天降之物.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    off[1] = 0;
    for (int i = 2;i <= len;i++)
        off[i] = off[i - 1] + (len - (i - 1) + 1);
    n = read(),m = read();
    num = (n + len - 1) / len;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) a[i] = read();
    for (int i = 1;i <= num;i++)
        l[i] = (i - 1) * len + 1, r[i] = min(i * len,n);
    for (int i = 1;i <= num;i++) init(i);
    int lastans = 0;
    while (m--) {
        int opt = read(),x = read(),y = read();
        x ^= lastans, y ^= lastans;
        if (opt == 1) modify(x,y);
        else {
            int ans = query(x, y);
            if (ans == INF) {
                writeIkaros();
                lastans = 0;
            } else {
                write(ans);
                lastans = ans;
            }
        }
    }
    FastIO::flush();
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\排序.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
    tr[id].sum = tr[lid].sum + tr[rid].sum;
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\排序.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l, tr[id].r = r, tr[id].len = r - l + 1, tr[id].lazy = -1;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].sum = b[l];
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid), build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    pushup(id);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\排序.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy != -1 && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].lazy = tr[rid].lazy = tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].sum = tr[lid].len * tr[id].lazy, 
        tr[rid].sum = tr[rid].len * tr[id].lazy;
        tr[id].lazy = -1;
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\排序.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy = val, 
        tr[id].sum = tr[id].len * val;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) modify(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) modify(rid,l,r,val);
    else modify(lid,l,mid,val), modify(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    pushup(id);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\排序.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) return tr[id].sum;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return query(lid,l,mid) + query(rid,mid + 1,r);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\未优化-天降之物.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int x,int y) {
    if (x == y) return ;
    for (int i = 1;i <= num;i++) {
        int idx = b[i].id[x],idy = b[i].id[y];
        if (idx == 0) continue;
        else if (idy == 0) {
            for (int j = b[i].l;j <= b[i].r;j++)
                if (a[j] == x) a[j] = y;
            b[i].id[x] = 0, b[i].id[y] = idx, b[i].val[idx] = y;
        }
        else init(1,i,x,y);
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\数据结构\未优化-天降之物.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    num = (n + len - 1) / len;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= num;i++) init(0,i,0,0);
    int lastans = 0;
    while (m--) {
        int opt, x, y;
        cin >> opt >> x >> y;
        x ^= lastans, y ^= lastans;
        if (opt == 1) modify(x,y);
        else {
            int ans = query(x, y);
            if (ans == INF) {
                cout << "Ikaros\n";
                lastans = 0;
            } else {
                cout << ans << '\n';
                lastans = ans;
            }
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Fib-tree.cpp: init ---
void init(int u,int pa,int blocked) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa || v == blocked) continue;
        fa[v] = u;
        init(v,u,blocked);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Fib-tree.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa,int blocked,int id) {
    if (siz[u] == fib[id] || siz[u] == fib[id - 1]) sep = u;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa || v == blocked) continue;
        dfs(v,u,blocked,id);
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Fib-tree.cpp: check ---
bool check(int u,int pa,int blocked) {
    init(u,pa,blocked);
    if (siz[u] <= 1) return true;
    int p = pos[siz[u]];
    if (p == 0) return false;
    sep = -1;
    dfs(u,pa,blocked,p - 1);
    if (sep == -1) return false;
    int f = fa[sep];
    for (auto it = G[f].begin();it != G[f].end();it++)
        if (*it == sep) {G[f].erase(it); break;}
    for (auto it = G[sep].begin();it != G[sep].end();it++)
        if (*it == f) {G[sep].erase(it); break;}
    while (f != u) siz[f] -= siz[sep], f = fa[f];
    siz[u] -= siz[sep];
    return check(u,sep,sep) && check(sep,u,u);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Fib-tree.cpp: main ---
int main() {

    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v), G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    for (int i = 0;i <= 26;i++)
        pos[fib[i]] = i;
    if (check(1,0,0)) cout << "YES" << endl;
    else cout << "NO" << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Tree-Factory.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    maxn[u] = 0;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        maxn[u] = max(maxn[u], maxn[v]);
    }
    maxn[u]++;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Tree-Factory.cpp: check ---
void check(int u,int pa) {
    multiset<pair<int,int>> s;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        s.insert(make_pair(maxn[v],v));
    }
    if (s.empty()) return;
    for (auto v = s.begin();v != s.end();v++) {
        bamboo.push_back(v -> second);
        check(v -> second,u);
    }
    int cur = s.begin() -> first;
    for (auto v = s.begin();v != s.end();v++) {
        if (v == s.begin()) continue;
        for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
            ans.push_back(v -> second);
        cur = v -> first;
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\Tree-Factory.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int p;
        cin >> p;
        G[i].push_back(p), G[p].push_back(i);
    }
    dfs(0,-1);
    check(0,-1);
    cout << 0 << ' ';
    for (auto id : bamboo) cout << id << ' ';
    cout << endl << ans.size() << endl;
    for (auto v : ans) cout << v << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

char nc() {
        if (p1 == p2) {
            p2 = (int)fread(buf, 1, BUF_SIZE, stdin);
            p1 = 0;
            if (p2 == 0) return EOF;
        }
        return buf[p1++];
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: read ---
int read() {
        int x = 0, f = 1;
        int ch = nc();
        while (ch != EOF && (ch < '0' || ch > '9')) {
            if (ch == '-') f = -1;
            ch = nc();
        }
        while (ch != EOF && ch >= '0' && ch <= '9') {
            x = x * 10 + ch - '0';
            ch = nc();
        }
        return x * f;
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: write ---
void write(int x) {
        static char out[32];
        int len = 0;
        if (x < 0) {
            putchar('-');
            x = -x;
        }
        do {
            out[len++] = static_cast<char>(x % 10 + '0');
            x /= 10;
        } while (x > 0);
        while (len--) putchar(out[len]);
        putchar('\n');
    }

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1, dfn[u] = ++idx, rev[idx] = u;
    int maxn = 0;
    first[u] = ++eidx, euler[eidx] = u;
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dis[v] = dis[u] + w, dep[v] = dep[u] + 1;
        dfs(v, u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
        if (siz[v] > maxn) maxn = siz[v], son[u] = v;
        euler[++eidx] = u;
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: cmp ---
int cmp(int x,int y) {return dep[x] < dep[y] ? x : y;}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    lg[0] = -1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= eidx;i++) lg[i] = lg[i >> 1] + 1, st[i][0] = euler[i];
    for (int j = 1;j <= lg[eidx];j++)
        for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= eidx;i++)
            st[i][j] = cmp(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << (j - 1))][j - 1]);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: lca ---
int lca(int u,int v) {
    int l = first[u], r = first[v];
    if (l > r) swap(l, r);
    int k = lg[r - l + 1];
    return cmp(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: add ---
void add(int x,int u) {
    auto it = s.lower_bound(x);
    if (it != s.end()) {
        int y = *it;
        if (lca(x,y) == u) a.push_back({x,y,pos[u]});
    }
    if (it != s.begin()) {
        int y = *prev(it);
        if (lca(y,x) == u) a.push_back({y,x,pos[u]});
    }
    s.insert(x);
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树上问题\rldcot.cpp: dfs2 ---
void dfs2(int u,int pa,bool keep) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa || v == son[u]) continue;
        dfs2(v,u,false);
    }
    if (son[u]) dfs2(son[u],u,true);
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa || v == son[u]) continue;
        for (int i = dfn[v];i < dfn[v] + siz[v];i++) add(rev[i],u);
    }
    add(u,u);
    if (!keep) s.clear();
}

int find(int x) {

}

int d(int x,int y) {
    if (x >= 0) return x / y + (x % y != 0);
    else return x / y;
}

void dfs(int u,int pa) {

    }

}

    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;

        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    init(1,0);
    int point = 0,mnsiz = 1e9;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (max(siz[1] - siz[i],mxs[i]) < mnsiz)
            mnsiz = max(siz[1] - siz[i],mxs[i]), point = i;
    }
    dep[0] = -1;
    dfs(point,0);
    cout << point << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\入门例题4.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    f[u][0] = f[u][1] = 0;
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        if (f[v][0] + w > f[u][1])
            f[u][1] = f[v][0] + w;
        if (f[u][1] > f[u][0])
            swap(f[u][1],f[u][0]);
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\入门例题4.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    memset(f,0,sizeof f);
    for (int i = 1;i < N;i++) G[i].clear();
    int n,s;
    cin >> n >> s;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int x,y,z;
        cin >> x >> y >> z;
        G[x].push_back({y,z}); G[y].push_back({x,z});
    }
    dfs(s,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << f[i][0] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << f[i][1] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    return ;
}

    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\入门例题5.cpp: dfs1 ---
void dfs1(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dep[v] = dep[u] + 1;
        dfs1(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v], dp[u] += dp[v] + w * siz[v];
    }
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\入门例题5.cpp: dfs2 ---
void dfs2(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dp[v] = dp[u] + w * (n - siz[v] - siz[v]);
        dfs2(v,u);
    }
}

	for (auto v : G[u]) {

}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\树上染色.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1, dp[u][0] = dp[u][1] = 0;
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
        for (int i = min(k,siz[u]);i >= 0;i--)
            for (int j = 0;j <= min(i,siz[v]);j++) {
                int tmp = w * (j * (k - j) + (siz[v] - j) * (n - k - siz[v] + j));
                dp[u][i] = max(dp[u][i],dp[u][i - j] + dp[v][j] + tmp);
            }
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\树上统计.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        maxn[u] = max(maxn[u],maxn[v]), 
        minn[u] = min(minn[u],minn[v]), 
        siz[u] += siz[v];
    }
    ans += (siz[u] == (maxn[u] - minn[u] + 1));
    return ;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\树上统计.cpp: main ---

        G[u].push_back(v);
        indeg[v]++;
    }
    int st = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) if (indeg[i] == 0) {st = i; continue;}
    dfs(st,0);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\树形dp\选课.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        for (int j = m;j >= 1;j--)
            for (int k = 0;k < j;k++)
                dp[u][j] = max(dp[u][j],dp[u][j - k] + dp[v][k]);
    }
}
