int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    m++;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int u;
        cin >> u >> sc[i];
        G[u].push_back(i); G[i].push_back(u);
        dp[i][1] = sc[i];
    }
    dfs(0,-1);
    cout << dp[0][m] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\01-on-Tree.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\01-on-Tree.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\Donation.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\Donation.cpp: kruskal ---
void kruskal() {
    sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.w < y.w;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
    cnt = n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u = a[i].u,v = a[i].v;
        if (find(u) == find(v)) continue;
        cnt++;
        tree[cnt].push_back(find(u)); tree[find(u)].push_back(cnt);
        tree[cnt].push_back(find(v)); tree[find(v)].push_back(cnt);
        f[find(u)] = f[find(v)] = cnt, treew[cnt] = a[i].w;
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\Donation.cpp: dfs_sum ---
void dfs_sum(int u,int pa) {
    sumB[u] = (u <= n ? B[u] : 0);
    for (auto v : tree[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs_sum(v,u);
        sumB[u] += sumB[v];
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\Donation.cpp: dfs_ans ---
void dfs_ans(int u,int pa,long long max_cost) {
    if (u <= n) {
        ans[u] = max(A[u],max_cost);
        return;
    }
    for (auto v : tree[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        long long cost = treew[u] - sumB[v];
        dfs_ans(v,u,max(max_cost,cost));
    }
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\Donation.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    memset(f,0,sizeof f); memset(ans,0,sizeof ans);
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> A[i] >> B[i];
        C[i] = max(A[i] - B[i],0LL);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].u >> a[i].v;
        a[i].w = max(C[a[i].u],C[a[i].v]);
    }
    kruskal();
    dfs_sum(cnt,0);
    dfs_ans(cnt,0,0);
    long long res = 1e18;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) res = min(res,ans[i]);
    cout << res << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- 2026-9-10-11\贪心与证明\Travelling-Merchant.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (auto [v,r,p,id] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[id]) continue;
            vis[id] = true;
            if (--out[v] == 0) q.push(v);
            if (dp[u] != 0x3f3f3f3f3f3f3f3f) dp[v] = min(dp[v],max(dp[u] - p,r));
        }
    }
}

--- CookieOI\校门外的树\std.cpp: mod_pow ---
int mod_pow(int a, int b) {
    int r = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) r = (long long)r * a % MOD;
        a = (long long)a * a % MOD;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return r;
}

--- CookieOI\校门外的树\std.cpp: init_rev ---
void init_rev() {
    for (int i = 0; i < NTT_LEN; ++i) {
        rev[i] = (rev[i >> 1] >> 1) | ((i & 1) ? (NTT_LEN >> 1) : 0);
    }
}

--- CookieOI\校门外的树\std.cpp: multiply ---
void multiply(int square) {
    for (int i = 0; i < NTT_LEN; ++i) {
        fa[i] = 0;
        fb[i] = 0;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < LIMB_CNT; ++i) {
        fa[i] = A[i];
        if (!square) fb[i] = R[i];
    }
    if (square) {
        for (int i = 0; i < LIMB_CNT; ++i) fb[i] = fa[i];
    }
    ntt(fa, 0);
    ntt(fb, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < NTT_LEN; ++i) {
        fa[i] = (long long)fa[i] * fb[i] % MOD;
    }
    ntt(fa, 1);
    long long carry = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < LIMB_CNT; ++i) {
        long long cur = carry + fa[i];
        R[i] = cur % BASE;
        carry = cur / BASE;
    }
}

--- CookieOI\校门外的树\std.cpp: quick_pow ---
void quick_pow(int p) {
    for (int i = 0; i < LIMB_CNT; ++i) {
        R[i] = 0;
        A[i] = 0;
    }
    R[0] = 1;
    A[0] = 2;
    while (p) {
        if (p & 1) {
            multiply(0);
        }
        multiply(1);
        for (int i = 0; i < LIMB_CNT; ++i) A[i] = R[i];
        p >>= 1;
    }
}

--- CookieOI\校门外的树\std.cpp: read_int ---
int read_int() {
    int x = 0;
    char c = getchar_unlocked();
    while (c < '0' || c > '9') c = getchar_unlocked();
    while (c >= '0' && c <= '9') {
        x = x * 10 + (c - '0');
        c = getchar_unlocked();
    }
    return x;
}

--- CookieOI\校门外的树\std.cpp: write_int ---
void write_int(int x) {
    if (x == 0) {
        putchar_unlocked('0');
        return;
    }
    char s[12];
    int len = 0;
    while (x) {
        s[len++] = '0' + (x % 10);
        x /= 10;
    }
    while (len--) putchar_unlocked(s[len]);
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\divide.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("divide.in", "r", stdin);
    freopen("divide.out", "w", stdout);
    int n, s;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &s);
    vector<long long> sum(n + 1, 0);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        int x;
        scanf("%d", &x);
        sum[i] = sum[i - 1] + x;
    }
    vector<long long> f(n + 1, 0);
    f[0] = 1;
    int l = 0;
    long long w = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        w += f[i - 1];
        if (w >= MOD) w -= MOD;
        while (l <= i - 1 && sum[l] < sum[i] - s) {
            w -= f[l];
            if (w < 0) w += MOD;
            ++l;
        }
        f[i] = w;
    }
    printf("%lld\n", f[n]);
    return 0;
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\eval.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("eval.in", "r", stdin);
    freopen("eval.out", "w", stdout);
    int n, k;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &k);
    vector<int> x(n), y(n), z(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) scanf("%d%d%d", &x[i], &y[i], &z[i]);
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
        vector<long long> v;
        v.reserve(n);
        int sx = SIGNS[t][0], sy = SIGNS[t][1], sz = SIGNS[t][2];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) v.push_back(1LL * x[i] * sx + 1LL * y[i] * sy + 1LL * z[i] * sz);
        sort(v.begin(), v.end(), greater<long long>());
        long long sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) sum += v[i];
        if (sum > ans) ans = sum;
    }
    printf("%lld\n", ans);
    return 0;
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\percom.cpp: get ---
int get(int x, int p) {
    int c = 0;
    while (x) {
        x /= p;
        c += x;
    }
    return c;
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\percom.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("percom.in", "r", stdin);
    freopen("percom.out", "w", stdout);
    int n, m;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &m);
    int n2 = get(n, 2), n5 = get(n, 5);
    int m2 = get(m, 2), m5 = get(m, 5);
    int nm2 = get(n - m, 2), nm5 = get(n - m, 5);
    int ans1 = min(n2 - nm2, n5 - nm5);
    int ans2 = min(n2 - m2 - nm2, n5 - m5 - nm5);
    printf("%d %d\n", ans1, ans2);
    return 0;
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\tree.cpp: init_primes ---
void init_primes() {
    const int LIM = 31623;          // sqrt(1e9)
    vector<bool> vis(LIM + 1, false);
    for (int i = 2; i <= LIM; ++i) {
        if (!vis[i]) {
            primes.push_back(i);
            if (1LL * i * i <= LIM) {
                for (long long j = 1LL * i * i; j <= LIM; j += i)
                    vis[j] = true;
            }
        }
    }
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\tree.cpp: smallest_prime_factor ---
int smallest_prime_factor(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return n;
    for (int p : primes) {
        if (1LL * p * p > n) break;
        if (n % p == 0) return p;
    }
    return n;   // n 本身是质数
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\std\tree.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("tree.in", "r", stdin);
    freopen("tree.out", "w", stdout);

    int Q;
    scanf("%d", &Q);
    init_primes();

    while (Q--) {
        int x, y;
        scanf("%d%d", &x, &y);

        vector<int> px = get_path(x);
        vector<int> py = get_path(y);

        // 从路径末尾（根节点 1）开始，寻找公共后缀长度
        int i = (int)px.size() - 1;
        int j = (int)py.size() - 1;
        int common = 0;
        while (i >= 0 && j >= 0 && px[i] == py[j]) {
            ++common;
            --i; --j;
        }

        // 树上距离 = (lenx - 1) + (leny - 1) - 2 * (common - 1)
        // 简化后 = lenx + leny - 2 * common
        int ans = (int)px.size() + (int)py.size() - 2 * common;
        printf("%d\n", ans);
    }

    return 0;
}

--- CookieOI\样卷\样例\divide\rand-4.cpp: generate_one ---
void generate_one() {
    int n = rand() % MAX_N + 1;
    vector<int> a(n);
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        a[i] = rand() % MAX_A + 1;
        total += a[i];
    }
    int s = total / (2 + rand() % 3);
    s = max(s, 1);
    printf("%d %d\n", n, s);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (i) putchar(' ');
        printf("%d", a[i]);
    }
    putchar('\n');
}

int diss(int i, int j) { return  (x[i]-x[j]) * (x[i]-x[j]) + (y[i]-y[j]) * (y[i]-y[j]); }

--- Homework\2026-4-29\Buy-or-Build\Buy-or-Build.cpp: addedge ---
void addedge(int k){
	for(int i = 1; i < k; i++) {
		edge_size++;
		a[edge_size].u = i; a[edge_size].v = k; a[edge_size].w = diss(i, k);
	}
}

--- Homework\2026-4-29\Buy-or-Build\Buy-or-Build.cpp: cmp ---
int cmp(edge x, edge y){
	return x.w < y.w;
}

--- Homework\2026-4-29\Buy-or-Build\Buy-or-Build.cpp: Kruskal ---
int Kruskal(int tot, int cnt, int op, int cost){
	int sum = cost;
	if(op){
		for(int i = 1; i <= cnt; i++){
		int fu = find(a[i].u), fv = find(a[i].v);
		if(fu != fv){
			fat[fu] = fv;
			tot++;
			sum += a[i].w;
			if(op)nw[tot] = a[i];
			if(tot == n-1)break;
		}
	}
	}
	else{
		for(int i = 1; i <= cnt; i++){
		int fu = find(nw[i].u), fv = find(nw[i].v);
		if(fu != fv){
			fat[fu] = fv;
			tot++;
			sum += nw[i].w;
			if(tot == n-1)break;
		}
	}
	}
	return sum;
	
}

--- Homework\2026-4-29\Buy-or-Build\Buy-or-Build.cpp: solve ---
int solve(int cost){
	
	int ans = cost;
    
	for(int S = 0; S < (1<<m); S++){
		
		reset();
		
		int tot = 0;
		int need = 0;
		for(int q = 1; q <= m; q++){
			if(S & (1<<(q-1))){
			
			need += mon[q];
			for(int i = 1; i < size[q]; i++){
				for(int j = i+1; j <= size[q]; j++){
					
					int u = find(st[q][i]), v = find(st[q][j]);
					if(u != v){
						fat[u] = v;
						tot++;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		}
		ans = min(ans, Kruskal(tot, n-1, 0, need));
	}
	return ans;
}

void solve() {
    int n;
    long long a[105];
    cin >> n;
    a[1] = 1;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * 3;
        if (a[i] > 1e9) {
            cout << "NO" << endl;
            return ;
        }
    }
    cout << "YES" << endl;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << a[i] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    return ;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\Lake-Counting-S.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	vis[x][y] = true;
	for (int i = 0;i < 8;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (a[nx][ny] == '.') continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			if (a[i][j] == 'W' && !vis[i][j])

--- J1-1\0 贪心\Social-Distancing-II-B.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node cowx, Node cowy)
{
	return cowx.x < cowy.x;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\Social-Distancing-II-B.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].s;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	int r = 1e9;
	for (int i = 2;i < n;i++)
	{
		if (a[i - 1].s && !a[i].s) r = min(a[i].x - a[i - 1].x,r);
		if (a[i + 1].s && !a[i].s) r = min(a[i + 1].x - a[i].x,r);
	}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (a[i].s && (!a[i - 1].s || a[i].x - a[i - 1].x >= r)) ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\商店.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> c[i];
	sort(w + 1,w + n + 1);
	sort(c + 1,c + m + 1);
	int ans = n,cur = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (w[i] < c[cur]) ans--;
		else cur++;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\序列的第k个数.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	LL a,b,c,k;
	cin >> a >> b >> c >> k;
	if (2 * b == a + c) cout << (a + (k - 1) * (b - a)) % mod << endl;
	if (b * b == a * c) cout << a * qpow(b / a,k - 1,mod) % mod << endl;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\海港.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	queue<B> q;
	int n,cnt = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int t,k;
		cin >> t >> k;
		for(int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
		{
			int x;
			cin >> x;
			q.push({t,x});
			v[x]++;
			if(v[x] == 1)
			{
				cnt++;
			}
			while(t - q.front().t >= 86400)
			{
				int tmp = q.front().k;
				q.pop();
				v[tmp]--;
				if (v[tmp] == 0)
				{
					cnt--;
				}
			}
		}
		cout << cnt << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\爱与愁的心痛.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	int ans = 1e9;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n - m + 1;i++)
	{
		int sum = 0;
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= i + m;j++)
		{
			sum += a[j];
		}
		ans = min(ans,sum);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\特技飞行.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
	{
		cin >> c[i];
	}
	sort(c + 1,c + k + 1,greater<int>());
	int ans = 0;
	for (int l = 1,r = n;l < r;l++,r--) ans += c[l] * (r - l);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\自行车比赛.cpp: check ---
bool check(int i)
{
	int cur = n - i;
	for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
	{
		if (b[i] + n < b[j] + cur) return false;
		cur--;
	}
	return true;
}

--- J1-1\0 贪心\自行车比赛.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	sort(b + 1,b + n + 1);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (check(i)) ans++;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\1 连通块\01迷宫.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	vis[x][y] = cnt;
	ans[cnt]++;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n) continue;
		if (a[x][y] == a[nx][ny]) continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny] > 0) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\1 连通块\DZY-Loves-Chessboard.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,char c)
{
	a[x][y] = c;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (a[nx][ny] != '.') continue;
		if (c == 'W') dfs(nx,ny,'B');
		else dfs(nx,ny,'W');
	}
	return ;
}

	ans[cnt]++;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > 10) continue;
		if (a[x][y] != a[nx][ny]) continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny] > 0) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\1 连通块\Mooyo-Mooyo-S.cpp: check ---
bool check()
{
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 10;j++)
			if (a[i][j] != '0' && !vis[i][j])
			{
				cnt++;
				dfs(i,j);
			}
	bool flag = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 10;j++)
			if (ans[vis[i][j]] >= k) a[i][j] = '0',flag = false;
	return flag;
}

--- J1-1\1 连通块\Mooyo-Mooyo-S.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 10;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	while (true)
	{
		cnt = 0;
		memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
		memset(ans,0,sizeof ans);
		if (check()) break;
		for (int j = 1;j <= 10;j++)
		{
			int x = n;
			for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
				if (a[i][j] != '0')
				{
					swap(a[i][j],a[x][j]);
					x--;
				}
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		for (int j = 1;j <= 10;j++)
			cout << a[i][j];
		cout << endl;
	}
    return 0;
}

--- J1-1\1 连通块\入门.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	vis[x][y] = true;
	ans++;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (a[nx][ny] == '#') continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\1 连通块\入门.cpp: main ---
int main()
{

		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			cin >> a[i][j];
			if (a[i][j] == '@') tmpx = i,tmpy = j;
		}
	dfs(tmpx,tmpy);
	cout << ans << endl;

}

--- J1-1\2 二维棋盘BFS\临冬城宝藏.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<Node> q;
	q.push({sx,sy,sz});
	dis[sx][sy][sz] = 0;
	vis[sx][sy][sz] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int x = q.front().x,y = q.front().y,z = q.front().z;
		q.pop();
		if (x == ex && y == ey && z == ez) return ;
		for (int i = 0;i < 6;i++)
		{
			int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i],nz = z + dz[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > r || ny < 1 || ny > c || nz < 1 || nz > l) continue;
			if (a[nz][nx][ny] == '#') continue;
			if (vis[nx][ny][nz]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny,nz});
			vis[nx][ny][nz] = true;
			dis[nx][ny][nz] = dis[x][y][z] + 1;
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\2 二维棋盘BFS\临冬城宝藏.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> l >> r >> c;
	for (int i = 1;i <= l;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= r;j++)
			for (int k = 1;k <= c;k++)
			{
				cin >> a[i][j][k];
				if (a[i][j][k] == 'S') sx = j,sy = k,sz = i;
				else if (a[i][j][k] == 'E') ex = j,ey = k,ez = i;
			}
	bfs();
	if (vis[ex][ey][ez]) cout << dis[ex][ey][ez] << endl;
	else cout << "Winter is Coming!" << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\2 二维棋盘BFS\好奇怪的游戏.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs(int x,int y)
{
	queue<Node> q;
	q.push({x,y});
	dis[x][y] = 0;
	vis[x][y] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int fx = q.front().x,fy = q.front().y;
		q.pop();
		if (fx == 1 && fy == 1) return;
		for (int i = 0;i < 12;i++)
		{
			int nx = fx + dx[i],ny = fy + dy[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > 20 || ny < 1 || ny > 20) continue;
			if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny});
			vis[nx][ny] = true;
			dis[nx][ny] = dis[fx][fy] + 1;
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\2 二维棋盘BFS\好奇怪的游戏.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int x1,y1,x2,y2;
	cin >> x1 >> y1 >> x2 >> y2;
	memset(dis,-1,sizeof dis);
	memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
	bfs(x1,y1);
	cout << dis[1][1] << endl;
	memset(dis,-1,sizeof dis);
	memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
	bfs(x2,y2);
	cout << dis[1][1] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\2 二维棋盘BFS\最后的迷宫.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<Node> q;
	q.push({sx,sy});
	dis[sx][sy] = 0;
	vis[sx][sy] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int x = q.front().x,y = q.front().y;
		q.pop();
		if (flag[x][y] == true)
		{
			ans = dis[x][y];
			return ;
		}
		for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
		{
			int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
			if (a[nx][ny] == 'X') continue;
			if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny});
			vis[nx][ny] = true;
			dis[nx][ny] = dis[x][y] + 1;
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\2 二维棋盘BFS\最后的迷宫.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	while (true)
	{
		 cin >> ex >> ey >> sx >> sy;
		 if (ex == 0) break;
		 memset(flag,false,sizeof flag);
		 memset(dis,-1,sizeof dis);
		 memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
		 for (int i = 0;i < 8;i++)
		 {
		 	int nx = ex,ny = ey;
		 	while (1 <= nx && nx <= n && 1 <= ny && ny <= m && a[nx][ny] == 'O')
		 	{
		 		flag[nx][ny] = true;
		 		nx += dx[i],ny += dy[i];
		 	}
		 }
		 ans = -1;
		 bfs();
		 if (ans != -1) cout << ans << endl;
		 else cout << "Poor Harry" << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

void bfs()
{

	dis[sx][sy] = 0;
	vis[sx][sy] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int x = q.front().x,y = q.front().y;
		q.pop();
		if (x == ex && y == ey) return ;
		for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
		{
			int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
			if (a[nx][ny] == '0') continue;
			if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny});
			vis[nx][ny] = true;
			dis[nx][ny] = dis[x][y] + 1;
		}
	}
}

{

	q.push({x,y});
	dis[x][y] = 0;
	vis[x][y] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int fx = q.front().x,fy = q.front().y;
		q.pop();

		{
			int nx = fx + dx[i],ny = fy + dy[i];

			if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny});
			vis[nx][ny] = true;
			dis[nx][ny] = dis[fx][fy] + 1;
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\3n+1问题.cpp: F ---
long long F(long long x)
{
	if (x > 1e7)
	{
		if (x % 2 == 0) return F(x / 2) + 1;
		else return F(x * 3 + 1) + 1;
	}
	if (ans[x] != -1) return ans[x];
	if (x == 1) return ans[x] = 1;
	if (x % 2 == 0) return ans[x] = F(x / 2) + 1;
	else return ans[x] = F(x * 3 + 1) + 1;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\3n+1问题.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int q;
	scanf("%d",&q);
	int x;
	memset(ans,-1,sizeof ans);
	while (q--)
	{
		scanf("%d",&x);
		printf("%d\n",F(x));
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\变形：从1到B.cpp: F ---
long long F(int x)
{
	if (x > b) return 2e9;
	if (ans[x] != -1) return ans[x];
	if (x == b) return ans[x] = 0;
	return ans[x] = min(F(x + d),F(p * x + 1)) + 1;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\变形：从1到B.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	memset(ans,-1,sizeof ans);
	cin >> b >> d >> p;
	if (F(1) < 2e9) cout << F(1) << endl;
	else cout << -1 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\战争模型.cpp: dfs ---
long long dfs(int x,int y)
{
	if (x <= 0 && y <= 0) return 0;
	if (x <= 0) return -y;
	if (y <= 0) return x;
	if (f[x][y] != -1) return f[x][y];
	return f[x][y] = dfs(x - (y + b - 1) / b,y - (x + a - 1) / a);
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\战争模型.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	memset(f,-1,sizeof f);
	cin >> a >> b >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= q;i++)
	{
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		if (dfs(x,y) > 0) cout << "X " << dfs(x,y) << endl;
		else if (dfs(x,y) < 0) cout << "Y " << -dfs(x,y) << endl;
		else cout << "EVEN" << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\斐波那契.cpp: dfs ---
long long dfs(int n)
{
	if (f[n] != 0) return f[n];
	if (n == 1) return f[1] = 1;
	if (n == 2) return f[2] = 1;
	return f[n] = dfs(n - 1) + dfs(n - 2);
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\斐波那契.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	cout << dfs(n) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\斐波那契.cpp: dfs ---
long long dfs(int n)
{
	if (n == 1 || n == 2) return 1;
	return dfs(n - 1) + dfs(n - 2);
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\母亲的牛奶.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,int z)
{
	if (vis[x][y][z]) return ;
	vis[x][y][z] = true;
	if (x == 0) box[z]++;
	int t;
	t = min(x,b - y);
	dfs(x - t,y + t,z);
	t = min(x,c - z);
	dfs(x - t,y,z + t);
	t = min(y,a - x);
	dfs(x + t,y - t,z);
	t = min(y,c - z);
	dfs(x,y - t,z + t);
	t = min(z,a - x);
	dfs(x + t,y,z - t);
	t = min(z,b - y);
	dfs(x,y + t,z - t);
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\母亲的牛奶.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> a >> b >> c;
	dfs(0,0,c);
	for (int i = 0;i <= c;i++)
		if (box[i] != 0) cout << i << ' ';
	cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\滑雪.cpp: dfs ---
int dfs(int x,int y)
{
	if (f[x][y] != -1) return f[x][y];
	int maxn = 0;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > r || ny < 1 || ny > c) continue;
		if (mp[nx][ny] >= mp[x][y]) continue;
		maxn = max(maxn,dfs(nx,ny));
	}
	return f[x][y] = maxn + 1;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\滑雪.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	memset(f,-1,sizeof f);
	cin >> r >> c;
	for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= c;j++)
			cin >> mp[i][j];
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= c;j++)
			ans = max(ans,dfs(i,j));
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\递归函数.cpp: F ---
long long F(int n,int k)
{
	if (f[n][k]) return f[n][k];
	if (n > 0 && k == 0) return f[n][k] = 0;
	if (n >= 0 && k == n) return f[n][k] = 1;
	if (k > n) return f[n][k] = 0;
	return f[n][k] = (F(n - 1,k - 1) + k * F(n - 1,k) % mod) % mod;
}

--- J1-1\3 记忆化搜索\递归函数.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	cout << F(n,k) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\八皇后+.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x)
{
	for (int i = 1;i <= x;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= x;j++)
		{
			if (a[i] == a[j]) return false;
			if (abs(a[i] - a[j]) == abs(i - j)) return false;
		}
	return true;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\八皇后+.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step)
{
	if (cnt >= k) return ;
	if (step > n)
	{
		cnt++;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << a[i] << ' ';
		cout << endl;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (vis[i]) continue;
		a[step] = i;
		if (!check(step)) continue;
		vis[i] = true;
		dfs(step + 1);
		vis[i] = false;
		a[step] = 0;
	}
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\八皇后+.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n >> k;
	dfs(1);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\八皇后.cpp: check ---
bool check()
{
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
		{
			if (a[i] == a[j]) return false;
			if (abs(a[i] - a[j]) == abs(i - j)) return false;
		}
	return true;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\八皇后.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step)
{
	if (flag) return ;
	if (step > n)
	{
		if (check())
		{
			for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << a[i] << ' ';
			flag = true;
		}
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (vis[i]) continue;
		a[step] = i;
		vis[i] = true;
		dfs(step + 1);
		vis[i] = false;
		a[step] = 0;
	}
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\八皇后.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n;
	dfs(1);
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\数独.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step)
{
	if (step > 80)
	{
		for (int i = 0;i <= 8;i++)
			for (int j = 0;j <= 8;j++)
			{
				cout << a[i][j] << " \n"[j == 8];
			}
		return ;
	}
	int x = step / 9,y = step % 9;
	int k = x / 3 * 3 + y / 3;
	if (a[x][y] != 0) dfs(step + 1);
	else 
	{
		for (int i = 1;i <= 9;i++)
		{
			if (!r[x][i] && !c[y][i] && !g[k][i])
			{
				a[x][y] = i;
				r[x][i] = true;
				c[y][i] = true;
				g[k][i] = true;
				dfs(step + 1);
				a[x][y] = 0;
				r[x][i] = false;
				c[y][i] = false;
				g[k][i] = false;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\数独.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	for (int i = 0;i <= 8;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= 8;j++)
		{
			cin >> a[i][j];
			if (a[i][j] != 0)
			{
				r[i][a[i][j]] = true;
				c[j][a[i][j]] = true;
				g[i / 3 * 3 + j / 3][a[i][j]] = true;
			}
		}
	dfs(0);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\验证八皇后.cpp: check ---
bool check()
{
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
		{
			if (a[i] == a[j]) return false;
			if (abs(a[i] - a[j]) == abs(i - j)) return false;
		}
	return true;
}

--- J1-1\4 回溯进阶\验证八皇后.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	if (check()) cout << "Yes" << endl;
	else cout << "No" << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\又着火了.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	while (q.size())
	{
		int fx = q.front().x,fy = q.front().y;
		q.pop();
		for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
		{
			int nx = fx + dx[i],ny = fy + dy[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
			if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny});
			vis[nx][ny] = true;
			dis[nx][ny] = dis[fx][fy] + 1;
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\又着火了.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n >> m;
	cin >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
	{
		cin >> x >> y;
		dis[x][y] = 0;
		vis[x][y] = true;
		q.push({x,y});
	}
	bfs();
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			ans = max(ans,dis[i][j]);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			if (dis[i][j] == ans) cout << i << ' ' << j << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\囚禁坏人.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	vis[x][y] = true;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (mp[nx][ny] == '#') continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\囚禁坏人.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> mp[i][j];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			if (mp[i][j] == 'B')
				for (int k = 0;k < 4;k++)
				{
					int nx = i + dx[k],ny = j + dy[k];
					if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
					if (mp[nx][ny] == 'G') 
					{
						cout << "No" << endl;
						return ;
					}
					if (mp[nx][ny] == 'B') continue;
					mp[nx][ny] = '#';
				}
	memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
	if (mp[n][m] != '#') dfs(n,m);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			if (mp[i][j] == 'G' && !vis[i][j])
			{
				cout << "No" << endl;
				return ;
			}
	cout << "Yes" << endl;
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\囚禁坏人.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\白银的迷宫城.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	vis[x][y] = id;
	ans[id]++;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (mp[nx][ny] == '*') continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\迷宫.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	if (x == ex && y == ey)
	{
		ans++;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		vis[nx][ny] = true;
		dfs(nx,ny);
		vis[nx][ny] = false;
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\5 二维搜索综合练习\迷宫.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n >> m >> t;
	cin >> sx >> sy >> ex >> ey;
	vis[sx][sy] = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= t;i++)
	{
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		vis[x][y] = true;
	}
	dfs(sx,sy);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

}

--- J1-1\6 位运算与快速幂\原根检验.cpp: check ---
bool check(int a)
{
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		if (qpow(a,x[i],p) == 1) return false;
	return true;
}

--- J1-1\6 位运算与快速幂\原根检验.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> p >> n;
	int t = p - 1;
	x[++cur] = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i * i <= t;i++)
		if (t % i == 0) x[++cur] = i,x[++cur] = t / i;
	for (int i = 2;i <= p - 1;i++)
		if (check(i))
		{
			cout << i << endl;
			if (--n == 0) break;
		}
}

void solve()
{
	int a,b,k;
	cin >> a >> b >> k;
	cout << a * qpow(10,k - 1,b) % b * 10 / b << endl;
}

--- J1-1\6 位运算与快速幂\越狱.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	LL m,n;
	cin >> m >> n;
	cout << (mod + qpow(m,n,mod) - m * qpow(m - 1,n - 1,mod) % mod) % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\7 阶段复习\八皇后 Checker Challenge.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step)
{
	if (step > n)
	{
		if (cnt >= 3)
		{
			cnt++;
			return ;
		}
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << a[i] << ' ';
		cout << endl;
		cnt++;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (vis[i]) continue;
		a[step] = i;
		if (c[i] || z[step - i + n] || f[step + i]) continue;
		c[i]++;
		z[step - i + n]++;
		f[step + i]++;
		vis[i] = true;
		dfs(step + 1);
		vis[i] = false;
		a[step] = 0;
		c[i]--;
		z[step - i + n]--;
		f[step + i]--;
	}
}

--- J1-1\7 阶段复习\八皇后 Checker Challenge.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> n;
	dfs(1);
	cout << cnt << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-1\阶段复习.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y)
{
	vis[x][y] = true;
	ans++;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
	{
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n) continuel
		if (a[nx][ny] = '#') continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny);
	}
	return ;
}

--- J1-1\阶段复习.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<Node> q;
	q.push({sx,sy});
	vis[sx][sy] = true;
	dis[sx][sy] = 0;
	while (!q.empty())
	{
		int x = q.front().x,y = q.front().y;
		q.pop();
		if (x == ex && y == ey) return ;
		for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++)
		{
			int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n) continuel
			if (a[nx][ny] = '#') continue;
			if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
			q.push({nx,ny});
			vis[nx][ny] = true;
			dis[nx][ny] = dis[x][y] + 1;
		}
	}
}

--- J1-1\阶段复习.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x)
{
	for (int i = 1;i <= x;i++)
	{
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= x;j++)
		{
			if (a[i] == a[j]) return false;
			if (abs(a[i] - a[j]) == abs(i - j)) return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

--- J1-1\阶段复习.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step)
{
	if (cnt >= k) return ;
	if (step > n)
	{
		cnt++;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << a[i] << ' ';
		cout << endl;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (vis[i]) continue;
		a[step] = i;
		if (!check(step)) continue;
		vis[i] = true;
		dfs(step + 1);
		a[step] = 0;
		vis[i] = false;
	}
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: s2BIG ---
void s2BIG (string s,int a[])
{
	int al = s.length();
	for (int i = 1;i <= al;i++) a[i] = s[al - i] - '0';
	a[0] = al;
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: printBIG ---
void printBIG(int a[])
{
	int al = a[0];
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) cout << a[i];
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: cmpBIG ---
bool cmpBIG(int a[],int b[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	if (al != bl) return al < bl;
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) if (a[i] != b[i]) return a[i] < b[i];
	return false;
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: addBIG ---
void addBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = max(al,bl);
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = u;
		if (i <= al) t += a[i];
		if (i <= bl) t += b[i];
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	if (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: subBIG ---
void subBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = al;
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = a[i] - u;
		if (i <= bl) t -= b[i];
		if (t < 0)
		{
			c[i] = t + 10;
			u = 1;
		}
		else 
		{
			c[i] = t;
			u = 0;
		}
	}
	while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\0 高精度模板&dp\_.cpp: divBIG ---
void divBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long r = 0;
	for (int i = cl;i >= 1;i--)
	{
		r = r * 10 + a[i];
		c[i] = r / b;
		r %= b;
	}
	while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
	c[0] = cl;
}

}

void subBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
    int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
    int cl = al;
    int u = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
    {
        int t = a[i] - u;
        if (i <= bl) t -= b[i];
        if (t < 0)
        {
            c[i] = t + 10;
            u = 1;
        }
        else 
        {
            c[i] = t;
            u = 0;
        }
    }
    while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
    c[0] = cl;
}

int main() {

    s2BIG(s1,a); s2BIG(s2,b);
    if (cmpBIG(a,b)) subBIG(b,a,c);
    else subBIG(a,b,c);
    printBIG(c);

}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\佩尔数列.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	s2BIG("1",x[1]); s2BIG("1",y[1]);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		addBIG(x[i - 1],y[i - 1],x[i]);
		addBIG(x[i],x[i - 1],y[i]);
	}
	printBIG(x[n]);
	cout << endl;
	printBIG(y[n]);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\小猴的数字.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int L1,R1,L2,R2;
	cin >> L1 >> R1 >> L2 >> R2;
	string s1,s2;
	for (int i = L1;i <= R1;i++) s1 += to_string(i);
	for (int i = L2;i <= R2;i++) s2 += to_string(i);
	s2BIG(s1,a); s2BIG(s2,b);
	addBIG(a,b,c);
	printBIG(c);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\斐波那契.cpp: s2BIG ---
void s2BIG (string s,int a[])
{
	int al = s.length();
	for (int i = 1;i <= al;i++) a[i] = s[al - i] - '0';
	a[0] = al;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\斐波那契.cpp: addBIG ---
void addBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = max(al,bl);
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = u;
		if (i <= al) t += a[i];
		if (i <= bl) t += b[i];
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	if (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

void printBIG(int a[])
{
	int al = a[0];
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) cout << a[i];
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\斐波那契前缀.cpp: check ---
bool check(int a[],int b[])
{
	if (a[0] < b[0]) return false;
	for (int i = 1;i <= b[0];i++) if (a[a[0] - i + 1] != b[b[0] - i + 1]) return false;
	return true;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\票选冠军.cpp: cmpBIG ---
bool cmpBIG(int a[],int b[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	if (al != bl) return al < bl;
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) if (a[i] != b[i]) return a[i] < b[i];
	return false;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\票选冠军.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		char c;
		string s;
		cin >> c >> s;
		memset(tmp,0,sizeof tmp);
		s2BIG(s,tmp);
		if (c == 'A') addBIG(a,tmp,a);
		else addBIG(b,tmp,b);
	}
	if (cmpBIG(a,b))
	{
		cout << 'B' << endl;
		printBIG(b);
	}
	else 
	{
		cout << 'A' << endl;
		printBIG(a);
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\高精度求和.cpp: addBIG ---
void addBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
    int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
    int cl = max(al,bl);
    int u = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
    {
        int t = u;
        if (i <= al) t += a[i];
        if (i <= bl) t += b[i];
        c[i] = t % 10;
        u = t / 10;
    }
    if (u > 0)
    {
        cl++;
        c[cl] = u;
    }
    c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\高精度求差.cpp: cmpBIG ---
bool cmpBIG(int a[],int b[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	if (al != bl) return al < bl;
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) if (a[i] != b[i]) return a[i] < b[i];
	return false;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\高精度求差.cpp: subBIG ---
void subBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = al;
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = a[i] - u;
		if (i <= bl) t -= b[i];
		if (t < 0)
		{
			c[i] = t + 10;
			u = 1;
		}
		else 
		{
			c[i] = t;
			u = 0;
		}
	}
	while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
	c[0] = cl;
}

	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\1 高精度1\高精度求差.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	s2BIG(s,a); s2BIG(s,b);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> s;
		memset(tmp,0,sizeof tmp);
		s2BIG(s,tmp);
		if (cmpBIG(a,tmp)) addBIG(tmp,zero,a);
		if (cmpBIG(tmp,b)) addBIG(tmp,zero,b);
	}
	subBIG(a,b,c);
	printBIG(c);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\总分.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int m,n;
	cin >> m >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> p[i] >> t[i];
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= t[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i & 1][j - t[i]] + p[i]); 
		}
	cout << dp[n & 1][m] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\整数划分方案2.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j] % mod;
			if (j >= i) dp[i][j] += dp[i][j - i] % mod;
		}
	cout << dp[n][n] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\最大效益.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int s,n,d;
	cin >> s >> n >> d;
	for (int i = 1;i <= d;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i] >> b[i];
		a[i] /= 100;
	}
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int y = 1;y <= n;y++)
	{
		int l = s / 100;
		for (int i = 1;i <= d;i++)
			for (int j = 0;j <= l;j++)
			{
				dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
				if (j >= a[i]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i][j - a[i]] + b[i]);
			}
		s += dp[d][l];
	}
	cout << s << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\火柴拼数.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> a[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,greater<int>());
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= num[a[i]])
			{
				dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i][j - num[a[i]]] + 1);
			}
		}
	int len = dp[m][n];
	for (int k = 1;k <= len;k++)
	{
		for  (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		{
			if (num[a[i]] <= n && dp[m][n - num[a[i]]] + 1 == dp[m][n])
			{
				n -= num[a[i]];
				cout << a[i];
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	return 0;
}

		for (int j = 0;j <= t;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= a[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i & 1][j - a[i]] + b[i]);

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\神奇的四次方数.cpp: main ---
int main() 
{
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	int n,t;
	cin >> n;
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i * i * i * i <= n;i++)
	{
		t = i * i * i * i;
		for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= i * i * i * i) dp[i & 1][j] = min(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i & 1][j - i * i * i * i] + 1);
		}
	}
	cout << dp[t & 1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\质数和分解.cpp: isPrime ---
bool isPrime(int x)
{
	if (x == 0 || x == 1) return false;
	for (int i = 2;i * i <= x;i++)
		if (x % i == 0) return false;
	return true;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\质数和分解.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (isPrime(i) && j >= i) dp[i & 1][j] += dp[i & 1][j - i];
		}
	cout << dp[n & 1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\10 完全背包\麦香牛块.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,g = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		g = __gcd(g,a[i]);
	}
	if (g != 1)
	{
		cout << 0 << endl;
		return 0;
	}
	dp[0][0] = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= 1e5;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= a[i]) dp[i][j] |= dp[i][j - a[i]];
		}
	for (int i = 1e5;i >= 0;i--)
		if (!dp[n][i])
		{
			cout << i << endl;
			return 0;
		}
	cout << 0 << endl;
	return 0;
}

		{

--- J1-2\11 dp练习1\经典上楼梯问题.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		int x;
		cin >> x;
		a[x] = true;
	}
	dp[0] = 1,dp[1] = !a[1];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (a[i]) continue;
		dp[i] = (dp[i - 1] + dp[i - 2]) % mod;
	}
	cout << dp[n] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\11 dp练习1\香蕉分组.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int k = 0;
		for (int j = i;j >= 1;j--)
		{
			k += a[j];
			if (k >= 0) dp[i] = max(dp[i],dp[j - 1] + 1);
		}
	}
	if (dp[n] <= 0) cout << "Impossible" << endl;
	else cout << dp[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\11 dp练习1\香蕉采购.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,W;
	cin >> n >> W;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i] >> p[i];
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 50000;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= p[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i & 1][j - p[i]] + w[i]);
		}
	for (int j = 1;j <= 50000;j++)
		if (dp[n & 1][j] >= W)
		{
			cout << j << endl;
			return 0;
		}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\Dividing.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(dp,false,sizeof dp);
	int cur = 0,sum = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 6;i++)
	{
		int z,tmp = 1;
		cin >> z;
		sum += z * i;
		while (z >= tmp)
		{
			v[++cur] = i * tmp;
			z -= tmp;
			tmp *= 2;
		}
		if (z > 0) v[++cur] = i * z;
	}
	dp[0][0] = true;
	if (sum % 2)
	{
		cout << "Can't be divided." << endl;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= sum / 2;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= v[i]) dp[i & 1][j] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j - v[i]];
		}
	if (dp[cur & 1][sum / 2]) cout << "Can be divided." << endl;
	else cout << "Can't be divided." << endl;
	return ;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\Dividing.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	for (int i = 1;i <= T;i++)
	{
		printf("Collection #%d:\n",i);
		solve();
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\多重背包.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,W;
	cin >> n >> W;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> v[i] >> w[i] >> m[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= W;j++)
			for (int t = 0;t <= m[i];t++)
				if (j >= t * w[i])
					dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - t * w[i]] + t * v[i]);
	cout << dp[n][W] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\太空电梯.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.a < y.a;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\太空电梯.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,cur = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int x,y,z;
		cin >> x >> y >> z;
		int tmp = 1;
		while (z >= tmp)
		{
			s[++cur].a = y;
			s[cur].h = x * tmp;
			z -= tmp;
			tmp *= 2;
		}
		if (z > 0)
		{
			s[++cur].a = y;
			s[cur].h = x * z;
		}
	}
	sort(s + 1,s + cur + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= 40000;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= s[i].h && j <= s[i].a)
				dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - s[i].h] + s[i].h);
		}
	int ans = -1e9;
	for (int j = 0;j <= 40000;j++) ans = max(ans,dp[cur & 1][j]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\宝物筛选.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,W;
	cin >> n >> W;
	int cur = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int x,y,z;
		cin >> x >> y >> z;
		int tmp = 1;
		while (z >= tmp)
		{
			v[++cur] = tmp * x;
			w[cur] = tmp * y;
			z -= tmp;
			tmp *= 2;
		}
		if (z > 0)
		{
			v[++cur] = z * x;
			w[cur] = z * y;
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= W;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= w[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i - 1 & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - w[i]] + v[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[cur & 1][W] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\提款机.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	int cash,n;
	cin >> cash >> n;
	int cur = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int x,z;
		cin >> x >> z;
		c[i] = x;
		int tmp = 1;
		while (x >= tmp)
		{
			s[++cur] = tmp * z;
			x -= tmp;
			tmp *= 2;
		}
		if (x >= 0)
		{
			s[++cur] = x * z;
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= cash;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= s[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - s[i]] + s[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[cur & 1][cash] << endl;
	return ;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\提款机.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\硬币.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
	int cur = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int x = a[i],z = c[i];
		int tmp = 1;
		while (z >= tmp)
		{
			v[++cur] = x * tmp;
			z -= tmp;
			tmp *= 2;
		}
		if (z > 0)
		{
			v[++cur] = x * z;
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= v[i]) dp[i & 1][j] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j - v[i]];
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		if (dp[cur & 1][j]) ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
}

--- J1-2\12 多重背包\硬币.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\13 LCS\Palindrome.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	string s,t;
	cin >> n >> s;
	t = s;
	reverse(t.begin(),t.end());
	s = '?' + s,t = '?' + t;
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j - 1],dp[i - 1][j]);
			if (s[i] == t[j]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1);
		}
	cout << n - dp[n][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\13 LCS\基因相似度.cpp: f ---
int f(char a,char b)
{
	int s,t;
	if (a == 'A') s = 0;
	else if (a == 'C') s = 1;
	else if (a == 'G') s = 2;
	else if (a == 'T') s = 3;
	else s = 4;
	if (b == 'A') t = 0;
	else if (b == 'C') t = 1;
	else if (b == 'G') t = 2;
	else if (b == 'T') t = 3;
	else t = 4;
	return score[s][t];
}

--- J1-2\13 LCS\基因相似度.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int l1,l2;
	string a,b;
	cin >> l1 >> a >> l2 >> b;
	a = '?' + a;
	b = '?' + b;
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= l1;i++) dp[i][0] = dp[i - 1][0] + f(a[i],'-');
	for (int j = 1;j <= l2;j++) dp[0][j] = dp[0][j - 1] + f(b[j],'-');
	for (int i = 1;i <= l1;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= l2;j++)
			dp[i][j] = max(dp[i - 1][j - 1] + f(a[i],b[j]),max(dp[i][j - 1] + f(b[j],'-'),dp[i - 1][j] + f(a[i],'-')));
	cout << dp[l1][l2] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\13 LCS\最短公共父序列.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int k,l;
	cin >> k >> l;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) cin >> c[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= l;i++) cin >> d[i];
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) dp[i][0] = i;
	for (int j = 1;j <= l;j++) dp[0][j] = j;
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= l;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = min(dp[i - 1][j] + 1,dp[i][j - 1] + 1);
			if (c[i] == d[j]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1);
		}
	cout << dp[k][l] << endl;
	return 0;
}

	B = '?' + B;

		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = max(dp[i - 1][j],dp[i][j - 1]);
			if (A[i] == B[j]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1);
		}

}

int main(){

	cin >> A >> B;

	A = '?' + A;
	B = '?' + B;
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = min(min(dp[i - 1][j] + 1,dp[i][j - 1] + 1),dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1);
			if (A[i] == B[j]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - 1]);
		}

}

--- J1-2\14 二维棋盘dp\收集金币2.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,p;
	cin >> n >> p;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][1][0] = 0,dp[1][0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			for (int k = 0;k < p;k++)
			{
				dp[i][j][k] = max(dp[i - 1][j][k],dp[i][j - 1][k]);
				int x = (k - a[i][j] % p + p) % p;
				dp[i][j][k] = max(dp[i][j][k],dp[i - 1][j][x] + a[i][j]);
				dp[i][j][k] = max(dp[i][j][k],dp[i][j - 1][x] + a[i][j]);
			}
	cout << dp[n][n][0] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\14 二维棋盘dp\方格取数2020.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[1][0][0] = 0,dp[0][1][0] = 0;
	for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
	{
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		{
			dp[i][j][2] = max(dp[i - 1][j][2],dp[i - 1][j][0]) + a[i][j];
			dp[i][j][0] = max(dp[i][j - 1][0],max(dp[i][j - 1][1],dp[i][j - 1][2])) + a[i][j];
		}
		for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
			dp[i][j][1] = max(dp[i + 1][j][0],dp[i + 1][j][1]) + a[i][j];
	}
	cout << max(dp[n][m][0],dp[n][m][2]) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\14 二维棋盘dp\最大正方形.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			if (!a[i][j]) dp[i][j] = 0;
			else dp[i][j] = min(dp[i - 1][j - 1],min(dp[i - 1][j],dp[i][j - 1])) + 1;
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			ans = max(ans,dp[i][j]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];

	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][0] = 0;
	for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
	{
		dp[1][j] = min(dp[1][j - 1],dp[n][j - 1]) + a[1][j];
		for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j - 1],dp[i - 1][j - 1]) + a[i][j];
	}

	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = min(ans,dp[i][m]);

	return 0;
}

		{

--- J1-2\15 区间dp\乘法拼图.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	a[0] = 1,a[n + 1] = 1;
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][i] = 0,dp[i][i + 1] = 0;
	for (int len = 3;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i + 1;k < j;k++)
				dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k][j] + a[i] * a[j] * a[k]);
		}
	cout << dp[1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\15 区间dp\环形石子合并.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = n + 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) a[i] = a[i - n];
	for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i];
	memset(dp2,0x3f,sizeof dp2);
	for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) dp2[i][i] = 0;
	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= 2 * n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i;k < j;k++)
			{
				dp1[i][j] = max(dp1[i][j],dp1[i][k] + dp1[k + 1][j] + s[j] - s[i - 1]);
				dp2[i][j] = min(dp2[i][j],dp2[i][k] + dp2[k + 1][j] + s[j] - s[i - 1]);
			}
		}
	int ans1 = 0,ans2 = 1e9;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans1 = max(ans1,dp1[i][i + n - 1]);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans2 = min(ans2,dp2[i][i + n - 1]);
	cout << ans2 << endl << ans1 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\15 区间dp\石子合并.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i];
	}
	memset(dp2,0x3f,sizeof dp2);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp2[i][i] = 0;
	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i;k < j;k++)
			{
				dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k + 1][j] + s[j] - s[i - 1]);
				dp2[i][j] = min(dp2[i][j],dp2[i][k] + dp2[k + 1][j] + s[j] - s[i - 1]);
			}
		}
	cout << dp2[1][n] << endl << dp[1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\15 区间dp\能量项链.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> t[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) w[i] = t[i + 1];
	w[n] = t[1];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) t[i + n] = t[i],w[i + n] = w[i];
	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= 2 * n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i;k < j;k++)
				dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k + 1][j] + t[i] * w[k] * w[j]);
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i][i + n - 1]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\16 dp练习2\Cash-Mashine.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int M,N;
	cin >> M >> N;
	int cur = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++)
	{
		int x,z;
		cin >> x >> z;
		int tmp = 1;
		while (z >= tmp)
		{
			d[++cur] = tmp * x;
			z -= tmp;
			tmp *= 2;
		}
		if (z > 0) d[++cur] = z * x;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= M;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= d[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - d[i]] + d[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[cur & 1][M] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\16 dp练习2\可怜的小猴.cpp: main ---
int main()
{

	{
		cin >> t[i];
		s[i] = s[i - 1] + t[i];
	}

	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i;k < j;k++)
			{
				dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k + 1][j])

		}

--- J1-2\16 dp练习2\论文相似度.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	string a,b;
	cin >> a >> b;
	a = '?' + a,b = '?' + b;
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			if (a[i] == b[j]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1);
			else dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j - 1],dp[i - 1][j]);
		}
	cout << 4 * dp[n][m] - n - m << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\16 dp练习2\马特与狼.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 0;i <= n + 1;i++) dp[i][i] = 0,dp[i][i + 1] = 0;
	for (int len = 3;len <= n + 2;len++)
		for (int i = 0;i + len - 1 <= n + 1;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i + 1;k < j;k++)
				dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k][j] + a[k] + b[i] + b[j]);
		}
	cout << dp[0][n + 1] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\a_b.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\a_b.cpp: s2BIG ---
void s2BIG (string s,int a[])
{
	int al = s.length();
	for (int i = 1;i <= al;i++) a[i] = s[al - i] - '0';
	a[0] = al;
}

}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\佩尔数列2.cpp: printBIG ---

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\佩尔数列2.cpp: subBIG ---
void subBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = al;
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = a[i] - u;
		if (i <= bl) t -= b[i];
		if (t < 0)
		{
			c[i] = t + 10;
			u = 1;
		}
		else 
		{
			c[i] = t;
			u = 0;
		}
	}
	while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\佩尔数列2.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

	for (int i = 3;i <= n;i++)
	{
		mulBIG(x[i - 1],4,tmp);
		subBIG(tmp,x[i - 2],x[i]);
	}
	printBIG(x[n]);
	return 0;
}

void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{

	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}

		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\多重组合数.cpp: divBIG ---
void divBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long r = 0;
	for (int i = cl;i >= 1;i--)
	{
		r = r * 10 + a[i];
		c[i] = r / b;
		r %= b;
	}
	while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\多重组合数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	s2BIG("1",ans);
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) cin >> x[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) mulBIG(ans,i,ans);
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= x[i];j++) divBIG(ans,j,ans);
	printBIG(ans);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\幂的前缀.cpp: check ---
bool check(int a[],int b[])
{
	if (a[0] < b[0]) return false;
	for (int i = 1;i <= b[0];i++) if (a[a[0] - i + 1] != b[b[0] - i + 1]) return false;
	return true;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\线性递推数列.cpp: addBIG ---
void addBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = max(al,bl);
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = u;
		if (i <= al) t += a[i];
		if (i <= bl) t += b[i];
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	if (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\线性递推数列.cpp: mulBIG ---

		u /= 10;

}

	for (int i = 3;i <= n;i++)

		mulBIG(x[i - 1],a,tmp);
		mulBIG(x[i - 2],b,x[i]);

void subBIG(int a[],int b[],int c[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	int cl = al;
	int u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = a[i] - u;
		if (i <= bl) t -= b[i];
		if (t < 0)
		{
			c[i] = t + 10;
			u = 1;
		}
		else 
		{
			c[i] = t;
			u = 0;
		}
	}
	while (c[cl] == 0 && cl > 1) cl--;
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\麦森数.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = min(a[0],500);
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\2 高精度2\麦森数.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int p;
	cin >> p;
	cout << int(p * log10(2)) + 1 << endl;
	ans[0] = ans[1] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= p / 20;i++) mulBIG(ans,1024 * 1024,ans);
	for (int i = 1;i <= p % 20;i++) mulBIG(ans,2,ans);
	ans[1] -= 1;
	for (int i = 500;i >= 1;i--)
	{
		cout << ans[i];
		if (i % 50 == 1) cout << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\Lazy-Faith.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int a,b,q;
	cin >> a >> b >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= a;i++) cin >> s[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= b;i++) cin >> t[i];
	sort(s + 1,s + a + 1);
	sort(t + 1,t + b + 1);
	while (q--)
	{
		long long x,ans = 1e18;
		cin >> x;
		int poss = upper_bound(s + 1,s + a + 1,x) - s;
		int post = upper_bound(t + 1,t + b + 1,x) - t;
		long long s1 = s[poss],s2 = s[poss - 1];
		long long t1 = t[post],t2 = t[post - 1];
		if (poss <= a && post <= b) ans = min(ans,max(s1,t1) - x);
		if (poss > 1 && post > 1) ans = min(ans,x - min(s2,t2));
		if (poss <= a && post > 1)
		{
			ans = min(ans,s1 - x + s1 - t2);
			ans = min(ans,x - t2 + s1 - t2);
		}
		if (poss > 1 && post <= b)
		{
			ans = min(ans,t1 - x + t1 - s2);
			ans = min(ans,x - s2 + t1 - s2);
		}
		cout << ans << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\Snuke-Festival.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	sort(b + 1,b + n + 1);
	sort(c + 1,c + n + 1);
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		long long pos1 = upper_bound(c + 1,c + n + 1,b[i]) - c;
		long long pos2 = lower_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,b[i]) - a;
		ans += (n - pos1 + 1) * (pos2 - 1);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\二分查找.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,Q;
	cin >> n >> Q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= Q;i++)
	{
		int b;
		cin >> b;
		int pos = lower_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,b) - a;
		if (a[pos] == b) cout << "Yes" << endl;
		else cout << "No" << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\寻找固定的和.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> x[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> y[i];
	sort(x + 1,x + n + 1);
	sort(y + 1,y + n + 1);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (x[i] == x[i - 1]) continue;
		int xlowpos = lower_bound(x + 1,x + n + 1,x[i]) - x;
		int xupppos = upper_bound(x + 1,x + n + 1,x[i]) - x;
		int yupppos = upper_bound(y + 1,y + n + 1,k - x[i]) - y;
		int ylowpos = lower_bound(y + 1,y + n + 1,k - x[i]) - y;
		ans += min(xupppos - xlowpos,yupppos - ylowpos);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\查找脚码.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,Q;
	cin >> n >> Q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		a[i] -= i;
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= Q;i++)
	{
		long long d;
		cin >> d;
		int pos = lower_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,d) - a;
		if (a[pos] == d) cout << "Yes" << endl;
		else cout << "No" << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\次小差值.cpp: fun ---
void fun(int x)
{
	if (x <= minn) seco = minn,minn = x;
	else if (x < seco) seco = x;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\次小差值.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> b[i];
	sort(b + 1,b + m + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int pos = lower_bound(b + 1,b + m + 1,a[i]) - b;
		if (pos <= m) fun(b[pos] - a[i]);
		if (pos + 1 <= m) fun(b[pos + 1] - a[i]);
		if (pos - 1 >= 1) fun(a[i] - b[pos - 1]);
		if (pos - 2 >= 1) fun(a[i] - b[pos - 2]);
	}
	cout << minn << ' ' << seco << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\烦恼的高考志愿.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int m,n;
	cin >> m >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		cin >> a[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + m + 1);
	long long ans = 0,score;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> score;
		int pos = lower_bound(a + 1,a + m + 1,score) - a;
		if (pos == m + 1) ans += score - a[m];
		else if (pos == 1) ans += a[1] - score;
		else ans += min(abs(a[pos] - score),abs(score - a[pos - 1]));
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\3 二分查找\田忌又赛马.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		copya[i] = a[i];
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		cin >> b[i];
		copyb[i] = b[i];
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	sort(b + 1,b + m + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int pos = upper_bound(b + 1,b + m + 1,copya[i]) - b;
		cout << pos - 1 << ' ';
	}
	cout << endl;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		int pos = upper_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,copyb[i]) - a;
		cout << pos - 1 << ' ';
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\4 二分答案\分巧克力.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid)
{
	long long cnt = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cnt += (h[i] / mid) * (w[i] / mid);
	return cnt >= k;
}

--- J1-2\4 二分答案\分巧克力.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i] >> w[i];
	int L = 1,R = 2e9,ans = 0;
	while (L <= R)
	{
		int mid = L + R >> 1;
		if (check(mid))
		{
			L = mid + 1;
			ans = mid;
		}
		else R = mid - 1;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\4 二分答案\地铁建设.cpp: check ---
bool check(long long mid)
{
	long long ret = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (mid <= z[i]) ret += a[i] * mid;
		else ret += a[i] * z[i] + b[i] * (mid - z[i]);
	}
	return ret >= p;
}

		if (check(mid)) R = mid - 1;
		else L = mid + 1;
	}

	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\4 二分答案\市场.cpp: check ---
bool check(long long mid)
{
	int posa = upper_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,mid) - a - 1;
	int posb = m - (lower_bound(b + 1,b + m + 1,mid) - b) + 1;
	return posa >= posb;
}

--- J1-2\4 二分答案\数列分段.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid)
{
	if (maxn > mid) return false;
	int cnt = 1,sum = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (sum + a[i] <= mid) sum += a[i];
		else cnt++,sum = a[i];
	}
	return cnt <= m;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\4人传球游戏.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	a[0] = 1;
	b[0] = 0;
	c[0] = 0;
	d[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		a[i] = b[i - 1] + c[i - 1] + d[i - 1];
		b[i] = a[i - 1] + c[i - 1] + d[i - 1];
		c[i] = a[i - 1] + b[i - 1] + d[i - 1];
		d[i] = a[i - 1] + b[i - 1] + c[i - 1];
	}
	cout << a[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\d好数.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m,d;
	cin >> n >> m >> d;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) dp[1][i] = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			for (int k = 1;k <= m;k++)
				if (abs(j - k) <= d)
					dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][k],dp[i][j] %= mod;
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) ans += dp[n][i],ans %= mod;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\传球游戏.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	f[1][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		f[1][i] = f[n][i - 1] + f[2][i - 1];
		for (int j = 2;j < n;j++) f[j][i] = f[j - 1][i - 1] + f[j + 1][i - 1];
		f[n][i] = f[n - 1][i - 1] + f[1][i - 1];
	}
	cout << f[1][m] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\假期.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i] >> b[i] >> c[i];
	dpa[1] = a[1],dpb[1] = b[1],dpc[1] = c[1];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dpa[i] = a[i] + max(dpb[i - 1],dpc[i - 1]);
		dpb[i] = b[i] + max(dpa[i - 1],dpc[i - 1]);
		dpc[i] = c[i] + max(dpa[i - 1],dpb[i - 1]);
	}
	cout << max(dpa[n],max(dpb[n],dpc[n])) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\幸运数字.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	if (n == 1) {cout << 9 << endl;return 0;}
	dp[1][1] = 8;
	dp[2][1] = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dp[1][i] = (dp[2][i - 1] + dp[1][i - 1] * 9) % mod;
		dp[2][i] = (dp[1][i - 1] + dp[2][i - 1] * 9) % mod;
	}
	cout << dp[1][n] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\整数划分方案.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1];
			if (i > j) dp[i][j] = (dp[i][j] + dp[i - j][j]) % mod;
		}
	cout << dp[n][k] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\青蛙1.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	dp[1] = 0,dp[2] = abs(h[2] - h[1]);
	for (int i = 3;i <= n;i++)
		dp[i] = min(dp[i - 1] + abs(h[i] - h[i - 1]),dp[i - 2] + abs(h[i] - h[i - 2]));
	cout << dp[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 递推\青蛙2.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	dp[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int maxn = 1e9;
		for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++)
		{
			if (i < j + 1) break;
			maxn = min(maxn,dp[i - j] + abs(h[i] - h[i - j]));
		}
		dp[i] = maxn;
	}
	cout << dp[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

	return cnt <= k;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\切木棍.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	int L = 1,R = 1e9,ans = -1;
	while (L <= R)
	{
		int mid = L + R >> 1;
		if (check(mid))
		{
			R = mid - 1;
			ans = mid;
		}
		else L = mid + 1;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\班长竞选.cpp: s2BIG ---
void s2BIG (string s,int a[])
{
	int al = s.length();
	for (int i = 1;i <= al;i++) a[i] = s[al - i] - '0';
	a[0] = al;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\班长竞选.cpp: printBIG ---
void printBIG(int a[])
{
	int al = a[0];
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) cout << a[i];
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\班长竞选.cpp: cmpBIG ---
bool cmpBIG(int a[],int b[])
{
	int al = a[0],bl = b[0];
	if (al != bl) return al < bl;
	for (int i = al;i >= 1;i--) if (a[i] != b[i]) return a[i] < b[i];
	return false;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\班长竞选.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\班长竞选.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,id = 0;
	cin >> n;
	memset(ans,0,sizeof ans);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		string s;
		cin >> s;
		s2BIG(s,a);
		if (cmpBIG(ans,a))
		{
			mulBIG(a,1,ans);
			id = i;
		}
	}
	cout << id << endl;
	printBIG(ans);
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\第k未被包含数.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,Q;
	cin >> n >> Q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= Q;i++)
	{
		long long k;
		cin >> k;
		int pos = upper_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,k - 1) - a;
		for (int i = 1;i <= 20;i++)
			pos = upper_bound(a + 1,a + n + 1,k + pos - 1) - a;
		cout << k + pos - 1 << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\阶乘数码.cpp: s2BIG ---
void s2BIG (string s,int a[])
{
	int al = s.length();
	for (int i = 1;i <= al;i++) a[i] = s[al - i] - '0';
	a[0] = al;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\阶乘数码.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\阶乘数码.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,a;
	cin >> n >> a;
	s2BIG("1",mul);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) mulBIG(mul,i,mul);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= mul[0];i++)
		if (mul[i] == a) ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\魔法集会.cpp: check ---
bool check(long long mid)
{
	long long cnt = 0;
	int tmp = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cnt += (c[i] + tmp) / mid;
		if (c[i] + tmp > mid)
			tmp = (c[i] + tmp) % mid;
		else tmp = c[i];
	}
	return cnt >= k;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\魔法集会.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(c,0,sizeof c);
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
	long long L = 1,R = 1e18,ans = 0;
	while (L <= R)
	{
		long long mid = L + R >> 1;
		if (check(mid))
		{
			L = mid + 1;
			ans = mid;
		}
		else R = mid - 1;
	}
	cout << ans * k << endl;
	return ;
}

--- J1-2\5 高精二分练习\魔法集会.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\删数最大子段和.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	int ans = -1e9;
	for (int t = 1;t <= n;t++)
	{
		memset(dp,-0x3f, sizeof dp);
		int tmp = -1e9;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		{
			if (i == t) continue;
			else if (i == t + 1)
				dp[i] = max(dp[i - 2] + a[i],a[i]),tmp = max(tmp,dp[i]);
			else
				dp[i] = max(dp[i - 1] + a[i],a[i]),tmp = max(tmp,dp[i]);
		}
		ans = max(ans,tmp);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

	cin >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= q;i++)
	{

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\单词的划分.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	string s;
	int n;
	cin >> s >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		string x;
		cin >> x;
		mp[x] = true;
	}
	int len = s.size();
	s = '?' + s;
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= len;i++)
	{
		string k = "";
		for (int j = i;j >= 1;j--)
		{
			k = s[j] + k;
			if (mp.count(k)) dp[i] = min(dp[i],dp[j - 1] + 1);
		}
	}
	cout << dp[len] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\单词解密.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	int n = s.size();
	s = '?' + s;
	dp[0] = 1,dp[1] = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int num = (s[i - 1] - '0') * 10 + (s[i] - '0');
		dp[i] += dp[i - 1],dp[i] %= mod;
		if (s[i] == '0') dp[i] -= dp[i - 1];
		if (num >= 10 && num <= 26) dp[i] += dp[i - 2],dp[i] %= mod;
	}
	cout << dp[n] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\数列划分.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,s;
	cin >> n >> s;
	sum[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum[i] = sum[i - 1] + a[i];
	}
	dp[0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++)
			if (sum[i] - sum[j - 1] <= s)
			{
				dp[i] += dp[j - 1];
				dp[i] %= mod;
			}
	cout << dp[n] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\最大子段和3.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	int ret = -1e9;
	for (int i = pos;i <= n;i++)
		dp[i] = max(dp[i - 1] + a[i],a[i]),ret = max(ret,dp[i]);
	cout << ret << endl;
	return ;
}

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\最大子矩形和.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			s[i][j] = s[i - 1][j] + a[i][j];
	int ans = 0;
	for (int x1 = 1;x1 <= n;x1++)
		for (int x2 = 1;x2 <= m;x2++)
		{
			memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
			for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			{
				dp[j] = max(dp[j - 1],0) + s[x2][j] - s[x1 - 1][j];
				ans = max(ans,dp[j]);
			}
		}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\6 序列dp\解码方法.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	int n = s.size();
	s = '?' + s;
	dp[0] = 1,dp[1] = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int num = (s[i - 1] - '0') * 10 + (s[i] - '0');
		dp[i] += dp[i - 1];
		if (num >= 10 && num <= 25) dp[i] += dp[i - 2];
	}
	cout << dp[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\7 LIS\信封问题.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	if (x.h != y.h) return x.h < y.h;
	else return x.w > y.w;
}

--- J1-2\7 LIS\信封问题.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].w;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].h;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	int cnt = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int pos = lower_bound(dp + 1,dp + cnt + 1,s[i].w) - dp;
		dp[pos] = s[i].w;
		cnt = max(cnt,pos);
	}
	cout << cnt << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\7 LIS\合唱队形.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> t[i];
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int pos = lower_bound(dp + 1,dp + ans + 1,t[i]) - dp;
		dp[pos] = t[i];
		ans = max(ans,pos);
		f1[i] = ans;
	}
	ans = 0;
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
	{
		int pos = lower_bound(dp + 1,dp + ans + 1,t[i]) - dp;
		dp[pos] = t[i];
		ans = max(ans,pos);
		f2[i] = ans;
	}
	int maxn = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) maxn = max(maxn,f1[i] + f2[i] - 1);
	cout << n - maxn << endl;
	return 0;
}

	{
		int pos = upper_bound(dp + 1,dp + cnt + 1,a[i]) - dp;

--- J1-2\7 LIS\接龙数列.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dp[i] = 1;
		for (int j = 0;j < i;j++)
			if (a[j][a[j].size() - 1] == a[i][0])
				dp[i] = max(dp[i],dp[j] + 1);
	}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
	cout << n - ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\7 LIS\游园安排.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	string tmp;
	cin >> tmp;
	tmp = '?' + tmp;
	int cur = 0;
	int len = tmp.size();
	for (int i = 1;i <= len;i++)
	{
		if (tmp[i] >= 'A' && tmp[i] <= 'Z')
			s[++cur] += tmp[i];
		else s[cur] += tmp[i];
	}
	int cnt = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= len;i++)
	{
		int pos = lower_bound(dp + 1,dp + cnt + 1,s[i]) - dp;
		dp[pos] = s[i];
		cnt = max(cnt,pos);
		ans[pos] = ans[pos - 1] + s[i];
	}
	cout << ans[cnt] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\01背包模型.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i] >> v[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= w[i]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - w[i]] + v[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[n][m] << endl;
	return 0;
}

		for (int j = 0;j <= c;j++)

			if (j >= v[i])
				dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - v[i]] + v[i]);

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\开心的金明.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> v[i] >> p[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= v[i]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - v[i]] + p[i] * v[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[m][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\最大约数和.cpp: factor_sum ---
int factor_sum(int x)
{
	int ret = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i * i <= x;i++)
	{
		if (x % i == 0) ret += i + x / i;
		if (i * i == x) ret -= i;
	}
	return ret - x;
}

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\最大约数和.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int s;
	cin >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= s;i++) sum[i] = factor_sum(i);
	for (int i = 1;i <= s;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= s;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= i) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - i] + sum[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[s][s] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\烹调方案.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.c * y.b < y.c * x.b;
}

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\烹调方案.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int t,n;
	cin >> t >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].a;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].b;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].c;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= t;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= s[i].c)
			{
				int tmp = dp[i - 1 & 1][j - s[i].c] + s[i].a - j * s[i].b;
				dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],tmp);
			}
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int j = 1;j <= t;j++) ans = max(dp[n & 1][j],ans);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\8 01背包基本模型\精卫填海.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int v,n,c;
	cin >> v >> n >> c;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> k[i] >> m[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= c;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= m[i])
				dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - m[i]] + k[i]);
		}
	if (dp[n & 1][c] < v) cout << "Impossible" << endl;
	else for (int j = 1;j <= c;j++)
		if (dp[n & 1][j] >= v) 
		{
			cout << c - j << endl;
			break;
		}
	return 0;
}
