--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\分割等和子集.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,sum = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum += a[i];
	}
	if (sum % 2 == 1)
	{
		cout << "No" << endl;
		return 0;
	}
	dp[0][0] = 1,sum /= 2;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= sum;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= a[i]) dp[i & 1][j] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j - a[i]];
		}
	if (dp[n & 1][sum]) cout << "Yes" << endl;
	else cout << "No" << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\分配礼物.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,sum = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum += a[i];
	}
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= sum;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= a[i]) dp[i][j] |= dp[i - 1][j - a[i]];
		}
	int ans = 1e9;
	for (int j = 0;j <= sum / 2;j++)
		if (dp[n][j])
			ans = min(ans,abs((sum - j) - j));
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\子集的和.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	if ((n * (n + 1)) % 4 != 0)
	{
		cout << 0 << endl;
		return 0;
	}
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	int sum = n * (n + 1) / 4;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= sum;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= i) dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][j - i];
		}
	cout << dp[n][sum] / 2 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\小A点菜.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= a[i]) dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][j - a[i]];
		}
	cout << dp[n][m] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\砝码称重.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int cur = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 6;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		for (int j = 1;j <= a[i];j++) w[++cur] = wei[i];
	}
	dp[0][0] = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= 1000;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= w[i]) dp[i][j] |= dp[i - 1][j - w[i]];
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int j = 1;j <= 1000;j++)
		if (dp[cur][j]) ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\符号添加.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m,sum = 0;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum += a[i];
	}
	dp[0][sum] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = -sum;j <= sum;j++)
		{
			if (j - a[i] >= -sum) dp[i][j + sum] = (dp[i - 1][j - a[i] + sum] + dp[i][j + sum]) % mod;
			if (j + a[i] <= m + sum) dp[i][j + sum] = (dp[i - 1][j + a[i] + sum] + dp[i][j + sum]) % mod;
		}
	cout << dp[n][m + sum] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\装箱问题.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int v,n;
	cin >> v >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= v;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= w[i]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - w[i]] + w[i]);
		}
	cout << v - dp[n][v] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\音量调节.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,bl,ml;
	cin >> n >> bl >> ml;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
	dp[0][bl] = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= ml;j++)
		{
			if (j >= c[i]) dp[i][j] |= dp[i - 1][j - c[i]];
			if (j + c[i] <= ml) dp[i][j] |= dp[i - 1][j + c[i]];
		}
	for (int j = ml;j >= 0;j--)
		if (dp[n][j])
		{
			cout << j << endl;
			return 0;
		}
	cout << -1 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J1-2\9 布尔型及计数形背包\飞盘队.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,f;
	cin >> n >> f;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> r[i];
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= f;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][(j - r[i] + f) % f];
			dp[i][j] %= mod;
		}
	cout << dp[n][0] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\三角形牧场.cpp: helen ---
double helen(int a,int b,int c)
{
	double p = (a + b + c) / 2.0;
	double x = p * (p - a) * (p - b) * (p - c);
	if (x < 0) return 0;
	else return sqrt(x);
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\三角形牧场.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,sum = 0,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> l[i];
		sum += l[i];
	}
	dp[0][0][0] = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= sum / 2;j++)
			for (int k = 0;k <= sum / 2;k++)
			{
				int l1 = j,l2 = k,l3 = sum - j - k;
				if (j >= l[i]) dp[i & 1][j][k] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j - l[i]][k];
				if (k >= l[i]) dp[i & 1][j][k] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j][k - l[i]];
				dp[i & 1][j][k] |= dp[i - 1 & 1][j][k];
			}
	for (int j = 0;j <= sum;j++)
		for (int k = 0;k <= sum;k++)
			if (dp[n & 1][j][k]) ans = max(ans,(int) floor(helen(sum - j - k,j,k) * 100));
	cout << (ans == 0 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\包裹分拣2.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,T;
	cin >> n >> T;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i] >> v[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= T;j++)
			for (int k = 0;k <= T;k++)
			{
				dp[i & 1][j][k] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j][k];
				if (j >= a[i])
					dp[i & 1][j][k] = max(dp[i & 1][j][k],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - a[i]][k] + v[i]);
				if (k >= a[i])
					dp[i & 1][j][k] = max(dp[i & 1][j][k],dp[i - 1 & 1][j][k - a[i]] + v[i]);
			}
	cout << dp[n & 1][T][T] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\挑选间谍.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m,x;
	cin >> n >> m >> x;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i] >> b[i] >> c[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= m;j++)
			for (int k = 0;k <= x;k++)
			{
				dp[i & 1][j][k] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j][k];
				if (j >= a[i] && k >= b[i])
					dp[i & 1][j][k] = max(dp[i & 1][j][k],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - a[i]][k - b[i]] + c[i]);
			}
	cout << dp[n & 1][m][x] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\监听音量.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,b;
	cin >> n >> b;
	for (int i = 1;i <= b;i++) cin >> v[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		if (a[i - 1] > 0) d[i] = a[i] - a[i - 1] + 1;
		else d[i] = a[i];
	}
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= b;i++)
		for (int j = v[i];j <= 1e5;j++)
			dp[j] = min(dp[j],dp[j - v[i]] + 1);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (d[i] < 0 || dp[d[i]] > 1e9)
		{
			cout << -1 << endl;
			return 0;
		}
		ans += dp[d[i]];
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\超大容量背包.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,W,sum = 0;
	cin >> n >> W;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> w[i] >> v[i];
		sum += v[i];
	}
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= sum;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= v[i]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - v[i]] + w[i]);
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int j = 0;j <= sum;j++)
		if (dp[n][j] <= W) ans = j;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\1 背包综合\面包师.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m >> c[0] >> d[0];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> a[i] >> b[i] >> c[i] >> d[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
			for (int k = 0;k <= a[i] / b[i] && k <= j / c[i];k++)
				dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - k * c[i]] + k * d[i]);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int j = 0;j <= n;j++)
		ans = max(ans,dp[m][j] + (n - j) / c[0] * d[0]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\2 升维技巧综合\修建支柱.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> v[i];
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][M] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = -M;j <= M;j++)
		{
			dp[i][M + j] = max(dp[i][M + j],dp[i - 1][M + j]);
			if (M + j >= h[i]) dp[i][M + j] = max(dp[i][M + j],dp[i - 1][M + j - h[i]] + v[i]);
			if (M + j + h[i] <= 2 * M) dp[i][M + j] = max(dp[i][M + j],dp[i - 1][M + j + h[i]] + v[i]);
		}
	cout << dp[n][M] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\2 升维技巧综合\兔子的工作.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	s = '?' + s;
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0][0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= i;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j][0] = min(dp[i - 1][j][0],dp[i - 1][j][1]);
			if (s[i] == '1')
				for (int t = 1;t <= j && s[i - t + 1] == '1';t++)
					dp[i][j][1] = min(dp[i][j][1],dp[i - t][j - t][0] + t * (t + 1) / 2);
		}
	for (int j = n;j >= 0;j--)
	{
		if (dp[n][j][1] <= k)
		{
			cout << j << endl;
			return 0;
		}
		if (dp[n][j][0] <= k)
		{
			cout << j << endl;
			return 0;
		}
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\2 升维技巧综合\小猴拿糖.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		dp[i][0] = max(dp[i - 1][0],dp[i - 1][1]),dp[i][1] = dp[i - 1][0] + a[i];
	cout << max(dp[n][0],dp[n][1]) << endl;
}

--- J2-1\2 升维技巧综合\小猴拿糖.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios :: sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\2 升维技巧综合\平衡的路径.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int h,w;
	cin >> h >> w;
	for (int i = 1;i <= h;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= w;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	for (int i = 1;i <= h;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= w;j++)
			cin >> b[i][j];
	memset(dp,false,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][1][M] = dp[1][0][M] = true;
	for (int i = 1;i <= h;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= w;j++)
			for (int k = -M;k <= M;k++)
			{
				int t = a[i][j] - b[i][j];
				if (M + k - t >= 0 && k + M - t <= 2 * M)
					dp[i][j][k + M] |= dp[i - 1][j][M + k - t],
					dp[i][j][k + M] |= dp[i][j - 1][M + k - t];
				if (M + k + t <= 2 * M && k + M + t >= 0)
					dp[i][j][k + M] |= dp[i - 1][j][M + k + t],
					dp[i][j][k + M] |= dp[i][j - 1][M + k + t];
			}
	for (int t = 0;t <= M;t++)
		if (dp[h][w][M + t] || dp[h][w][M - t])
		{
			cout << t << endl;
			return 0;
		}
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\2 升维技巧综合\股票买卖.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (i == 1) dp[i][1] = -a[i];
		else dp[i][1] = max(dp[i - 1][1],dp[i - 2][0] - a[i]);
		if (i > 1) dp[i][0] = max(dp[i - 1][0],dp[i - 1][1] + a[i]);
		else dp[i][0] = 0;
	}
	cout << dp[n][0] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\小明的U盘.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.w < y.w;
}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\小明的U盘.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m,l,s;
	cin >> n >> m >> l >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].w;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].v;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= s + m;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= a[i].w) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - a[i].w] + a[i].v);
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 0;i <= m;i++)
	{
		int c = l + m - i,t = n;
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			if (a[j].w > c)
			{
				t = j - 1;
				break;
			}
		ans = max(ans,dp[t][s + i]);
	}
	cout << ans << endl; 
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\改造过山车.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j < i;j++)
		{
			if (h[i] > h[j]) dp1[i] = max(dp1[i],dp1[j] + 1);
			f[i] = max(f[i - 1],dp1[i]);
		}
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= n + 1;j++)
		{
			if (h[i] > h[j]) dp2[i] = max(dp2[i],dp2[j] + 1);
			g[i] = max(g[i + 1],dp2[i]);
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		ans = max(ans,dp1[i] + dp2[i] - 1),ans = max(ans,f[i - 1] + g[i + 1]);
	cout << n - ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\最大子段乘积.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	lmax[0] = -1e9,lmin[0] = 1e9;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dpmax1[i] = max(a[i],dpmax1[i - 1] + a[i]);
		dpmin1[i] = min(a[i],dpmin1[i - 1] + a[i]);
		lmax[i] = max(lmax[i - 1],dpmax1[i]);
		lmin[i] = min(lmin[i - 1],dpmin1[i]);
	}
	rmax[n + 1] = -1e9,rmin[n + 1] = 1e9;
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
	{
		dpmax2[i] = max(a[i],dpmax2[i + 1] + a[i]);
		dpmin2[i] = min(a[i],dpmin2[i + 1] + a[i]);
		rmax[i] = max(rmax[i + 1],dpmax2[i]);
		rmin[i] = min(rmin[i + 1],dpmin2[i]);
	}
	long long ans = -2e18;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n - 1;i++)
		ans = max(ans,1ll * lmax[i - 1] * rmax[i + 1]),
		ans = max(ans,1ll * lmin[i - 1] * rmin[i + 1]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\杠杆原理.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int l,f,w,n,m;
	cin >> l >> f >> w >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i] >> b[i];
	cin >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> c[i] >> d[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= w;j++)
		{
			dp1[i & 1][j] = dp1[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= b[i]) dp1[i & 1][j] = max(dp1[i & 1][j],dp1[i - 1 & 1][j - b[i]] + a[i]);
		}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= w;j++)
		{
			dp2[i & 1][j] = dp2[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= d[i]) dp2[i & 1][j] = max(dp2[i & 1][j],dp2[i - 1 & 1][j - d[i]] + c[i]);
		}
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int j = 0;j <= w;j++)
		ans = max(ans,1ll * (l + dp1[n & 1][j]) * (f + dp2[m & 1][w - j]));
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\点菜.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,t;
	cin >> n >> t;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].a >> s[i].b;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= 6000;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			if (j >= s[i].a && j - s[i].a < t) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - s[i].a] + s[i].b);
		}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 0;i <= 6000;i++)
		ans = max(ans,dp[n][i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\3 动规综合\魔法货船.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,m,maxs = 0;
	cin >> n >> m >> s[0];
	maxs = s[0];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> v[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {cin >> s[i]; maxs = max(maxs,s[i]);}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> c[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= maxs;j++)
		{
			dp[i & 1][j] = dp[i - 1 & 1][j];
			if (j >= w[i]) dp[i & 1][j] = max(dp[i & 1][j],dp[i - 1 & 1][j - w[i]] + v[i]);
		}
	int ans = -1e9;
	for (int i = 0;i <= m;i++)
		ans = max(ans,dp[n & 1][s[i]] - c[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\k阶回文子串.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	int n = s.size();
	s = '?' + s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dp[i][i] = 1,cnt[1]++;
		if (s[i] == s[i - 1]) dp[i - 1][i] = 2,cnt[2]++;
	}
	for (int len = 3;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			if (s[i] == s[j] && dp[i + 1][j - 1] > 0)
				dp[i][j] = dp[i][i + len / 2 - 1] + 1;
			cnt[dp[i][j]]++;
		}
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) ans[i] = ans[i + 1] + cnt[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << ' ';
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\合唱队.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i],dp[i][i][0] = 1;
	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			if (a[i] < a[i + 1]) dp[i][j][0] += dp[i + 1][j][0],dp[i][j][0] %= mod;
			if (a[i] < a[j]) dp[i][j][0] += dp[i + 1][j][1],dp[i][j][0] %= mod;
			if (a[j] > a[j - 1]) dp[i][j][1] += dp[i][j - 1][1],dp[i][j][1] %= mod;
			if (a[j] > a[i]) dp[i][j][1] += dp[i][j - 1][0],dp[i][j][1] %= mod;
		}
	cout << (dp[1][n][0] + dp[1][n][1]) % mod << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\平方拼图.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int len = 3;len <= n + 2;len++)
		for (int i = 0;i + len - 1 <= n + 1;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			for (int k = i + 1;k <= j - 1;k++)
				dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k][j] + (a[i] + a[k] + a[j]) * (a[i] + a[k] + a[j]));
		}
	cout << dp[0][n + 1] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\涂色.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	int n = s.size();
	s = '?' + s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][i] = 1;
	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			dp[i][j] = dp[i + 1][j] + 1;
			for (int k = i + 1;k <= j;k++)
				if (s[i] == s[k]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i + 1][k] + dp[k + 1][j]);
		}
	cout << dp[1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\祖玛.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dp[i][i] = 1;
		if (c[i] == c[i - 1]) dp[i - 1][i] = 1;
		else dp[i - 1][i] = 2;
	}
	for (int len = 3;len <= n;len++)
	{
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			if (c[i] == c[j]) dp[i][j] = dp[i + 1][j - 1];
			for (int k = i;k < j;k++) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k + 1][j]);
		}
	}
	cout << dp[1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\美味的零食.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> v[i],s[i] = s[i - 1] + v[i],dp[i][i] = v[i];
	for (int len = 2;len <= n;len++)
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			dp[i][j] = max(dp[i + 1][j] + v[i] + s[j] - s[i],dp[i][j - 1] + v[j] + s[j - 1] - s[i - 1]);
		}
	cout << dp[1][n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\4 区间动规进阶\长为k的回文子串.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	int n = s.size();
	s = '?' + s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dp[i][i] = true;
		if (s[i] == s[i - 1]) dp[i - 1][i] = true;
	}
	for (int len = 1;len <= n;len++)
	{
		int ans = 0;
		for (int i = 1;i + len - 1 <= n;i++)
		{
			int j = i + len - 1;
			if (s[i] == s[j] && len != 1 && len != 2) dp[i][j] |= dp[i + 1][j - 1];
			if (dp[i][j]) ans++;
		}
		cout << ans << ' ';
	}
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\5 dp练习3\_删数最大子段和.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) l[i] = max(l[i - 1] + a[i],a[i]);
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) r[i] = max(r[i + 1] + a[i],a[i]);
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,max(l[i - 1] + r[i + 1],max(l[i - 1],r[i + 1])));
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\5 dp练习3\声控打字机.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	string s;
	cin >> n >> s;
	s = '?' + s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (s[i] == 'm' || s[i] == 'w')
		{
			cout << 0 << endl;
			return 0;
		}
	int cnt = 0;
	dp[0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		dp[i] = dp[i - 1];
		if (s[i] == s[i - 1] && (s[i] == 'n' || s[i] == 'v')) dp[i] = (dp[i - 1] + dp[i - 2]) % mod;
	}
	cout << dp[n] % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\5 dp练习3\接苹果2.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int t,w,c;
	cin >> t >> w >> c;
	for (int i = 1;i <= t;i++) cin >> a[i];
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0][1] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= t;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= w;j++)
			for (int k = 1;k <= c;k++)
			{
				dp[i][j][k] = max(dp[i][j][k],dp[i - 1][j][k]);
				if (a[i] == k) dp[i][j][a[i]] = max(dp[i][j][a[i]], dp[i - 1][j][k] + 1);
				else if (j >= 1) dp[i][j][a[i]] = max(dp[i][j][a[i]],dp[i - 1][j - 1][k] + 1);
			}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= c;i++)
		for (int j = 0; j <= w; j++)
			ans = max(ans,dp[t][j][i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\5 dp练习3\货币系统.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	dp[0][0] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 0;j <= m;j++)
		{
			dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
			for (int k = 1;j >= k * a[i];k++)
				dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][j - k * a[i]];
		}
	cout << dp[n][m] << endl;
	return 0;
}

		int s,e;

		d[s]++,d[e + 1]--;
	}
	for (int i = 0;i <= l;i++)

		tmp += d[i];
		if (tmp == 0) ans++;
	}

--- J2-1\6 二位前缀和与差分\最大子矩形和.cpp: main ---
int main(){

	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
		{
			cin >> a[i][j];
			s[i][j] = s[i - 1][j] + s[i][j - 1] - s[i - 1][j - 1] + a[i][j];
		}
	for (int x1 = 1;x1 <= n;x1++)
		for (int x2 = x1;x2 <= n;x2++)
			for (int y1 = 1;y1 <= m;y1++)
				for (int y2 = y1;y2 <= m;y2++)
					ans = max(ans,s[x2][y2] - s[x1 - 1][y2] - s[x2][y1 - 1] + s[x1 - 1][y1 - 1]);

}

--- J2-1\6 二位前缀和与差分\激光炸弹.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int x,y,v;
		cin >> x >> y >> v;
		x++,y++;
		a[x][y] += v;;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= 5001;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 5001;j++)
			s[i][j] = s[i - 1][j] + s[i][j - 1] - s[i - 1][j - 1] + a[i][j];
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i + m - 1 <= 5001;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j + m - 1 <= 5001;j++)
		{
			int x = i + m - 1,y = j + m - 1;
			ans = max(ans,s[x][y] - s[x][j - 1] - s[i - 1][y] + s[i - 1][j - 1]);
		}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\6 二位前缀和与差分\焚风现象.cpp: cal ---
long long cal(int x)
{
	if (x > 0) return -1ll * x * s;
	else return -1ll * x * t;
}

--- J2-1\6 二位前缀和与差分\焚风现象.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n >> q >> s >> t;
	for (int i = 0;i <= n;i++) {cin >> a[i]; d[i] = a[i] - a[i - 1];}
	long long T = 0;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		{
			T += cal(d[i]);
		}
	while (q--)
	{
		int l,r,x;
		cin >> l >> r >> x;
		T -= cal(d[l]),d[l] += x,T += cal(d[l]);
		if (r + 1 <= n) T -= cal(d[r + 1]),d[r + 1] -= x,T += cal(d[r + 1]);
		cout << T << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\6 二位前缀和与差分\老式录像机.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	if (x.c != y.c) return x.c < y.c;
	return x.s < y.s;
}

--- J2-1\6 二位前缀和与差分\老式录像机.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,c,d,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> c >> d;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].s >> a[i].t >> a[i].c;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	Node b = a[1];
	for (int i = 2;i <= n + 1;i++)
	{
		if (b.c == a[i].c && a[i].s - b.t < d) b.t = a[i].t;
		else b.t += d,diff[b.s]++,diff[b.t]--,b = a[i];
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= 1e6;i++)
		s[i] = s[i - 1] + diff[i],ans = max(ans,s[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-1\7 初等数论\N的连续数拆分.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	long long n,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (long long i = 1;1ll * i * i <= 2 * n;i++)
		if (2 * n % i == 0 && (i % 2) != ((2 * n / i) % 2)) ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\7 初等数论\余数的记忆.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,k;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> k;
	if (k == 0) {cout << 1ll * n * n << endl; return 0;}
	for (int b = k + 1;b <= n;b++)
		ans += 1ll * (b - k) * (n / b) + 1ll * max(0,n % b - k + 1);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\7 初等数论\加权约数个数和.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += 1ll * (i + n / i * i) * (n / i) / 2;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\7 初等数论\因数个数.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(a,0,sizeof a); memset(b,0,sizeof b);
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		for (int j = 2;j * j <= a[i];j++)
			while (a[i] % j == 0) b[j]++,a[i] /= j;
		if (a[i] > 1) b[a[i]]++;
	}
	bool flag = true; long long ans = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 1e4;i++)
	{
		if (b[i] % 2 != 0) {flag = false; break;}
		ans *= b[i] / 2 + 1,ans %= mod;
	}
	if (!flag) {cout << -1 << endl; return ;}
	else cout << ans % mod << endl;
}

	cin >> t;
	while (t--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\7 初等数论\细胞分裂.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m1,m2,cur = 0,ans = 1e9;
	cin >> n >> m1 >> m2;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i];
	for (int i = 2;i * i <= m1;i++)
	{
		if (m1 % i == 0) p[++cur] = i;
		while (m1 % i == 0) k[cur]++,m1 /= i;
	}
	if (m1 > 1) p[++cur] = m1,k[cur] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int t = 0;
		for (int j = 1;j <= cur;j++)
		{
			if (s[i] % p[j] != 0) {t = 1e9; break;}
			int y = 0;
			while (s[i] % p[j] == 0) y++,s[i] /= p[j];
			t = max(t,(k[j] * m2 + y - 1) / y);
		}
		ans = min(ans,t);
	}
	cout << (ans == 1e9 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\7 初等数论\跳跃移动.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,X,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> X;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> x[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		ans = __gcd(ans,abs(X - x[i]));
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\8 组合计数\探索洞窟.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,k,p;
	long long ans = 1;
	cin >> n >> k >> p;
	for (int i = 0;i <= n;i++)
	{
		c[i][0] = 1;
		for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++)
			c[i][j] = (c[i - 1][j] + c[i - 1][j - 1]) % p;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
	{
		int x;
		cin >> x;
		ans *= c[n][x],ans %= p,n -= x;
	}
	cout << ans % p << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-1\8 组合计数\非回文串.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,cnt = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> c[i];
		box[c[i]]++;
	}
	for (int i = 0;i <= n;i++)
	{
		comb[i][0] = 1;
		for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++)
			comb[i][j] = (comb[i - 1][j] + comb[i - 1][j - 1]) % mod;
	}
	long long tot = 1,ans = 1; int l = n / 2;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) tot *= i,tot %= mod;
	for (int i = 'a';i <= 'z';i++)
	{
		cnt += box[i] % 2,ans *= comb[l][box[i] / 2],ans %= mod,l -= box[i] / 2;
		for (int j = 1;j <= box[i];j++) ans *= j,ans %= mod;
	}
	if (cnt > 1) ans = 0;
	cout << (tot - ans + mod) % mod << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\与怪物战斗.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,a,b,k;
	cin >> n >> a >> b >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> h[i];
		h[i] = h[i] % (a + b) != 0 ? h[i] % (a + b) : a + b;
	}
	sort(h + 1,h + n + 1);
	int sum = 0,ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (sum + (h[i] + a - 1) / a - 1 <= k) sum += (h[i] + a - 1) / a - 1,ans++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\均富卡.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	memset(s,0,sizeof s);
	int n,x;
	cin >> n >> x;
	
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,greater<int> () );
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i];
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--)
		if (s[i] / i >= x) {cout << i << endl; return ;}
	cout << 0 << endl;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\均富卡.cpp: main ---

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\奶牛杂技团.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {
	return max(-x.s,x.w - y.s) < max(y.w - x.s,-y.s);
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\奶牛杂技团.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].w >> a[i].s;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	int sum = 0,ans = -1e9;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		ans = max(ans,sum - a[i].s);
		sum += a[i].w;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\无法入睡.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) if (!a[i] && a[i - 1] && a[i + 1]) ans++,a[i + 1] = 0;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\真空扫地机器人.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {
	return x.s * y.h > y.s * x.h;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\真空扫地机器人.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> a[i].t;
		for (auto c : a[i].t)
			if (c == 's') a[i].s++;
			else a[i].h++;
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	long long ans = 0,cnts = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (auto c : a[i].t)
			if (c == 'h') ans += cnts;
			else cnts++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\货仓选址.cpp: main ---
int main(){

	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	int x = a[n / 2 + 1];
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += abs(x - a[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {

}

--- J2-2\1 贪心综合练习\货场选址.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	long long n,sumw = 0,sum = 0,x,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].a;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].w,sumw += s[i].w;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) 
	{
		sum += s[i].w;
		if (sum >= sumw / 2 + 1) {x = s[i].a; break;}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += 1ll * s[i].w * abs(x - s[i].a);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\图的遍历.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int st) {
	if (vis[u] != 0) return ;
	vis[u] = st;
    for (auto v : G[u]) 
        if (!vis[v]) dfs(v,st);
}

    }

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\寄包柜.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,q;
	cin >> n >> q;
	while (q--) {
		int op;
		cin >> op;
		switch (op) {
			case 1: {
				int i,j,k;
				cin >> i >> j >> k;
				if (k == 0) G[i][j] = 0;
				else {
					while (G[i].size() <= j) G[i].push_back(0);
					G[i][j] = k;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 2: {
				int i,j;
				cin >> i >> j;
				cout << G[i][j] << endl;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\封锁阳光大学.cpp: dfs ---
bool dfs(int u,int c) {
	color[u] = c;
	if (c == 1) sum1++;
	else sum2++;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (color[v] == 0) {
			if (!dfs(v,-c)) return false;
		}
		else if (color[v] == c) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\封锁阳光大学.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v);
		G[v].push_back(u);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (color[i] == 0) {
			sum1 = 0,sum2 = 0;
			if (!dfs(i,1)) {
				cout << "Impossible" << endl;
				return 0;
			}
			ans += min(sum1,sum2);
		}
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\排位赛.cpp: dfs ---
int dfs(int u) {
	vis[u] = true;
	int ret = 1;
	for (auto v : G[u])
		if (!vis[v]) ret += dfs(v);
	return ret;
}

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\排位赛.cpp: rdfs ---
int rdfs(int u) {
	rvis[u] = true;
	int ret = 1;
	for (auto v : rG[u])
		if (!rvis[v]) ret += rdfs(v);
	return ret;
}

--- J2-2\10 图上深搜\排位赛.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		G[a].push_back(b);
		rG[b].push_back(a);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
		memset(rvis,0,sizeof rvis);
		int cnt = dfs(i) + rdfs(i);
		if (cnt == n + 1) ans++;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\SwapPlace.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    q.push({1,n});
    vis[1][n] = true,dis[1][n] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front().a,v = q.front().b;
        q.pop();
        for (auto a : G[u])
            for (auto b : G[v]) {
                if (vis[a][b]) continue;
                if (c[a] == c[b]) continue;
                q.push({a,b});
                vis[a][b] = true,dis[a][b] = dis[u][v] + 1;
            }
    }
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\SwapPlace.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	memset(c,0,sizeof c); memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis); memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
	for (int i = 1;i <= 2000;i++) G[i].clear();
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v);
		G[v].push_back(u);
	}
    bfs();
    cout << (vis[n][1] ? dis[n][1] : -1) << endl;
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\SwapPlace.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\奶酪.cpp: check ---
bool check(Node x,Node y) {
    int dis = sqrt((x.x - y.x) * (x.x - y.x) + (x.y - y.y) * (x.y - y.y) + (x.z - y.z) * (x.z - y.z));
    if (dis <= 2 * r) return true;
    return false;
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\奶酪.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    queue<int> q;
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
    q.push(0);
    vis[0] = true;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (auto v : G[u]) {
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            q.push(v);
            vis[v] = true;
        }
    }
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\奶酪.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++) G[i].clear();
    cin >> n >> h >> r;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y >> a[i].z;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (a[i].z - r <= 0) G[0].push_back(i);
        if (a[i].z + r >= h) G[n + 1].push_back(i);
        for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
            if (check(a[i],a[j])) {
                G[i].push_back(j);
                G[j].push_back(i);
            }
    }
    for (auto u : G[0]) bfs();
    cout << (vis[n + 1] ? "Yes" : "No") << endl;
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\奶酪.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\寻找道路.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    q.push(s);
    vis[s] = true,dis[s] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (auto v : G[u]) {
            if (flag[v] || vis[v]) continue;
            q.push(v);
            vis[v] = true,dis[v] = dis[u] + 1;
        }
    }
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\寻找道路.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        G[x].push_back(y);
        rG[y].push_back(x);
    }
    cin >> s >> t;
    dfs(t);
    for (int u = 1;u <= n;u++)
        for (auto v : G[u]) flag[u] |= (!vis[v]);
    if (flag[s]) {cout << -1 << endl; return 0;}
    bfs();
    cout << (dis[t] > 1e9 ? -1 : dis[t]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

void bfs() {

	q.push(1);
	vis[1] = true;
	cout << 1 << ' ';
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front();
		q.pop();
		vis[u] = true;
		for (auto v : G[u])
			if (!vis[v]) {
				q.push(v);
				vis[v] = true;

			}
	}
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\营救.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    q.push(s);
    dis[s] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            int r = max(dis[u],w);
            if (r < dis[v]) {
                dis[v] = r;
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
}

--- J2-2\11 图上广搜\营救.cpp: main ---

    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v,w;
        cin >> u >> v >> w;
        G[u].push_back({v,w});
        G[v].push_back({u,w});
    }
    bfs();
    cout << dis[t] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\复杂的按钮.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top(); q.pop();
        topo[++cur] = u;
        for (auto v : G[u])
            if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
    }
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\复杂的按钮.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> m;
        for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++) {
            cin >> b;
            G[i].push_back(b);
            in[b]++;
        }
    }
    topo_sort();
    if (cur != n) cout << "no solution" << endl;
    else {
        cout << n << endl;
        for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << topo[i] << ' ';
        cout << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\奖金.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
        dp[i] = 100;
    }
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (auto v : G[u]) {
            if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
            dp[v] = max(dp[v],dp[u] + 1);
        }
    }
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\奖金.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[b].push_back(a);
        in[a]++;
    }
    topo_sort();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (in[i] != 0) {
            cout << "Poor Xed" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += dp[i];
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\挤奶顺序.cpp: topo_sort1 ---
bool topo_sort1() {
	cur = 0;
	queue<int> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		topo[++cur] = u;
		for (auto v : G[u])
			if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
	}
	return cur == n;
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\挤奶顺序.cpp: topo_sort2 ---
bool topo_sort2() {
	cur = 0;
	priority_queue<int,vector<int>,greater<int> > q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top(); q.pop();
		topo[++cur] = u;
		for (auto v : G[u])
			if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
	}
	return cur == n;
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\挤奶顺序.cpp: check1 ---
bool check1(int mid) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		G[i].clear();
		in[i] = 0;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= mid;i++) {
		int len = a[i].size();
		for (int j = 0;j < len - 1;j++) {
			G[a[i][j]].push_back(a[i][j + 1]);
			in[a[i][j + 1]]++;
		}
	}
	return topo_sort1();
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\挤奶顺序.cpp: check2 ---
bool check2(int mid) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		G[i].clear();
		in[i] = 0;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= mid;i++) {
		int len = a[i].size();
		for (int j = 0;j < len - 1;j++) {
			G[a[i][j]].push_back(a[i][j + 1]);
			in[a[i][j + 1]]++;
		}
	}
	return topo_sort2();
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\挤奶顺序.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int mi;
		cin >> mi;
		for (int j = 1;j <= mi;j++) {
			int tmp;
			cin >> tmp;
			a[i].push_back(tmp);
		}
	}
	int l = 0,r = m + 1,ans = 0;
	while (l <= r) {
		int mid = l + r >> 1;
		if (check1(mid)) l = mid + 1,ans = mid;
		else r = mid - 1;
	}
	check2(ans);
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) cout << topo[i] << ' ';
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\杂务.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) {
			q.push(i);
			dp[i] = len[i];
		}
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		topo[++cur] = u;
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
			dp[v] = max(dp[v],dp[u] + len[v]);
		}
	}
}

--- J2-2\12 拓扑排序1\杂务.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> v >> len[i];
		while (cin >> u) {
			if (u == 0) break;
			G[u].push_back(v);
			in[v]++;
		}
	}
	topo_sort();
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\Test for Job.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {

		if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			dp[v] = max(dp[v],dp[u] + a[v]);
			if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
		}
	}
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\巧克力牛奶.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {

		if (tmp[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		topo[++cur] = u;

			if (--tmp[v] == 0) q.push(v);

}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\巧克力牛奶.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp1[i] = dp2[i] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b);
        in[b]++,tmp[b]++;
    }
    topo_sort();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int u = topo[i];
        for (auto v : G[u]) dp1[v] += dp1[u];
    }
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
        int u = topo[i];
        for (auto v : G[u]) dp2[u] += dp2[v];
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (in[i] != 0 && dp1[i] + dp2[i] == n + 1) cout << i << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\旅行计划.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			dp[v] = max(dp[v],dp[u] + 1);
			if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
		}
	}
	return ;
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\旅行计划.cpp: main ---

		G[x].push_back(y);
		in[y]++;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) dp[i] = 1;
	topo_sort();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << dp[i] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\最大食物链计数-topo内dp.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (tmp[i] == 0) {
			dp[i] = 1;
			q.push(i);
		}
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			dp[v] += dp[u];
			dp[v] %= mod;
			if (--tmp[v] == 0) q.push(v);
		}
	}
	return ;
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\车站分级.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
	cur = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (tmp[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		topo[++cur] = u;
		for (int v = 1;v <= n;v++)
			if (G[u][v] && --tmp[v] == 0) q.push(v);
	}
}

--- J2-2\13 拓扑排序2\车站分级.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int s,cur = 0;
		cin >> s;
		memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
		for (int i = 1;i <= s;i++) {
			cin >> a[i];
			vis[a[i]] = true;
		}
		for (int i = a[1];i <= a[s];i++)
			if (vis[i] == false) b[++cur] = i;
		for (int i = 1;i <= s;i++)
			for (int j = 1;j <= cur;j++) G[a[i]][b[j]] = 1;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			if (G[i][j]) in[j]++,tmp[j]++,out[i]++;
	topo_sort();
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) dp[i] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int u = topo[i];
		for (int v = 1;v <= n;v++)
			if (G[u][v]) dp[v] = max(dp[v],dp[u] + 1);
		if (out[u] == 0) ans = max(ans,dp[u]);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\14 阶段练习\丰收节.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
	vis[u] = true;
	for (auto v : rG[u])
		if (!vis[v]) dfs(v);
}

--- J2-2\14 阶段练习\丰收节.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int k,n,m,ans = 0;
	cin >> k >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		rG[b].push_back(a);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
		dfs(i);
		bool flag = true;
		for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++) flag &= vis[a[j]];
		if (flag) ans++;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\14 阶段练习\距离分级.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs(int s) {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
	q.push(s);
	vis[s] = true, dis[s] = 0;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		if (tmp != dis[u]) {
			sort(sl + 1,sl + cur + 1);
			for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) cout << sl[i] << ' ';
			cout << endl;
			memset(sl,0,sizeof sl);
			tmp = dis[u],cur = 0;
		}
		sl[++cur] = u;
		for (auto v : G[u])
			if (!vis[v]) {
				q.push(v);
				vis[v] = true, dis[v] = dis[u] + 1;
			}
	}
}

--- J2-2\14 阶段练习\距离分级.cpp: main ---
int main() {

	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int s,t;
		cin >> s >> t;
		G[s].push_back(t);
	}
	bfs(x);
    sort(sl + 1,sl + cur + 1);
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) cout << sl[i] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    memset(sl,0,sizeof sl);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (!vis[i]) cout << i << ' ';
    cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

	}

--- J2-2\14 阶段练习\递增路径.cpp: main ---
int main() {

	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {

		cin >> u >> v;

			G[u].push_back(v);
			in[v]++;
		}
		else {
			G[v].push_back(u);
			in[u]++;
		}
	}
    memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[1] = 1;
	topo_sort();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;

--- J2-2\2 优先队列\x轴最大距离.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	char op;
	cin >> op;
	if (op == '+') {
		int x; cin >> x;
		vis[++cur] = true,pq1.push({x,cur}), pq2.push({x,cur});
	}
	else if (op == '-') {
		int n; cin >> n;
		vis[n] = false;
	}
	else {
		int x; cin >> x;
		while (!vis[pq1.top().id]) pq1.pop();
		while (!vis[pq2.top().id]) pq2.pop();
		int ans = max(abs(x - pq1.top().x),abs(x - pq2.top().x));
		cout << ans << endl;
	}
}

	cin >> q;
	while (q--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\2 优先队列\体育馆售票.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int m,n;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> m >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		int x; cin >> x;
		pq.push(x);
	}
	while (n--)
	{
		int tmp = pq.top();
		pq.pop(); 
		ans += tmp;
		pq.push(tmp - 1);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\2 优先队列\动态中位数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,mid;
    cin >> n >> mid;
    cout << mid << endl;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        int a;
        cin >> a;
        if (i % 2 == 0) {
            if (a < mid) L.push(a),R.push(mid);
            else R.push(a),L.push(mid);
        }
        else {
            if (a < L.top()) {mid = L.top(); L.pop(); L.push(a);}
            else if (a > R.top()) {mid = R.top(); R.pop(); R.push(a);}
            else mid = a;
            cout << mid << endl;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\2 优先队列\合并果子.cpp: main ---
int main()
{

	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int a;
		cin >> a;
		pq.push(a);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int tmp = 0;
		tmp += pq.top(); pq.pop();
		tmp += pq.top(); pq.pop();
		pq.push(tmp);
		ans += tmp;
	}

}

--- J2-2\2 优先队列\实时晋级.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	string op;
	cin >> op;
	if (op == "submit") {
		Node x;
		cin >> x.id >> x.score;
		pq.push(x);
	}
	else {
		int x;
		cin >> x;
		if (pq.top().score < x) {cout << "none" << endl; return ;}
		while (pq.size() && pq.top().score >= x) {
			cout << pq.top().id << ' ';
			pq.pop();
		}
		cout << endl;
	}
}

	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\2 优先队列\实时评分.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	string op;
	cin >> op;
	if (op == "score")
	{
		int x;
		cin >> x;
		pq.push(x);
		sum += x;
	}
	if (op == "delete")
	{
		sum -= pq.top();
		pq.pop();
	}
	if (op == "display")
		printf("%.3lf\n",1.0 * sum / pq.size());
}

	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\4 List容器\书籍摆放.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	while (m--) {
		string op;
		cin >> op;
		if (op == "add") {
			int x,a,b; bool d;
			cin >> x >> a >> b >> d;
			if (!d) while (b--) ls[x].push_front(a);
			else while (b--) ls[x].push_back(a);
		}
		else if (op == "remove") {
			int x,a;
			cin >> x >> a;
			ls[x].remove(a);
		}
		else if (op == "move") {
			int x,y; bool d;
			cin >> x >> y >> d;
			if (!d) ls[y].splice(ls[y].begin(),ls[x]);
			else ls[y].splice(ls[y].end(),ls[x]);
		}
		else if (op == "reverse") {
			int x;
			cin >> x;
			ls[x].reverse();
		}
		else if (op == "query") {
			int x;
			cin >> x;
			for (auto t : ls[x]) cout << t << ' ';
			cout << endl;
		}
	}
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\4 List容器\悲剧的键盘.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	auto pos = ls.begin();
	for (int i = 0;i < s.size();i++) {
		if (s[i] == '[') pos = ls.begin();
		else if (s[i] == ']') pos = ls.end();
		else ls.insert(pos,s[i]);
	}
	for (auto t : ls) cout << t;
	cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\4 List容器\悲剧的键盘II.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	auto pos = ls.begin();
	for (int i = 0;i < s.size();i++) {
		if (s[i] == '[') pos = ls.begin();
		else if (s[i] == ']') pos = ls.end();
		else if (s[i] == '#' && pos != ls.begin()) pos = ls.erase(--pos);
		else if (s[i] != '#') ls.insert(pos,s[i]);
	}
	for (auto t : ls) cout << t;
	cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\4 List容器\移动盒子.cpp: Left ---
void Left(int x,int y) {
	ls.insert(a[y],x);
	ls.erase(a[x]);
	a[x] = a[y], a[x]--;
	return ;
}

--- J2-2\4 List容器\移动盒子.cpp: Right ---
void Right(int x,int y) {
	auto pos = a[y]; pos++;
	ls.insert(pos,x);
	ls.erase(a[x]);
	a[x] = a[y], a[x]++;
	return ;
}

--- J2-2\4 List容器\移动盒子.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	long long cnt = 0,ans = 0;
	bool flag = false;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		ls.push_back(i);
		a[i] = ls.end();
		a[i]--;
	}
	while (m--) {
		int op;
		cin >> op;
		switch (op) {
			case 1: {
				int x,y;
				cin >> x >> y;
				if (flag) Right(x,y);
				else Left(x,y);
				continue;
			}
			case 2: {
				int x,y;
				cin >> x >> y;
				if (!flag) Right(x,y);
				else Left(x,y);
				continue;
			}
			case 3: {
				int x,y;
				cin >> x >> y;
				swap(a[x],a[y]);
				*a[x] = x, *a[y] = y;
				continue;
			}
			case 4: {
				flag = !flag;
				continue;
			}
		}
	}
	if (flag) ls.reverse();
	for (auto t : ls) {
		cnt++;
		if (cnt % 2) ans += t;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\5 表达式递归\Kitty猫基因密码.cpp: check ---
int check(string s) {
	bool flaga = true,flagb = true;
	for (auto c : s) flaga &= (c == '0'),flagb &= (c == '1');
	return (flaga ? 1 : (flagb ? 2 : 0));
}

--- J2-2\5 表达式递归\logo语言.cpp: solve ---
int solve() {
	char c;
	int ans = 0,x,t;
	while (cin >> c) {
		if (c == 'D') {cin >> x; ans += x;}
		else if (c == 'K') {cin >> x; ans -= x;}
		else if (c == 'T') {
			cin >> t >> c;
			ans += t * solve();
		}
		else if (c == ']') return ans;
	}
	return ans;
}

	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\5 表达式递归\小数表达式.cpp: solve ---
double solve() {
	char c; int t = 0;
	double sum = 0,mul = 1; bool f = true;
	while (cin >> c) {
		if (c >= '0' && t <= '9') t = t * 10 + (c - '0');
		else {
			if (f) mul *= t;
			else mul /= t;
			t = 0;
			if (c == '*') f = 1;
			else if (c == '/') f = 0;
			else if (c == '+') sum += mul,mul = 1,f = 1;
			else if (c == '-') sum += mul,mul = -1,f = 1;
		}
	}
	if (f) mul *= t;
	else mul /= t;
	sum += mul;
	return sum;
}

--- J2-2\5 表达式递归\小数表达式.cpp: main ---

	printf("%.3lf",solve());
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\【模板】归并排序.cpp: merge_sort ---
void merge_sort(int a[],int l,int r) {
    if (l >= r) return ;
    int mid = l + r >> 1;
    merge_sort(a,l,mid);
    merge_sort(a,mid + 1,r);
    int i = l,j = mid + 1,cur = l;
    while (i <= mid && j <= r) {
        if (a[i] < a[j]) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
        else tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
    }
    while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
    while (j <= r) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\【模板】归并排序.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) cin >> a[i];
    merge_sort(a,1,n);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) cout << a[i] << " ";
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\【模板】快速排序.cpp: quick_sort ---
void quick_sort(int a[],int l,int r) {
    if (l >= r) return ;
    int x = a[(l + r) >> 1];
    int i = l,j = r;
    while (i <= j) {
        while (a[i] < x) i++;
        while (a[j] > x) j--;
        if (i <= j) swap(a[i++],a[j--]);
    }
    quick_sort(a,l,j);
    quick_sort(a,i,r);
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\【模板】快速排序.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) cin >> a[i];
    quick_sort(a,1,n);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) cout << a[i] << " ";
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\求第k小的数.cpp: kth ---
int kth(int a[],int l,int r,int k) {
    int x = a[(l + r) >> 1];
    int i = l,j = r;
    while (i <= j) {
        while (a[i] < x) i++;
        while (a[j] > x) j--;
        if (i <= j) swap(a[i++],a[j--]);
    }
    if (k <= j - l + 1) return kth(a,l,j,k);
    else if (k >= i - l + 1) return kth(a,i,r,k - (i - l));
    else return x;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\求第k小的数.cpp: main ---
int main() {

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\火柴排队.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {
	return x.num < y.num;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\火柴排队.cpp: merge ---
int merge(int a[],int l,int r) {
	if (l >= r) return 0;
	int mid = l + r >> 1;
	long long lcnt = merge(a,l,mid) % mod,rcnt = merge(a,mid + 1,r) % mod,cnt = 0,i = l,j = mid + 1,cur = l;
	while (i <= mid && j <= r) {
		if (a[i] <= a[j]) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = a[j++],cnt = (cnt + mid - i + 1) % mod;
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
    while (j <= r) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
	return (cnt + lcnt + rcnt) % mod;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\火柴排队.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> a[i].num;a[i].id = i;}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> b[i].num;b[i].id = i;}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp); sort(b + 1,b + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) c[a[i].id] = b[i].id;
	cout << merge(c,1,n) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\6 分治法基础\逆序对.cpp: merge ---
long long merge(int a[],int l,int r) {
	if (l >= r) return 0;
    int mid = l + r >> 1;
    long long lcnt = merge(a,l,mid),rcnt = merge(a,mid + 1,r),cnt = 0,i = l,j = mid + 1,cur = l;
    while (i <= mid && j <= r) {
        if (a[i] <= a[j]) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
        else tmp[cur++] = a[j++],cnt += mid - i + 1;
    }
    while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
    while (j <= r) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
    return cnt + lcnt + rcnt;
}

	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\7 树与二叉树1\二叉树深度.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int dep) {
	ans = max(ans,dep);
	if (l[u]) dfs(l[u],dep + 1);
	if (r[u]) dfs(r[u],dep + 1);
	return ;
}

		p[l[i]] = p[r[i]] = i;

	dfs(rt,1);

--- J2-2\7 树与二叉树1\层次遍历.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(1);
	a[1].dep = 1;
	int cnt = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		if (a[u].dep != cnt) {cnt++; cout << endl;}
		cout << u << ' ';
		if (a[u].lch) {q.push(a[u].lch); a[a[u].lch].dep = a[u].dep + 1;}
		if (a[u].rch) {q.push(a[u].rch); a[a[u].rch].dep = a[u].dep + 1;}
	}
}

		char lc,rc;
		cin >> node[i] >> lc >> rc;
		l[node[i]] = lc;
		r[node[i]] = rc;
		p[lc] = p[rc] = node[i];
	}
	char rt;
	for (char i = 1;i <= n;i++) if (!p[node[i]]) rt = node[i];
	first_search(rt);
	cout << endl;

}

--- J2-2\7 树与二叉树1\求先序排列.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int l,int r,int t) { // t表示根在后序的编号
    if (l > r) return ;
    char root = b[t];
    int pos = l;
    for (int i = l; i <= r; i++)
        if (m[i] == root)
            pos = i;
    cout << root;
    dfs(l,pos - 1,t - (r - pos) - 1);
    dfs(pos + 1,r,t - 1);
}

    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\二叉查找树.cpp: insert ---
void insert(int id,int root) {
	if (a[id].p > a[root].p) {
		if (a[root].rch == 0) a[root].rch = id;
		else insert(id,a[root].rch);
	}
	else {
		if (a[root].lch == 0) a[root].lch = id;
		else insert(id,a[root].lch);
	}
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\二叉树输出.cpp: build ---
char build(int l,int r,int t) {
	if (l > r) return 0;
	char root = s1[t];
	int pos = l;
	for (int i = l;i <= r;i++)
		if (s2[i] == root) pos = i;
	char L = build(l,pos - 1,t + 1),R = build(pos + 1,r,t + pos - l + 1);
	if (L == 0 && R == 0) a[root] = 1;
	else a[root] = a[L] + a[R];
	return root;
}

}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\二叉树问题.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int root,int depth) {
	ans1 = max(ans1,depth);
	dep[root] = depth;
	bre[depth]++;
	if (l[root]) dfs(l[root],depth + 1);
	if (r[root]) dfs(r[root],depth + 1);
	return ;
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\二叉树问题.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,u,v,rt;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> l[i] >> r[i];
		p[l[i]] = p[r[i]] = i;
	}
	cin >> u >> v;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) if (!p[i]) rt = i;
	dfs(rt,1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= ans1;i++) ans2 = max(ans2,bre[i]);
	while (u != v) {
		if (dep[u] > dep[v]) u = p[u];
		else v = p[v];
		ans3++;
	}
	cout << ans1 << endl << ans2 << endl << ans3 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\分裂二叉树.cpp: last_search ---
void last_search(int u) {
    if (l[u]) last_search(l[u]);
    if (r[u]) last_search(r[u]);
    s[u] = s[l[u]] + s[r[u]] + a[u];
    return ;
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\分裂二叉树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> l[i] >> r[i];
    last_search(1);
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,1ll * s[i] * (s[1] - s[i]));
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\医院设置.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int root,int depth) {
    sum += 1ll * depth * num[root];
    vis[root] = 1;
    if (p[root] && !vis[p[root]]) dfs(p[root],depth + 1);
    if (l[root] && !vis[l[root]]) dfs(l[root],depth + 1);
    if (r[root] && !vis[r[root]]) dfs(r[root],depth + 1);
}

--- J2-2\8 树与二叉树2\医院设置.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> num[i] >> l[i] >> r[i];
        p[l[i]] = p[r[i]] = i;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
        sum = 0;
        dfs(i,0);
        ans = min(sum,ans);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- J2-2\9 邻接矩阵与欧拉回路\景区游玩.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int dis) {
	ans = max(ans,dis);
	for (int v = 1;v <= n;v++) {
		if (G[u][v] == -1) continue;
		if (vis[v]) continue;
		vis[v] = true;
		dfs(v,dis + G[u][v]);
		vis[v] = false;
	}
}

--- J2-2\9 邻接矩阵与欧拉回路\景区游玩.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	memset(G,-1,sizeof G);
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b,c;
		cin >> a >> b >> c;
		G[a][b] = G[b][a] = c;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		vis[i] = true;
		dfs(i,0);
		vis[i] = false;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		deg[a]++,deg[b]++;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (deg[i] % 2 == 1) cnt++;
	cout << ((cnt == 0) ? 1 : cnt / 2) << endl;

}

--- J2-2\9 邻接矩阵与欧拉回路\骑马修栅栏.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
	for (int v = 1;v <= 500;v++)
		if (G[u][v]) {
			G[u][v]--,G[v][u]--;
			dfs(v);
		}
	ans[++cur] = u;
}

--- J2-2\9 邻接矩阵与欧拉回路\骑马修栅栏.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int m,cnt = 0;
	cin >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u][v]++,G[v][u]++;
		deg[u]++,deg[v]++;
	}
	for (int u = 1;u <= 500;u++)
		if (deg[u] % 2) {
			dfs(u);
			cnt++;
			break;
		}
	if (cnt == 0)
		for (int u = 1;u <= 500;u++)
			if (deg[u] != 0) {
				dfs(u);
				break;
			}
	for (int i = cur;i >= 1;i--) cout << ans[i] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- NOICA\10\spring\spring-.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- NOICA\10\spring\spring-.cpp: merge ---
void merge(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- NOICA\10\spring\spring-.cpp: check ---
bool check() {
    double num;
    if (a[2].x - a[1].x == 0) num = 1e9;
    else num = 1.0 * (a[2].y - a[1].y) / (a[2].x - a[1].x);
    for (int i = 3;i <= n;i++) {
        double tmp;
        if (a[i].x - a[i - 1].x == 0) tmp = 1e9;
        else tmp = 1.0 * (a[i].y - a[i - 1].y) / (a[i].x - a[i - 1].x);
        if (abs(tmp - num) >= eps) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

--- NOICA\10\spring\spring.cpp: cal ---
bool cal(Point a,Point b,Point c)
{return (b.x - a.x) * (c.y - a.y) == (b.y - a.y) * (c.x - a.x);}

--- NOICA\10\toy\toy.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> b >> a >> n >> s;
    s = ' ' + s;
    int l1 = 1,r1 = a;
    int l2 = 1,r2 = b;
    ql = 1, qr = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= b;i++) q[++qr] = i;
    for (int i = b + 1;i <= a;i++) {
        if (s[i] == '0') continue;
        q[++qr] = i, ql++;
    }
    long long ans = 0;
    while (true) {
        if (l2 == l1)
            ans += b, l2 = q[ql], r2 = q[qr];
        l1++, r1++;
        if (s[r1] != '0') q[++qr] = r1, ql++;
        ++ans;
        if (!(l1 <= l2 && r2 <= r1)) {
            cout << "IMPOSSIBLE" << endl;
            return ;
        }
        if (r1 == n) break;
    }
    if (r2 != n) ans += b;
    cout << ans << endl;
    return ;
}

    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\11\parrot\parrot.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    memset(num,0,sizeof num);
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    int cnt = 1,diff = 0,maxnum = 0; // diff 记录有多少物品被排挤
    for (int i = 1;i <= n * m;i++) {
        cin >> c[i];
        if (c[i] == c[i - 1]) cnt++;
        else 
            diff += cnt / 2, num[c[i - 1]] += cnt / 2, maxnum = max(maxnum,num[c[i - 1]]), cnt = 1; // 连续段则至少要有 cnt / 2 个被驱逐
    }
    diff += cnt / 2, num[c[n * m]] += cnt / 2, maxnum = max(maxnum,num[c[n * m]]), cnt = 1; // 最后一组
    int ans = n * m - diff;
    if (maxnum >= diff - maxnum) // 如果有一个被驱逐数量比总量大，则其会排挤其他正确的
        ans -= maxnum - (diff - maxnum);
    cout << ans << endl;
}

--- NOICA\12\assassin\assassin.cpp: calc ---
int calc(int x) {
    if (x <= 0) return 0;
    if (x <= cnt1 && a0[x] < a1[1])
        return s[x - 1] + a1[1];
    return s[x];
}

--- NOICA\12\forest\forest.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    memset(deg,0,sizeof deg);
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        cin >> u[i] >> v[i];
        deg[u[i]]++, deg[v[i]]++;
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++)
        if (deg[u[i]] > 1 && deg[v[i]] > 1) ans++;
    if (ans & 1) cout << "wizard" << endl;
    else cout << "villager" << endl;
    return ;
}

}

--- NOICA\12\trace\trace.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra(int s,vector<Edge> G[],long long dis[],long long cnt[]) {
    fill(dis,dis + n + 1,inf);
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cnt[i] = 0;
    priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[s] = 0, cnt[s] = 1;
    q.push({s,dis[s]});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = 1;
        for (auto p : G[u]) {
            int v = p.v,w = p.w;
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + w, cnt[v] = cnt[u];
                q.push({v,dis[v]});
            }
            else if (dis[v] == dis[u] + w) cnt[v] = (cnt[v] + cnt[u]) % mod;
        }
    }
}

--- NOICA\13\B\B.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x,int y,int n) {
    int sum = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        sum += x / a[i];
    return sum <= y;
}

--- NOICA\13\B\B.cpp: calc ---
long long calc(int x) {
    int m = cur,ret = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < (1 << m); i++) {
        long long lcm = 1;
        bool flag = true;
        for (int j = 0;j < m;j++)
            if ((i >> j) & 1) {
                int tmp = (int)lcm / __gcd(lcm,b[j + 1]) * b[j + 1];
                if (tmp > x) {
                    flag = false;
                    break;
                }
                lcm = tmp;
            }
        if (flag)
            if (__builtin_popcount(i) & 1) ret += x / lcm;
            else ret -= x / lcm;
    }
    return ret;
}

--- NOICA\13\B\B.cpp: solve ---
long long solve(int x,int n) {
    int l = 0,r = min(x * b[1],(int)1e18),ans = 0;
    while (l <= r) {
        int mid = l + r >> 1;
        if (check(mid,x,n)) l = mid + 1, ans = mid;
        else r = mid - 1;
    }
    return calc(ans);
}

--- NOICA\13\permutation\permutation.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("permutation.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("permutation.out","w",stdout);
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0), cout.tie(0);
    int n,s = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        if (a[i] == n) s = i;
    }
    int cur = n,ans = 1;
    for (int i = s;i >= 1;i--)
        if (a[i] == cur - 1) cur--, ans++;
    cout << n - ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\13\traverse\traverse-.cpp: init ---
void init(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        init(v,u);
    }
}

--- NOICA\13\traverse\traverse-.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i])
            u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- NOICA\13\traverse\traverse-.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("traverse.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("traverse.out","w",stdout);
    int n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        id[i] = i;
    }
    sort(id + 1,id + n + 1,[](int x,int y) {return a[x] < a[y];});
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v), G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    init(1,0);
    int r = 0,last = 1,ans = 2 * (n - 1) + a[1];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int now = id[i];
        if (vis[now]) continue;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\13\traverse\traverse.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
    }
    sort(G[u].begin(),G[u].end(),[](int u,int v) {return max(dp[u],2LL * siz[u] + dp[v]) < max(dp[v],2LL * siz[v] + dp[u]);});
    int maxn = a[u],cur = 0;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        maxn = max(maxn,1LL + cur + dp[v]);
        cur += 2 * siz[v];
    }
    dp[u] = maxn;
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\13\variance\variance.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("variance.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("variance.out","w",stdout);
    int n,l,r;
    scanf("%d%d%d",&n,&l,&r);
    int half = n / 2;
    double ave = 1.00 * (1.00 * (l * half) + 1.00 * (r * (n - half))) / n;
    double ans = 1.00 * (1.00 * half * (1.00 * ave - l) * (1.00 * ave - l) + 1.00 * (n - half) * (1.00 * r - ave) * (1.00 * r - ave)) / n;
    printf("%.2lf",ans);
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\14\number\number.cpp: qpow ---
ll qpow(ll a,ll b,ll p) {
    ll ans = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ans = ans * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return ans;
}

--- NOICA\14\number\number.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    ll n,p;
    cin >> n >> p;
    cout << (qpow(3,n,p) - qpow(2,n,p) + p) % p << ' ';
    cout << n % p * (qpow(2,2 * n - 1,p) - qpow(3,n - 1,p) + p) % p << endl;
    return ;
}

    freopen("number.out","w",stdout);

    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\14\path\path.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int u) {
    dfn[u] = low[u] = ++idx;
    stk[++top] = u,flag[u] = true;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (!dfn[v]) {
            tarjan(v);
            low[u] = min(low[u],low[v]);
        }
        else if (flag[v]) low[u] = min(low[u],dfn[v]);
    }
    if (low[u] == dfn[u]) {
        cnt++;
        int v;
        do {
            v = stk[top--],flag[v] = false;
            scc[v] = cnt;
            mn[cnt] = min(mn[cnt],w[v]);
            mx[cnt] = max(mx[cnt],w[v]);
        } while (v != u);
    }
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\14\refrain\refrain.cpp: lowbit ---
int lowbit(int x) {return x & -x;}

--- NOICA\14\refrain\refrain.cpp: add ---
void add(int x,int v,int op)
{while (x <= lim) tree[op][x] = max(tree[op][x],v),x += lowbit(x);}

--- NOICA\14\refrain\refrain.cpp: query ---
int query(int x,int op) {
    int ret = -1e18;
    while (x) ret = max(ret,tree[op][x]),x -= lowbit(x);
    return ret;
}

--- NOICA\14\refrain\refrain.cpp: clear ---
void clear(int x,int op)
{while (x <= lim) tree[op][x] = -1e18,x += lowbit(x);}

--- NOICA\14\refrain\refrain.cpp: cdq ---
void cdq(int l,int r) {
    if (l == r) return ;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    cdq(l, mid);
    for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) tmp[i] = i;
    sort(tmp + l,tmp + mid + 1,[](int x,int y) {return p[x] < p[y];});
    sort(tmp + mid + 1,tmp + r + 1,[](int x,int y) {return p[x] < p[y];});
    int i = l, j = mid + 1;
    while (j <= r) {
        while (i <= mid && p[tmp[i]] < p[tmp[j]]) {
            add(q[tmp[i]] + n + 1,dp[tmp[i]],tmp[i] & 1);
            i++;
        }
        int res = query(q[tmp[j]] + n,(tmp[j] & 1) ^ 1);
        dp[tmp[j]] = max(dp[tmp[j]],res + 1);
        j++;
    }
    for (int k = l;k < i;k++) clear(q[tmp[k]] + n + 1,tmp[k] & 1);
    cdq(mid + 1,r);
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\14\refrain\refrain.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i],cpy[i] = a[i];
    sort(cpy + 1,cpy + n + 1);
    int m = cpy[n / 2 + 1];
    int cnt = 0,val = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (a[i] < m) val--;
        else if (a[i] == m) cnt++;
        else val++;
        p[i] = cnt + val, q[i] = cnt - val;
    }
    p[0] = 0, q[0] = 0;
    lim = 2 * n + 5;
    for (int i = 0;i <= lim;i++) tree[0][i] = tree[1][i] = -1e18;
    for (int i = 0;i <= n;i++) dp[i] = -1e18;
    dp[0] = 0;
    cdq(0,n);
    cout << dp[n] << endl;
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\15\fruit\fruit.cpp: qpow ---
int qpow(int a,int b,int p) {
    int res = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) res = res * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

--- NOICA\15\fruit\fruit.cpp: exgcd ---
void exgcd(int a,int b,int &x,int &y) {
    if (b == 0) {
        x = 1, y = 0;
        return ;
    }
    exgcd(b,a % b,x,y);
    int z = x; x = y, y = z - y * (a / b);
}

--- NOICA\15\fruit\fruit.cpp: inv ---
int inv(int a,int p) {
    int x,y;
    exgcd(a,p,x,y);
    return (x % p + p) % p;
}

--- NOICA\15\fruit\fruit.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    f2[0] = f5[0] = 0;
    fr[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i < N;i++) {
        int tmp = i,c2 = 0,c5 = 0;
        while (!(tmp & 1)) c2++, tmp /= 2;
        while (tmp % 5 == 0) c5++, tmp /= 5;
        f2[i] = f2[i - 1] + c2, f5[i] = f5[i - 1] + c5, fr[i] = fr[i - 1] * tmp % mod;
    }
    for (int i = 0;i < N;i++) ifr[i] = inv(fr[i],mod);
}

--- NOICA\15\fruit\fruit.cpp: calc ---
int calc(int n,int r) {
    if (n < 0 || r < 0 || r > n) return 0;
    int e2 = 0,e5 = 0,res = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++) {
        int num = n - i + 1, den = i;
        while (!(num & 1)) e2++, num /= 2;
        while (num % 5 == 0) e5++, num /= 5;
        while (!(den & 1)) e2--, den /= 2;
        while (den % 5 == 0) e5--, den /= 5;
        res = res * (num % mod) % mod, res = res * inv(den % mod,mod) % mod;
    }
    res = res * qpow(2,e2,mod) % mod, res = res * qpow(5,e5,mod) % mod;
    return res;
}

--- NOICA\15\fruit\fruit.cpp: C ---
int C(int n,int r) {
    if (n < 0 || r < 0 || r > n) return 0;
    if (n < N) {
        int e2 = f2[n] - f2[r] - f2[n - r];
        int e5 = f5[n] - f5[r] - f5[n - r];
        int res = qpow(2,e2,mod) * qpow(5,e5,mod) % mod;
        res = res * fr[n] % mod * ifr[r] % mod * ifr[n - r] % mod;
        return res;
    }
    else return calc(n,r);
}

--- NOICA\15\land\land.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    int mx = 0,mn = 0;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        mx = max(mx,ad[v]), mn = min(mn,mi[v]);
    }
    int k = (h - a[u]) - (mx + mn);
    if (k > 0) ad[u] = mx + k, mi[u] = mn;
    else ad[u] = mx, mi[u] = mn + k;
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\4\bag\bag.cpp: init ---
void init() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        start[i] = order[i] = i + 1;
    sort(start, start + n, [](int x, int y) {return a[x].in != a[y].in ? a[x].in < a[y].in : a[x].out > a[y].out;});
    sort(order, order + n, [](int x, int y) {return a[x].out - a[x].in < a[y].out - a[y].in;});
}

--- NOICA\4\card\card.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid,int x,int y,int z) {
    if (mid == 0) return true;
    return 2 * mid + 1 <= x + y + z && mid - x <= mid / 2 && mid - y <= mid / 2 && mid - z <= mid / 2 && max(0ll,mid - x) + max(0ll,mid - y) + max(0ll,mid - z) < mid;
    // 总共牌数是 2 * mid + 1；
    // 最优策略的保留牌肯定不会连续两个花色一样，设其中保留牌数量 k，则 k < mid / 2 且 mid - k <= 牌数，变一下就是 mid - k <= mid / 2 了
    // 最后要保证三个颜色一起安排的下
}

--- NOICA\4\card\card.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int x,y,z;
    cin >> x >> y >> z;
    int l = 0,r = (x + y + z - 1) / 2;
    while (l < r) {
        int mid = (l + r + 1) >> 1;
        if (check(mid,x,y,z)) l = mid;
        else r = mid - 1;
    }
    cout << l << endl;
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\5\graph\graph-50.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int last,int len) {
    ans = max(ans,len);
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (w <= last) continue;
        dfs(v,w,len + 1);
    }
}

    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int s,t,u;
        cin >> s >> t >> u;
        G[s].push_back({t,u});

    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dfs(i,0,0);
    cout << ans << endl;

}

--- NOICA\5\graph\graph.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("graph.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("graph.out","w",stdout);
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int s,t,u;
        cin >> s >> t >> u;
        edge[++cur] = {s,t,u};
    }
    sort(edge + 1,edge + cur + 1,[](Edge x,Edge y) {return x.w < y.w;});
    int j = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i = j + 1) {
        j = i;
        while (edge[j + 1].w == edge[i].w) ++j;
        for (int k = i;k <= j;k++) cpy[edge[k].u] = dp[edge[k].u], cpy[edge[k].v] = dp[edge[k].v];
        for (int k = i;k <= j;k++) dp[edge[k].v] = max(dp[edge[k].v],cpy[edge[k].u] + 1);
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\5\one\one.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid) {
    // 排序结束后，想要完全覆盖，就要步步都踩在点子上
    int cur = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cur += mid;
        // 检验: 必须覆盖到这个点
        if (cur < a[i]) return false; // 太晚
        if (cur > a[i] + mid - 1) cur = a[i] + mid - 1; // 过早，必须退回
    }
    if (cur < n) return false; // 到最后也无法覆盖
    return true;
}

--- NOICA\5\traffic\traffic.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    memset(s,0,sizeof s);
    memset(b,0,sizeof b);
    memset(deg,0,sizeof deg);
    memset(a,0,sizeof a);
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        s[a[i]]++;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        if (a[u] != a[v]) deg[u]++, deg[v]++;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (deg[i] != 0) b[a[i]]++;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        if (deg[i] > 0) cout << s[a[i]] - 1 + 2 * s[!a[i]] - deg[i] << ' ';
        else cout << s[a[i]] - 1 + 3 * s[!a[i]] - b[!a[i]] << ' ';
    }
    cout << endl;
    return ;
}

    freopen("traffic.out","w",stdout);
    int c,T;
    cin >> c >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\6\road\road.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra(int s,int dis[],bool vis[],vector<Edge> G[]) {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dis[i] = 1e9, vis[i] = 0;
    priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[s] = 0;
    q.push({s,dis[s]});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = 1;
        for (auto p : G[u]) {
            int v = p.v,w = p.w;
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
                q.push({v,dis[v]});
            }
        }
    }
}

--- NOICA\6\universe\universe.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    freopen("universe.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("universe.out","w",stdout);
    int n,k,p;
    cin >> n >> k >> p;
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        cin >> x[i] >> y[i] >> m[i];
        for (int X = 1;X <= n;X++)
            for (int Y = 1;Y <= n;Y++)
                a[X][Y] += max(0,m[i] - max(abs(X - x[i]),abs(Y - y[i])));
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
            ans += (a[i][j] <= p);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\7\permutation.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l, tr[id].r = r, tr[id].mn = INF;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].mn = a[l];
        return;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].mn = min(tr[lid].mn,tr[rid].mn);
}

--- NOICA\7\permutation.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int pos,int val) {
    if (tr[id].l == tr[id].r) {
        tr[id].mn = val;
        return;
    }
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (pos <= mid) modify(lid,pos,val);
    else modify(rid,pos,val);
    tr[id].mn = min(tr[lid].mn,tr[rid].mn);
}

--- NOICA\7\permutation.cpp: query ---
int query(int id, int l, int r) {
    if (tr[id].l > r || tr[id].r < l) return INF;
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) return tr[id].mn;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    return min(query(lid,l,r),query(rid,l,r));
}

--- NOICA\7\permutation.cpp: get ---
int get(int s) {
    if (m == n) return INF;
    int ret = INF;
    if (s + m <= n + 1) {
        if (s > 1) ret = min(ret,query(1,1,s - 1));
        if (s + m <= n) ret = min(ret,query(1,s + m,n));
        return ret;
    }
    int left = (s + m - 1) % n + 1;
    int right = (s - 2 + n) % n + 1;
    if (left <= right) return query(1,left,right);
    return INF;
}

--- NOICA\7\permutation\permutation.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {

        tr[id].mn = a[l];
        return;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    tr[id].mn = min(tr[lid].mn,tr[rid].mn);
}

        tr[id].mn = val;
        return;
    }
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (pos <= mid) modify(lid,pos,val);
    else modify(rid,pos,val);

}

--- NOICA\7\permutation\permutation.cpp: query ---
int query(int id, int l, int r) {
    if (tr[id].l > r || tr[id].r < l) return INF;
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) return tr[id].mn;
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    return min(query(lid,l,r),query(rid,l,r));
}

--- NOICA\7\permutation\permutation.cpp: get ---
int get(int s) {
    if (m == n) return INF;
    int ret = INF;
    if (s + m <= n + 1) {
        if (s > 1) ret = min(ret,query(1,1,s - 1));
        if (s + m <= n) ret = min(ret,query(1,s + m,n));
        return ret;
    }
    int left = (s + m - 1) % n + 1;
    int right = (s - 2 + n) % n + 1;
    if (left <= right) return query(1,left,right);
    return INF;
}

--- NOICA\8\attach\attach.cpp: pushup ---
void pushup(int id) {
    tr[id].mn = min(tr[lid].mn,tr[rid].mn);
    return ;
}

--- NOICA\8\attach\attach.cpp: build ---
void build(int id,int l,int r) {
    tr[id].l = l,tr[id].r = r;
    if (l == r) {
        tr[id].mn = 0;
        return ;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    build(lid,l,mid);
    build(rid,mid + 1,r);
    pushup(id);
}

--- NOICA\8\attach\attach.cpp: pushdown ---
void pushdown(int id) {
    if (tr[id].lazy && tr[id].l != tr[id].r) {
        tr[lid].mn += tr[id].lazy, tr[rid].mn += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[lid].lazy += tr[id].lazy, tr[rid].lazy += tr[id].lazy;
        tr[id].lazy = 0;
    }
}

--- NOICA\8\attach\attach.cpp: modify ---
void modify(int id,int l,int r,int val) {
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) {
        tr[id].lazy += val, tr[id].mn += val;
        return ;
    }
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) modify(lid,l,r,val);
    else if (l > mid) modify(rid,l,r,val);
    else modify(lid,l,mid,val), modify(rid,mid + 1,r,val);
    pushup(id);
}

--- NOICA\8\attach\attach.cpp: query ---
int query(int id,int l,int r) {
    if (l > r) return 0;
    if (l <= tr[id].l && tr[id].r <= r) return tr[id].mn;
    pushdown(id);
    int mid = (tr[id].l + tr[id].r) >> 1;
    if (r <= mid) return query(lid,l,r);
    else if (l > mid) return query(rid,l,r);
    else return min(query(lid,l,mid),query(rid,mid + 1,r));
}

--- NOICA\8\attach\attach.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("attach.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("attach.out","w",stdout);
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> p[i];
        pos[p[i]] = i;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> q[i];
    build(1,1,n);
    int ans = n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        while (ans > 0 && query(1,pos[ans],n) - min(query(1,1,pos[ans] - 1),0) < 0) {
            modify(1,pos[ans],n,1);
            ans--;
        }
        cout << ans << ' ';
        modify(1,q[i],n,-1);
    }
    return 0;
}

--- NOICA\8\bike\bike.cpp: write ---
void write(i128 x) {
    if (x < 0) {putchar('-'); x = -x;}
    if (x > 9) write(x / 10);
    putchar(x % 10 + '0');
}

--- NOICA\8\bike\bike.cpp: check ---
bool check(i128 k) {
    if (k < 0) return false;
    i128 tmpmin = x * (x + 1) / 2 + k * v * (v + 1) / 2, tmpmax = (x + k * (v - 1)) * (x + k * (v - 1) + 1) / 2 + k * x + v * k * (k + 1) / 2 - k * (k - 1) / 2;
    return s >= tmpmin && s <= tmpmax && (s % v == tmpmin % v);
}

--- NOICA\9\signin\signin.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> c[i];
    sort(c + 1,c + m + 1);
    long long mul = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) mul = mul * c[i] % mod;
    pre[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) pre[i] = pre[i - 1] * a[i] % mod;
    suf[n + 1] = 1;
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) suf[i] = suf[i + 1] * a[i] % mod;
    long long sum = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int pos = lower_bound(c + 1, c + m + 1, a[i]) - c;
        if (!(pos <= m && c[pos] == a[i])) 
            sum = (sum + pre[i - 1] * suf[i + 1] % mod) % mod;
    }
    if (sum == mul) cout << "Y" << endl;
    else cout << "N" << endl;
}

    return 0;
}

int main() {
    int h,w,x1 = 1e9,x2 = 0,y1 = 1e9,y2 = 0;
    cin >> h >> w;
    for (int i = 1;i <= h;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= w;j++) {
            cin >> a[i][j];
            if (a[i][j] == '#') x1 = min(x1,i), x2 = max(x2,i), y1 = min(y1,j), y2 = max(y2,j);
            else if (a[i][j] == '.') pos.push_back(make_pair(i,j));
        }
    for (auto i : pos)
        if (i.first >= x1 && i.first <= x2 && i.second >= y1 && i.second <= y2) {
            cout << "No" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    cout << "Yes" << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- OJ\AtCoder\AT_abc390\AT_abc390_d [ABC390D] Stone XOR.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,long long cnt,long long sum) {
    if (step > n) {
        mp[sum] = true;
        return ;
    }
    s[cnt] = a[step];
    dfs(step + 1,cnt + 1,a[step] ^ sum);
    s[cnt] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i < cnt;i++) {
        long long tmp = s[i] ^ sum;
        s[i] += a[step];
        dfs(step + 1,cnt,s[i] ^ tmp);
        s[i] -= a[step];
    }
    return ;
}

--- OJ\POJ\1001.cpp: s2BIG ---
void s2BIG (string s,int a[])
{
	int al = s.length();
	for (int i = 1;i <= al;i++) a[i] = s[al - i] - '0';
	a[0] = al;
}

--- OJ\POJ\1001.cpp: mulBIG ---
void mulBIG(int a[],int b,int c[])
{
	int cl = a[0];
	long long u = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cl;i++)
	{
		int t = 1ll * a[i] * b + u;
		c[i] = t % 10;
		u = t / 10;
	}
	while (u > 0)
	{
		cl++;
		c[cl] = u % 10;
		u /= 10;
	}
	c[0] = cl;
}

	freopen("jump.out","w",stdout);
	cin >> n >> d;
	int id = 1,ans1 = 0,ans2 = 0;
	while (!flag1[change1(id)])
	{
		id = change1(id);
		flag1[id] = true;
		ans1++;
	}
	id = 1;
	while (!flag2[change2(id)])
	{
		id = change2(id);
		flag2[id] = true;
		ans2++;
	}
	cout << max(ans1,ans2) - 1 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- OJ\小明\小明月赛\2024.12\triangle.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	freopen("triangle.in","r",stdin);
	freopen("triangle.out","w",stdout);
    double x,y,a,b,c;
    scanf("%lf%lf",&x,&y);
    a = min(x,y);
    b = max(x,y);
    if (b * b - a * a >= 0) {
        c = max(sqrt(b * b - a * a),sqrt(a * a + b * b));
    } else {
        c = sqrt(a * a + b * b);
    }
    printf("%.4lf\n",c);
    return 0;
}

--- OJ\小明\小明月赛\2025.10 - Div.3\friends.cpp: count ---
int count(int x) {
    int res = 0;
    while (x) {
        res += x & 1;
        x >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
