--- OJ\小明\小明月赛\2025.10 - Div.3\prime.cpp: is_prime ---
bool is_prime(int x) {
    if (x < 2) return false;
    for (int i = 2;i * i <= x;i++)
        if (x % i == 0) return false;
    return true;
}

--- OJ\小明\小明月赛\2025.10 - Div.3\prime.cpp: main ---

    for (int i = 1;i <= n / 2;i++)
        if (is_prime(i) && is_prime(n - i)) {

        }
    return 0;

--- OJ\小明\小明月赛\2025.10 - Div.3\xor.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("xor.in","r",stdin);
    freopen("xor.out","w",stdout);
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--) {
        string s,a,b;
        cin >> s;
        int len = s.size();
        a.resize(len),b.resize(len);
        bool flag = false;
        for (int i = 0;i < len;i++) {
            if (s[i] == '1') {
                if (flag) a[i] = '0',b[i] = '1';
                else a[i] = '1',b[i] = '0',flag = true;
            }
            else if (s[i] == '2') {
                if (flag) a[i] = '0',b[i] = '2';
                else a[i] = '1',b[i] = '1';
            }
            else a[i] = b[i] = '0';
        }
        cout << a << endl << b << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++) cin >> a[i][j];
    for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) dp[n][j] = a[n][j];
    for (int i = n - 1;i >= 1;i--)
        for (int j = 1;j <= i;j++)
            dp[i][j] = max(dp[i + 1][j + 1],dp[i + 1][j]) + a[i][j];
    cout << dp[1][1] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- OJ\洛谷\『1&0』OI Round 1\test.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    freopen("test.txt","w",stdout);
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    n = (1 << n) - 1;
    cout << n << endl;
    vector<int> a(n+1), cp(n+1);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) a[i] = i;
    for (int round = 1; round <= 1000; round++) {
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            int next = i == n ? 1 : i + 1;
            cp[i] = a[i] ^ a[next];
        }
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            a[i] = cp[i];
            cout << a[i];
            if (i < n) cout << ' ';
        }
        cout << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- Practice\P1807.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
    memset(dis,-0x3f,sizeof dis);
    queue<int> q;
    dis[1] = 0,vis[1] = true;
    q.push(1);
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = false;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (dis[v] >= dis[u] + w) continue;
            dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
            if (!vis[v]) {
                q.push(v);
                vis[v] = true;
            }
        }
    }
    return ;
}

--- Practice\P1807.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v,w;
        cin >> u >> v >> w;
        G[u].push_back({v,w});
    }
    spfa();
    cout << (dis[n] <= -1e9 ? -1 : dis[n]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- Pre\bfs\bfs模板.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(/*起点*/);
	dis[/*起点*/] = 0;
	vis[/*起点*/] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int now = q.front();
		q.pop();
		if (now == /*终点*/) return ;
		int nxt = /*now的某种变换*/;
		if (nxt /*在题目的某种范围内*/ && !vis[nxt])
		{
			dis[nxt] = dis[now] + 1;
			vis[nxt] = true;
			q.push(nxt);
		}
	}
}

--- Pre\bfs\【模板】BFS.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<int> q;
	q.push();
	dis[] = 0;
	vis[] = true;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int now = q.front();
		q.pop();
		if (now == ) return ;
		int nxt = ;
		if (nxt && !vis[nxt])
		{
			dis[nxt] = dis[now] + 1;
			vis[nxt] = true;
			q.push(nxt);
		}
	}
}

		sum[i] = sum[i - 1] + a[i];

	for (int l = 1;l <= n;l++)

	return 0;
}

--- Pre\前缀和\收获香蕉.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	sum[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum[i] = sum[i - 1] + a[i];
	}
	for (int l = 1;l <= n;l++)
	{
		for (int r = l;r <= n;r++)
		{
			if (sum[r] - sum[l - 1] >= k) ans++;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\前缀和\收获香蕉（数据强化）.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	long long n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	sum[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum[i] = sum[i - 1] + a[i];
	}
	int pos = 1;
	for (int l = 1;l <= n;l++)
	{
		if (sum[n] - sum[l - 1] < k) break;
		for (int r = pos;r <= n;r++)
		{
			if (sum[r] - sum[l - 1] >= k) 
			{
				ans += n - r + 1;
				pos = r;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\前缀和\连续自然数之和.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	sum[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		sum[i] = sum[i - 1] + a[i];
	}
	int minn = 1e9;
	for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
	{
		ans = max(ans,sum[j] - minn);
		minn = min(minn,sum[j]);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\映射\Big-Array.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	long long n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i] >> b[i];
		mp[a[i]] += b[i];
	}
	for (auto kv : mp)
	{
		k -= kv.second;
		if (k <= 0)
		{
			cout << kv.first << endl;
			break;
		}
	}
    return 0;
}

	{
		string s;
		cin >> s;
		mp[s]--;
	}

		{
			cout << "OK" << endl;

	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		string s;
		cin >> s;
		mp[s] = 0;
	}
	int m;
	cin >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		string s;
		cin >> s;
		if (mp.count(s) == 0)
		{
			cout << "WRONG" << endl;
			continue;
		}
		if (!mp[s])
		{
			cout << "OK" << endl;
			mp[s] = true;
		}
		else 
		{
			cout << "REPEAT" << endl;
		}
	}
    return 0;
}

--- Pre\映射\等比数列.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,q;
	cin >> n >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
		mp2[a[i]]++;
	}
	mp1[a[1]]++;
	mp2[a[1]]--;
	mp2[a[2]]--;
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int j = 2;j <= n - 1;j++)
	{
		if (a[j] % q == 0)
			ans += 1ll * mp1[a[j] / q] * mp2[a[j] * q];
		mp1[a[j]]++;
		mp2[a[j + 1]]--;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

	cin >> n;
	while (n--)
	{
		int op;
		cin >> op;
		if (op == 1)
		{
			int x,y;
			cin >> x >> y;
			cnt[v[x]]--;
			if (cnt[v[x]] <= 0) cnt.erase(v[x]);
			v[x] = y;
			cnt[y]++;
		}
		else if (op == 2)
		{
			cout << cnt.begin() -> first << endl;
		}
		else 
		{
			cout << cnt.rbegin() -> first << endl;
		}
	}

--- Pre\贪心\Mixing Milk.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.p < y.p;
}

--- Pre\贪心\Mixing Milk.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		cin >> s[i].p >> s[i].a;
	}
	sort(s + 1,s + m + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		if (n >= s[i].a)
		{
			n -= s[i].a;
			ans += s[i].a * s[i].p;
		}
		else
		{
			ans += s[i].p * n;
			break;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\Saruman's Army.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(int x,int y)
{
	return x < y;
}

--- Pre\贪心\Saruman's Army.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int r,n;
	cin >> r >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> x[i];
	}
	sort(x + 1,x + n + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int pos = i;
		while (pos + 1 <= n && x[pos + 1] - x[i] <= r) pos++;
		ans++;
		i = pos;
		while (pos + 1 <= n && x[pos + 1] - x[i] <= r) pos++;
		i = pos;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0; 
}

--- Pre\贪心\乘船问题.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n,k;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i];
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
	int l = 1,r = n;
	int ans = 0;
	while (l <= r)
	{
		if (a[l] + a[r] <= k)
		{
			l++; 
		}
		ans++;
		r--;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\会场安排.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.e < y.e;
}

--- Pre\贪心\会场安排.cpp: solve ---
void solve()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].b >> s[i].e;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 1,pik = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (s[i].b > s[pik].e)
		{
			ans++;
			pik = i;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
}

--- Pre\贪心\会场安排.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int m;
	cin >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\区间覆盖问题.cpp: cmp ---

{
	return x.b < y.b;
}

--- Pre\贪心\区间覆盖问题.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 0,pik = 1;
	int maxe = -1e9; 
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (a[i].b <= s && s <= a[i].e)
		{
			maxe = max(maxe,a[i].e);
		}
		if (i == n || a[i + 1].b > s)
		{
			ans++;
			s = maxe;
			if (s >= t) break;
			maxe = -1e9;
		}
	}
	if (s < t) cout << -1 << endl;
	else cout << ans << endl;
	return 0; 
}

--- Pre\贪心\导弹拦截2010.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.d1 < y.d1;
}

--- Pre\贪心\导弹拦截2010.cpp: main ---
int main(){ 
	int x1,y1,x2,y2,n;
	cin >> x1 >> y1 >> x2 >> y2 >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		a[i].d1 = (x1 - x) * (x1 - x) + (y1 - y) * (y1 - y);
		a[i].d2 = (x2 - x) * (x2 - x) + (y2 - y) * (y2 - y);
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	int r1 = a[n].d1,r2 = 0,ans = r1;
	for (int i = n - 1;i >= 1;i--)
	{
		r1 = a[i].d1;
		r2 = max(r2,a[i + 1].d2);
		ans = min(ans,r1 + r2);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\排队接水.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp (Node x,Node y)
{
	if (x.t != y.t) return x.t < y.t;
	else return x.num < y.num;
}

--- Pre\贪心\排队接水.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> s[i].t;
		s[i].num = i;
	}
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cout << s[i].num << " ";
		ans += s[i].t * (n - i);
	}
	printf("\n%.2lf\n",ans * 1.00 / n);
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\水壶.cpp: main ---

		s[i] += s[i - 1] + a[i];
	}
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = k + 1;i <= n;i++)

--- Pre\贪心\泥泞路.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.b < y.b;
}

--- Pre\贪心\泥泞路.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,l;
	cin >> n >> l;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> a[i].b >> a[i].e;
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 0,p = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		int st = max(a[i].b,p);
		int len = a[i].e - st;
		int cnt = max(0,(len + l - 1) / l);
		ans += cnt;
		p = st + cnt * l;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\营养膳食.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,k;
	cin >> n >> m >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
	{
		cin >> cnt[i];
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> s[i].a >> s[i].b;
	}
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		if (cnt[s[i].b] > 0 && m > 0)
		{
			m--;
			cnt[s[i].b]--;
			ans += s[i].a;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\采购奖品.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y)
{
	return x.p < y.p;
}

--- Pre\贪心\采购奖品.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int m,n;
	cin >> m >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		cin >> s[i].p >> s[i].a;
	}
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,cmp);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
	{
		if (m - s[i].a * s[i].p >= 0)
		{
			m -= s[i].a * s[i].p;
			ans += s[i].a;
		}
		else
		{
			ans += m / s[i].p;
			break;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\贪心\黑心企业.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int n,m,k;
	cin >> n >> m >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> p[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> w[i];
	sort(p + 1,p + n + 1);
	sort(w + 1,w + m + 1);
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1,j = 1;i <= n && j <= m;i++,j++)
	{
		while(p[i] < w[j]) i++;
		ans += 1ll * k * p[i];
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\队列\Blah数集.cpp: main ---
int main(){

	cin >> a >> n;
	q1.push(2 * a + 1);
	q2.push(3 * a + 1);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
	{
		a = min(q1.front(),q2.front());
		q1.push(2 * a + 1);
		q2.push(3 * a + 1);
		if (a == q1.front()) q1.pop();
		if (a == q2.front()) q2.pop();
	}
	cout << a << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\队列\丑数.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	int k,n;
	cin >> k >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++)
	{
		cin >> p[i];
		q[i].push(p[i]);
	}
	LL minn = 1e18;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
	{
		minn = q[1].front();
		for (int j = 2;j <= k;j++)
		{
			minn = min(minn,q[j].front());
		}
		for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++)
		{
			q[j].push(minn * p[j]);
			if (minn == q[j].front()) q[j].pop();
		}
	}
	cout << minn;
	return 0;
}

	cin >> T;
	for (int i = 1;i <= T;i++)
	{
		string s;
		cin >> s;
		if (s == "ENQUEUE")
		{
			int z;
			cin >> z;
			q[z / 1000].push(z);
			if (q[z / 1000].size() == 1) Q.push(z / 1000);
		}
		else if (s == "DEQUEUE")
		{
			int t = Q.front();
			printf("%.06d\n",q[t].front());

		}

--- Pre\队列\第k个d好数.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<int> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 9;i++) q.push(i);
	while (q.size())
	{
		int now = q.front();
		q.pop();
		cnt++;
		if (cnt == k)
		{
			cout << now << endl;
			return ;
		}
		for (int i = 0;i <= 9;i++)
		{
			if (abs(now % 10 - i) > d) continue;
			int nxt = now * 10 + i;
			q.push(nxt);
		}
	}
}

--- Pre\队列\第k个d好数.cpp: main ---
int main()
{
	cin >> d >> k;
	bfs();
	
	return 0;
}

--- Pre\队列\青蛙路线计数.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs()
{
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(1);
	dis[1] = 0;
	vis[1] = true;
	cnt[1] = 1;
	while (q.size())
	{
		int now = q.front();
		q.pop();
		for (int i = 1;i <= a[now];i++)
		{
			int nxt = now + i;
			if (1 <= nxt && nxt <= n && !vis[nxt])
			{
				dis[nxt] = dis[now] + 1;
				vis[nxt] = true;
				cnt[nxt] = (cnt[nxt] + cnt[now]) % mod;
				q.push(nxt);
			}
			else if (1 <= nxt && nxt <= n && dis[now] + 1 == dis[nxt])
			{
				cnt[nxt] = (cnt[nxt] + cnt[now]) % mod;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- Pre\队列\青蛙路线计数.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		cin >> a[i];
	bfs();
	cout << dis[n] << endl;
	cout << cnt[n] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\单词查找树.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int step) {
	if (step >= s.size()) return ;
	if (a[u].child[s[step]] == 0) a[u].child[s[step]] = ++cur;
	dfs(a[u].child[s[step]],step + 1);
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\单词查找树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	while (cin >> s) dfs(1,0);
	cout << cur << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\树的路径求和.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	f[u] = pa,sum[u] = sum[pa] + a[u];
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u);
	}
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\树的路径求和.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
	}
	dfs(1,0);
	for (int v = 1;v <= n;v++) {
		int u = v;
		while (sum[v] - sum[f[u]] < s && u != 0) u = f[u];
		if (sum[v] - sum[f[u]] == s) ans++;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\猫猫和企鹅.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa,int step) {
    if (step > d) return ;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u,step + 1);
        if (step + 1 <= d) ans++;
    }
}

    }
    dfs(1,0,0);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\电脑病毒.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    int son = G[u].size();
    if (u != 1) son--;
    int tmp = 1;
    while (tmp <= son) ans++,tmp *= 2;
    ans += son;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u);
	}
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\联合权值.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	long long ansmax = 0,anssum = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> w[i];
	dfs(1,0);
	for (int u = 1;u <= n;u++) {
		int max1 = 0,max2 = 0,s = 0;
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			if (w[v] > max1) max2 = max1,max1 = w[v];
			else if (w[v] > max2) max2 = w[v];
			s += w[v],s %= mod;
		}
		ansmax = max(ansmax,1ll * max1 * max2);
		ansmax = max(ansmax,1ll * w[u] * w[f[f[u]]]);
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			if (v == f[u]) continue;
			anssum += 1ll * w[v] * (s - w[v]),anssum %= mod;
		}
		anssum += 1ll * w[u] * w[f[f[u]]],anssum %= mod;
	}
	cout << ansmax << ' ' << anssum << endl;
	return 0;
}

	}
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\道路修建.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	s[u] = 1;
	for (auto p : G[u]) {
		int v = p.v,w = p.w;
		if (v == pa) continue;
		f[v] = w;
		dfs(v,u);
		s[u] += s[v];
	}
}

--- S1-1\1 多叉树\道路修建.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	long long ans =  0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int a,b,c;
		cin >> a >> b >> c;
		G[a].push_back({b,c}); G[b].push_back({a,c});
	}
	dfs(1,0);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += 1ll * f[i] * abs(s[i] - (n - s[i]));
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\2 树的递推\信号放大器.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u);
		if (s[v] + w >= m) ans++,s[u] = max(s[u],w);
		else s[u] = max(s[u],s[v] + w);
	}
}

			cin >> u >> w;
			G[i].push_back({u,w});
			maxw = max(maxw,w);
		}
	}
	cin >> m;
	if (maxw >= m) {
		cout << "No solution." << endl;
		return 0;
	}
	dfs(1,0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int u;
		cin >> w[i] >> u;
		if (u != 0) {G[u].push_back(i); G[i].push_back(u);}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		memset(dep,0,sizeof dep);
		dep[0] = -1;
		dfs(i,0);
		long long tmp = 0;
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) tmp += 1ll * dep[j] * w[j];
		ans = min(ans,tmp);
	}

	return 0;
}

void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	if (pa == 0) dis[u] = 0;
	for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
		dfs(v,u);
	}
}

--- S1-1\2 树的递推\树的分解.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	dp[u] = 1;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u);
		dp[u] += dp[v];
	}
	if (dp[u] == k) dp[u] = 0,cnt++;
}

--- S1-1\2 树的递推\树的分解.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	cnt = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) G[i].clear();
	memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
	}
	if (n % k != 0) {
		cout << "NO" << endl;
		return ;
	}
	dfs(1,0);
	if (cnt == n / k) cout << "YES" << endl;
	else cout << "NO" << endl;
	return ;
}

--- S1-1\2 树的递推\树的分解.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\2 树的递推\植树.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	dp[u] = 1;
	bool flag = true;
	int tmp = 0;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u);
		dp[u] += dp[v];
		if (tmp == 0) tmp = dp[v];
		else if (tmp != dp[v]) flag = false;
	}
	if (tmp != 0 && dp[u] != n && n - dp[u] != tmp) flag = false;
	if (flag) ans.push_back(u);
}

--- S1-1\2 树的递推\植树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
	}
	dfs(1,0);
	sort(ans.begin(),ans.end());
	for (auto i : ans) cout << i << ' ';
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\3 多源最短路\【模板】传递闭包.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
            cin >> a[i][j];
    for (int k = 1;k <= n;k++)
        for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
            for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
                a[i][j] |= a[i][k] && a[k][j];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
            cout << a[i][j] << ' ';
        cout << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S1-1\3 多源最短路\【模板】多源最短路.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b,c;
		cin >> a >> b >> c;
		dp[a][b] = min(dp[a][b],c);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][i] = 0;
	for (int k = 1;k <= n;k++)
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
				dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k][j]);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			cout << (dp[i][j] == 0x3f3f3f3f ? -1 : dp[i][j]) << ' ';
		cout << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\3 多源最短路\无向图最小环.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	int n,m;
	long long ans = 1e9;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			dp[i][j] = 1e9;
	while (m--) {
		int a,b,c;
		cin >> a >> b >> c;
		dp[a][b] = dp[b][a] = c;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			G[i][j] = dp[i][j];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][i] = 0;
	for (int k = 1;k <= n;k++) {
		for (int i = 1;i < k;i++)
			for (int j = i + 1;j <= k;j++)
				ans = min(ans,dp[i][j] + G[i][k] + G[k][j]);
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
				dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],dp[i][k] + dp[k][j]);
	}
	if (ans == 1e9) cout << "No Simple Cycle" << endl;
	else cout << ans << endl;
	return ;
}

--- S1-1\3 多源最短路\无向图最小环.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\3 多源最短路\灾后重建.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> t[i];
	memset(G,0x3f,sizeof G);
	while (m--) {
		int i,j,w;
		cin >> i >> j >> w;
		i++,j++;
		G[i][j] = G[j][i] = min(G[i][j],w);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) G[i][i] = 0;
	int q,k = 1;
	cin >> q;
	while (q--) {
		int x,y,day;
		cin >> x >> y >> day;
		x++,y++;
		if (t[x] > day || t[y] > day) {
			cout << -1 << endl;
			continue;
		}
		while (t[k] <= day && k <= n) {
			for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
				for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
					G[i][j] = min(G[i][j],G[i][k] + G[k][j]);
			k++;
		}
		if (G[x][y] > 1e9) cout << -1 << endl;
		else cout << G[x][y] << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\4 01BFS\KATHTHI.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    deque<Node> q;
    dis[1][1] = 0;
    q.push_back({1,1});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        Node p = q.front(); q.pop_front();
        int x = p.x,y = p.y;
        for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++) {
            int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i],w;
            if (nx < 1 || nx > r || ny < 1 || ny > c) continue;
            if (a[x][y] == a[nx][ny]) w = 0;
            else w = 1;
            if (dis[nx][ny] > dis[x][y] + w) {
                dis[nx][ny] = dis[x][y] + w;
                if (w) q.push_back({nx,ny});
                else q.push_front({nx,ny});
            }
        }
    }
}

--- S1-1\4 01BFS\KATHTHI.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    cin >> r >> c;
    for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= c;j++)
            cin >> a[i][j];
    bfs();
    cout << dis[r][c] << endl;
    return ;
}

--- S1-1\4 01BFS\KATHTHI.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- S1-1\4 01BFS\Small Multiple.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
    deque<int> q;
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    dis[1] = 1;
    q.push_back(1);
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int x = q.front(); q.pop_front();
        int y = x * 10 % k;
        if (dis[x] < dis[y]) {
            dis[y] = dis[x];
            q.push_front(y);
        }
        y = (x + 1) % k;
        if (dis[x] + 1 < dis[y]) {
            dis[y] = dis[x] + 1;
            q.push_back(y);
        }
    }
}

    cin >> k;
    bfs();
    cout << dis[0] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\单源最短路径（弱化版）.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(s);
	dis[s] = 0,vis[s] = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				if (!vis[v]) q.push(v);
				vis[v] = 1;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\单源最短路径（弱化版）.cpp: main ---

	cin >> n >> m >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		G[u].push_back({v,w});
	}
	spfa();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ((dis[i] == 0x3f3f3f3f) ? 2147483647 : dis[i]) << ' ';
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\奶牛派对.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa(int s,int dis[],vector<Edge> G[]) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dis[i] = 1e9;
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(s);
	dis[s] = 0,vis[s] = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				if (!vis[v]) q.push(v);
				vis[v] = 1;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\奶牛派对.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> m >> x;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		G[u].push_back({v,w}); rG[v].push_back({u,w});
	}
	spfa(x,dis,G);
	spfa(x,rdis,rG);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dis[i] + rdis[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\牛奶管道.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa(int minc) {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(1);
	dis[1] = 0,vis[1] = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,l = p.l,c = p.c;
			if (c < minc) continue;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + l) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + l;
				if (vis[v] == 0) q.push(v);
				vis[v] = 1;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\牛奶管道.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m >> x;
	for (int k = 1;k <= m;k++) {
		int i,j,l;
		cin >> i >> j >> l >> c[k];
		G[i].push_back({j,l,c[k]}); G[j].push_back({i,l,c[k]});
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		spfa(c[i]);
		ans = min(ans,dis[n] + x / c[i]);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
 	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\航空路线.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	memset(cnt,0x3f,sizeof cnt);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(a);
	dis[a] = 0,vis[a] = 1,cnt[a] = 0;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w,d = p.d;
			if (dis[v] < dis[u] + w) continue;
			if (dis[v] == dis[u] + w && cnt[v] <= cnt[u] + d) continue;
			dis[v] = dis[u] + w,cnt[v] = cnt[u] + d;
			if (vis[v] == 0) q.push(v);
			vis[v] = 1;
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\航空路线.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> a >> b >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int cost,num;
		cin >> cost >> num;
		for (int j = 1;j <= num;j++) cin >> tmp[j];
		for (int j = 1;j <= num;j++)
			for (int k = j + 1;k <= num;k++)
				G[tmp[j]].push_back({tmp[k],cost,k - j});
	}
	spfa();
	cout << (dis[b] == 0x3f3f3f3f3f3f3f3f ? -1 : dis[b]) << ' ' << (cnt[b] == 0x3f3f3f3f ? -1 : cnt[b]) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\营救.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(s);
	dis[s] = 0,vis[s] = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] > max(dis[u],w)) {
				dis[v] = max(dis[u],w);
				if (!vis[v]) q.push(v);
				vis[v] = 1;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\5 spfa算法\营救.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m >> s >> t;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		G[u].push_back({v,w}); G[v].push_back({u,w});
	}
	spfa();
	cout << dis[t] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\【模板】单源最短路径（标准版）.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Node> q;
    dis[s] = 0;
    q.push({s,dis[s]});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = 1;
        for (auto p : G[u]) {
            int v = p.v,w = p.w;
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
                q.push({v,dis[v]});
            }
        }
    }
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\【模板】单源最短路径（标准版）.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m >> s;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v,w;
        cin >> u >> v >> w;
        G[u].push_back({v,w});
    }
    dijkstra();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (i == s) cout << 0 << ' ';
        else if (dis[i] == 0x3f3f3f3f) cout << 2147483647 << ' ';
        else cout << dis[i] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

bool cmpx(Point x,Point y) {return x.x < y.x;}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\平面上的点.cpp: cmpy ---
bool cmpy(Point x,Point y) {return x.y < y.y;}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\平面上的点.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	priority_queue<Node> q;
	dis[1] = 0;
	q.push({1,dis[1]});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				q.push({v,dis[v]});
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\平面上的点.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y; a[i].id = i;}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmpx);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
		int u = a[i - 1].id,v = a[i].id;
		int w = min(abs(a[i].x - a[i - 1].x),abs(a[i].y - a[i - 1].y));
		G[u].push_back({v,w}); G[v].push_back({u,w});
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmpy);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
		int u = a[i - 1].id,v = a[i].id;
		int w = min(abs(a[i].x - a[i - 1].x),abs(a[i].y - a[i - 1].y));
		G[u].push_back({v,w}); G[v].push_back({u,w});
	}
	dijkstra();
	cout << dis[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\热浪.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	dis[s] = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int u = 0;
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			if (!vis[j] && dis[j] < dis[u]) u = j;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			dis[v] = min(dis[v],dis[u] + w);
		}
	}
}

		cin >> u >> v >> w;

	}

	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\爱笑的小莫.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dis[i] = 1e18;
	priority_queue<Node> q;
	dis[1] = 0;
	q.push({1,dis[1]});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			w += val[v];
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				q.push({v,dis[v]});
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\爱笑的小莫.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m >> k >> t;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		G[u].push_back({v,w});
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= t;i++) {
		int p;
		cin >> p;
		val[p] = k;
	}
	dijkstra();
	cout << (dis[n] == 1e18 ? -1 : dis[n]) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\路径统计.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	priority_queue<Node> q;
	dis[1] = 0,cnt[1] = 1;
	q.push({1,dis[1]});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				cnt[v] = cnt[u];
				q.push({v,dis[v]});
			} else if (dis[v] == dis[u] + w) cnt[v] += cnt[u];
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\6 Dijkstra算法\路径统计.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> e;
	for (int i = 1;i <= e;i++) {
		int u,v,c;
		cin >> u >> v >> c;
		G[u].push_back({v,c});
	}
	dijkstra();
	if (dis[n] == 0x3f3f3f3f) cout << "No answer" << endl;
	else cout << dis[n] << ' ' << cnt[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\奶牛过马路.cpp: bfs ---
void bfs() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	deque<Node> q;
	q.push_front({x1,y1});
	dis[x1][y1] = 0;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		Node u = q.front(); q.pop_front();
		int x = u.x,y = u.y;
		for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++) {
			int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
			if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n) continue;
			bool w = G[x][y][i];
			if (dis[nx][ny] > dis[x][y] + w) {
				dis[nx][ny] = dis[x][y] + w;
				if (w == 1) q.push_back({nx,ny});
				else q.push_front({nx,ny});
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\奶牛过马路.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> r;
	for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++) {
		int r1,c1,r2,c2;
		cin >> r1 >> c1 >> r2 >> c2;
		if (r1 == r2 && c1 == c2 + 1)
			G[r1][c1][0] = G[r2][c2][1] = true;
		else if (r1 == r2 && c2 == c1 + 1)
			G[r1][c1][1] = G[r2][c2][0] = true;
		else if (r1 == r2 + 1 && c1 == c2)
			G[r1][c1][2] = G[r2][c2][3] = true;
		else G[r1][c1][3] = G[r2][c2][2] = true;
	}
	cin >> x1 >> y1 >> x2 >> y2;
	bfs();
	cout << dis[x2][y2] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\彩灯祝福.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	for (auto p : G[u]) {
		int v = p.v,w = p.w;
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dfs(v,u);
		dp[u] = max(dp[u],dp[v] + w);
	}
	for (auto p : G[u]) {
		int v = p.v,w = p.w;
		if (v == pa) continue;
		ans += dp[u] - dp[v] - w;
	}
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\彩灯祝福.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,s;
	cin >> n >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int a,b,t;
		cin >> a >> b >> t;
		G[a].push_back({b,t}); G[b].push_back({a,t});
	}
	dfs(s,0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\树上最长路径.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	for (auto p : G[u]) {
		int v = p.v,w = p.w;
		if (v == pa) continue;
		dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
		dfs(v,u);
	}
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\树上最长路径.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	long long n,id = 0,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		G[u].push_back({v,w}); G[v].push_back({u,w});
	}
	dfs(1,0);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (dis[i] > dis[id]) id = i;
	memset(dis,0,sizeof dis);
	dfs(id,0);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,dis[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

	dis[s] = 0;
	q.push({s,dis[s]});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = 1;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;

		}
	}
}

--- S1-1\7 综合\畅通工程.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++) G[i].clear();
	memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		u++,v++;
		G[u].push_back({v,w}); G[v].push_back({u,w});
	}
	cin >> s >> t;
	s++,t++;
	dijkstra();
	cout << (dis[t] == 0x3f3f3f3f3f3f3f3f ? -1 : dis[t]) << endl;
	return ;
}

	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\【模板】最长路.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa() {
	memset(dis,-0x3f,sizeof dis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(1);
	dis[1] = 0,vis[1] = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] < dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				if (!vis[v]) {
					q.push(v);
					vis[v] = true;
					if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return false;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\【模板】负环.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(1);
	dis[1] = 0,vis[1] = true;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = false;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				if (!vis[v]) {
					q.push(v);
					vis[v] = true;
					if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return false;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\【模板】负环.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	memset(cnt,0,sizeof cnt);
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) G[i].clear();
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		if (w >= 0) {G[u].push_back({v,w}); G[v].push_back({u,w});}
		else G[u].push_back({v,w});
	}
	cout << (spfa() ? "YES" : "NO") << endl;
	return ;
}

	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\天路.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa(double mid) {
    for (int i = 0;i <= n;i++) dis[i] = -1e9;
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
    memset(cnt,0,sizeof cnt);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(0);
    dis[0] = 0,vis[0] = 1;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = 0;
        for (auto [v,V,P] : G[u])
            if (dis[v] < dis[u] + V - mid * P) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + V - mid * P;
                if (!vis[v]) {
                    q.push(v);
                    vis[v] = true;
                    if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
                }
            }
    }
    return false;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\天路.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x,y,v,p;
        cin >> x >> y >> v >> p;
        G[x].push_back({y,v,p});
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) G[0].push_back({i,0});
	double L = 0,R = 200,ans = -1;
	while (R - L >= eps) {
		double mid = (L + R) / 2;
	    if (spfa(mid)) {
	        ans = mid;
	        L = mid;
	    } else R = mid;
	}
    if (ans != -1) printf("%.1lf\n",ans);
    else cout << -1 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\找工作.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa() {
	memset(dis,-0x3f,sizeof dis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(s);
	dis[s] = d,vis[s] = 1;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = 0;
		for (auto p : G[u]) {
			int v = p.v,w = p.w;
			if (dis[v] < dis[u] + w) {
				dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
				if (!vis[v]) {
					q.push(v);
					vis[v] = true;
					if (++cnt[v] >= c) return true;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return false;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\找工作.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> d >> p >> c >> f >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= p;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		G[a].push_back({b,d});
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= f;i++) {
		int j,k,t;
		cin >> j >> k >> t;
		G[j].push_back({k,d - t});
	}
	if (spfa()) cout << -1 << endl;
	else {
		int ans = -1e9;
		for (int i = 1;i <= c;i++) ans = max(ans,dis[i]);
		cout << ans << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\最大得分.cpp: check ---
bool check(double mid) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) c[i] = a[i] - mid * b[i];
	sort(c + 1,c + n + 1,greater<double>());
	double sum = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n - k;i++) sum += c[i];
	return (sum >= 0);
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\最大得分.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	double L = 0,R = 1,ans = 0;
	while (R - L >= eps) {
	    double mid = (L + R) / 2;
	    if (check(mid)) {
	        ans = mid;
	        L = mid;
	    } else R = mid;
	}
	cout << round(ans * 100) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\1 负环和分数规划\观光奶牛.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa(double mid) {
	for (int i = 0;i <= l;i++) dis[i] = -1e9;
    memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
    memset(cnt,0,sizeof cnt);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(0);
    dis[0] = 0,vis[0] = 1;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = 0;
        for (auto p : G[u]) {
            int v = p.v,w = p.w;
            if (dis[v] < dis[u] + f[v] - mid * w) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + f[v] - mid * w;
                if (!vis[v]) {
                    q.push(v);
                    vis[v] = true;
                    if (++cnt[v] >= l) return true;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

int main() {

		int l1,l2,t;

		G[l1].push_back({l2,t});
	}
    for (int i = 1;i <= l;i++) G[0].push_back({i,0});

		double mid = (L + R) / 2;
	    if (spfa(mid)) {
	        ans = mid;
	        L = mid;
	    } else R = mid;
	}
	printf("%.2lf",ans);
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\【模板】次短路.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	memset(dis2,0x3f,sizeof dis2);
	priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[1] = 0;
    q.push({1,0});
    while (!q.empty()) {
    	int u = q.top().v,d = q.top().w; q.pop();
    	for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
    		int t = d + w;
    		if (t < dis[v]) {
    			dis2[v] = dis[v];
    			dis[v] = t;
    			q.push({v,dis[v]});
    		}
            else if (t > dis[v] && t < dis2[v]) {
            	dis2[v] = t;
            	q.push({v,dis2[v]});
            }
    	}
    }
    return ;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\神经网络.cpp: topo_sort ---
void topo_sort() {
	queue<int> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		topo[++cur] = u;
		for (auto [v,w] : G[u])
			if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
	}
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\神经网络.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> p;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i] >> u[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= p;i++) {
		int u,v,w;
		cin >> u >> v >> w;
		G[u].push_back({v,w});
		in[v]++,out[u]++;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (in[i]) c[i] = -u[i];
	topo_sort();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int u = topo[i];
		for (auto [v,w] : G[u])
			if (c[u] > 0) c[v] += w * c[u];
	}
	bool flag = false;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (out[i] == 0 && c[i] > 0) {cout << i << ' ' << c[i] << endl; flag = true;}
	if (!flag) cout << "NULL" << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\骑士游戏.cpp: readint ---
long long readint() {
    long long k = 0;
    int f = 1;
    uint8_t c = gc();
    while (c != '-' && (c < '0' || c > '9')) c = gc();
    if (c == '-') f = -1, c = gc();
    while (c >= '0' && c <= '9') {
        k = k * 10 + c - '0';
        c = gc();
    }
    return k * f;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\骑士游戏.cpp: put ---
void put(long long d) {
    if (d < 0) putchar_unlocked('-'),d = -d;
    if (d > 9) put(d / 10);
    putchar_unlocked(d % 10 + '0');
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\骑士游戏.cpp: push ---
void push(int x) {
    q[tail] = x;
    tail = (tail + 1) % n;
    cnt++;
    vis[x] = true;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\骑士游戏.cpp: pop ---
int pop() {
    int x = q[head];
    head = (head + 1) % n;
    cnt--;
    vis[x] = false;
    return x;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\骑士游戏.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
    head = tail = cnt = 0;

    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        dis[i] = sent[i] = k[i];
        sum[i] = s[i];
        for (int w : G[i]) sum[i] += k[w];
    }

    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (sum[i] < dis[i]) dis[i] = sum[i],push(i);

    while (cnt) {
        int u = pop();
        long long delta = dis[u] - sent[u];
        if (delta == 0) continue;
        sent[u] = dis[u];

        for (int v : rG[u]) {
            sum[v] += delta;
            if (sum[v] < dis[v]) {
                dis[v] = sum[v];
                if (!vis[v]) push(v);
            }
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    struct stat z;
    fstat(0,&z);
    str = (uint8_t*)mmap(NULL,z.st_size,PROT_READ,MAP_PRIVATE,0,0);
    bufEnd = str + z.st_size;

    n = readint();
    for (int u = 1;u <= n;u++) {
        s[u] = readint(),k[u] = readint(),r[u] = readint();
        for (int j = 1;j <= r[u];j++) {
            int v = readint();
            G[u].push_back(v);
            rG[v].push_back(u);
        }
    }

    spfa();
    put(dis[1]);
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\黑暗城堡.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[1] = 0;
    q.push({1,0});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = 1;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] > dis[u] + w) {
                dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
                q.push({v,dis[v]});
            }
        }
    }
    return ;
}

--- S1-2\10 复杂图论\黑暗城堡.cpp: main ---
int main() {

    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x,y,l;
        cin >> x >> y >> l;
        G[x].push_back({y,l}); G[y].push_back({x,l});
    }
    dijkstra();
    long long ans = 1;
    for (int u = 1;u <= n;u++)
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u])
            if (dis[u] + w == dis[v]) cnt[v]++;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) ans = ans * cnt[i] % mod;
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\GEPPETTO.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m,ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> x[i] >> y[i];
    for (int i = 0;i <= (1 << n) - 1;i++) {
        bool flag = true;
        for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
            if (((i >> (x[j] - 1)) & 1) && ((i >> (y[j] - 1)) & 1)) {flag = false; break;}
        ans += flag;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\内建函数实现.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    // 1. 求二进制中1的个数

    __builtin_popcount(n); // O(log log n)

    int cnt = 0;
    while (n)
        cnt += n & 1,n >>= 1; // O(log n)

    // 2. 求二进制中1的个数的奇偶性

    __builtin_parity(n); // O(log log n)

    int cnt = 0;
    while (n)
        cnt ^= n & 1,n >>= 1; // O(log n)
    bool parity = cnt & 1;

    // 3. 求前导0个数

    __builtin_clz(n); // O(log log n)

    (1 << j) > x

    // 4. 求末尾0的个数

    __builtin_ctz(n); // O(log log n)

    x & -x
    (x & (1 << j)) == 1;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\异或非或.cpp: check ---
bool check(long long tmp) {
    if ((s[n] & tmp) != 0) return false;
    memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
    unordered_map<long long,int> mp;
    mp[s[0] & tmp] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        long long target = s[i] & tmp;
        if (mp.count(target))
            dp[i] = mp[target] + 1;
        if (dp[i] > 0) mp[target] = max(dp[i],mp[target]);
    }
    return dp[n] >= m;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\异或非或.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    long long ans = 0,mask = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        s[i] = s[i - 1] ^ a[i];
    }
    for (int k = 62;k >= 0;k--) {
        long long tmp = mask | (1ll << k);
        if (check(tmp)) mask = tmp;
        else ans |= (1ll << k);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\找筷子.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n,ans = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int a;
        cin >> a;
        ans ^= a;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\浏览器.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,a,b,c,d;
    long long cnt1 = 0,cnt2 = 0;
    cin >> n >> a >> b >> c >> d >> x[0];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        x[i] = 1ll * (a * x[i - 1] % d * x[i - 1] % d + b * x[i - 1] % d + c) % d;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (__builtin_parity(x[i])) cnt1++;
        else cnt2++;
    cout << 1ll * cnt1 * cnt2 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\绝世好题.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,maxn = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        maxn = 0;
        for (int k = 0;k <= 30;k++)
            if (a[i] & (1 << k)) maxn = max(maxn,dp[k] + 1);
        for (int k = 0;k <= 30;k++)
            if (a[i] & (1 << k)) dp[k] = maxn;
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 0;i <= 30;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\11 位运算进阶\起床困难综合症.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    long long ans = 0,s = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> op[i] >> t[i];
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--) {
        bool flag1 = true,flag2 = false;
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
            bool tmp = (t[j] >> i) & 1;
            if (op[j] == "AND") flag1 &= tmp,flag2 &= tmp;
            else if (op[j] == "OR") flag1 |= tmp,flag2 |= tmp;
            else flag1 ^= tmp,flag2 ^= tmp;
        }
        if (flag2) ans |= (1 << i);
        else if (flag1 && (s | (1 << i)) <= m)
            s |= 1 << i,ans |= 1 << i;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\2 差分约束\奶牛排队.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa(int s) {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	memset(vis,0,sizeof vis);
	memset(cnt,0,sizeof cnt);
	queue<int> q;
	if (s == 0)
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
			dis[i] = 0;
			vis[i] = cnt[i] = 1;
			q.push(i);
		}
	else {dis[s] = 0,vis[s] = 1; q.push(s);}
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = false;
		for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
			if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + w) continue;
			dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
			if (!vis[v]) {
				q.push(v);
				vis[v] = true;
				if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
			}
		}
	}
	return false;
}

--- S1-2\2 差分约束\奶牛排队.cpp: main ---

		int a,b,d;

	for (int i = 1;i <= md;i++) {
		int a,b,d;
		cin >> a >> b >> d;
		G[b].push_back({a,-d});
	}
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) G[i + 1].push_back({i,0});
	if (spfa(0)) cout << -1 << endl;
	else {
		spfa(1);
		if (dis[n] >= 1e9) cout << -2 << endl;
		else cout << dis[n] - dis[1] << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\2 差分约束\小K的农场.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        vis[i] = cnt[i] = 1;
        dis[i] = 0;
        q.push(i);
    }
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = false;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + w) continue;
            dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
            if (!vis[v]) {
                q.push(v);
                vis[v] = true;
                if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

--- S1-2\2 差分约束\工程规划.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        vis[i] = cnt[i] = 1;
        dis[i] = 0;
        q.push(i);
    }
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = false;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + w) continue;
            dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
            if (!vis[v]) {
                vis[v] = true;
                q.push(v);
                if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

--- S1-2\2 差分约束\糖果.cpp: spfa ---
bool spfa() {
    memset(dis,-0x3f,sizeof dis);
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        dis[i] = vis[i] = cnt[i] = 1;
        q.push(i);
    }
    int tot = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        vis[u] = false;
        if (++tot > 1e7) return true;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (dis[v] >= dis[u] + w) continue;
            dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
            if (!vis[v]) {
                q.push(v);
                vis[v] = true;
                if (++cnt[v] >= n) return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

            case 2 : {G[a].push_back({b,1}); break;}
            case 3 : {G[b].push_back({a,0}); break;}

        }
    }

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\RoadBlock.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	dis[1] = 0;
	for (int t = 1;t <= n;t++) {
		int u = 0;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			if (!vis[i] && dis[i] < dis[u]) u = i;
		vis[u] = true;
		for (int v = 1;v <= n;v++)
			if (G[u][v] && dis[v] > dis[u] + G[u][v])
				dis[v] = dis[u] + G[u][v],f[v] = u;
	}
	return ;
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\RoadBlock.cpp: dijkstra2 ---
void dijkstra2() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
	dis[1] = 0;
	for (int t = 1;t <= n;t++) {
		int u = 0;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			if (!vis[i] && dis[i] < dis[u]) u = i;
		vis[u] = true;
		for (int v = 1;v <= n;v++)
			if (G[u][v] && dis[v] > dis[u] + G[u][v])
				dis[v] = dis[u] + G[u][v];
	}
	return ;
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\RoadBlock.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b,l;
		cin >> a >> b >> l;
		G[a][b] = G[b][a] = l;
	}
	dijkstra();
	int t = dis[n],u = n;
	while (u != 1) {
		int v = f[u];
		G[u][v] *= 2,G[v][u] *= 2;
		dijkstra2();
		ans = max(ans,dis[n] - t);
		G[u][v] /= 2,G[v][u] /= 2;
		u = v;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\包裹运送.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra(int dis[N],int s) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= 40000;i++) dis[i] = 1e9,vis[i] = 0;
	priority_queue<Node> pq;
	dis[s] = 0;
	pq.push({s,0});
	while (!pq.empty()) {
		int u = pq.top().v; pq.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = true;
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + 1) continue;
			dis[v] = dis[u] + 1;
			pq.push({v,dis[v]});
		}
	}
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\包裹运送.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int b,e,p,n,m,ans = 1e9;
	cin >> b >> e >> p >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
	}
	dijkstra(dis1,1); dijkstra(dis2,2); dijkstra(dis3,n);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = min(ans,b * dis1[i] + e * dis2[i] + p * dis3[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\最优贸易.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa() {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(1);
	dis[1] = a[1],vis[1] = true;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		vis[u] = false;
		for (auto v : G[u]) {
			if (dis[v] <= min(dis[u],a[v])) continue;
			dis[v] = min(dis[u],a[v]);
			if (!vis[v]) {
				q.push(v);
				vis[v] = true;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\最优贸易.cpp: Rspfa ---
void Rspfa() {
	memset(rdis,-0x3f,sizeof rdis);
	queue<int> q;
	q.push(n);
	rdis[n] = a[n],rvis[n] = true;
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.front(); q.pop();
		rvis[u] = false;
		for (auto v : rG[u]) {
			if (rdis[v] >= max(rdis[u],a[v])) continue;
			rdis[v] = max(rdis[u],a[v]);
			if (!rvis[v]) {
				q.push(v);
				rvis[v] = true;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-2\3 单源最短路进阶\最优贸易.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int x,y,z;
		cin >> x >> y >> z;
		G[x].push_back(y); rG[y].push_back(x);
		if (z == 2) {G[y].push_back(x); rG[x].push_back(y);}
	}
	spfa(); Rspfa();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,rdis[i] - dis[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\反转.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Edge> q;
    dis[1] = 0;s
    q.push({1,0});
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = true;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[v]) continue;
            if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + w) continue;
            dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
            q.push({v,dis[v]});
        }
    }
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\反转.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m >> x;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back({v,1}); G[v + n].push_back({u + n,1});
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {G[i].push_back({i + n,x}); G[i + n].push_back({i,x}); }
    dijkstra();
    cout << min(dis[n],dis[2 * n]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\回家的路.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra(int s) {
	memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
	priority_queue<Edge> q;
	dis[s] = dis[s + m] = 0;
	q.push({s,0}); q.push({s + m,0});
	while (!q.empty()) {
		int u = q.top().v; q.pop();
		if (vis[u]) continue;
		vis[u] = true;
		for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
			if (vis[v]) continue;
			if (dis[v] <= dis[u] + w) continue;
			dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
			q.push({v,dis[v]});
		}
	}
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\旅游巴士.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Node> q;
    q.push({1,0,0});
    dis[0][1] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().u,l = q.top().l; q.pop();
        if (vis[l][u]) continue;
        vis[l][u] = true;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            int tim = dis[l][u],nxt = (l + 1) % k;
            if (tim < w) tim += (w - tim + k - 1) / k * k;
            if (vis[nxt][v]) continue;
            if (dis[nxt][v] <= tim + 1) continue;
            dis[nxt][v] = tim + 1;
            q.push({v,nxt,dis[nxt][v]});
        }
    }
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\旅游巴士.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v,a;
        cin >> u >> v >> a;
        G[u].push_back({v,a});
    }
    dijkstra();
    cout << (dis[0][n] == 0x3f3f3f3f3f3f3f3f ? -1 : dis[0][n]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\飞行路线.cpp: dijkstra ---
void dijkstra() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    priority_queue<Node> q;
    q.push({s,0,0});
    dis[0][s] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().u,l = q.top().l; q.pop();
        if (vis[l][u]) continue;
        vis[l][u] = true;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[l][v]) continue;
            if (dis[l][v] <= dis[l][u] + w) continue;
            dis[l][v] = dis[l][u] + w;
            q.push({v,l,dis[l][v]});
        }
        if (l == k) continue;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (vis[l + 1][v]) continue;
            if (dis[l + 1][v] <= dis[l][u]) continue;
            dis[l + 1][v] = dis[l][u];
            q.push({v,l + 1,dis[l + 1][v]});
        }
    }
}

--- S1-2\4 分层图\飞行路线.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m >> k >> s >> t;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int a,b,c;
        cin >> a >> b >> c;
        G[a].push_back({b,c}); G[b].push_back({a,c});
    }
    dijkstra();
    long long ans = 1e18;
    for (int i = 0;i <= k;i++) ans = min(ans,dis[i][t]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\关闭农场.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m,cnt = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> a[i]; f[i] = i;}
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
        int u = a[i];
        vis[u] = true;
        for (auto v : G[u]) {
            if (!vis[v]) continue;
            if (find(u) != find(v)) {cnt++; Union(u,v);}
        }
        if (cnt == n - i) ans[i] = true;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << (ans[i] ? "YES" : "NO") << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\好友圈.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\好友圈.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx == fy) return ;
    if (size[fx] < size[fy]) f[fx] = fy,size[fy] += size[fx],ans = max(ans,size[fy]);
    else f[fy] = fx,size[fx] += size[fy],ans = max(ans,size[fx]);
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\好友圈.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i,size[i] = 1;
    cnt = n;
    while (m--) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        if (find(x) != find(y)) {Union(x,y); cnt--;}
        cout << cnt << ' ' << ans << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\家谱.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    string s,fa;
    char c;
    while (cin >> c) {
        if (c == '$') break;
        cin >> s;
        if (f.count(s) == 0) f[s] = s;
        if (c == '#') fa = s;
        else if (c == '+') Union(s,fa);
        else cout << s << ' ' << find(s) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\扩散.cpp: qdis ---
int qdis(Point x,Point y) {return abs(x.x - y.x) + abs(x.y - y.y);}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\扩散.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
			if (qdis(a[i],a[j]) <= 2 * mid) Union(i,j);
	int cnt = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (f[i] == i) cnt++;
	return cnt == 1;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\扩散.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y;
	int l = 1,r = 1e9,ans;
	while (l <= r) {
		int mid = l + r >> 1;
		if (check(mid)) r = mid - 1,ans = mid;
		else l = mid + 1;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\联通的圆环.cpp: check1 ---
bool check1(int x1,int y1,int r,int x2,int y2) {return 1ll * (x2 - x1) * (x2 - x1) + 1ll * (y2 - y1) * (y2 - y1) == 1ll * r * r;}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\联通的圆环.cpp: check2 ---
bool check2(int x1,int y1,int r1,int x2,int y2,int r2) {
    long long d = 1ll * (x2 - x1) * (x2 - x1) + 1ll * (y2 - y1) * (y2 - y1);
    return 1ll * (r1 - r2) * (r1 - r2) <= d && d <= 1ll * (r1 + r2) * (r1 + r2);
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\联通的圆环.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\联通的圆环.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\联通的圆环.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    memset(f,0,sizeof f);
    int n,sx,sy,tx,ty,s,t;
    cin >> n >> sx >> sy >> tx >> ty;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int x,y,r;
        cin >> x >> y >> r;
        a[i] = {x,y,r},f[i] = i;
        if (check1(x,y,r,sx,sy)) s = i;
        if (check1(x,y,r,tx,ty)) t = i;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
            int x1 = a[i].x,y1 = a[i].y,r1 = a[i].r,x2 = a[j].x,y2 = a[j].y,r2 = a[j].r;
            if (check2(x1,y1,r1,x2,y2,r2)) Union(i,j);
        }
    cout << ((find(s) == find(t)) ? "Yes" : "No") << endl;
    return ;
}

--- S1-2\5 并查集基础\联通的圆环.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\6 并查集进阶\关押罪犯.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        cin >> s[i].a >> s[i].b >> s[i].c;
    sort(s + 1,s + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.c > y.c;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int a = s[i].a,b = s[i].b,c = s[i].c;
        if (find(a) == find(b)) {cout << c << endl; return 0;}
        else {Union(a,b + n); Union(b,a + n);}
    }
    cout << 0 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\6 并查集进阶\团伙.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 2 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		char opt;
		int p,q;
		cin >> opt >> p >> q;
		if (opt == 'F') Union(p,q);
		else {Union(q + n,p); Union(p + n,q);}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += (f[i] == i);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\6 并查集进阶\家庭作业.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		cin >> a[i].t >> a[i].s;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.s > y.s;});
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int s = a[i].s,t = a[i].t;
		for (int j = find(t);j >= 1;j = find(j)) {
			ans += s;
			f[j] = find(j - 1);
			break;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\6 并查集进阶\疯狂的馒头.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(); cout.tie();
	int n,m,p,q;
	cin >> n >> m >> p >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n + 1;i++) f[i] = i;
	for (int i = m;i >= 1;i--) {
		int a = (i * p + q) % n + 1,b = (i * q + p) % n + 1;
		if (a > b) swap(a,b);
		for (int j = find(a);j <= b;j = find(j)) {
			if (!c[j]) c[j] = i;
			f[j] = find(j + 1);
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << c[i] << '\n';
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\6 并查集进阶\食物链.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k,ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 3 * n;i++) f[i] = i;
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        int op,x,y;
        cin >> op >> x >> y;
        if (x > n || y > n) {
            ans++;
            continue;
        }
        if (op == 1) {
            if (find(x) == find(y + n) || find(y) == find(x + n)) ans++;
            else {Union(x,y); Union(x + n,y + n); Union(x + 2 * n,y + 2 * n);}
        } else {
            if (find(x) == find(y) || find(y) == find(x + 2 * n)) ans++;
            else {Union(x,y + 2 * n); Union(x + n,y); Union(x + 2 * n,y + n);}
        }
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\堆方块.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {
    if (f[x] == x) return x;
    int root = find(f[x]);
    s[x] += s[f[x]];
    return f[x] = root;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\堆方块.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx == fy) return ;
    s[fx] += siz[fy],siz[fy] += siz[fx],f[fx] = fy;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\堆方块.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int p;
    cin >> p;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30000;i++) f[i] = i,siz[i] = 1;
    while (p--) {
        char op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == 'M') {
            int x,y;
            cin >> x >> y;
            Union(x,y);
        } else {
            int x;
            cin >> x;
            find(x);
            cout << s[x] << endl;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\奇偶游戏.cpp: get ---
int get(int x) {
    if (mp.count(x)) return mp[x];
    return mp[x] = ++cnt;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\奇偶游戏.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {
    if (f[x] == x) return x;
    int root = find(f[x]);
    d[x] ^= d[f[x]];
    return f[x] = root;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\奇偶游戏.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y,int s) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx == fy) return ;
    d[fx] = d[y] ^ d[x] ^ s,f[fx] = fy;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\奇偶游戏.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 1e5;i++) f[i] = i;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int l,r;
        string op;
        cin >> l >> r >> op;
        int x = get(l - 1),y = get(r);
        bool s = (op == "odd");
        if (find(x) != find(y)) Union(x,y,s);
        else if ((d[x] ^ d[y]) != s) {
            cout << i - 1 << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    cout << m << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\推导部分和.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {
    if (f[x] == x) return x;
    int root = find(f[x]);
    d[x] += d[f[x]];
    return f[x] = root;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\推导部分和.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y,long long s) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx == fy) return ;
    f[fx] = fy,d[fx] = d[y] - d[x] - s;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\推导部分和.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m,q;
    cin >> n >> m >> q;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
    while (m--) {
        int l,r;
        long long s;
        cin >> l >> r >> s;
        Union(l - 1,r,s);
    }
    while (q--) {
        int l,r;
        cin >> l >> r;
        if (find(l - 1) == find(r)) cout << d[r] - d[l - 1] << endl;
        else cout << "UNKNOWN" << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\职业确认.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {
    if (f[x] == x) return x;
    int root = find(f[x]);
    d[x] = (d[x] + d[f[x]]) % 4;
    return f[x] = root;
}

--- S1-2\7 带权并查集\职业确认.cpp: Union ---
void Union (int x,int y,int s) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx == fy) return ;
    if (fx == 0) f[fy] = 0,d[fy] = (d[x] - s - d[y] + 8) % 4;
    else f[fx] = fy,d[fx] = (s + d[y] - d[x] + 4) % 4;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\【模板】最小生成树kruskal.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\【模板】最小生成树kruskal.cpp: kruskal ---
int kruskal() {
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x = a[i].x,y = a[i].y,z = a[i].z;
        if (find(x) == find(y)) continue;
        ans += z;
        Union(x,y);
    }
    return ans;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\【模板】最小生成树kruskal.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y >> a[i].z;
    sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y){return x.z < y.z;});
    int ans = kruskal();
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (f[i] == i) cnt++;
    if (cnt > 1) cout << "orz" << endl;
    else cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\【模板】最小生成树prim.cpp: prim ---
void prim() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
    priority_queue<Node> q;
    q.push({1,0});
    dis[1] = 0;
    cnt = 0,ans = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.top().v,d = q.top().w; q.pop();
        if (vis[u]) continue;
        vis[u] = true;
        ans += d,cnt++;
        for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
            if (dis[v] <= w) continue;
            dis[v] = w;
            q.push({v,dis[v]});
        }
    }
    return ;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\【模板】最小生成树prim.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x,y,z;
        cin >> x >> y >> z;
        G[x].push_back({y,z}); G[y].push_back({x,z});
    }
    prim();
    if (cnt != n) cout << "orz" << endl;
    else cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\北极通讯网络.cpp: kruskal ---
double kruskal() {
	if (n == k) return 0;
    double ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) {
        int u = a[i].u,v = a[i].v;
        double w = a[i].w;
        if (find(u) == find(v)) continue;
        Union(u,v);
        ans = w;
        if (++cnt == n - k) break;
    }
    return ans;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\北极通讯网络.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> x[i] >> y[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
            a[++cur] = {i,j,sqrt((x[i] - x[j]) * (x[i] - x[j]) + (y[i] - y[j]) * (y[i] - y[j]))};
    sort(a + 1,a + cur + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.w < y.w;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
    printf("%.2lf",kruskal());
    return 0;
}

    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        int u = a[i].i,v = a[i].j,w = a[i].m;
        if (find(u) == find(v)) continue;

        Union(u,v);
    }

}

        sum += a[i].m;
    }
    sort(a + 1,a + k + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.m < y.m;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
    long long ans = kruskal();
    cout << sum - ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\灌溉农田.cpp: distance ---
int distance(int a,int b) {return (x[a] - x[b]) * (x[a] - x[b]) + (y[a] - y[b]) * (y[a] - y[b]);}

--- S1-2\8 最小生成树\灌溉农田.cpp: prim ---
void prim() {
    memset(dis,0x3f,sizeof dis);
    memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
    dis[1] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int u = 0;
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++)
            if (!vis[j] && dis[j] < dis[u]) u = j;
        vis[u] = true;
        if (u != 0) cnt++;
        ans += dis[u];
        for (int y = 1;y <= n;y++) {
            int d = distance(x,y);
            if (d < c) continue;
        }
    }
}

    prim();
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\安慰奶牛.cpp: kruskal ---
int kruskal() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= p;i++) {
		int fx = find(a[i].s),fy = find(a[i].e);
		if (fx == fy) continue;
		Union(fx,fy);
		ans += a[i].l;
	}
	return ans;
}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\安慰奶牛.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int minn = 1e9;
	cin >> n >> p;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> c[i];
		minn = min(minn,c[i]);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= p;i++) {
		cin >> a[i].s >> a[i].e >> a[i].l;
		a[i].l = c[a[i].s] + c[a[i].e] + 2 * a[i].l;
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + p + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.l < y.l;});
	cout << kruskal() + minn << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\构造完全图.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? f[x] : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\构造完全图.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
	int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
	if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy,siz[fy] += siz[fx];
}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\构造完全图.cpp: kruskal ---
long long kruskal() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i,siz[i] = 1;
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int fx = find(a[i].u),fy = find(a[i].v);
		if (fx == fy) continue;
		ans += 1ll * (a[i].w + 1) * siz[fx] * siz[fy] - 1;
		Union(fx,fy);
	}
	return ans;
}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\构造完全图.cpp: main ---

	sort(a + 1,a + n,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.w < y.w;});
	cout << kruskal() << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\9 最小生成树进阶\道路修复.cpp: kruskal ---
long long kruskal() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n + k;i++) f[i] = i;
	long long ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) {
		int fx = find(a[i].u),fy = find(a[i].v);
		if (fx == fy) continue;
		ans += a[i].w;
		Union(a[i].u,a[i].v);
	}
	return ans;
}

		cin >> c[i + n];
		for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
			int x;
			cin >> x;
			a[++cur] = {n + i,j,x};
		}
	}
	sort(a + 1,a + cur + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.w < y.w;});
	cout << kruskal() << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\Blockade.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x) {
	dfn[x] = low[x] = ++idx;
	siz[x] = 1;
	bool flag = false;
	int child = 0,ss = n - 1;
	for (auto y : G[x]) {
		if (!dfn[y]) {
			tarjan(y);
			child++;
			siz[x] += siz[y];
			low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
			if (low[y] >= dfn[x]) flag = true,ans[x] += 1ll * siz[y] * (n - siz[y]),ss -= siz[y];
		}
		else low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
	}
	if (x == 1 && child < 2) flag = false;
	if (!flag) ans[x] = 2 * (n - 1);
	else ans[x] += n - 1 + 1ll * ss * (n - ss);
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\Blockade.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		G[a].push_back(b);
		G[b].push_back(a);
	}
	tarjan(1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\分离的路径.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x,int pre) {
	dfn[x] = low[x] = ++idx;
    st[++top] = x;
    for (auto [y,i] : G[x]) {
        if (i == pre) continue;
        if (!dfn[y]) {
            tarjan(y,i);
            low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
            if (low[y] > dfn[x]) bridge[i] = true;
        }
        else low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
    }
    if (low[x] == dfn[x]) {
        int y;
        cnt_ecc++;
        do {
            y = st[top--];
            id[y] = cnt_ecc;
        } while (x != y);
    }
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\分离的路径.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int f,r,ans = 0;
	cin >> f >> r;
	for (int i = 1;i <= r;i++) {
		int u,v;
		cin >> u >> v;
		G[u].push_back({v,i});
		G[v].push_back({u,i});
	}
    tarjan(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= f;i++)
        for (auto [j,t] : G[i]) {
            int x = id[i],y = id[j];
            if (x != y) d[x]++;
        }
    for (int i = 1;i <= cnt_ecc;i++)
        if (d[i] == 1) ans++;
    cout << (ans + 1) / 2 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\旅游航道.cpp: init ---
void init() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++) G[i].clear();
	memset(dfn,0,sizeof dfn);
	memset(low,0,sizeof low);
	idx = 0,ans = 0;
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\旅游航道.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x,int pre) { // 从pre这条边走到x
	dfn[x] = low[x] = ++idx;
	for (auto y : G[x]) {
		if (y == pre) continue;
		if (!dfn[y]) {
			tarjan(y,x);
			low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
			if (low[y] > dfn[x]) ans++;
		}
		else low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
	}
}

	}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\网络.cpp: init ---
void init() {
	for (int i = 1;i < N;i++) G[i].clear();
	memset(dfn,0,sizeof dfn);
	memset(low,0,sizeof low);
	idx = 0,ans = 0;
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\网络.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x) {
	dfn[x] = low[x] = ++idx;
	bool flag = false;
	int child = 0;
	for (auto y : G[x]) {
		if (!dfn[y]) {
			child++;
			tarjan(y);
			low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
			if (low[y] >= dfn[x]) flag = true;
		}
		else low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
	}
	if (x == 1 && child < 2) flag = false;
	if (flag) ans++;
}

--- S1-2\=10 割点与桥\网络.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	while (true) {
		int n;
		cin >> n;
		if (n == 0) break;
		init();
		while (true) {
			int x;
			cin >> x;
			if (x == 0) break;
			while (true) {
				int y;
				cin >> y;
				G[x].push_back(y);
				G[y].push_back(x);
				if (getchar() == '\n') break;
			}
		}
		tarjan(1);
		cout << ans << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=11 表达式求值\计算中缀表达式.cpp: id ---
int id (char c) {
	if (c == '+' || c == '-') return 1;
	if (c == '*' || c == '/') return 2;
	if (c == '^') return 3;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=11 表达式求值\计算中缀表达式.cpp: cal ---
int cal(int x,int y,char c) {
	if (c == '+') return x + y;
	if (c == '-') return x - y;
	if (c == '*') return x * y;
	if (c == '/') return x / y;
	if (c == '^') return pow(x,y);
}

--- S1-2\=11 表达式求值\计算中缀表达式.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	s = '(' + s + ')';
	for (int i = 0;i < s.size();i++) {
		if ('0' <= s[i] && s[i] <= '9') {
			int x = 0;
			while ('0' <= s[i] && s[i] <= '9') {
				x = x * 10 + s[i] - '0';
				i++;
			}
			num[++top1] = x,i--;
		}
		else if (s[i] == '(') op[++top2] = s[i];
		else if (s[i] == ')') {
			while (op[top2] != '(') {
				int y = num[top1--],x = num[top1--];
				char c = op[top2--];
				num[++top1] = cal(x,y,c);
			}
			top2--;
		}
		else {
			while (top2 > 0 && id(op[top2]) >= id(s[i])) {
                if (s[i] == '^') break;
				int y = num[top1--],x = num[top1--];
				char c = op[top2--];
				num[++top1] = cal(x,y,c);
			}
			op[++top2] = s[i];
		}
	}
	cout << num[top1] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=11 表达式求值\逻辑表达式.cpp: id ---
int id (char c) {
	if (c == '|') return 1;
	if (c == '&') return 2;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=11 表达式求值\逻辑表达式.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    string s;
	cin >> s;
	s = '(' + s + ')';
	for (int i = 0;i < s.size();i++) {
		if (s[i] == '0' || s[i] == '1') num[++top1] = Node{s[i] - '0',0,0};
		else if (s[i] == '(') op[++top2] = s[i];
		else if (s[i] == ')') {
			while (op[top2] != '(') {
				Node y = num[top1--],x = num[top1--];
				char c = op[top2--];
				if (c == '|') num[++top1] = x | y;
				else num[++top1] = x & y;
			}
			top2--;
		} else {
			while (top2 > 0 && id(op[top2]) >= id(s[i])) {
				Node y = num[top1--],x = num[top1--];
				char c = op[top2--];
				if (c == '|') num[++top1] = x | y;
				else num[++top1] = x & y;
			}
			op[++top2] = s[i];
		}
	}
	cout << num[1].d << endl << num[1].c1 << ' ' << num[1].c2 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\切除线段.cpp: link ---
void link(int x,int y) {R[x] = y, L[y] = x;}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\切除线段.cpp: Delete ---
void Delete(int x) {link(L[x],R[x]);}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\切除线段.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	int sz = 0,cnt = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n + 1;i++) {
		if (a[i] == a[i - 1]) cnt++;
		else sz++, e[sz].num = a[i - 1], e[sz].cnt = cnt, e[sz].id = sz, cnt = 1;
	}
	head = 0,tail = sz + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= sz;i++) {
		link(i - 1,i);
		q.push(e[i]);
	}
	link(sz,tail);
	while (R[head] != tail)
	{
		int x = q.top().id;
		q.pop();
		while (vis[x]) {x = q.top().id; q.pop();}
		Delete(x);
		vis[x] = true;
		if (L[x] != head && e[L[x]].num == e[R[x]].num) {
			e[L[x]].cnt += e[R[x]].cnt;
			Delete(R[x]);
			vis[R[x]] = true;
			q.push(e[L[x]]);
		}
		ans++;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\双向链表.cpp: link ---
void link(int x,int y) {R[x] = y, L[y] = x;}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\双向链表.cpp: insertL ---
void insertL(int x,int y) {link(L[x],y); link(y,x);}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\双向链表.cpp: insertR ---
void insertR(int x,int y) {link(y,R[x]); link(x,y);}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\双向链表.cpp: Delete ---
void Delete(int x) {link(L[x],R[x]);}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\双向链表.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	string op;
	int k,x;
	cin >> op;
	if (op == "L") {
		cin >> x;
		num[++cur] = x;
		insertR(head,cur);
	}
	else if (op == "R") {
		cin >> x;
		num[++cur] = x;
        insertL(tail,cur);
	}
	else if (op == "D") {
		cin >> k;
		Delete(k);
	}
	else if (op == "IL") {
		cin >> k >> x;
		num[++cur] = x;
        insertL(k,cur);
	}
    else {
        cin >> k >> x;
        num[++cur] = x;
        insertR(k,cur);
    }
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\双向链表.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int m;
	cin >> m;
    head = 0,tail = m + 1;
    link(head,tail);
	while (m--) solve();
    for (int i = R[head];i != tail;i = R[i]) cout << num[i] << ' ';
    cout << endl;
	return 0;
}
