--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: find ---
int find(int k) {
	int x = head;
	while (k--) x = R[x];
	return x;
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: newup ---
void newup(int k) {
	int x = find(k);
	n++;
	insertL(x,n);
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: newdown ---
void newdown(int k) {
	newup(k + 1);
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: topdown ---
void topdown(int k) {
	int x = find(k);
	link(L[tail],R[head]);
	link(head,R[x]);
	link(x,tail);
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: bottomup ---
void bottomup(int k) {
	topdown(n - k);
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: middleup ---
void middleup(int k1, int k2) {
	int x = find(k1),y = find(k2);
	link(L[x],R[y]);
	link(y,R[head]);
	link(head,x);
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: middledown ---
void middledown(int k1,int k2) {
	int x = find(k1),y = find(k2);
	link(L[x],R[y]);
	link(L[tail],x);
	link(y,tail);
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	string op;
	int x,y;
	cin >> op;
	if (op == "NEWUP") {
		cin >> x;
		newup(x);
	}
	else if (op == "NEWDOWN") {
		cin >> x;
		newdown(x);
	}
	else if (op == "TOPDOWN") {
		cin >> x;
		topdown(x);
	}
	else if (op == "BOTTOMUP") {
		cin >> x;
		bottomup(x);
	}
	else if (op == "MIDDLEUP") {
		cin >> x >> y;
		middleup(x,y);
	}
	else if (op == "MIDDLEDOWN") {
		cin >> x >> y;
		middledown(x,y);
	}
}

--- S1-2\=12 链表进阶一\花式切牌.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	head = 0,tail = 5001;
	link(head,1);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) link(i - 1,i);
	link(n,tail);
	while (m--) solve();
	for (int i = R[head];i != tail;i = R[i]) cout << i << ' ';
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\家庭作业-反悔贪心.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {return x.t < y.t;}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\家庭作业-反悔贪心.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 0;
	scanf("%d",&n);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) scanf("%d%d",&a[i].t,&a[i].s);
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		if (pq.size() < a[i].t) {
			pq.push(a[i].s);
			ans += a[i].s;
		}
		else if (a[i].s > pq.top()) {
			ans -= pq.top();
			pq.pop();
			pq.push(a[i].s);
			ans += a[i].s;
		}
	}
	printf("%d\n",ans);
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\家庭作业-并查集.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {return x.s > y.s;}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\家庭作业-并查集.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {
	if (f[x] == x) return x;
	return f[x] = find(f[x]);
}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\家庭作业-并查集.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].t >> a[i].s;
        m = max(m,a[i].t);
    }
    sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) f[i] = i;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int pos = find(a[i].t);
        if (pos == 0) continue;
        ans += a[i].s;
        f[pos] = pos - 1;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\疯狂的馒头.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {
	if (f[x] == x) return x;
	return f[x] = find(f[x]);
}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\疯狂的馒头.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,p,q;
	cin >> n >> m >> p >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n + 1;i++) f[i] = i;
	for (int i = m;i >= 1;i--) {
		int l = (i * p + q) % n + 1,r = (i * q + p) % n + 1;
		if (l > r) swap(l,r);
		for (int j = find(l);j <= r;j = find(j + 1)) {
			color[j] = i;
			f[j] = j + 1;
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << color[i] << '\n';
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\邻值查找.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {return x.num < y.num;}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\邻值查找.cpp: link ---
void link(int x,int y) {R[x] = y, L[y] = x;}

--- S1-2\=13 链表进阶二\邻值查找.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> a[i]; p[i].num = a[i], p[i].id = i;}
    sort(p + 1,p + n + 1,cmp);
    int head = 0,tail = n + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) link(p[i - 1].id,p[i].id);
    link(p[n].id,tail);
    for (int i = n;i >= 2;i--) {
    	int x = a[L[i]],y = a[R[i]];
    	x = abs(x - a[i]),y = abs(y - a[i]);
    	if (L[i] == head) x = 2e9;
    	if (R[i] == tail) y = 2e9;
    	if (x <= y) ans[i] = L[i];
    	else ans[i] = R[i];
    	link(L[i],R[i]);
    }
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) cout << abs(a[i] - a[ans[i]]) << ' ' << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\信息传递.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x) {
	dfn[x] = low[x] = ++inx;
	st[++top] = x,vis[x] = true;
	for (auto y : G[x]) {
		if (!dfn[y]) {
			tarjan(y);
			low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
		}
		else if (vis[y]) low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
	}
	if (dfn[x] == low[x]) {
		cnt_scc++;
		int y;
		do {
			y = st[top--];
			vis[y] = false;
			id[y] = cnt_scc;
			siz[cnt_scc]++;
		} while (x != y);
	}
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\信息传递.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 1e9;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> t[i];
		G[i].push_back(t[i]);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (!dfn[i]) tarjan(i);
	for (int i = 1;i <= cnt_scc;i++) 
		if (siz[i] > 1) ans = min(ans,siz[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\抢掠计划.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x) {
	dfn[x] = low[x] = ++inx;
	st[++top] = x,vis[x] = true;
	for (auto y : G[x]) {
		if (!dfn[y]) {
			tarjan(y);
			low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
		}
		else if (vis[y]) low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
	}
	if (dfn[x] == low[x]) {
		cnt_scc++;
		int y;
		do {
			y = st[top--];
			vis[y] = false;
			id[y] = cnt_scc;
			siz[cnt_scc]++;
			money[cnt_scc] += a[y];
		} while (x != y);
	}
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\抢掠计划.cpp: spfa ---
void spfa(int s) {
	queue<int> q;
	memset(vis,false,sizeof vis);
	dis[s] = money[s];
	q.push(s);
	vis[s] = true;
	while (q.size()) {
		int x = q.front();q.pop();
		vis[x] = false;
		for (auto y : G2[x]) {
			if (dis[y] >= dis[x] + money[y]) continue;
			dis[y] = dis[x] + money[y];
			if (!vis[y]) {
				q.push(y);
				vis[y] = true;
			}
		}
	}
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\抢掠计划.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,s,p;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		G[x].push_back(y);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (!dfn[i]) tarjan(i);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (auto j : G[i]) {
			int x = id[i],y = id[j];
			if (x != y) G2[x].push_back(y);
		}
	cin >> s >> p;
	for (int i = 1;i <= p;i++) {
		int x;
		cin >> x;
		flag[id[x]] = true;
	}
	spfa(id[s]);
	int ans = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= cnt_scc;i++)
		if (flag[i]) ans = max(ans,dis[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

	for (auto y : G[x]) {
		if (!dfn[y]) {
			tarjan(y);
			low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
		}
		else if (vis[y]) low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
	}
	if (dfn[x] == low[x]) {
		cnt_scc++;
		int y;
		do {
			y = st[top--];

			siz[cnt_scc]++;

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\校园网.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans1 = 0,ans2 = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int m;
		while (true) {
			cin >> m;
			if (m == 0) break;
			else G[i].push_back(m);
		}
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (!dfn[i]) tarjan(i);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (auto j : G[i]) {
			int x = id[i],y = id[j];
			long long hash = 1ll * x * N + y;
			if (x != y && s.count(hash) == 0) {
				in[y]++,out[x]++;
				s.insert(hash);
			}
		}
	for (int i = 1;i <= cnt_scc;i++) {
		if (in[i] == 0) ans1++;
		if (out[i] == 0) ans2++;
	}
	cout << ans1 << endl;
	if (cnt_scc == 1) cout << 0 << endl;
	else cout << max(ans1,ans2) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\消息扩散.cpp: tarjan ---
void tarjan(int x) {
    dfn[x] = low[x] = ++idx;
    st[++top] = x;
    vis[x] = true;
    for (auto y : G[x]) {
        if (!dfn[y]) {
            tarjan(y);
            low[x] = min(low[x],low[y]);
        }
        else if (vis[y]) low[x] = min(low[x],dfn[y]);
    }
    if (dfn[x] == low[x]) {
        cnt_scc++;
        int y;
        do {
            y = st[top--];
            vis[y] = false;
            id[y] = cnt_scc;
            siz[cnt_scc]++;
        } while (x != y);
    }
}

--- S1-2\=9 强联通分量\消息扩散.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int b,e;
		cin >> b >> e;
		G[b].push_back(e);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (!dfn[i]) tarjan(i);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (auto j : G[i]) if (id[i] != id[j]) in[id[j]]++;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cnt_scc;i++)
        if (in[i] == 0) ans++;
    cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\Balanced-Lineup-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,q;
	cin >> n >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) stmax[i][0] = stmin[i][0] = h[i];
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
			stmax[i][j] = max(stmax[i][j - 1],stmax[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]),
			stmin[i][j] = min(stmin[i][j - 1],stmin[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
	while (q--) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		int k = lg[b - a + 1];
		cout << max(stmax[a][k],stmax[b - (1 << k) + 1][k]) - min(stmin[a][k],stmin[b - (1 << k) + 1][k]) << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\【模板】ST表.cpp: read ---
int read()
{
	int x = 0,f = 1; char ch = getchar();
	while (ch < '0' || ch > '9') {if (ch == '-') f = -1; ch = getchar(); }
	while (ch >= '0' && ch <= '9') {x = x * 10 + ch - 48; ch = getchar(); }
	return x * f;
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\【模板】ST表.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	n = read(), m = read();
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) a[i] = read();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = a[i];
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
			st[i][j] = max(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
	while (m--) {
		int l,r;
		l = read(),r = read();
		int k = lg[r - l + 1];
		printf("%d\n",max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]));
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\忠诚.cpp: main ---
int main() {

	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= m;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) st[i][0] = a[i];
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[m];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= m;i++)
			st[i][j] = min(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
	while (n--) {
		int l,r;
		cin >> l >> r;
		int k = lg[r - l + 1];

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\最大数.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	return max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\最大数.cpp: insert ---
void insert() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	n = (n + t) % d,a[++len] = n,st[len][0] = a[len];
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[len];j++)
		st[len - (1 << j) + 1][j] = max(query(len - (1 << j) + 1,len - 1),a[len]);
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\最大数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> m >> d;
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= m;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	while (m--) {
		char op;
		cin >> op;
		if (op == 'Q') {
            int l;
            cin >> l;
            t = query(len - l + 1,len);
            cout << t << endl;
        }
		else insert();
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\1 RMQ与ST表1\特别忠诚.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int m,n;
	cin >> m >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 2;i <= m;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) st[i][0] = a[i],stid[i][0] = i;
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[m];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= m;i++) {
			st[i][j] = min(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
			if (st[i][j] == st[i][j - 1]) stid[i][j] = stid[i][j - 1];
			else stid[i][j] = stid[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1];
		}
	while (n--) {
		int l,r;
		cin >> l >> r;
		int k = lg[r - l + 1];
		cout << ((st[l][k] <= st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]) ? stid[l][k] : stid[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]) << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\2 RMQ与ST表2\与众不同.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
    int k = lg[r - l + 1];
    return max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- S2-1\2 RMQ与ST表2\与众不同.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        s[i] = max(s[i - 1],mp[a[i]] + 1);
        f[i] = i - s[i] + 1;
        mp[a[i]] = i;
    }
    lg[1] = 0;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = f[i];
    for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
        for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
            st[i][j] = max(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
    while (m--) {
        int l,r;
        cin >> l >> r;
        l++,r++;
        int p = lower_bound(s + l,s + r + 1,l) - s;
        if (p > r) cout << r - l + 1 << endl;
        else cout << max(p - l,query(p,r)) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-1\2 RMQ与ST表2\维护gcd.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	return __gcd(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- S2-1\2 RMQ与ST表2\维护gcd.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,q;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = a[i];
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
			st[i][j] = __gcd(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int j = i;
		while (j <= n) {
			int t = query(i,j);
			int L = j,R = n,pos = -1;
			while (L <= R) {
				int mid = L + R >> 1;
				if (query(i,mid) == t) pos = mid,L = mid + 1;
				else R = mid - 1;
			}
			mp[t] += pos - j + 1;
			j = pos + 1;
		}
	}
	cin >> q;
	while (q--) {
		int x;
		cin >> x;
		cout << mp[x] << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\平面最近点对.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {return x.x < y.x;}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\平面最近点对.cpp: cald ---
double cald(double x1,double y1,double x2,double y2) {
	return (x2 - x1) * (x2 - x1) + (y2 - y1) * (y2 - y1);
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\平面最近点对.cpp: merge ---
void merge(int L,int R,int mid) {
	int i = L,j = mid + 1,cur = L;
	while (i <= mid && j <= R) {
		if (a[i].y < a[j].y) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
	while (j <= R) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\平面最近点对.cpp: merge_sort ---
double merge_sort(int L,int R) {
	if (L >= R) return 1e9;
	int mid = (L + R) >> 1,cur = 0;
	double midx = a[mid].x;
	double disL = merge_sort(L,mid),disR = merge_sort(mid + 1,R),dis = min(disL,disR);
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++)
		if ((a[i].x - midx) * (a[i].x - midx) <= dis) b[++cur] = a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= cur;j++)
			dis = min(dis,cald(b[i].x,b[i].y,b[j].x,b[j].y));
	merge(L,R,mid);
	return dis;
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\平面最近点对.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,cmp);
	printf("%.4lf\n",sqrt(merge_sort(1,n)));
	return 0;
}

void merge(int L,int R,int mid) {

		if (a[i].r < a[j].r) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
	while (j <= R) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\线段包含问题.cpp: merge_sort ---
void merge_sort(int L,int R) {
	if (L >= R) return ;
	int mid = (L + R) >> 1;
	merge_sort(L,mid); merge_sort(mid + 1,R); 
	for (int j = mid + 1,i = L;i <= mid;i++) {
		while (a[i].r >= a[j].r && j <= R) j++;
		ans[a[i].id] += j - mid - 1;
	}
	merge(L,R,mid);
}

		cin >> a[i].l >> a[i].r;
		a[i].id = i;
	}

	merge_sort(1,n);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\翻转对.cpp: merge ---
void merge(int L,int R,int mid) {
	int i = L,j = mid + 1,cur = L;
	while (i <= mid && j <= R) {
		if (num[i] < num[j]) tmp[cur++] = num[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = num[j++];
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = num[i++];
	while (j <= R) tmp[cur++] = num[j++];
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++) num[i] = tmp[i];
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\翻转对.cpp: merge_sort ---
long long merge_sort(int L,int R) {
	if (L >= R) return 0;
	int mid = (L + R) >> 1;
	long long cntL = merge_sort(L,mid),cntR = merge_sort(mid + 1,R),cnt = 0;
	for (int j = mid + 1,i = L;j <= R;j++) {
		while (2ll * num[j] >= num[i] && i <= mid) i++;
		cnt += mid - i + 1;
	}
	merge(L,R,mid);
	return cntL + cntR + cnt;
}

	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\逆序对.cpp: merge ---
void merge(int L,int R,int mid) {
	int i = L,j = mid + 1,cur = L;
	while (i <= mid && j <= R) {
		if (a[i] < a[j]) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
	while (j <= R) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
}

--- S2-1\3 分治法1\逆序对.cpp: merge_sort ---
long long merge_sort(int L,int R) {
	if (L >= R) return 0;
	int mid = (L + R) >> 1;
	long long cntL = merge_sort(L,mid),cntR = merge_sort(mid + 1,R);
	long long cnt = 0;
	for (int j = mid + 1,i = L;j <= R;j++) {
		while (a[j] >= a[i] && i <= mid) i++;
		cnt += mid - i + 1;
	}
	merge(L,R,mid);
	return cntL + cntR + cnt;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Minimum-Sum.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	int ret = (a[st[l][k]] < a[st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]]) ? st[l][k] : st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k];
	return ret;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Minimum-Sum.cpp: merge_sort ---
long long merge_sort(int L,int R) {
	if (L > R) return 0;
	int mid = query(L,R);
	long long cntL = merge_sort(L,mid - 1),cntR = merge_sort(mid + 1,R);
	long long cnt = 1ll * a[mid] * (mid - L + 1) * (R - mid + 1);
	return cntL + cntR + cnt;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Minimum-Sum.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = i;
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
			st[i][j] = (a[st[i][j - 1]] < a[st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]]) ? st[i][j - 1] : st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1];
	cout << merge_sort(1,n) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Power.cpp: query ---
int query(int L,int R) {
	int k = lg[R - L + 1];
	int ret = (v[st[L][k]] > v[st[R - (1 << k) + 1][k]]) ? st[L][k] : st[R - (1 << k) + 1][k];
	return ret;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Power.cpp: merge ---
long long merge(int L,int R,long long start,long long end) {
	if (L > R) return 0;
	int mid = query(L,R);
	long long emax = min(e,start + (mid - L) * r),emin = max(0ll,end - (R - mid + 1) * r);
	long long ans = (emax - emin) * v[mid] + merge(L,mid - 1,start,emax) + merge(mid + 1,R,min(emin + r,e),end);
	return ans;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Power.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	cin >> e >> r >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> v[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = i;
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
			st[i][j] = (v[st[i][j - 1]] > v[st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]]) ? st[i][j - 1] : st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1];
	cout << merge(1,n,e,0) << endl;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\Power.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= 500000;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\平面最近点对（加强版）.cpp: cmp ---
bool cmp(Node x,Node y) {return x.x < y.x;}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\平面最近点对（加强版）.cpp: cald ---
long long cald(int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
	return 1ll * (x2 - x1) * (x2 - x1) + 1ll * (y2 - y1) * (y2 - y1);
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\平面最近点对（加强版）.cpp: merge ---
void merge(int L,int R,int mid) {
	int i = L,j = mid + 1,cur = L;
	while (i <= mid && j <= R) {
		if (a[i].y < a[j].y) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
		else tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	}
	while (i <= mid) tmp[cur++] = a[i++];
	while (j <= R) tmp[cur++] = a[j++];
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++) a[i] = tmp[i];
}

--- S2-1\4 分治法2\平面最近点对（加强版）.cpp: merge_sort ---
long long merge_sort(int L,int R) {
	if (L >= R) return 1e18;
	int mid = (L + R) >> 1,cur = 0;
	int midx = a[mid].x;
	long long disL = merge_sort(L,mid),disR = merge_sort(mid + 1,R),dis = min(disL,disR);
	merge(L,R,mid);
	for (int i = L;i <= R;i++)
		if (1ll * (a[i].x - midx) * (a[i].x - midx) < dis) b[++cur] = a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= cur && j <= i + 7;j++) {
			// long long dy = a[i].y - a[j].y;
			// if (dy * dy > dis) break;
			dis = min(dis,cald(b[i].x,b[i].y,b[j].x,b[j].y));
		}
	return dis;
}

	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y;

	printf("%.4lf",sqrt(1.00 * merge_sort(1,n)));
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: copy ---
void copy(int a[N][N],int b[N][N]) {
	for (int i = 1;i <= 7;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++)
			a[i][j] = b[i][j];
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: check ---
bool check() {
	for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++)
		if (mp[1][j]) return false;
	return true;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: down ---
void down() {
	for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
		int b[10] = {},cnt = 0;
		for (int i = 1;i <= 7;i++)
			if (mp[i][j]) b[++cnt] = mp[i][j];
		for (int i = 1;i <= 7;i++) mp[i][j] = b[i];
 	}
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: remove ---
bool remove() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= 7;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
			if (!mp[i][j]) continue;
			int c =  mp[i][j];
			if (mp[i - 1][j] == c && mp[i + 1][j] == c)
				vis[i - 1][j] = vis[i][j] = vis[i + 1][j] = true;
			if (mp[i][j - 1] == c && mp[i][j + 1] == c)
				vis[i][j - 1] = vis[i][j] = vis[i][j + 1] = true;
		}
	bool flag = false;
	for (int i = 1;i <= 7;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++)
			if (vis[i][j]) mp[i][j] = vis[i][j] = 0,flag = true;
	return flag;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: move ---
void move(int i,int j,int x) {
	swap(mp[i][j],mp[i][j + x]);
	down();
	while (remove()) down();
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step) {
	if (flag) return ;
	if (check()) {
		flag = true;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i][1] << ' ' << ans[i][2] << ' ' << ans[i][3] << endl;
		return ;
	}
	if (step > n) return ;
	int tmp[N][N] = {};
	copy(tmp,mp);
	for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++)
		for (int i = 1;i <= 7;i++) {
			if (!mp[i][j]) break;
			if (j < 5 && mp[i][j] != mp[i][j + 1]) {
				// cout << i << ' ' << j << endl;
				ans[step][1] = j - 1,ans[step][2] = i - 1,ans[step][3] = 1;
				move(i,j,1);
				dfs(step + 1);
				copy(mp,tmp);
			}
			if (j > 1 && !mp[i][j - 1]) {
				ans[step][1] = j - 1,ans[step][2] = i - 1,ans[step][3] = -1;
				move(i,j,-1);
				dfs(step + 1);
				copy(mp,tmp);
			}
		}
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\Mayan游戏.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
		int x,cnt = 0;
		while (cin >> x) {
			if (x == 0) break;
			mp[++cnt][j] = x;
		}
	}
	dfs(1);
	if (!flag) cout << -1 << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\世界冰球锦标赛.cpp: dfsL ---
void dfsL(int step,long long sum) {
	if (sum > m) return ;
	if (step > n / 2) {
		L[++curL] = sum;
		return ;
	}
	dfsL(step + 1,sum + a[step]);
	dfsL(step + 1,sum);
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\世界冰球锦标赛.cpp: dfsR ---
void dfsR(int step,long long sum) {
	if (sum > m) return ;
	if (step > n) {
		R[++curR] = sum;
		return ;
	}
	dfsR(step + 1,sum + a[step]);
	dfsR(step + 1,sum);
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\世界冰球锦标赛.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	dfsL(1,0);
	dfsR(n / 2 + 1,0);
	sort(R + 1,R + curR + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= curL;i++)
		ans += (upper_bound(R + 1,R + curR + 1,m - L[i]) - R) - 1;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\方程的解数.cpp: qpow ---
int qpow(int a,int b) {
	int ans = 1;
	while (b) {
		if (b & 1) ans = ans * a;
		a = a * a;
		b >>= 1;
	}
	return ans;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\方程的解数.cpp: dfsL ---
void dfsL(int step,int sum) {
	if (step > n / 2) {
		L[++curL] = sum;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		dfsL(step + 1,sum + k[step] * qpow(i,p[step]));
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\方程的解数.cpp: dfsR ---
void dfsR(int step,int sum) {
	if (step > n) {
		R[++curR] = sum;
		return ;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
		dfsR(step + 1,sum + k[step] * qpow(i,p[step]));
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\方程的解数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> k[i] >> p[i];
	dfsL(1,0); dfsR(n / 2 + 1,0);
	sort(L + 1,L + curL + 1);
	sort(R + 1,R + curR + 1);
	for (int i = 1;i <= curL;i++) {
		int pos1 = upper_bound(R + 1,R + curR + 1,0 - L[i]) - R;
		int pos2 = lower_bound(R + 1,R + curR + 1,0 - L[i]) - R;
		int pos3 = upper_bound(L + 1,L + curL + 1,L[i]) - L;
		ans += (pos3 - i) * (pos1 - pos2);
		i = pos3 - 1;
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\魔法阵.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,int id,int maxn) {
	if (maxn > ans) return ;
	if (vis[x - 1][y] && vis[x + 1][y] && !vis[x][y - 1] && !vis[x][y + 1]) return ;
	if (!vis[x - 1][y] && !vis[x + 1][y] && vis[x][y - 1] && vis[x][y + 1]) return ;
	pos[id] = {x,y};
	if (id >= n * m / 2) {
		int i = id - n * m / 2;
		int t = k1 * abs(x - pos[i].x) + k2 * abs(y - pos[i].y);
		if (t > maxn) maxn = t;
	}
	if (id == n * m - 1) {
		ans = min(ans,maxn);
		return ;
	}
	vis[x][y] = true;
	for (int i = 0;i <= 3;i++) {
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		dfs(nx,ny,id + 1,maxn);
	}
	vis[x][y] = false;
}

--- S2-1\5 搜索优化进阶1\魔法阵.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m >> k1 >> k2;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) vis[i][0] = vis[i][m + 1] = 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) vis[0][i] = vis[n + 1][i] = 1;
	dfs(1,m,0,0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\反素数.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,int k,long long s,long long g) {
    if (s > n) return ;
    if (g > maxg || (g == maxg && s < ans)) maxg = g,ans = s;
    long long t = s;
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        t *= p[step];
        if (t > n) break;
        dfs(step + 1,i,t,g * (i + 1));
    }
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\反素数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    dfs(1,31,1,1);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\彩票.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,double sum,int cnt) {
	if (cnt == n) {
		if (abs(sum - p) < eps) ans++;
		return ;
	}
	if (step > m || cnt > n) return ;
	if (m - step + 1 < n - cnt) return ;
	if (sum + s[step + n - cnt - 1] - s[step - 1] < p - eps) return ;
	if (sum + s[m] - s[m - n + cnt] > p + eps) return ;
	dfs(step + 1,sum + 1.0 / step,cnt + 1);
	dfs(step + 1,sum,cnt);
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\彩票.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m >> x >> y;
	p = x * 1.0 / y;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) s[i] = s[i - 1] + 1.0 / i;
	dfs(1,0,0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\斗地主 加强版.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x) {
	if (x >= ans) return ;
	memset(c,0,sizeof c);
	for (int i = 2;i <= 14;i++) c[s[i]]++;
	ans = min(ans,x + dp[c[1] + s[0]][c[2]][c[3]][c[4]]);
	if (s[0] == 2) ans = min(ans,x + dp[c[1]][c[2]][c[3]][c[4]] + 1);
	for (int a = 1;a <= 3;a++)
		for (int i = 3;i <= 14;i++) {
			int cnt = 0;
			for (int j = i;j <= 14;j++) {
				if (s[j] >= a) cnt++;
				else break;
				if (a * cnt >= 5) {
					for (int k = i;k <= j;k++) s[k] -= a;
					dfs(x + 1);
					for (int k = i;k <= j;k++) s[k] += a;
				}
			}
		}
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\斗地主 加强版.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	memset(s,0,sizeof s);
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		s[a]++;
	}
	s[14] = s[1];
	ans = n;
	dfs(0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return ;
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\斗地主 加强版.cpp: main ---
int main(){
	cin >> T >> n;
	init();
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\生日蛋糕.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,int r,int h,int v,int s) {
	if (s >= ans) return ;
	if (step > m) {
		if (v == n)
			ans = min(ans,s);
		return ;
	}
	int vmax = v,vmin = v,k = 0;
	for (int i = step;i <= m;i++) {
		k++;
		vmax += (r - k) * (r - k) * (h - k);
		vmin += k * k * k;
	}
	if (vmax < n || vmin > n) return ;
	for (int i = r - 1;i >= m - step + 1;i--)
		for (int j = h - 1;j >= m - step + 1;j--)
			dfs(step + 1,i,j,v + i * i * j,s + 2 * i * j);
}

--- S2-1\6 搜索优化进阶2\生日蛋糕.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = sqrt(n);i >= m;i--)
		for (int j = n / (i * i);j >= m;j--)
			dfs(2,i,j,i * i * j,i * i + 2 * i * j);
	if (ans == 1e9) ans = 0;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\区间询问.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	return max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\区间询问.cpp: queryid ---
int queryid(int l,int r) { // 查最大值的下标
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	return (st[l][k] > st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]) ? stid[l][k] : stid[r - (1 << k) + 1][k];
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\区间询问.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	lg[0] = 1;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = a[i],stid[i][0] = i;
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++) {
			st[i][j] = max(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
			
			if (st[i][j] == st[i][j - 1]) stid[i][j] = stid[i][j - 1];
			else stid[i][j] = stid[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1];
		}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		int l,r,maxn,maxid,scmaxn;
		cin >> l >> r;
		maxn = query(l,r);
		maxid = queryid(l,r);
		if (maxid == 1) scmaxn = query(maxid + 1,r);
		else if (maxid == n) scmaxn =
		scmaxn = max(query(l,maxid - 1),query(maxid + 1,r));
		cout << maxn << ' ' << maxid << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\暴力\区间询问[30pts].cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	while (m--) {
		int l,r,maxn = 0,scmaxn = 0;
		cin >> l >> r;
		for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) maxn = max(maxn,a[i]);
		for (int i = l;i <= r;i++)
			if (a[i] != maxn) scmaxn = max(scmaxn,a[i]);
		cout << maxn << ' ' << scmaxn << '\n';
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\暴力\时钟刻度[30pts].cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,long long sum) {
	if (step > n) {
		ans = max(ans,sum % m);
		return ;
	}
	dfs(step + 1,(sum + a[step]) % m);
	dfs(step + 1,sum);
}

	dfs(1,0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\暴力\财富计算[40pts].cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
			ans += max(a[j] - a[i],0);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-1\7 阶段练习\暴力\频繁的数据[50pts].cpp: main ---
int main() {
	while (true) {
		int n,q;
		cin >> n;
		if (!n) break;
		cin >> q;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
		while (q--) {
			int l,r,ans = 0;
			cin >> l >> r;
			map<int,int> mp;
			for (int i = l;i <= r;i++) mp[a[i]]++,ans = max(ans,mp[a[i]]);
			cout << ans << endl;
		}
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\反对称串.cpp: check ---
bool check(int i,int mid) {
    int L = i - mid + 1,R = i + mid;
    int tL = n - R + 1,tR = n - L + 1;
    return hs[R] - hs[L - 1] * p[R - L + 1] == ht[tR] - ht[tL - 1] * p[tR - tL + 1];
}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\反对称串.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    string s,t = "";
    cin >> n >> s;
    for (int i = 0;i < s.size();i++) t += (s[i] == '0') ? '1' : '0';
    reverse(t.begin(),t.end());
    s = ' ' + s,t = ' ' + t;
    p[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * P;
        hs[i] = hs[i - 1] * P + s[i];
        ht[i] = ht[i - 1] * P + t[i];
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int L = 1,R = min(i,n - i),maxlen = 0;
        while (L <= R) {
            int mid = L + R >> 1;
            if (check(i,mid)) maxlen = mid,L = mid + 1;
            else R = mid - 1;
        }
        ans += maxlen;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\字符串匹配.cpp: Hash ---
long long Hash(int l,int r) {return (hs[r] - hs[l - 1] * p[r - l + 1] % mod + mod) % mod;}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\字符串匹配.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    string s,t;
    cin >> s >> t;
    int n = s.size(),m = t.size();
    s = ' ' + s,t = ' ' + t;
    p[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * P % mod;
        hs[i] = (hs[i - 1] * P + s[i]) % mod;
    }
    long long ht = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) ht = (ht * P + t[i]) % mod;
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i + m - 1 <= n;i++) {
        int x = Hash(i,i + m - 1);
        if (x == ht) ans++;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\最短循环节.cpp: Hash ---
long long Hash(int l,int r) {return (h[r] - h[l - 1] * p[r - l + 1] % mod + mod) % mod;}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\最短循环节.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,q;
    string s;
    cin >> n >> s >> q;
    s = ' ' + s;
    p[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * P % mod;
        h[i] = (h[i - 1] * P + s[i]) % mod;
    }
    while (q--) {
        int a,b,ans = 1e9;
        cin >> a >> b;
        for (int l = 1;l * l <= b - a + 1;l++) {
            if ((b - a + 1) % l != 0) continue;
            if (Hash(a,b - l) == Hash(l + a,b)) {
                ans = l;
                break;
            }
            if (Hash(a,b - (b - a + 1) / l) == Hash((b - a + 1) / l + a,b))
                ans = min(ans,(b - a + 1) / l);
        }
        cout << ans << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\最长回文子串.cpp: check1 ---
bool check1(int mid) {
    for (int i = mid + 1;i <= n - mid;i++) {
        int L = i - mid,R = i + mid;
        int tL = n - R + 1,tR = n - L + 1;
        if (hs[R] - hs[L - 1] *  p[R - L + 1] == ht[tR] - ht[tL - 1] * p[tR - tL + 1]) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

--- S2-2\1 hash法1\最长回文子串.cpp: check0 ---
bool check0(int mid) {
    for (int i = mid;i <= n - mid;i++) {
        int L = i - mid + 1,R = i + mid;
        int tL = n - R + 1,tR = n - L + 1;
        if (hs[R] - hs[L - 1] *  p[R - L + 1] == ht[tR] - ht[tL - 1] * p[tR - tL + 1]) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\A-and-B-and-Lecture-Rooms.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
    }
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\A-and-B-and-Lecture-Rooms.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i])
            u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\A-and-B-and-Lecture-Rooms.cpp: dis ---
int dis(int u,int v) {return dep[u] + dep[v] - 2 * dep[LCA(u,v)];}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\A-and-B-and-Lecture-Rooms.cpp: get_k_parent ---
int get_k_parent(int u,int k) {
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (k & (1 << i)) u = fa[u][i];
    return u;
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\A-and-B-and-Lecture-Rooms.cpp: solve ---
void solve(int x,int y) {
    int lca = LCA(x,y);
    int dist = dis(x,y);
    if (dist % 2 != 0) {
        cout << 0 << endl;
        return ;
    }
    if (dep[x] == dep[y]) {
        int xx = get_k_parent(x,dep[x] - dep[lca] - 1);
        int yy = get_k_parent(y,dep[y] - dep[lca] - 1);
        cout << n - siz[xx] - siz[yy] << endl;
    } else {
        if (dep[x] > dep[y]) swap(x,y);
        int yy = get_k_parent(y,dist / 2 - 1);
        int k =  get_k_parent(y,dist / 2);
        cout << siz[k] - siz[yy] << endl;
    }
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\A-and-B-and-Lecture-Rooms.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    cin >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        solve(x,y);
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\Misha,-Grisha-and-Underground.cpp: dis ---

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\Misha,-Grisha-and-Underground.cpp: func ---
int func(int s,int t,int f) {return (dis(s,f) + dis(t,f) - dis(s,t)) / 2 + 1;}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\城市设计.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\城市设计.cpp: LCA ---

}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\城市设计.cpp: dist ---
int dist(int u,int v) {return dis[u] + dis[v] - 2 * dis[LCA(u,v)];}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\城市设计.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n;
    while (cin >> n) {
        for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) G[i].clear();
        for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
            int a,b,l;
            cin >> a >> b >> l;
            a++,b++;
            G[a].push_back({b,l}); G[b].push_back({a,l});
        }
        dfs(1,0);
        int q;
        cin >> q;
        while (q--) {
            int x,y,z;
            cin >> x >> y >> z;
            x++,y++,z++;
            cout << (dist(x,y) + dist(y,z) + dist(x,z)) / 2 << '\n';
        }
        cout << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\点的距离.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\点的距离.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i]) u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- S2-2\10 LCA1\点的距离.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        G[x].push_back(y); G[y].push_back(x);
    }
    dep[1] = 1;
    dfs(1,0);
    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        cout << dep[x] + dep[y] - 2 * dep[LCA(x,y)] << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\树上询问.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
    }
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\树上询问.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i]) u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\树上询问.cpp: dis ---
int dis(int a,int b) {return dep[a] + dep[b] - 2 * dep[LCA(a,b)];}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\树上询问.cpp: C ---
int C(int c,int x) {
    if (c == x) return 0;
    if (LCA(c,x) == c) {
        for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
            if (dep[fa[x][i]] > dep[c]) x = fa[x][i];
        return siz[x];
    }
    else return n - siz[c];
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\树上询问.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    cin >> n >> q;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    dep[1] = 1;
    dfs(1,0);
    while (q--) {
        int a,b,c;
        cin >> a >> b >> c;
        if (dis(a,b) == dis(a,c) + dis(b,c)) cout << n - C(c,a) - C(c,b) << endl;
        else cout << 0 << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\货车运输.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\货车运输.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (fx != fy) f[fx] = fy;
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\货车运输.cpp: kruskal ---
void kruskal() {
    sort(a + 1,a + m + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.z > y.z;});
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) f[i] = i;
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x = a[i].x,y = a[i].y,z = a[i].z;
        if (find(x) == find(y)) continue;
        Union(x,y);
        G[x].push_back({y,z}); G[y].push_back({x,z});
        if (++cnt == n - 1) break;
    }
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\货车运输.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa,int W) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    minn[u][0] = W;
    vis[u] = true;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1],
        minn[u][i] = min(minn[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1],minn[u][i - 1]);
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u,w);
    }
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\货车运输.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    int ans = 1e9;
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v])
            ans = min(ans, minn[u][i]),u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return ans;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i])
            ans = min(ans,minn[u][i]),
            ans = min(ans,minn[v][i]),
            u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return min(ans,min(minn[u][0],minn[v][0]));
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\货车运输.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int x,y,z;
        cin >> x >> y >> z;
        a[i] = {x,y,z};
    }
    kruskal();
    dep[1] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (!vis[i]) dfs(i,0,1e9);
    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        if (find(x) != find(y)) cout << -1 << endl;
        else cout << LCA(x,y) << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\路径编号最小点.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
	dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    minn[u][0] = (pa == 0) ? u : min(u,pa);
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1],
        minn[u][i] = min(minn[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1],minn[u][i - 1]);
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
    	if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\路径编号最小点.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
	if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return v;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i])
            u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\路径编号最小点.cpp: query ---
int query(int a,int b) {
	int ans = min(a,b);
	if (dep[a] < dep[b]) swap(a,b);
	for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
		if (dep[fa[a][i]] >= dep[b]) ans = min(ans,minn[a][i]),a = fa[a][i];
	if (a == b) return ans;
	for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
		if (fa[a][i] != fa[b][i])
			ans = min(ans,minn[a][i]),
			ans = min(ans,minn[b][i]),
			a = fa[a][i],
			b = fa[b][i];
	return min(ans,min(minn[b][0],minn[a][0]));
}

--- S2-2\11 LCA2\路径编号最小点.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
	int n,m,s;
	cin >> n >> m >> s;
	for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		G[x].push_back(y); G[y].push_back(x);
	}
	dep[s] = 1;
	dfs(s,0);
	while (m--) {
		int a,b;
		cin >> a >> b;
		cout << query(a,b) << endl;
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\Max-Flow-P.cpp: add ---
void add(int u,int pa) {

    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        add(v,u);

    }
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\Max-Flow-P.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int x,y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        G[x].push_back(y); G[y].push_back(x);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= k;i++) {
        int s,t;
        cin >> s >> t;
        int lca = LCA(s,t);
        d[s] += 1,d[t] += 1,d[lca] -= 1,d[fa[lca][0]] -= 1;
    }
    add(1,0);
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,a[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m,ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        int lca = LCA(a,b);
        d[a]++,d[b]++,d[lca] -= 2;
    }
    add(1,0);
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        if (a[i] == 0) ans += m;
        if (a[i] == 1) ans++;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\直通路径.cpp: add ---
void add(int u,int pa,long long sum) {
    ans[u] = sum + d[u];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        add(v,u,ans[u]);
    }
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\直通路径.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i].u >> a[i].v;
        G[a[i].u].push_back(a[i].v); G[a[i].v].push_back(a[i].u);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int op,e,k;
        cin >> op >> e >> k;
        int u = a[e].u,v = a[e].v;
        int father,child;
        if (dep[u] > dep[v]) father = v,child = u;
        else father = u,child = v;
        if (op == 1)
            if (father == u) d[1] += k,d[v] -= k;
            else d[u] += k;
        else
            if (father == v) d[1] += k,d[u] -= k;
            else d[v] += k;
    }
    add(1,0,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\运输计划.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    dep[u] = dep[pa] + 1;
    fa[u][0] = pa;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 30;i++)
        fa[u][i] = fa[fa[u][i - 1]][i - 1];
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dis[v] = dis[u] + w;
        edge[v] = w;
        dfs(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\运输计划.cpp: LCA ---
int LCA(int u,int v) {
    if (dep[u] < dep[v]) swap(u,v);
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (dep[fa[u][i]] >= dep[v]) u = fa[u][i];
    if (u == v) return u;
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--)
        if (fa[u][i] != fa[v][i]) u = fa[u][i],v = fa[v][i];
    return fa[u][0];
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\运输计划.cpp: dist ---
int dist(int u,int v) {return dis[u] + dis[v] - 2 * dis[LCA(u,v)];}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\运输计划.cpp: add ---
void add(int u,int pa) {
    a[u] = d[u];
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        add(v,u);
        a[u] += a[v];
    }
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\运输计划.cpp: check ---
bool check(int mid) {
    memset(d,0,sizeof d);
    int cnt = 0,maxn = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++)
        if (path[i].dis > mid) {
            cnt++;
            d[path[i].u]++;
            d[path[i].v]++;
            d[path[i].lca] -= 2;
            maxn = max(maxn,path[i].dis);
        }
    if (cnt == 0) return true;
    memset(a,0,sizeof a);
    add(1,0);
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++)
        if (a[i] >= cnt && maxn - edge[i] <= mid) return true;
    return false;
}

--- S2-2\12 树上差分\运输计划.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b,t;
        cin >> a >> b >> t;
        G[a].push_back({b,t}); G[b].push_back({a,t});
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        cin >> path[i].u >> path[i].v;
        path[i].dis = dist(path[i].u,path[i].v),path[i].lca = LCA(path[i].u,path[i].v);
    }
    sort(path + 1,path + m + 1,[](Path x,Path y) {return x.dis < y.dis;});
    int L = 0,R = 3e8;
    long long ans = 0;
    while (L <= R) {
        int mid = (L + R) >> 1;
        if (check(mid)) R = mid - 1,ans = mid;
        else L = mid + 1;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\nearby-cows.cpp: dfs1 ---
void dfs1(int u,int pa) {
    dp[u][0] = c[u];
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs1(v,u);
        for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++)
            dp[u][j] += dp[v][j - 1];
    }
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\nearby-cows.cpp: dfs2 ---
void dfs2(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        int tmp[25] = {0};
        tmp[0] = dp[v][0];
        for (int j = 1;j <= k;j++) {
            tmp[j] = dp[v][j];
            int p = dp[u][j - 1];
            if (j >= 2)
                p -= dp[v][j - 2];
            tmp[j] += p;
        }
        for (int j = 0;j <= k;j++)
            dp[v][j] = tmp[j];
        dfs2(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\nearby-cows.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> c[i];
    dfs1(1,0);
    dfs2(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int j = 0;j <= k;j++) sum += dp[i][j];
        cout << sum << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\三色二叉树.cpp: build ---
int build() {
    if (cur >= s.size()) return 0;
    int u = ++id;
    char c = s[cur++];
    if (c == '0')
        L[u] = R[u] = 0;
    else if (c == '1') {
        L[u] = build();
        R[u] = 0;
    } else {
        L[u] = build();
        R[u] = build();
    }
    return u;
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\三色二叉树.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
    if (u == 0) return ;
    if (L[u]) dfs(L[u]);
    if (R[u]) dfs(R[u]);
    int lc = L[u],rc = R[u];
    if (L[u] == 0 && R[u] == 0) {
        dp[u][0] = 0,dp[u][1] = 1;
        dp2[u][0] = 0,dp2[u][1] = 1;
    } else if (R[u] == 0) {
        dp[u][0] = max(dp[lc][1],dp[lc][0]),dp[u][1] = 1 + dp[lc][0];
        dp2[u][0] = min(dp2[lc][1],dp2[lc][0]),dp2[u][1] = 1 + dp2[lc][0];
    } else {
        dp[u][1] = 1 + dp[lc][0] + dp[rc][0],dp[u][0] = max(dp[lc][1] + dp[rc][0],dp[lc][0] + dp[rc][1]);
        dp2[u][1] = 1 + dp2[lc][0] + dp2[rc][0],dp2[u][0] = min(dp2[lc][1] + dp2[rc][0],dp2[lc][0] + dp2[rc][1]);
    }
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\三色二叉树.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    memset(dp2,0x3f,sizeof dp2);
    cin >> s;
    int root = build();
    dfs(1);
    cout << max(dp[1][0],dp[1][1]) << ' ' << min(dp2[1][0],dp2[1][1]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\手机信号网络.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    bool flag = true;
    int minn = 1e9;
    dp[u][0] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        dp[u][0] += min(dp[v][0],min(dp[v][1],dp[v][2]));
        dp[u][1] += min(dp[v][0],dp[v][2]);
        dp[u][2] += min(dp[v][0],dp[v][2]);
        if (dp[v][0] <= dp[v][2]) flag = false;
        minn = min(minn,dp[v][0] - dp[v][2]);
    }
    if (flag) dp[u][2] += minn;
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\手机信号网络.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    cout << min(dp[1][0],dp[1][2]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\没有上司的舞会.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        dp[u][0] += max(dp[v][0],dp[v][1]);
        dp[u][1] += dp[v][0];
    }
    dp[u][1] += r[u];
}

--- S2-2\13 树型动规1\没有上司的舞会.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> r[i];
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int l,k;
        cin >> l >> k;
        G[l].push_back(k); G[k].push_back(l);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    cout << max(dp[1][0],dp[1][1]) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\Distributing-Integers.cpp: qpow ---
long long qpow(long long a,long long b,long long p) {
    long long ret = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) ret = ret * a % p;
        a = a * a % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return ret;
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\Distributing-Integers.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1,dp[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
        dp[u] *= dp[v] * qpow(fact[siz[v]],mod - 2,mod) % mod;
        dp[u] %= mod;
    }
    dp[u] = dp[u] * fact[siz[u] - 1] % mod;
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\Distributing-Integers.cpp: dfs2 ---
void dfs2(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        ans[v] = (ans[u] * siz[v]) % mod * qpow(n - siz[v],mod - 2,mod) % mod;
        dfs2(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\Distributing-Integers.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        G[a].push_back(b); G[b].push_back(a);
    }
    fact[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) fact[i] = fact[i - 1] * i % mod;
    dfs(1,0);
    ans[1] = dp[1];
    dfs2(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\Surgey-and-Subway.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dep[v] = dep[u] + 1;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
        if (dep[v] % 2 == 0) cnt2++;
        else cnt1++;
        ans1 += (siz[v]) * (n - siz[v]);
    }
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\Surgey-and-Subway.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int u,v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        G[u].push_back(v); G[v].push_back(u);
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    ans2 += cnt1 * cnt2;
    cout << (ans1 + ans2) / 2 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\市政服务大厅.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    siz[u] = p[u];
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
        dp[u] += dp[v] + 1ll * siz[v] * w;
    }
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\市政服务大厅.cpp: change ---
void change(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dp[v] = dp[u] + 1ll * (siz[1] - 2 * siz[v]) * w;
        change(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\市政服务大厅.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,id = 0;;
    long long ans = 1e18;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int a,b,l;
        cin >> a >> b >> l;
        G[a].push_back({b,l}); G[b].push_back({a,l});
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> p[i];
    dfs(1,0);
    change(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (ans > dp[i]) id = i,ans = dp[i];
    cout << id << endl << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\最长简单路径.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        if (dp1[v] + w > dp1[u]) {
            best[u] = v;
            dp2[u] = dp1[u];
            dp1[u] = dp1[v] + w;
        }
        else if (dp1[v] + w > dp2[u]) dp2[u] = dp1[v] + w;
    }
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\最长简单路径.cpp: dfs2 ---
void dfs2(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        if (v != best[u]) f[v] = max(f[u],dp1[u]) + w;
        else f[v] = max(f[u],dp2[u]) + w;
        dfs2(v,u);
    }
}

--- S2-2\14 树型动规2\最长简单路径.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) {
        int x,y,z;
        cin >> x >> y >> z;
        G[x].push_back({y,z}); G[y].push_back({x,z});
    }
    dfs(1,0);
    dfs2(1,0);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << dp1[i] + dp2[i] + f[i] - min(f[i],min(dp1[i],dp2[i])) << '\n';
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\Blue-Mary的战役地图.cpp: Hash ---
long long Hash(unsigned long long h[N][N],int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
    unsigned long long ret = h[x2][y2] - h[x1 - 1][y2] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] - h[x2][y1 - 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1]
     + h[x1 - 1][y1 - 1] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1];
     return ret;
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\Matrix-Matcher.cpp: Hash ---
long long Hash(int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
    long long ret = h[x2][y2] - h[x1 - 1][y2] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] % mod - h[x2][y1 - 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1] % mod
     + h[x1 - 1][y1 - 1] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] % mod * q[y2 - y1 + 1] % mod;
     return (ret % mod + mod) % mod;
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\Matrix-Matcher.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    p[0] = q[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= N;i++) p[i] = p[i - 1] * P % mod,q[i] = q[i - 1] * Q % mod;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\不重复数字.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int T;

	while (T--) {
		mp.clear();
		int n;
		cin >> n;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
			int x;
			cin >> x;
			if (!mp[x]) {cout << x << ' '; mp[x] = true;}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\内存杀手.cpp: Hash ---
long long Hash(unsigned long long h[N][N],int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
    return h[x2][y2] - h[x1 - 1][y2] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] - h[x2][y1 - 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1] + h[x1 - 1][y1 - 1] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1];
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\内存杀手.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
    return Hash(h,x1,y1,x2,y2) == Hash(h1,n - x2 + 1,m - y2 + 1,n - x1 + 1,m - y1 + 1);
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\对称的正方形.cpp: Hash ---
long long Hash(unsigned long long h[N][N],int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
    return h[x2][y2] - h[x1 - 1][y2] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] - h[x2][y1 - 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1] + h[x1 - 1][y1 - 1] * p[x2 - x1 + 1] * q[y2 - y1 + 1];
}

--- S2-2\2 hash法2\对称的正方形.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x1,int y1,int x2,int y2) {
    return Hash(h,x1,y1,x2,y2) == Hash(h1,n - x2 + 1,y1,n - x1 + 1,y2) && Hash(h,x1,y1,x2,y2) == Hash(h2,x1,m - y2 + 1,x2,m - y1 + 1);
}

	return ans;
}

--- S2-2\3 树状数组1\HH的项链.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	cin >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		cin >> s[i].l >> s[i].r;
		s[i].id = i;
	}
	sort(s + 1,s + m + 1,cmp);
	for (int i = 1,j = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		while (j <= s[i].r) {
			if (pos[a[j]]) change(pos[a[j]],-1);
			change(j,1);
			pos[a[j]] = j;
			j++;
		}
		ans[s[i].id] = sum(s[i].r) - sum(s[i].l - 1);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
	return 0;
}

}

--- S2-2\4 树状数组2\【模板】树状数组3.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        change(i,a[i] - a[i - 1],c1);
        change(i,(i - 1) * (a[i] - a[i - 1]),c2);
    }
    while (m--) {
        int op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == 1) {
            int x,y,k;
            cin >> x >> y >> k;
            change(x,k,c1); change(y + 1,-k,c1);
            change(x,1ll * x * k - k,c2); change(y + 1,1ll *-y * k,c2);
        } else {
            int x,y;
            cin >> x >> y;
            cout << query(y) - query(x - 1) << endl;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\4 树状数组2\回转寿司.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> l >> r;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i];
        ss[++cur] = s[i];
        ss[++cur] = s[i] - l;
        ss[++cur] = s[i] - r;
    }
    ss[++cur] = 0;
    sort(ss + 1,ss + cur + 1);
    m = unique(ss + 1,ss + cur + 1) - ss - 1;
    change(lower_bound(ss + 1,ss + m + 1,0) - ss,1);
    for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
        int L = lower_bound(ss + 1,ss + m + 1,s[j] - r) - ss;
        int R = lower_bound(ss + 1,ss + m + 1,s[j] - l) - ss;
        ans += sum(R) - sum(L - 1);
        change(lower_bound(ss + 1,ss + m + 1,s[j]) - ss,1);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\4 树状数组2\晋升计数.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u) {
    long long s = sum(m) - sum(p[u]);
    change(p[u],1);
    for (auto v : G[u]) dfs(v);
    ans[u] = sum(m) - sum(p[u]) - s;
}

--- S2-2\4 树状数组2\晋升计数.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> p[i];
        b[i] = p[i];
    }
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        int f;
        cin >> f;
        G[f].push_back(i);
    }
    sort(b + 1,b + n + 1);
    m = unique(p + 1,p + n + 1) - p - 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) p[i] = lower_bound(b + 1,b + m + 1,p[i]) - b; 
    dfs(1);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\4 树状数组2\理发.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        a[i]++;
        change(a[i],1);
        long long s = i - sum(a[i]);
        d[a[i]] += s;
    }
    for (int i = 1;i < n;i++) ans[i] = ans[i - 1] + d[i];
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\4 树状数组2\逆序对.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        b[i] = a[i];
    }
    sort(b + 1,b + n + 1);
    int m = unique(b + 1,b + n + 1) - b - 1;
    long long ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        a[i] = lower_bound(b + 1,b + m + 1,a[i]) - b;
        change(a[i],1);
        ans += i - sum(a[i]);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\5 双指针与尺取法\Cow-Lineup-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	int cnt = 0;
	for (int l = 1,r = 1;r <= n;r++) {
		if (++mp[b[r]] == 1) cnt++;
		while (cnt > k + 1 && mp[b[l]] > 0) {
			if (--mp[b[l]] == 0) cnt--;
			l++;
		}
		ans = max(ans,mp[b[r]]);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\5 双指针与尺取法\Cow-Lineup-S.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 1e9;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		int x,id;
		cin >> x >> id;
		a[i] = {x,id};
		vis[id] = true;
	}
	int k = vis.size(),cnt = 0;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.x < y.x;});
	for (int l = 1,r = 1;r <= n;r++) {
		if (++mp[a[r].id] == 1) cnt++;
		while (cnt == k && mp[a[l].id] > 1) mp[a[l].id]--,l++;
		if (cnt == k) ans = min(ans,a[r].x - a[l].x);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\5 双指针与尺取法\Haybale-Feast-G.cpp: query ---
int query(int l,int r) {
	int k = lg[r - l + 1];
	return max(st[l][k],st[r - (1 << k) + 1][k]);
}

--- S2-2\5 双指针与尺取法\Haybale-Feast-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,ans = 1e9;
	long long m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> f[i] >> s[i];
		sf[i] = sf[i - 1] + f[i];
	}
	lg[1] = 0;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) lg[i] = lg[i / 2] + 1;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) st[i][0] = s[i];
	for (int j = 1;j <= lg[n];j++)
		for (int i = 1;i + (1 << j) - 1 <= n;i++)
			st[i][j] = max(st[i][j - 1],st[i + (1 << j - 1)][j - 1]);
	for (int l = 1,r = 1;r <= n;r++)
		while (sf[r] - sf[l - 1] >= m) ans = min(ans,query(l,r)),l++;
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\5 双指针与尺取法\Perform-Easily.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> a[1] >> a[2] >> a[3] >> a[4] >> a[5] >> a[6];
	int n,ans = 1e9;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> b[i];
	int cur = 0;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= 6;j++)
			c[++cur] = {b[i] - a[j],i};
	sort(c + 1,c + n * 6 + 1,[](Node x,Node y){return x.a < y.a;});
	int cnt = 0;
	for (int l = 1,r = 1;r <= 6 * n;r++) {
		if (++mp[c[r].b] == 1) cnt++;
		while (cnt == n && mp[c[l].b] > 0) {
			ans = min(ans,c[r].a - c[l].a);
			if (--mp[c[l].b] == 0) cnt--;
			l++;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

bool check(char c) {return (c == 'a' || c == 'e' || c == 'i' || c == 'o' || c == 'u');}

--- S2-2\5 双指针与尺取法\哈布斯堡王朝.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,k;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> k;
	string s;
	cin >> s;
	int len = s.size(); s = '?' + s;
	for (int i = 2;i <= len;i++) {
		if (check(s[i - 1]))
			Lcnt[i] = Lcnt[i - 1] + 1;
		else Lcnt[i] = 0;
	}
	int sum = 0,cnt = 0;
	for (int l = 1,r = 1;r <= n;r++) {
		if (check(s[r])) {
			if (++mp[s[r]] == 1) cnt++;
			sum++;
		}
		while (cnt == 5 && r - l + 1 - sum > k) {
			if (check(s[l])) {
				sum--;
				if (--mp[s[l]] == 0) cnt--;
			}
			l++;
		}
		if (cnt == 5 && r - l + 1 - sum == k) {
			while (check(s[l]) && mp[s[l]] > 1) sum--,mp[s[l]]--,l++;
			ans += Lcnt[l] + 1;
		}
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

		while (!st.empty() && a[st.top()] <= a[i]) st.pop();

	}
    cout << ans << endl;

}

--- S2-2\6 单调栈\奶牛扔飞盘.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!stl.empty() && h[stl.top()] <= h[i]) stl.pop();
		l[i] = stl.empty() ? 0 : stl.top();
		if (l[i]) ans += i - l[i] + 1;
		stl.push(i);
	}
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
		while (!str.empty() && h[str.top()] <= h[i]) str.pop();
		r[i] = str.empty() ? 0 : str.top();
		if (r[i]) ans += r[i] - i + 1;
		str.push(i);
	}
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\6 单调栈\海边冲浪.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n;
	long long ans = 0;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!st.empty() && a[st.top()] >= a[i]) st.pop();
		int j = st.empty() ? 0 : st.top();
		f[i] = f[j] + 1ll * (i - j) * a[i];
		st.push(i);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans = max(ans,f[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\6 单调栈\玉蟾宫.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			cin >> a[i][j];
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++) {
			if (a[i][j] == 'R') s[i][j] = 0;
			else s[i][j] = s[i - 1][j] + 1;
		}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++) {
			while (!st[i].empty() && s[i][st[i].top()] >= s[i][j]) st[i].pop();
			l[j] = st[i].empty() ? 0 : st[i].top();
			st[i].push(j);
		}
		for (int j = m;j >= 1;j--) {
			while (!st1[i].empty() && s[i][st1[i].top()] >= s[i][j]) st1[i].pop();
			r[j] = st1[i].empty() ? m + 1 : st1[i].top();
			st1[i].push(j);
		}
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
			ans = max(ans,(r[j] - l[j] - 1) * s[i][j]);
	}
	cout << 3 * ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\7 单调队列\Crowded-Cowd-S.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,d,ans = 0;
	cin >> n >> d;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].x >> s[i].h;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.x < y.x;});
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!q.empty() && s[q.back()].x + d < s[i].x) q.pop_back();
		while (!q.empty() && s[q.front()].h <= s[i].h) q.pop_front();
		q.push_front(i);
		if (s[q.back()].h >= 2 * s[i].h) l[i] = true;
	}
	q.clear();
	for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
		while (!q.empty() && s[q.front()].x - d > s[i].x) q.pop_front();
		while (!q.empty() && s[q.back()].h <= s[i].h) q.pop_back();
		q.push_back(i);
		if (s[q.front()].h >= 2 * s[i].h) r[i] = true;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) ans += (l[i] && r[i]);
	cout << ans << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\7 单调队列\WIL.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,d;
    long long p;
    cin >> n >> p >> d;
    int ans = d;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {cin >> w[i]; s[i] = w[i] + s[i - 1];}
    for (int i = d;i <= n;i++) sd[i] = s[i] - s[i - d];
    for (int L = 1,R = d + 1;R <= n;R++) {
        while (!q.empty() && q.back() - L + 1 < d) q.pop_back();
        while (!q.empty() && sd[q.front()] <= sd[R]) q.pop_front();
        q.push_front(R);
        while (!q.empty() && s[R] - s[L - 1] - sd[q.back()] > p) {
            L++;
            while (!q.empty() && q.back() - L + 1 < d) q.pop_back();
        }
        ans = max(ans,R - L + 1);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\7 单调队列\花盆.cpp: check ---
bool check(int w) {
	q.clear();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!q.empty() && a[q.back()].x + w < a[i].x) q.pop_back();
		while (!q.empty() && a[q.front()].y >= a[i].y) q.pop_front();
		q.push_front(i);
		minn[i] = a[q.back()].y;
	}
	q.clear();
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!q.empty() && a[q.back()].x + w < a[i].x) q.pop_back();
		while (!q.empty() && a[q.front()].y <= a[i].y) q.pop_front();
		q.push_front(i);
		maxn[i] = a[q.back()].y;
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (maxn[i] - minn[i] >= d) return true;
	return false;
}

--- S2-2\7 单调队列\花盆.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> d;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i].x >> a[i].y;
	sort(a + 1,a + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.x < y.x;});
	int L = 1,R = 1e6,ans = 0;
	while (L <= R) {
		int mid = L + R >> 1;
		if (check(mid)) R = mid - 1,ans = mid;
		else L = mid + 1;
	}
	cout << (ans == 0 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\8 单调性优化1\Pencils-and-Boxes.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int n,k,d;
    cin >> n >> k >> d;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    sort(a + 1,a + n + 1);
    memset(dp,false,sizeof dp);
    dp[0] = true;
    int left = 1;
    s[1] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        while (left < i && a[i] - a[left] > d) left++;
        int L = left - 1, R = i - k;
        // [L,R] 上一个盒子的结尾所在范围
        if (L <= R)
            if (s[R + 1] - s[L]) dp[i] = true;
        s[i + 1] = s[i] + (dp[i] == true);
    }
    cout << (dp[n] ? "YES" : "NO") << endl;
    return ;
}

--- S2-2\8 单调性优化1\Pencils-and-Boxes.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) solve();
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\8 单调性优化1\Telephone-Wire.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,c,ans = 1e9;
    cin >> n >> c;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
    memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
    for (int j = h[1];j <= 100;j++)
        dp[1][j] = (j - h[1]) * (j - h[1]);
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        int minn = 2e9;
        for (int j = 1;j <= 100;j++) {
            minn = min(minn,dp[i - 1][j] - c * j);
            if (j >= h[i]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],(j - h[i]) * (j - h[i]) + c * j + minn);
        }
        minn = 2e9;
        for (int j = 100;j >= 1;j--) {
        	minn = min(minn,dp[i - 1][j] + c * j);
        	if (j >= h[i]) dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],(j - h[i]) * (j - h[i]) - c * j + minn);
        }
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= 100;i++) ans = min(ans,dp[n][i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\8 单调性优化1\促销返现.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,c;
    cin >> n >> c;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i];
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        while (!q.empty() && q.back() < i - c + 1) q.pop_back();
        while (!q.empty() && a[q.front()] >= a[i]) q.pop_front();
        q.push_front(i);
        dp[i] = dp[i - 1] + a[i];
        if (i >= c) dp[i] = min(dp[i],dp[i - c] + s[i] - s[i - c] - a[q.back()]);
    }
    cout << dp[n] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\Little-Bird.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[1] = 0;
	deque<int> q;
	q.push_front(1);
	int k;
	cin >> k;
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!q.empty() && q.back() + k < i) q.pop_back();
		if (d[q.back()] <= d[i]) dp[i] = min(dp[i],dp[q.back()] + 1);
		else dp[i] = min(dp[i],dp[q.back()]);
		while (!q.empty() && (dp[i] < dp[q.front()] || dp[i] == dp[q.front()] && d[i] >= d[q.front()])) q.pop_front();
		q.push_front(i);
	}
	cout << dp[n] << endl;
}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\Little-Bird.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> d[i];
	int q;
	cin >> q;
	while (q--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\课后答疑.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m,k,d;
	cin >> n >> m >> k >> d;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	deque<int> q;
	dp[1] = a[1];
	q.push_front(1);
	for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
		while (!q.empty() && i - q.back() - 1 > m) q.pop_back();
		dp[i] = dp[q.back()] - 1ll * d * q.back() + k + 1ll * d * i - d + a[i];
		while (!q.empty() && dp[i] - 1ll * d * i < dp[q.front()] - 1ll * d * q.front()) q.pop_front();
		q.push_front(i);
	}
	cout << dp[n] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\购买饲料.cpp: f ---
long long f(int i,int t) {return dp[i - 1][t] + 1ll * (s[i].x - s[i - 1].x) * t * t - 1ll * t * s[i].c;}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\购买饲料.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int K,e,n;
	cin >> K >> e >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> s[i].x >> s[i].f >> s[i].c;
	sort(s + 1,s + n + 1,[](Node x,Node y) {return x.x < y.x;});
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0][0] = 0;
	deque<int> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		q.clear();
		for (int k = 0;k <= K;k++) {
			while (!q.empty() && q.back() < max(0,k - s[i].f)) q.pop_back();
			while (!q.empty() && f(i,k) <= f(i,q.front())) q.pop_front();
			q.push_front(k);
			int t = q.back();
			dp[i][k] = dp[i - 1][t] + 1ll * (s[i].x - s[i - 1].x) * t * t - 1ll * t * s[i].c + 1ll * k * s[i].c;
		}
	}
	cout << dp[n][K] + 1ll * (e - s[n].x) * K * K << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\跳房子.cpp: check ---
bool check(int g) {
	memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0] = 0;
	int dis1 = max(1,d - g),dis2 = d + g,cur = 0;
	deque<int> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		while (x[i] - x[cur] >= dis1 && cur < i) {
			while (!q.empty() && dp[cur] >= dp[q.front()]) q.pop_front();
			q.push_front(cur);
			cur++;
		}
		while (!q.empty() && x[i] - x[q.back()] > dis2) q.pop_back();
		if (q.empty()) continue;
		dp[i] = max(dp[i],dp[q.back()] + s[i]);
		if (dp[i] >= k) return true;
	}
	return false;
}

--- S2-2\9 单调性优化2\跳房子.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	cin >> n >> d >> k;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> x[i] >> s[i];
	int L = 0,R = x[n],ans = 0;
	while (L <= R) {
		int mid = L + R >> 1;
		if (check(mid)) R = mid - 1,ans = mid;
		else L = mid + 1;
	}
	cout << (ans == 0 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\First!-G.cpp: insert ---
void insert(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
        x = ch[x][i];
        sum[x]++;
    }
    cnt[x]++;
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\First!-G.cpp: topo_sort ---
bool topo_sort() {
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 0;i < 26;i++)
        if (in[i] == 0) q.push(i);
    int cnt = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        cnt++;
        for (auto v : G[u])
            if (--in[v] == 0) q.push(v);
    }
    if (cnt < 26) return true;
    else return false;
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\First!-G.cpp: check ---
bool check(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (int i = 0;i < 26;i++) G[i].clear();
    memset(in,0,sizeof in);
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (cnt[x] == 1) return false;
        for (int j = 0;j < 26;j++)
            if (ch[x][j] && j != i) {
                G[i].push_back(j);
                in[j]++;
            }
        x = ch[x][i];
    }
    if (topo_sort()) return false;
    else return true;
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\First!-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,cur = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> s[i];
        insert(s[i]);
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (check(s[i])) ans[++cur] = s[i];
    cout << cur << endl;
    for (int i = 1;i <= cur;i++) cout << ans[i] << endl;
    return 0;
}

void insert(string s) {

    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
        x = ch[x][i];
        sum[x]++;
    }
    cnt[x]++;
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\【模板】字典树.cpp: query ---
int query(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) return 0;
        x = ch[x][i];
    }
    return sum[x];
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\秘密信息.cpp: query ---
int query(string s) {
    int x = 1,ret = 0;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - '0';
        if (!ch[x][i]) return ret;
        x = ch[x][i];
        ret += cnt[x];
    }
    return ret + sum[x] - cnt[x];
}

void insert(string s,int m) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
        x = ch[x][i];
        sum[x]++;
    }
    cnt[x]++;
    a[x][m] = true;
}

--- S3-1\1 字典树综合\阅读理解.cpp: query ---
void query(string s) {
    int x = 1;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (!ch[x][i]) {
            cout << endl;
            return ;
        }
        x = ch[x][i];
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (a[x][i]) cout << i << ' ';
    cout << endl;
    return ;
}

        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {

--- S3-1\10 阶段测试\爬塔游戏.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> c[i];
    for (int i = 0;i < (1 << n);i++) {
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++)
            if ((i >> j) & 1) h[i] += a[j];
    }
    for (int i = 0;i < (1 << n);i++) // 现在的状态
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++) // 上次跳是哪一位
            if ((i >> j) & 1) dp[i] = max(dp[i],dp[i ^ (i & -i)] + c[h[i]]); // 取出第 j 位的状态加上现在的高度
    cout << dp[(1 << n) - 1] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\10 阶段测试\魔法草药.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
		cin >> x[i] >> y[i];
		flag[x[i] - 1][y[i] - 1] = flag[y[i] - 1][x[i] - 1] = true;
	}
	dp[0] = 0,ans[0]++;
	for (int i = 1;i < (1 << n);i++) { // 顺序不影响，不妨设最后一个加入的是倒数第一个 1
		int x = i & -i,cur = __lg(x);
		int tmp = i ^ x,cnt = 0; // 变为 0
		for (int j = 0;j < n;j++)
			if ((tmp >> j) & 1 && flag[cur][j]) cnt++;
		dp[i] = dp[tmp] + cnt;
		ans[dp[i]]++;
	}
	for (int i = 0;i <= m;i++) cout << ans[i] << ' ';
	cout << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\【模板】字典树.cpp: insert ---
void insert(string s) {
    Node *x = root;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (x -> ch[i] == nullptr) x -> ch[i] = newNode();
        x = x -> ch[i];
        x -> sum++;
    }
    x -> cnt++;
}

--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\【模板】字典树.cpp: query ---
int query(string s) {
    Node *x = root;
    for (auto c : s) {
        int i = c - 'a';
        if (x -> ch[i] == nullptr) return 0;
        x = x -> ch[i];
    }
    return x -> sum;
}
