--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\会场预约.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    while (n--) {
        char op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == 'A') {
            int start,end,cnt = 0;
            cin >> start >> end;
            Node t = {start,end};
            auto it = s.find(t);
            while (it != s.end()) {
                cnt++;
                s.erase(it);
                it = s.find(t);
            }
            s.insert(t);
            cout << cnt << endl;
        }
        else cout << s.size() << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\文本编辑器.cpp: link ---
void link(Node *x,Node *y) {
    x -> r = y;
    y -> l = x;
}

--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\文本编辑器.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    char op;
    cin >> op;
    switch (op) {
        case 'I' : {
            p = new Node;
            cin >> (p -> val);
            link(now -> l,p);
            link(p,now);
            break ;
        }
        case 'D' : {
            if (now == tail) break;
            p = now;
            now = now -> r;
            link(p -> l,p -> r);
            delete p;
            break;
        }
        case 'R' : {
            if (now == tail) break;
            now = now -> r;
            break;
        }
        case 'L' : {
            if (now -> l == head) break;
            now = now -> l;
            break;
        }
    }
    return ;
}

--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\文本编辑器.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    head = new Node;
    tail = new Node;
    link(head,tail);
    now = new Node;
    now = tail;
    while (n--) solve();
    for (Node *x = head -> r;x != tail;x = x -> r) cout << (x -> val);
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\2 指针和set容器\送花.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int op;
    while (cin >> op) {
        if (op == -1) break;
        switch (op) {
            case 1 : {
                int w,c;
                cin >> w >> c;
                if (s.find(c) == s.end()) {
                    mp[c] = w;
                    s.insert(c);
                }
                break;
            }
            case 2 : {
                if (s.size()) s.erase(--s.end());
                break;
            }
            case 3 : {
                if (s.size()) s.erase(s.begin());
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    int sumw = 0,sumc = 0;
    for (auto t : s)
        sumc += t,sumw += mp[t];
    cout << sumw << ' ' << sumc << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\KLO-Building-blocks.cpp: balance ---
void balance() {
    if (l.size() > r.size()) {
        auto it = l.end();
        it--;
        suml -= *it, sumr += *it;
        l.erase(it); r.insert(*it);
    } else if (l.size() < r.size() - 1) {
        auto it = r.begin();
        suml += *it, sumr -= *it;
        l.insert(*it); r.erase(it);
    }
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\KLO-Building-blocks.cpp: ins ---
void ins(int x) {
    if (x < *r.begin()) l.insert(x),suml += x;
    else r.insert(x),sumr += x;
    balance();
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\KLO-Building-blocks.cpp: del ---
void del(int x) {
    if (x < *r.begin()) l.erase(l.find(x)), suml -= x;
    else r.erase(r.find(x)),sumr -= x;
    balance();
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\KLO-Building-blocks.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n,k;
    long long ans = 1e18;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> h[i];
    for (int i = 1;i < k;i++) ins(h[i]);
    for (int i = k;i <= n;i++) { // 右端点
        ins(h[i]);
        if (i - k > 0) del(h[i - k]);
        ans = min(ans,sumr - suml - (k % 2) * (*r.begin()));
    }
    cout << ans << '\n';
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\Monkey-King.cpp: find ---
int find(int x) {return x == f[x] ? x : f[x] = find(f[x]);}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\Monkey-King.cpp: Union ---
void Union(int x,int y) {
    int fx = find(x),fy = find(y);
    if (s[fx].size() > s[fy].size()) swap(fx,fy);
    auto it = --s[fx].end();
    s[fx].insert(*it / 2);
    s[fx].erase(it);
    it = --s[fy].end();
    s[fy].insert(*it / 2);
    s[fy].erase(it);
    for (auto t : s[fx]) s[fy].insert(t);
    s[fx].clear();
    f[fx] = fy;
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\Monkey-King.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	cin >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		f[i] = i;
		s[i].insert(a[i]);
	}
	while (m--) {
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		if (find(x) == find(y)) {
			cout << -1 << endl;
			continue;
		}
		Union(x,y);
		cout << *--s[find(y)].end() << endl;
	}
	return ;
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\Monkey-King.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	while (cin >> n) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\报表统计.cpp: insert_s ---
void insert_s(int x) {
    auto it = num.lower_bound(x);
    if (it != num.end()) minn = min(minn,*it - x);
    if (it != num.begin()) minn = min(minn,abs(x - *(--it)));
    num.insert(x);
}

--- S3-1\3 set容器进阶\报表统计.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        insert_s(a[i]);
        last[i] = a[i];
        if (i > 1) gap.insert(abs(a[i] - a[i - 1]));
    }
    while (m--) {
        string op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == "INSERT") {
            int i,k;
            cin >> i >> k;
            gap.insert(abs(k - last[i]));
            if (i < n) {
                gap.insert(abs(k - a[i + 1]));
                gap.erase(gap.find(abs(a[i + 1] - last[i])));
            }
            last[i] = k;
            insert_s(k);
        }
        else if (op == "MIN_GAP") cout << *gap.begin() << endl;
        else cout << minn << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\addition-chains.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step) {
    if (a[step - 1] * (1 << (m - step + 1)) < n) return ;
    if (flag) return ;
    if (step > m) {
        if (a[step - 1] == n) {
            flag = true;
            for (int i = 0;i <= m;i++) cout << a[i] << ' ';
            cout << endl;
        }
        return ;
    }
    for (int i = 0;i < step;i++)
        for (int j = i;j < step;j++) {
            a[step] = a[i] + a[j];
            if (a[step] <= a[step - 1]) continue;
            if (a[step] > n) break;
            dfs(step + 1);
        }
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\addition-chains.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	flag = false,a[0] = 1;
    for (m = 0; ;m++) {
        dfs(1);
        if (flag) break;
    }
    return ;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\addition-chains.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    while (true) {
        cin >> n;
        if (n == 0) break;
        solve();
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\八数码难题.cpp: evaluate ---
int evaluate() {
    int s = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 3;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 3;j++)
            s += abs(e[a[i][j]][0] - i) + abs(e[a[i][j]][1] - j);
    return s;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\八数码难题.cpp: sep ---
void sep(int n) {
    for (int i = 3;i >= 1;i--)
        for (int j = 3;j >= 1;j--) {
            a[i][j] = n % 10,n /= 10;
            if (a[i][j] == 0) X = i,Y = j;
        }
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\八数码难题.cpp: merge ---
int merge() {
    int ret = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 3;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 3;j++)
            ret = ret * 10 + a[i][j];
    return ret;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\八数码难题.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,int cnt) {
    int d = evaluate();
    if (d == 0) flag = true,ans = min(ans,cnt);
    if (flag) return ;
    if (cnt + d > m) return ;
    for (int i = 0;i <= 3;i++) {
        int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
        if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nx > 3 || ny > 3) continue;
        swap(a[x][y],a[nx][ny]);
        dfs(nx,ny,cnt + 1);
        swap(a[x][y],a[nx][ny]);
    }
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\八数码难题.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    sep(n);
    for (m = evaluate(); ;m++) {
        dfs(X,Y,0);
        if (flag) break;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\铁盘整理.cpp: evaluate ---
int evaluate() {
    int s = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (abs(a[i] - a[i + 1]) != 1) s++;
    return s;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\铁盘整理.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int cnt) {
    int d = evaluate();
    if (d == 0) flag = true,ans = min(ans,cnt);
    if (flag) return ;
    if (cnt + d > m) return ;
    for (int i = 2;i <= n;i++) {
        reverse(a + 1,a + i + 1);
        dfs(cnt + 1);
        reverse(a + 1,a + i + 1);
    }
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\铁盘整理.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        r[i] = a[i];
    }
    sort(r + 1,r + n + 1);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        a[i] = lower_bound(r + 1,r + n + 1,a[i]) - r;
    a[n + 1] = n + 1;
    for (m = evaluate(); ;m++) {
        dfs(0);
        if (flag) break;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\骑士精神.cpp: evaluate ---
int evaluate() {
    int s = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 5;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
            if (c[i][j] == '*') continue;
            if (c[i][j] != e[i][j]) s++;
        }
    return s;
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\骑士精神.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,int cnt) {
    int d = evaluate();
    if (d == 0) flag = true,ans = min(ans,cnt);
    if (flag) return ;
    if (cnt + d > m) return ;
    for (int i = 0;i <= 7;i++) {
        int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
        if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nx > 5 || ny > 5) continue;
        swap(c[x][y],c[nx][ny]);
        dfs(nx,ny,cnt + 1);
        swap(c[x][y],c[nx][ny]);
    }
}

--- S3-1\4 迭代加深\骑士精神.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
    int X,Y;
    flag = 0,ans = 1e9;
    for (int i = 1;i <= 5;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= 5;j++) {
            cin >> c[i][j];
            if (c[i][j] == '*') X = i,Y = j;
        }
    for (m = evaluate();m <= 15;m++) {
        dfs(X,Y,0);
        if (flag) break;
    }
    cout << (ans == 1e9 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
    return ;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\书架整理.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
	int n,q;
	long long sum = 0;
	cin >> n >> q;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> a[i];
		sum += a[i];
	}
	cout << sum << '\n';
	while (q--) {
		int l,r,x;
		sum = 0;
		cin >> l >> r >> x;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			if (a[i] >= l && a[i] <= r) a[i] = x;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) sum += a[i];
		cout << sum << '\n';
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\序列.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
	while (m--) {
		int x,y;
		cin >> x >> y;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			if (a[i] == x) a[i] = y;
		int ans = 2147483647;
		for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
			for (int j = i + 1;j <= n;j++)
				if (a[i] == a[j]) ans = min(ans,j - i);
		cout << ans << '\n';
	}
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\逃脱魔法.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int x,int y,int cnt) {
	if (c[x][y] == 'S' && cnt >= 4) { // 可以成为环
		flag = true,ans = cnt;
		return ;
	}
	if (c[x][y] == 'S' && cnt >= 1 && cnt < 4) return ;
	if (flag) return ;
	if (cnt > M) return ;
	for (int i = 0;i < 4;i++) {
		int nx = x + dx[i],ny = y + dy[i];
		if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > m) continue;
		if (c[nx][ny] == '#') continue;
		if (vis[nx][ny]) continue;
		vis[nx][ny] = true;
		dfs(nx,ny,cnt + 1);
		vis[nx][ny] = false;
	}
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\逃脱魔法.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++) {
			cin >> c[i][j];
			if (c[i][j] == 'S') X = i,Y = j;
			if (c[i][j] == '.') len++;
		}
	for (M = 4;M <= min(1200,len);M++) {
		dfs(X,Y,0);
		if (flag) break;
	}
	cout << (flag ? ans : -1) << '\n';
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\通讯录.cpp: insert ---
void insert(string s) {
	int x = 1;
	for (auto c : s) {
		int i = c - '0';
		if (!ch[x][i]) ch[x][i] = ++id;
		x = ch[x][i];
		sum[x]++;
	}
	cnt[x]++;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\通讯录.cpp: query ---
bool query(string s) {
	int x = 1;
	for (auto c : s) {
		int i = c - '0';
		if (!ch[x][i]) return false;
		x = ch[x][i];
	}
	return sum[x] >= 2;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\通讯录.cpp: solve ---
void solve() {
	id = 1;
	memset(ch,0,sizeof ch);
	memset(cnt,0,sizeof cnt);
	memset(sum,0,sizeof sum);
	int n;
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> s[i];
		insert(s[i]);
	}
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
		if (query(s[i])) { // 自己不能算
			cout << "Yes" << endl;
			return ;
		}
	cout << "No" << endl;
	return ;
}

--- S3-1\5 阶段测试\通讯录.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int T;
	cin >> T;
	while (T--) solve();
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\GEPPETTO.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m,ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) cin >> x[i] >> y[i];
    for (int i = 0;i <= (1 << n) - 1;i++) {
        bool flag = true;
        for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++)
            if (((i >> (x[j] - 1)) & 1) && ((i >> (y[j] - 1)) & 1)) {flag = false; break;}
        ans += flag;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\内建函数实现.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    // 1. 求二进制中1的个数

    __builtin_popcount(n); // O(log log n)

    int cnt = 0;
    while (n)
        cnt += n & 1,n >>= 1; // O(log n)

    // 2. 求二进制中1的个数的奇偶性

    __builtin_parity(n); // O(log log n)

    int cnt = 0;
    while (n)
        n -= n & -n,cnt++; // O(log n)
    bool parity = cnt & 1;

    // 3. 求前导0个数

    __builtin_clz(n); // O(log log n)

    (1 << j) > x

    // 4. 求末尾0的个数

    __builtin_ctz(n); // O(log log n)

    x & -x
    (x & (1 << j)) == 1;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\异或非或.cpp: check ---
bool check(long long tmp) {
    if ((s[n] & tmp) != 0) return false;
    memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
    unordered_map<long long,int> mp;
    mp[s[0] & tmp] = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        long long target = s[i] & tmp;
        if (mp.count(target))
            dp[i] = mp[target] + 1;
        if (dp[i] > 0) mp[target] = max(dp[i],mp[target]);
    }
    return dp[n] >= m;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\异或非或.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    long long ans = 0,mask = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> a[i];
        s[i] = s[i - 1] ^ a[i];
    }
    for (int k = 62;k >= 0;k--) {
        long long tmp = mask | (1ll << k);
        if (check(tmp)) mask = tmp;
        else ans |= (1ll << k);
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\找筷子.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    int n,ans = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int a;
        cin >> a;
        ans ^= a;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\浏览器.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,a,b,c,d;
    long long cnt1 = 0,cnt2 = 0;
    cin >> n >> a >> b >> c >> d >> x[0];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        x[i] = 1ll * (a * x[i - 1] % d * x[i - 1] % d + b * x[i - 1] % d + c) % d;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        if (__builtin_parity(x[i])) cnt1++;
        else cnt2++;
    cout << 1ll * cnt1 * cnt2 << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\绝世好题.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,maxn = 0;
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        maxn = 0;
        for (int k = 0;k <= 30;k++)
            if (a[i] & (1 << k)) maxn = max(maxn,dp[k] + 1);
        for (int k = 0;k <= 30;k++)
            if (a[i] & (1 << k)) dp[k] = maxn;
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 0;i <= 30;i++) ans = max(ans,dp[i]);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\6 位运算综合\起床困难综合症.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    long long ans = 0,s = 0;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> op[i] >> t[i];
    for (int i = 30;i >= 0;i--) {
        bool flag1 = true,flag2 = false;
        for (int j = 1;j <= n;j++) {
            bool tmp = (t[j] >> i) & 1;
            if (op[j] == "AND") flag1 &= tmp,flag2 &= tmp;
            else if (op[j] == "OR") flag1 |= tmp,flag2 |= tmp;
            else flag1 ^= tmp,flag2 ^= tmp;
        }
        if (flag2) ans |= (1 << i);
        else if (flag1 && (s | (1 << i)) <= m)
            s |= 1 << i,ans |= 1 << i;
    }
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\Balanced-Cow-Subsets-G.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,int sum,int mask) {
    if (step > lim) {
        if (lim == n / 2) L[++cntl] = {sum,mask};
        else R[++cntr] = {sum,mask};
        return ;
    }
    dfs(step + 1,sum,mask);
    dfs(step + 1,sum + a[step],mask | (1 << (step - 1)));
    dfs(step + 1,sum - a[step],mask | (1 << (step - 1)));
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\Balanced-Cow-Subsets-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    lim = n / 2;
    dfs(1,0,0);
    lim = n;
    dfs(n / 2 + 1,0,0);
    sort(L + 1,L + cntl + 1);
    sort(R + 1,R + cntr + 1);
    for (int i = 1;i <= cntl;i++) {
        int pos1 = lower_bound(R + 1,R + cntr + 1,L[i]) - R;
        int pos2 = upper_bound(R + 1,R + cntr + 1,L[i]) - R - 1;
        for (int j = pos1;j <= pos2;j++)
            mp[(R[j].mask | L[i].mask)] = true;
    }
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i < (1 << n);i++) ans += mp[i];
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\还是N皇后.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,int col,int lr,int rl) {
    if (step >= n) {
        ans++;
        return ;
    }
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) {
        if (c[step][i] == '*' && !((col >> i) & 1) && !((lr >> i) & 1) && !((rl >> i) & 1))
            dfs(step + 1,col | (1 << i),(lr | (1 << i)) << 1,(rl | (1 << i)) >> 1);
    }
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\还是N皇后.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++) cin >> c[i][j];
    dfs(0,0,0,0);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\还是N皇后.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,int col,int lr,int rl) {
    if (step >= n) {
        ans++;
        return ;
    }
    int t = col | lr | rl;
    t = ~t;
    t &= ((1 << n) - 1);
    while (t) {
        int i = t & -t;
        t -= i;
        if (c[step][int(log2(i))] == '.') continue;
        dfs(step + 1,col | i,(lr | i) << 1,(rl | i) >> 1);
    }
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\还是N皇后.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++) cin >> c[i][j];
    dfs(0,0,0,0);
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\邦德.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int step,int col,double m) {
    if (m < ans) return ;
    if (step >= n) {
        ans = max(ans,m);
        return ;
    }
    int t = ~col;
    t &= (1 << n) - 1;
    while (t) {
        int x = t & -t;
        t -= x;
        dfs(step + 1,col | x,m * a[step][__lg(x)] / 100.0);
    }
}

            scanf("%d",&a[i][j]);
    dfs(0,0,100.00);
    printf("%.6lf\n",ans);
    return 0;

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\邦德.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++)
            scanf("%d",&a[i][j]);
    dp[0] = 100;
    for (int i = 0;i < (1 << n);i++) {
        int x = -1;
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++)
            if ((i >> j) & 1) x++;
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++)
            if (((i >> j) % 1) == 0)
                dp[i | (1 << j)] = max(dp[i | (1 << j)],dp[i] * a[j][x + 1] / 100.0);
    }
    printf("%.6lf\n",dp[(1 << n) - 1]);
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\7 状态压缩基础1\邦邦的大合唱站队.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	int n,m;
	cin >> n >> m;
	for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
		cin >> a[i];
		for (int j = 1;j <= m;j++) s[i][j] = s[i - 1][j];
		s[i][a[i]]++;
	}
	memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
	dp[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 0;i < (1 << m);i++) {
		int L = 0;
		for (int j = 0;j < m;j++)
			if ((i >> j) & 1) L += s[n][j + 1];
		L++; // 新队伍起点
		for (int j = 0;j < m;j++) {
			if ((i >> j) & 1) continue;
			int R = L + s[n][j + 1] - 1;
			dp[i | (1 << j)] = min(dp[i | (1 << j)],dp[i] + s[n][j + 1] - (s[R][j + 1] - s[L - 1][j + 1]));
		}
	}
	cout << dp[(1 << m) - 1] << endl;
	return 0;
}

--- S3-1\8 状态压缩基础2\Mixed-up-Cows-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,k;
    long long ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> k;
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) cin >> s[i];
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) dp[1 << i][i] = 1;
    for (int i = 0;i <= (1 << n) - 1;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j < n;j++) {
            if ((i >> j) & 1 == 0) continue;
            for (int p = 0;p < n;p++)
                if ((i >> p) & 1 && abs(s[j] - s[p]) > k)
                    dp[i][j] += dp[i ^ (1 << j)][p];
        }
    for (int j = 0;j < n;j++) ans += dp[(1 << n) - 1][j];
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\8 状态压缩基础2\Moovie-Mooving-G.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,l,ans = 1e9;
    cin >> n >> l;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> d[i] >> c[i];
        for (int j = 1;j <= c[i];j++) cin >> a[i][j];
    }
    memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0] = 0;
    for (int i = 0;i < (1 << n);i++) {
        int s = i,cnt = 0;
        while (s) {
            int p = s & -s;
            int j = __lg(p) + 1;
            int k = upper_bound(a[j] + 1,a[j] + c[j] + 1,dp[i ^ p]) - a[j] - 1;
            if (k > 0) dp[i] = max(dp[i],a[j][k] + d[j]);
            s -= p,cnt++;
        }
        if (dp[i] >= l) ans = min(ans,cnt);
    }
    cout << (ans == 1e9 ? -1 : ans) << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\8 状态压缩基础2\PRZ.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int W,n;
    cin >> W >> n;
    for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) cin >> t[i] >> w[i];
    for (int i = 1;i < (1 << n);i++) {
        int j = i & -i;
        j = __lg(j);
        sum[i] = sum[i ^ (1 << j)] + w[j];
        maxt[i] = max(maxt[i ^ (1 << j)],t[j]);
    }
    memset(dp,0x3f,sizeof dp);
    dp[0] = 0;
    for (int i = 0;i < (1 << n);i++) {
        for (int j = i;j;j = i & (j - 1))
            if (sum[j] <= W) dp[i] = min(dp[i],dp[i ^ j] + maxt[j]);
    }
    cout << dp[(1 << n) - 1] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\互不侵犯.cpp: f ---
int f(int x) {
    int ret = 0;
    while (x) ret += x & 1,x >>= 1;
    return ret;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\互不侵犯.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,K,cnt = 0;
    long long ans = 0;
    cin >> n >> K;
    for (int i = 0;i < (1 << n);i++) {
        if ((i & (i << 1))) continue;
        p[++cnt] = i;
    }
    dp[0][1][0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 1;j <= cnt;j++) // 当前行状态
            for (int m = f(p[j]);m <= K;m++) {
                for (int k = 1;k <= cnt;k++) { // 第 i - 1 行状态
                    if (p[j] & p[k]) continue;
                    if ((p[j] >> 1) & p[k]) continue;
                    if ((p[j] << 1) & p[k]) continue;
                    dp[i][j][m] += dp[i - 1][k][m - f(p[j])];
                }
            }
    for (int j = 1;j <= cnt;j++) ans += dp[n][j][K];
    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\水果蛋糕.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x) {
    int last = 4;
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        if (x % 3 == last) return false;
        last = x % 3,x /= 3;
    }
    return true;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\水果蛋糕.cpp: and3 ---
bool and3(int x,int y) {
    for (int i = 1;i <= m;i++) {
        if (x % 3 == y % 3) return false;
        x /= 3,y /= 3;
    }
    return true;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\炮兵阵地.cpp: f ---
int f(int x) {
    int ret = 0;
    while (x) ret += x & 1,x >>= 1;
    return ret;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\蒙德里安的梦想.cpp: check ---
bool check(int x) {
    int cnt = 0;
    for (int i = 1;i <= w;i++) {
        if (!(x & 1)) cnt++;
        else {
            if (cnt & 1) return false;
            cnt = 0;
        }
        x >>= 1;
    }
    if (cnt & 1) return false;
    return true;
}

--- S3-1\9 棋盘状压DP\蒙德里安的梦想.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    while (cin >> h >> w) {
        if (h == 0 && w == 0) break;
        memset(dp,0,sizeof dp);
        for (int i = 0;i < (1 << w);i++)
            if (check(i)) dp[1][i] = 1;
        for (int i = 2;i <= h;i++)
            for (int j = 0;j < (1 << w);j++)
                for (int k = 0;k < (1 << w);k++)
                    if (((j & k) == 0) && check(j | k)) dp[i][j] += dp[i - 1][k];
        cout << dp[h][0] << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\分组背包.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,W;
    cin >> n >> W;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        cin >> s[i];
        for (int j = 1;j <= s[i];j++)
            cin >> w[i][j] >> v[i][j];
    }
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++)
        for (int j = 0;j <= W;j++) {
            dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j];
            for (int k = 1;k <= s[i];k++)
                if (j >= w[i][k]) dp[i][j] = max(dp[i][j],dp[i - 1][j - w[i][k]] + v[i][k]);
        }
    cout << dp[n][W] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\有线电视网.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto [v,w] : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[u] += siz[v];
        for (int j = siz[u];j >= 1;j--)
            for (int k = 1;k <= min(siz[v],j);k++)
                dp[u][j] = max(dp[u][j],dp[u][j - k] + dp[v][k] - w);
    }
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\有线电视网.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    int n,m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n - m;i++) {
        cin >> k[i];
        for (int j = 1;j <= k[i];j++) {
            int x,c;
            cin >> x >> c;
            G[x].push_back({i,c}); G[i].push_back({x,c});
        }
    }
    for (int i = n - m + 1;i <= n;i++) cin >> a[i];
    memset(dp,-0x3f,sizeof dp);
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) dp[i][0] = 0;
    for (int i = n - m + 1;i <= n;i++)
        siz[i] = 1, dp[i][1] = a[i];
    dfs(1,0);
    for (int i = n;i >= 1;i--) {
        if (dp[1][i] >= 0) {
            cout << i << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\选课-增强版.cpp: init ---
void init(int u,int pa) {
	dp[u][1] = sc[u];
	siz[u] = 1;
	for (auto v : G[u]) {
		if (v == pa) continue;
		init(v,u);
		siz[u] += siz[v];
	}
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\选课-增强版.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        for (int j = min(siz[u],m);j >= 1;j--)
            for (int k = 0;k <= min(j - 1,siz[v]);k++)
                dp[u][j] = max(dp[u][j],dp[u][j - k] + dp[v][k]);
    }
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\选课-增强版.cpp: main ---
int main() {
	ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
	cin.tie(0); cout.tie(0);
    cin >> n >> m;
    m++;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int u;
        cin >> u >> sc[i];
        G[u].push_back(i); G[i].push_back(u);
    }
    init(0,-1);
    dfs(0,-1);
    cout << dp[0][m] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\选课.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        for (int j = m;j >= 1;j--)
            for (int k = 0;k < j;k++)
                dp[u][j] = max(dp[u][j],dp[u][j - k] + dp[v][k]);
    }
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\选课.cpp: main ---
int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    m++;
    for (int i = 1;i <= n;i++) {
        int u;
        cin >> u >> sc[i];
        G[u].push_back(i); G[i].push_back(u);
        dp[i][1] = sc[i];
    }
    dfs(0,-1);
    cout << dp[0][m] << endl;
    return 0;
}

--- S3-2\1 树上背包\选课线性版.cpp: dfs ---
void dfs(int u,int pa) {
    int id = ++tim;
    val[id] = sc[u];
    siz[id] = 1;
    for (auto v : G[u]) {
        if (v == pa) continue;
        dfs(v,u);
        siz[id] += siz[dfn[v]];
    }
    dfn[u] = id;
}
